Uniform title: parameśvarīmatatantra
Manuscript : NGMPP manuscript 5-5148 Reel no: B 133/3
Notes:

Revision 0: Sept. 18, 2010 chapter 15 to end added

Publication country : India
########################################################################### 


oṃ namaḥ śrīmahābhairavāya


śrīdevyuvāca


śrutaṃ devamayāsarvaṃ tvatprasādāvadhāritaṃ |
adhunāśrotumicchāmi pāraṃparya kramāgataṃ ||

kathaṃ meruḥ samutpanna uddhārastasya kīdṛśaḥ |
santānaṃ kīdṛśaṃ deva yatrotpannaṃ carācaraṃ ||

ṣaḍvidhañca kulāmnāyaṃ ṣoḍhānyāsenasaṃyutaṃ |
mantramudrāśca vidyāśca anekākāravismayam ||

kathaṃ kena prakāreṇa meru madhye vyavasthitam |
vāgīśvarī mahāmāyā cāmuṇḍā kulanāyikā ||

mṛtyuṃjayā mahākālī troṭalātripurabhairavī |
samaya vidyā kulākārākhyā siddhāmārttaṇḍabhairavī ||

mahāsammohanī nityā kṛtāntī muktidāparā |
kulamātā mahādeva saṭprakārāṃ vyavasthitā ||

yatprasādāt svayaṃ brahmā sṛjate sa carācaram |
māyāśaktiḥ parāguhyā yayā sidhojanārdanaḥ ||

māyājālaṃ ca kurūte rūpāṇi ca vahūni ca |
haṃsavāgeśvarī nāma śeṣasampāditāt tu yā ||

sarasvatī mahāvidyā yā dattā ṣaḍmukhasya ca |
kāmanirnāśanī caiva punastasya prakopanī ||

sa vāṇadhanuṣaścaiva mohayanti carācaram |
yasyā vidyā prasādena siddhosau makaradhvajaḥ ||

vakraprastārikā vidyā brahmanityā(vidyā) tathaiva ca |
saṃjīvanī mahāvidyā yayā kālo vaśīkṛtaḥ ||

garbhayāgaṃ punardeva putrasyotpatti kāraṇaṃ |
viṣasaṃkramaṇī vidyā punastasya praṇāśinī ||

vidyākāmadughānāma saptapātālasādhanī |
dū(tṛ)tīyaṃ ca vidhānaṃ ca kanyānāṃ ca sureśvara ||

p. 1b)

meru madhye kathaṃ devasamutpannaṃ carācaraṃ |
etat sarvaṃ samāsena prasādaṃ kuru bhairava ||


śrībhairava uvāca


śṛṇu devi paraṃ guhyaṃ merorūdhāramuttamaṃ |
yaṃ dṛṣṭvā vidhiyogena sarvapāpaiḥ pramucyate ||

karmaṇā manasā vācā yatpāpaṃ samupārjjitaṃ |
tatsarvaṃ kṣayamāpnoti dṛṣṭvā meruṃ paraṃ śubhaṃ ||

sa yoniḥ sarvavidyānāṃ kaulikasya viśeṣataḥ |
santānantata utpanna ṣaḍvidhañca kulakramaṃ ||

kulaṃśarīramityāhuḥ śaktirūpaṃ vyavasthitaṃ |
punaścākāśa rūpeṇa jñānaṃ kaulamudāhṛtam ||

tatra madhye samutpannā ājñāparamadurllabhā |
navakoṭi pramāṇena pūrvoktaṃ kubjikāmataṃ ||

caturāśītisāhasraṃ tatra madhye mayoddhṛtaṃ |
punastatraivamadhye tu uddhṛtaṃ merumuttamaṃ ||

pāraṃparyakramaṃ divyaṃ tatra madhye vyavasthitam |
uddhāraṃ sarvavidyānāṃ utpannaṃ tatra madhyataḥ ||

śāstre śāstre sthitā mantrā uddhṛtāni bahudhāni ca |
sāmartha rahitāḥ sarve sakhilāvighnadāyikāḥ (kīrtitā) ||

na sidhyaṃti varārohe ye sthitā meruvāhyataḥ |
makālamāye repho'gniruviṣṇurvidrutāmbara ||

yaścabhūtāmnayā devi merurmelāpa rodhanāt |
merumadhyoddhṛtā vidyā merumadhyoddhṛtaḥ kramaḥ ||

mahāguru prasādena sidhyate nātrasaṃśayaḥ |
na dīkṣā na kulaṃ caryā na jñānaṃ kaulikodbhavaṃ ||

ājñā caivādhikāraśca yāvat meruṃ na paśyati |
tāvadbhramati saṃsāre vikalendriyavatsadā ||

sa pāapaḥ paśuvaddevi tāvannaive dṛśī gatiḥ |

merau dṛṣṭe bhaveddikṣā merau dṛṣṭe malakṣayaḥ ||

merau dṛṣṭe parāṃ śāntimacireṇādhigachati |
tasmāt sarvaprayatnena draṣṭavyo varavarṇini ||

p. 2a)

ājñāsiddhiḥ pravartteta yatkāmaṃ taddhruvaṃ labhet |
yatra sthāne śubhe ramye uddhṛtaṃ ca mahānagaṃ ||

vidyāprastārasaṃyuktaṃ paṃcāśīti purodbhavaṃ |
devadānavasidhāśca yakṣagandharvakinnarāḥ ||

antarikṣasthitāḥ sarve paśyanti varavarṇini |
namaskāraṃ prakurvanti nirmalatvena hetunā ||

sarvapāpaiḥ pramucyante dṛṣṭvāmeruṃ paraṃ śubhaṃ |
susaṃpūrṇaḥ kulācāryā etacchāstrārtha bodhakaḥ ||

uddharedvidhiyuktātmā paripūrṇā niścitāmatiḥ (paripūrṇo viniścayaḥ; pūrṇo 
niścitadhīḥ kramāt) |
vidhihīnaṃ na karttavyaṃ yadi sākṣātmaheśvaraḥ ||

tasmāt sarvaprayatnena vidhānaṃ śobhanaṃ śubhaṃ |
paṃcopacārasaṃyuktaṃ gandhapuṣpasamanvitam ||

karpūrakuṃkumairmiśuṃliptvā meruṃ samuddharet |
bhūmipīṭhe paṭhe vāpi atha śaile suśobhane ||

khaṭikāyāṃ likhetprājñe rekhāśca bhuvanāni ca |
pūrvapaścimataścaiva uttaraṃ dakṣiṇena tu ||

koṣṭakānivicitrāṇi śatamekona saptatiḥ |
nābhimālāṃ parityajya aṣṭamādi guhānanāḥ ||

kumāraṃ agrataḥ kṛtvā dve dve vṛddhāni yāni ca |
vāmadakṣiṇabhagnāni urddhabhāgakrameṇa tu ||

netra veda ritu varjaṃ yathā saṃkhyā diśoraviḥ |
bhagnāścaivamadhobhāge ṣaṭprakārāṇi pūrvavat ||

bhagne bhagne mahādevi ye sthitāḥ kathayāmite |
kaivalyaṃ lokabhūtāni ṛṣayo navakastathā ||

ekādaśena saṃyuktamata ūrdhaṃ tataḥ śṛṇu (ūrddha bhāga krameṇa tu) |
viśve(viṣṇu)rūdrā guhāścaiva mātaro bhairavā nanāḥ ||

nāmaikaṃ merusaṃjñaṃ ca tatrasarvaṃ samuddharet |
p. 2b) kailāsaṃ pūrva bhāge tu dakṣiṇe gandhamādanam ||

mandaraṃ paścimasthaśca uttare niṣadhālayam |
catuḥśṛṃgo mahāmeruḥ sarvakāmārthasādhakaḥ ||

prastāraṃ tatra kurvīta sarvavidyālayaṃ param |
akārādi visargāntaṃ soḍaśaiva svarāllikhet ||

catustriṃśati varṇāni paṃcasvaravibhūṣitān |
prathamaṃ svarahīnaśca dvitīyaṃ svarasaṃyutaṃ ||

ekaikasya tu varṇasya bheda dvayaṃ prakīrtītam (dvayamudāhṛtam) |
pūrvaṃ pūrveṇasaṃyuktaṃ dvitīyena dvitīyakaṃ ||

ṣaḍardhena tṛtīyaṃ tu caturthena caturthakaṃ |
paṃcame paṃcamaṃ yojyaṃ oṃkārādi samudharet ||

dhruvanābhigataṃ kuryāt pūrvā (nte, di) varṇa pūrvakaṃ |
tasyānte gandhaśikharaṃ mandarānte tṛtīyakam ||

uttarānte punarddevi vedavarṇaṃ vyavasthitaṃ |
dhruvādau dhruvamanteṣu (dhruvādau vā dhruvampāno?; dhruvādau dhruva 
madhyānte) varṇā bhūtarasāni ca ||

rāmānāṃ vedamadhye tu sthitāścaiva napuṃsakāḥ |
punasteṣvādi cānteṣu pūrvādārabhya cottare ||

sthitāste kramayogena varṇāstu vasavonighe (vasu saṃkhyayā) |
kailāse tatra varṇānāṃ madhaścatvārimandirāt (kailāse tu ṛvarṇānāmadho 
bhāge varānane; kailāse tva ṛvarṇābhyāṃ catvāri bhuvanānyadhaḥ) |
kāntādi saṃsthitāstatra saṃkhyāsaṃkhyairvyavasthitā (veda saṃkhyā) ||

kramamadhyagatāstrīṇi saṃsthitā gandhamādane |
mandare i ḷ urdhe tu vedavarṇā vyavasthitāḥ ||

ḷ bhrumadhyagatāstrīṇi (madhyāstu yo * *?) uttare niṣadālaye |
ākāśe mandare devi kailāse bhairavānane ||

tasyādiścaiva anteṣu (dāvevacānteṣu) sthitaṃ varṇacatuṣṭayaṃ |
kṣakārādi sthitāstrīṇi tasyānte evameva hi ||

p. 3a)

ādau catvāro varṇāśca ānte vedagṛheṣu ca |
kānte yugasamākhyātā guḍo(ogu)madhye tathaiva ca ||

manare bhūtacattvāraḥ sthitāste vāmadakṣiṇe |
ṛṣimadhya parityajya koṣakāni tu saṃsthitāḥ ||

teṣu sa vyāpasavyeṣu rasavarṇāvyavasthitāḥ |
navake ca punarddevi vasavaḥ parikīrttitāḥ ||

chā(kā)dau vedāḥ samākhyātā anya catvāri yāni ca |
teṣu catvārivarṇāni (catvāryanyāni yāni ca) siddhaṃ prastāramuttamaṃ ||

eṣā yoniḥ parā guhyā mantrasṛṣṭirvyavasthitam |
yonir madhyagatā yoniryāvat koṇāścaturdaśa ||

eṣakramagato meruścatuḥ śṛgaḥ surālayaḥ |
atramadhye samutpannāḥ siddhavidhyā anekadhā |
pāraṃparyakrameṇaiva guru vaktrāt tu labhyate ||

iti pārameśvarīmatenavakoṭisaṃhitāyāṃ caturāśītisahasrameruddhāraḥ 
caturviṃśatimaḥ paṭalaḥ ||





devyuvāca


adya me saphalaṃ janmaḥ adya me saphalaṃ tapaḥ |
śrutaṃ meruṃ paraṃguhyaṃ sarvapāpakṣayaṅkaraṃ ||

nirmalatvaṃ ca saṃprāptaṃ tvayi tuṣṭe sureśvara (tvayā tuṣṭena śaṅkara) |
adhunāśrotumichāmi vidyā nāmāni yāni ca ||

kathaṃ sidhyanti deveśa sāmarthyaṃ kīdṛśaṃ bhavet |


śrībhairava uvāca


ādau dīkṣābhiṣekaṃ ca sarvapāpapranāśanaṃ ||

paścāddevikramaṃ jñātvā ṣoḍhānyāsenasaṃyutaṃ |
śabdārāśikukārākhyaṃ aṅgavaktre tu saṃyutāṃ (śabdarāśiḥ kukārākhyā 
aṅgavaktreṣu saṃyutā) ||

punastaṃ (tataḥ) kramayogena navātmānādinī paraṃ(daṃ) |
vidyā trayaṃ paraṃ guhyaṃ aghorāṣṭakameva ca ||

aṅgadvādaśakaṃ cānyaṃ saḍaṅgaśca tataḥ punaḥ |
granthādikaṃ tato nyāsaṃ tribhirbhedaiśca tadgrahaṃ ||

p. 3b)

dvādaśāni ca ślokāni (daśaiva tathā ślokā) paṃcaratnāni yāni ca |
pīṭhaścatuḥ kramaṃ divyaṃ ṣoḍhānyāsenasaṃyutaṃ ||

kuladīkṣābhiṣiktasya pāraṃparya kramasya ca |
manyante kramajyeṣṭhāni ye yuktāśca kulāgame ||

tasya deyamidaṃ jñānaṃ soḍhānyāsaṃ kramodbhavaṃ |
vinyasedvidhiyuktātmā triṣkālañca divāniśi ||

ye ke citpāpasaṃghātāḥ sapta janmāntareṣu ca |
dahate nyāsamātreṇa kalpānte bhāskaro yathā ||

śastrasaṃghātasaṅkīrṇe saṃgrāmeṣvā padeṣu ca |
na bhayaṃ vidyate tasya sa vajrapañjaro yathā || 

kāmaṃ krodhantathā lobhaṃ māyāhaṃkārameva ca |
saṃparkābhojanālāpaṃ na kuryāt kenacit saha ||

namaskāraṃ na kurvīta jyeṣṭasyāpi kadācana |
gurorapi na patetpādau mriyate nātra saṃśayaḥ ||

tasmāt sarva prayatnena namaskāraṃmvivarjjayet |
ṣaṇmāsābhyāsayogena sāmarthyaṃ ca vadāmyahaṃ ||

kāṣṭhapāṣāṇadhātūnāṃ bhūtagrāmaṃ caturvidhaṃ |
namaskārāt kṣayaṃ yānti girirvajra hato yathā ||

abdaiḥ ṣoḍaśabhirdevi khecaratvaṃ bhaviṣyati | 


devyuvāca 


na jānāmya pyahaṃnātha ṣoḍhānyāsaṃ ca kīdṛśaṃ ||

vaktumarhasi deveśa yadi tuṣṭosi me prabho |


śrībhairava uvāca


yo sau devi mayākhyātaścatuḥśṛṅgo mahānagaḥ ||

uddhāraṃ sarvavidyānāṃ tatra madhye vyavasthitaṃ |
tadahaṃsaṃpravakṣyāmi uddhāraṃ yā dṛśaṃ bhavet ||

kailāsaśikhare devi ṣadvarṇaṃ varṇapūriktaṃ |
śirasthaṃ taṃ vijānīyā chrīkaṇṭhaṃstatra devatā ||

punastasyaiva traiva ante ca śikhare gandhamādane |

p. 4a)

anantaṃ tamvijānīyāt saṃsthitaṃ vaktramaṇḍale ||

mandarānte tṛtīyaṃ ca bhairavaṃ tatra saṃsthitaṃ |
dakṣanetrasya madhyasthaṃ trimūrtti vāmanetragaṃ ||

niṣadhāntaṃ gṛhaṃ tasya vedavarṇasamudbhavaṃ |
dhruvādi bhūtavarṇasthaṃ ama(rā)rīśaṃ pratiṣṭhitaṃ ||

dakṣiṇe śravaṇe nyasya arghīśaṃ rituvarṇagaṃ |
vāmaśravaṇavinyāsaṃ sarvapāpakṣayaṃkaraṃ ||

kailāse vāma madhye tu śrīkaṇṭhasya adhaḥ sthitaṃ |
bhairavobhārabhūtistu bhānunāsā puṭe nyaset ||

atithiḥ so madhye tu aṣṭamaṃ gandhamādane |
anusvāravisargāntaṃ lokasthaṃ mandare vidau ||

sthāṇurudraṃ vijānīyāddakṣagaṇḍasthale nyaset |
niṣadekākamadhyasthaṃ rudranāmaharammahat ||

vāmagaṇḍasthale nyāsaṃ karttavyaṃ varavarṇini |
kailāse kabya ādisthaṃ karṇāśaṃ nāmanāmataḥ ||

dantapaṃktau nyaseddhīmān bhūtīśaṃ daṃṣṭramadhyataḥ |
tasya bījaṃ ru ante ca ādau caiva samuddharet ||

vāmaṃ nāsā puṭe pūrvaṃ viśvavarṇaṃ vyavasthitaṃ |
sadyojātaṃ vijānīyāt nyastamoṣṭasya (mastake cāṣṭa) madhyataḥ ||

rudro anugrahīśaśca mahātejo mahāmanāḥ |
tasmāt sarvaprayatnena ḷ ante adhare nyaset ||

aṃkrūraṃ ghaṃṭikāgre ca jihvā mūle visargajaṃ |
mahāsenaṃ vijānīyād rudraścaiva mahābalaṃ ||

vāmagaṇḍādi utpannaṃ bhujamūle tu dakṣiṇe |
krodhaṃ nāma mahārudraṃ nyāsaṃ kṛtvā tu yatnataḥ ||

punaścākāśamadhyasthaṃ caṃśu? nāmānamiśvaraṃ |

p. 4b)

tatraivaṃ vāhumadhye tu nyāsaṃ kṛtvā sureśvari ||

paścāntaṃ ca vijānīyāt kha adhaḥ kūrparā nyaset |
kaṅkāle (kaṅkaṇe) śivamityāhurghakāraṃ varṇapūrvakaṃ ||

ekapādaṃ kare nyasya arghīśānte vyavasthitaṃ |
kūrmpaṃ?cakāramadhyasthaṃ vāmamūle vyavasthitaṃ ||

ekahastasthitaṃ vāhuvva pūrvaṃ vāma *?nyaset |
caturānanenasaṃyuktaṃ jakāraṃkūrpare sthitaṃ ||

rūkāraṃ kaṅkaṇe nyastaṃ ajitastatra devatā |
svānte (yānta) 
varṇasya(varṇena)saṃbhūtakare(navarṇasaṃbhūti)śarma(savye)vyavasthitā ||

evaṃ nyāsaṃ mahādevi ubhauvāhū pratisthitaṃ |
jaṃghā mūle śaśāṅkārddhaṃ tatra someśvaraṃ viduḥ ||

dakṣiṇe taddvijānīyāt pūrṇendu mūrubhūṣitaṃ (ndutarūsaṃsthitaṃ) |
lāṃgalīśo mahādevi tatra sthāne niyojayet ||

dārukaṃ jānumadhyasthaṃ nyāsaṃ kṛtvātrapūrvakaṃ |
grahamālāsthitaṃ pūrvaṃ jaṃghāmadhye vyavasthitaṃ ||

ardhanārīśvaraṃ devaṃ nyāsaṃ kṛtvānu yatnataḥ |
umākāntaṃ ṇamadhyasthaṃ nyāsaṃpāspadakṣiṇe ||

takāraṃ vāmamūle tu āṣāḍhīnāmanāmataḥ |
tatrasthāne kṛtaṃ nyāsaṃ taddvitīyaṃ adhaḥ sthitaṃ ||

daṇḍinaṃ merumadhyasthaṃ nyāsaṃ kṛtvā sureśvari |
ḍhakāraṃ yasya ūrddhe tu cha(hi?)kāramadhaḥ saṃsthitaṃ ||

jānunyāsaṃ vijānīyāddhātrīśo nāmanāmataḥ |
mīnaṃ dadhestu (dhakāra)madhyasthaṃ jaṃghāsthāne niyojayet ||

takārasya adhasthāne dhruvānte ūrdhasaṃsthitaṃ |
meṣonāma mahārudro vāmapāde vyavasthitaḥ ||

lohitaṃ dakṣakukṣisthaṃ vinyasedvaravarṇini |
p. 5a) tha(ṭha)kāraṃ yasya mūrdhisthaṃ adhoṣakārameva ca (suvarṇaṃ tasya 
pūrvakaṃ) ||

bhūtādi mandarasthaṃ ca vāmakukṣau niyojayet |
śikhivāhimahārudraṃ tatra sthāne prati sthitaṃ ||

mokāraṃ yasya urddhaṃ tu pṛṣṭha madhye vyavasthitaṃ |
chagalaṇḍaṃ (* *?) vijānīyāttatra rudraṃ vyavasthitaṃ ||

mandare ṛṣimālāyāṃ ri(dhi)kārasya adhaḥ sthitaḥ |
nābhisthāne vijānīyādviraṇḍantatra devatā ||

tatraiva ṛṣimālāyāṃ bhe ca madhe vyavasthitaṃ |
mahākālaṃ vijānīyāddhṛdi madhe vyavasthitaṃ ||

ya(*?)kāramadhakoṣṭhe tu go pūrve yasya saṃsthitaṃ |
vāgīśo bhairavastatra tvacinpāsottamotamaḥ ||

tasyānte rakta(vaktra) madhye tu navake pūrvasaṃsthitaṃ |
bhūjaṅga bhairavastatra piśitasya(stho) pinākinaḥ ||

sa(ga)kārasya adhaḥ sthāne ri(?)pūrve yasya saṃsthitaṃ |
etadbījaṃ vijānīyāt nyastaṃ tatra pinākinaḥ ||

vecāmadhye sthitaṃ bījaṃ snāyu madhye vyavasthitaṃ |
khaḍgīśo bhairavastatra vinyasedvaravarṇiṇi ||

ve antamāśrayaṃ yasya vā(*?)gīśānta(nai) madhaḥ(rvyava)sthitam |
asthimadhye sthitaṃ rudram samyakaṃ nāmanāmataḥ ||

adho yasya ra(na)kārastu kakāraṃ pūrvasaṃsthitaṃ |
tadvarṇaṃmadhyamadhyasthaṃ bhairavaśceta nāmataḥ ||

sivāraṃ yasya ante(śva) ca pinākī adhaḥ saṃsthitam |
śukrasthaṃ tadvijānīyāt bhairavo bhṛgunāmataḥ ||

khaḍgīśaṃ bhairavaṃ yatra tatra urddhe vyavasthitaṃ |
lakulīśaḥ prāṇamityāhuḥ arghīśasya adhaḥ sthitaṃ ||

krodhamadhye sthitaṃ rudraṃ varṇānte yadvyavasthitaṃ |
saṃvarttakaṃ vijānīyādyugānteyaḥ kṣayaṃkaraḥ ||

śabdarāśimidaṃ proktaṃ sarvapāpa kṣayaṃkaraṃ |
nyastaṃ sarvāṅga deśeṣu paṃcapraṇavasaṃpuṭaṃ ||

durgamārga jalāgnīṣu duṣṭadaṃṣṭrī bhayeṣu ca |
vāde vivāde saṃgrāme na bhayaṃ vidyate kvacit ||

yatra kutra ciddeśeṣu tiṣṭhate sādhakeśvaraḥ |
tasya saṃparka mālāyādrudralokaṃ sa gacchati ||

iti pārameśvarīmate navakoṭi saṃhitāyāṃ caturāśītisāhasre 
śakarāśipaṃcaviṃśatimaḥ paṭalaḥ ||




śrīdevyuvāca


paṃcapraṇavayogena nyāsamuktaṃ tvayā prabho |
tamahaṃ śrotumichāmi tvatprasādādyabhairava ||


śrībhairava uvāca


śṛṇu devi paraṃguhyaṃ uddhāraṃ durlabhañca yat |
nyāsaṃ pūjā ca saṃskāraṃ na tena rahitaṃ bhavet ||

tamahaṃ kathayiṣyāmi pañcapraṇavadevatā |
kailāse yasya varṇasya pūrvapūrve napuṃsake ||

lokāntā(di)śirasākrāntaṃ mandire yadvyavasthitam |
pūrvapraṇavayogoyaṃ yatrotpannaṃ carācaram ||

chā(a)ntaṃ varṇaṃ samuddhṛtya la(na)kuśa(la) yatrasaṃsthitaṃ |
navake saṃsthitaṃ devi āsanaṃ tasya saṃsthitaṃ ||

trimūrtināśirobhinnaṃ bindumastakabhūṣitaṃ |
paraśakti samutpannaṃ dvitīyaṃ praṇavaṃ viduḥ ||

adhasthaṃ yasya vāgī(lī)śo urddhe krodhākṣaraṃ bhavet |
tadvarṇaṃ raktamāruḍhaṃ caturthasvarasaṃyutam ||

krūravarṇaṃ śire dattaṃ bījamaiścaryadāyakaṃ |
p. 6a) mandare bhūta pūrvaṃ tu saṃgṛhyaśikhivāhanaṃ ||

jhaṇṭīśaṃ mastake dattaṃ bhujaṅgāsanasaṃsthitam |
caturthapraṇavaṃ proktaṃ bindumastakabūṣitaṃ ||

etadghorataraṃ bījaṃ jita(datta?)kālānalapra(mālanāśu)bhaṃ |
yasya smaraṇamātreṇa brahmāṇḍaṃ parikalpate ||

śikāraṃ pūrvavarṇasya prāṇamūrddhni sthitaṃ |
sāvitrī mastake dattaṃ uccāraśca sa bindukam ||

praṇavaṇ nirvāṇamityuktaṃ pāvakāsanasaṃsthitam |
etepaṃcamahābījā ādibhūtākulāgame ||

nyāsa pūjā ca saṃsthānaṃ(skāraṃ) nā(svā?)nena rahitaṃ bhavet |
uktaṃ devi paraṃguhyaṃ paṃcapraṇavasaṃpuṭaṃ ||

nanenaivavidhānena nyāsapūjā sadā kuru |
kramikantaṃ vijānīyāccaturvarga phalaṃ labhet ||


devyuvāca


dvātriṃśadakṣarāṃ devī sarvakāmārtha sādhanīṃ |
brahmāviṣṇuśca rudraśca īśvaraśca sadāśivaḥ ||

etāni pañca devāni yena siddhāḥ sureśvarāḥ |
ādi bhūtāṃ parāṃ sūkṣmāṃ śrotumichāmi tattvataḥ ||

janmajanmāntaraṃ pāpaṃ bhasmasādyānti tatkṣaṇāt |


śrībhairava uvāca

ādauvarṇaṃ samutpannaṃ rudrāṇāṃ vā(ṛ)ṣimandire |
yatra madhye mahādevi meṣo(māyā)rudro mahābalaḥ ||

pūrvaṃ tu tadvijānīyānmālinī śirasi sthitam |
pūrvaṃ vāgīśa varṇasya strāyurūrddhvaṃ vyavasthitaṃ ||

dvītīyañca samutpannaṃ nābhi rudraṃ tṛtīyakaṃ |
grahamālāsthitaṃ varṇaṃ tato madhye vyavasthitam ||

p. 6b)

snāyu rudraṃ tadagre ca paṃcamaṃ parameśvari |
vāgīśe śāntaṃ mārūḍhaṃ bhūtīśaṃ mastake sthitaṃ ||

ṣaṣṭhe bījaṃ vijānīyāt tasyānte śikhivāhanaṃ |
lorddhe ca mastake tasya rakārāsana saṃsthitaṃ ||

jhaṇṭīśaṃ śirase caiva kūrantasyoparisthitaṃ |
hṛdbījaṃ paramaṃ devi sarvasiddhipradāyakaṃ ||

krodhavarṇaṃ samuddhṛtya amarīśāsana sthitam |
aṣṭamañca bhavedbījaṃ vakārantasya agrataḥ ||

sūkṣmarudraṃ śiredattaṃ caturvaktramadhasthitam |
rudramālādi tasyāgre vāgīśaṃ bhūtisaṃyutam ||

la(na)kulīraktamārūḍhaṃ vyañjanaṃ śirasisthitam |
anantenantenasamāyuktaṃ dvādaśaṃ bījamuttamam ||

punaḥ prāṇaṃ samuddhṛtya dviḥsthāne tu maheśvari |
bhujaṅgamāsanasteṣāṃ ubhauvarṇe pradāpayet ||

vyañjanaścaiva varṇena trimūrtte na vibhuṣitam |
arghīśamāsanaścaiva vyañjanorddhvamataḥparam ||

krodhakūrmmānta varṇānāṃ ṭatavargāni māni ca |
kevalañca samuddhṛtyamekārānteṣu agrataḥ ||

śrīkaṇṭhaṃ yadbhavedvarṇaṃ kevalaṃ ca tataḥ punaḥ |
sadyojātenasaṃyuktaṃ śiveśaparameśvaram ||

sarvapāpaharaṃ bījaṃ ekaviṃśe sureśvari |
anantenasamāyuktaṃ tasyānte raktasaṃsthitam ||

mahākālaṃ punarddevi savyakarṇāsanasthitaṃ |
krodhavargadvitīyañca trimūrttena tu bheditam ||

caturviṃśatimaṃbījamakṣaraṃ siddhidāyakaṃ |
prāṇādi saṃsthitaṃ yacca kūrmmantasyoparisthitaṃ ||

p. 7a)

krūrānantenasaṃyuktaṃ paṃcaviśatimaṃ viduḥ |
punastaṃ krūrasaṃyuktaṃ vedasvaravibhūṣitam ||

agre tasya mahādevi krodharudrālayaṃ param |
sukṣmarudreṇasaṃyuktaṃ saptaviṃśatimaṃ mahat ||

dakṣapādodbhavaṃ varṇamumā(kā)ntasya mandiram |
aṣṭaviṃśatimaṃ bījaṃ sūkṣmaṃ tasyoparisthitaṃ ||

punaraitava bījeta ubhe agre vyavasthite |
punaḥ snāyusthitaṃ varṇaṃ sukṣmarudrasamanvitam ||

jhaṇṭīśenasāmāyuktaṃ kūrmantasyoparisthitaṃ |
eṣāvidyāparāguhyā parāśaktervyavasthitā ||

brahmāviṣṇuśca rudraśca īśvaraśca sadāśivaḥ |
paṃcavaktrodbhavāhyete atravidyānaneṣu ca ||

pūrvavaktre samutpanno bhūtāmāṃ ca pitāmahaḥ |
punarnārāyaṇe devi saṃbhūtaścottarānane ||

ekādaśāni rudrāṇi yāmya vaktrodbhavāni ca |
siddhāste nātrasaṃdeho asyā vidyā prabhāvataḥ ||

īśvaraśca mahādevi prācī vaktrasamudbhavam |
parāpare śivaḥ siddhaḥ ūrddhvavaktre sureśvari ||

vakre vakre mahādevi siddhā hyete surottamāḥ |
siddhāprajāyate nyopi siddhaistu saṃhitā purā ||

hṛdayena mahādevi sānuṣṭhāna parāyaṇāḥ |
śiregāṃdharvamityāhuḥ (śirasāgandhrva āhuḥ; gandhavoḥ śirasā siddhāḥ) 
pūjārādhanatatparāḥ ||

gandharvatvaṃ ca saṃprāptaḥ śirodevyāḥ prabhāvataḥ |
maharjjanatapaḥ satyaṃ ye siddhā atra saṃsthitāḥ ||

dvātriṃśadakṣarā devyā śivārādhanatatparāḥ |
jñānasiddhāḥ samākhyātā ūrdhvaloka nivāsinaḥ ||

p. 7b)

indrādyā devatāḥ sarve dharmmarājo yamo nilaḥ |
ni-ṛtirvaruṇaścaiva ye yakṣādhanadādayaḥ ||

siddhāste nātrasaṃdehaḥ kavace varavaravarṇini |
candrādityā guhā athāta ḍinmeṣādi rāśayaḥ ||

netrārādhanayogena siddhāste nātrasaṃśayaḥ |
nāgā vidyādharāḥ sarve parvatāḥ sāgarāḥ sarit ||

yadbhūtā bhuvi pātāle astra yonisamudbhavāḥ |
anuṣṭhānena deveśi svasiddhisahitāḥ purā ||

sāmarthyaṃ yasya yo devi varttate bhuvanāntare |
dvātriṃśadakṣarī vidyā tvatprasādāt sureśvari ||

vidyāyonisamutpannaṃ trailokyaṃ sa carācaraṃ |
eṣādevi parāmāyā mantra sṛṣṭi(mūrti)vyavasthitā ||

pūjāmānena deveśi trailokyaṃ pūjitaṃ bhavet |
tasyāṅgāni ca vaktrāṇi nyāsaṃ kuryād varānane ||


devyuvāca


śrutaṃ deva paraṃguhyaṃ devyānuddhāra(taddhyānaṃ)muttamaṃ |
tasyāṅgāni ca vaktrāṇi tvayā coktāni yāni ca ||

tamahaṃ śrotumichāmi tvat prasādācca bhairava |


śrībhairava uvāca


kathayāmi na sandeho ya tvayā paripṛcchitaṃ |
devyāḥ saptā(rudrā)kṣaraṃ mūlaṃ kasyānte yādṛśaṃ bhavet ||

tamahaṃ saṃpravakṣyāmi hṛdi devyā na saṃśayaḥ |
khaṇḍapāvakamārūḍhaṃ khaṇḍamāyādhaḥ saṃsthitaṃ ||

prāṇamūrdhagataṃ kuryādāṣāḍhī tasya cāgrataḥ |
krodhavarṇāsanārūdhaṃ mahākālaṃ ca kevalaṃ ||

pinākī ma?dbhavedvarṇaṃ kevalaṃ ca tataḥ punaḥ |
etāni vedavarṇāni vidyā saptāntasaṃsthitāḥ ||

p. 8a)

vidyāsapteti catvāri asyānte te samālikhet |
tasyānte hṛdayaṃ caiva daśameṣṭākṣaraṃ viduḥ ||

icchāsiddhikaraṃ divyaṃ hṛdayaṃ kubjikasya tu |
rudrānte saṃsthitā trīṇi mūlavidyānu yāni ca ||

mastakantadvijāniyātmārttaṇḍa bhairavī saha |
bhuvanānte bhūtabījāni mūlavidyā tu saṃsthitā ||

devījñānaśikhānāma vauṣaḍantā ca saṃsthitā |
śikhānte paṃcavarṇāni kavacaṃ parikīrttitaṃ ||

sarvasarvādarī devyā kuliśaḥ pañjaraṃ iva |
tasya purata ye vedāstrinetrāsaṃsthitāni ca ||

trailokya darśanī devī saṃbhūtā caturakṣarī |
vidyānte yugavarṇāni devyāstresaṃvyavasthitaṃ ||

sarvārtharakṣaṇī devī diśāsu vidiśāsu ca |
namaḥ svāhā vauṣaḍantaṃ vaṣaṭ huṃphaṭ ca saṃyutaṃ ||


śrībhairava uvāca


athātaḥ saṃpravakṣyāmi devyā vaktrāṇi yāni ca |
nakārādi yai(e?)kārāntaṃ ye varṇā rudrasaṃsthitāḥ ||

sṛṣṭiśaktirnamonte ca pūrvavaktre prapūjayet |
tasyānte ca su(sva)varṇāni saṃbhūtāścottarānane ||

māyāśaktirnamontaśca pūjitavyaṃ prayatnataḥ |
dakṣiṇāsye punardevi bhūtavarṇā vyavasthitāḥ ||

raudrīśaktisamāyogānnamontaṃ pūjanaṃ bhavet |
paścime ṛtuvarṇāni śaktimaiśvaryadāyike ||

namontaṃ pūjanantasya icchāśakti phalaṃ labhet |
ūrddhavarṇai sthitau netro mokṣaśaktirudāhṛtā ||

pūjitavyaṃ vidhānena sarvapāpaiḥ(bandhai) pramucyate |
eteṣu paṃcavaktreṣu vidyābījāni yāni ca ||

p. 8b)

ādau uccāraṇaṃ kuryādyo yasya yā dṛśāni ca |
aṅgāni paṃcavakrāṇi nyāsaṃ praṇavasaṃyutam ||

mūlavidyāṃ jape devi sarvasiddhi pradāyikīṃ |

iti pārameśvarīmate paṃcapraṇavamūlavidyāṅgavaktroddhāraḥ 
ṣaḍviṃśatimaḥ paṭalaḥ ||





śrībhairava uvāca


athātaḥ saṃpravakṣyāmi navātmā nyāsamuttamaṃ |
nābhivarṇaṃ samuddhṛtya prāṇaṃ tasyāsanaṃ bhavet ||

prāṇaṃ raktasamārūḍhaṃ raktaṃ krodhāsanasthitaṃ |
mahākālāsanaṃ krodhaṃ mahākālaṃ pinākinaṃ ||

pinākī snāyumārūḍhaṃ snāyu vāgīśamurddhagaṃ |
dhruvānte sama(rva)mārūḍhaṃ nādabinduvibhūṣitaṃ ||

etatsarvāṅgadeheṣu nyāsaṃ kuryādanātmanaḥ |
(mahākālaṃ pinākinaṃ pinokī snāyumārūḍhaṃ ||
dhruvānte samārūḍhaṃ nādabinduvibhūṣitaṃ |
etatsarvāṅga deheṣu nyāsaṃ kurvāta yatnata ||)???????

na bhayaṃ vidyate tasya trailokye sa carācare |


śrībhairava uvāca


yā sā devi parāguhyā mālinī mātṛkodbhavā |
bhāsayantī svateje(dehe)na mā(li)naṃ sa carācaraṃ ||

tasyoddhāraṃ paraṃguhyaṃ śṛṇuṣvekāgramānasāḥ |
meṣonāmamahārudro mūlaṃ vidyādi saṃsthitaḥ ||

tadvarṇaṃ śirasi nyastaṃ nādinī devi saṃsthitaṃ |
nāthimadhyagataṃ varṇaṃ lohitasyoparisthitaṃ ||

grasanītisamākhyātaṃ saṃsthitā vaktramaṇḍale |
p. 9a) bhārabhūspa?tithiścaiva nāsikāgre vyavasthitau ||

dakṣagaṇḍasthalaṃ vāmamete varṇā napuṃsakāḥ |
nivṛtyāyai pratiṣṭhāyai vidyāyai śāntimeva ca ||

eṣādevi śiromālā mālinī śirasi sthitā |
kūrmmavarṇaṃ punardevi tṛtīyaṃ netrasaṃsthitaṃ ||

nyāsaṃ kṛtvā tu cāmuṇḍā sarvavighnapraṇāśanī |
punaścakṣuśca(dva)yormadhye yā devyā priyadarśanī ||

mīnarudrodbhavaṃ varṇaṃ vinyasedvidhipūrvakaṃ |
punarnārāyaṇī devī śravaṇābhyāmadha(ṇa yormadhya saṃ)sthitā ||

vinyasedvidhi pūrvaṃ tu umākarṇe ca yā sthitā |
dhruvādi mohinī devī dakṣiṇe karṇa bhūṣaṇaṃ ||

dhruvāntaṃ vāmakarṇe tu prajñāyai tatra saṃsthitam |
kaivalyaṃ mandarasthaṃ ca nāsikāgre vyavasthitaṃ ||

guhyaśaktiṃ vijānīyādvajrinīvajra(kra)madhyataḥ |
va(ka)kāntaṃ(rā)sthitaṃ tatra khaḍgīśaṃ yatrasaṃsthitam ||

karālī ca kapālī ca śivāghorā vaśi(*?)rvarā |
pañcaitāḥ kramayogena krodhavarge vyavasthitāḥ ||

nyāsaṃ kurvīta deveśi adhorddhaṃ dantapaṃktiṣu |
aṣṭavāgeśvarī vācā trimūrttiśca sarasvatī ||

jihvānyāsaṃ ca kurvīta va(ca)kāraṃ kaṇṭhamadhyataḥ |
tatramadhyasthitā devī śikhivāhīti viśrutā ||

ṛṣipūrvaṃ samuddhṛtya tatra bhīmā vyavasthitā |
dakṣiṇe tu bhuje daṇḍe śikharaṃ yasya mandira(ṇḍita)m ||

tatraiva ṛṣimālāyāṃ yāme madhye vyavasthitam |
śikharaṃ vāmahastasya vāyuvegā pratiṣṭhitā ||

p. 9b)

namo dakṣa bhujādaṇḍe dā(cā)ruvarṇāsanasthitā |
pūnarvināyakī devī grahādivarṇasaṃsthitā ||

nyāsaṃ vāmabhujādaṇḍe pūrṇimākaramadhyataḥ |
ḍhāntavarṇasamāyogā nyāsaṃ kṛtvā kareṣu ca ||

rū(kaṃ)kāriṇī ca yā devyā ajitāvarṇasaṃsthitā |
aṅgulīnyāsaṃ karttavyaṃ dakṣiṇasya karasya tu ||

punarvāma kare devi aṃgulī nyāsa pārvatī |
sarvavarṇasamāyogādākāśānte vyavasthitā ||

dīpanī śūladaṇḍaṃ ca bhujaṃge dakṣiṇe kare |
jayantī śūlamityāhurjakārasya tu pūrvakaṃ ||

kare vāme kapālañca varṇe someśvarāsanaṃ |
kapālīnāmavikhyātā vāmahaste pratiṣṭhitā ||

pavanī hṛdaye yo hya lohitaṃ varṇapūrvikā |
aṃbikā ca tato devi chahu(mama; vakra)madhye vyavasthitām ||

nyāsaṃ prāṇasya karttavyaṃ ichāśakti hṛdisthitam |
lokānte tasya varṇasya uddhāraṃ pūrvatasthitam ||

sakāraṃ bindusaṃyuktaṃ hṛdimadhye niyojayet |
devī tu paramātmāyai nyāsaṃ kṛtvā prayatnataḥ ||

ekahastasya yadvarṇaṃ chāgalī tatrasaṃsthitā |
dakṣiṇastanamadhyasthaṃ vāme vai pūtanāṃ viduḥ ||

pinākī tatra varṇaṃ ca pūrve yarddhe(datvā) ca vinyaset |
anantenasamāyogādāmoṭīkṣīramadhyataḥ ||

punastaṃ laṃbinī devī āsane svenasaṃyutaṃ |
nyāsaṃkurvīta yatnena udaramadhye tu saṃsthitam ||

saṃvarttakasya madhyasthaṃ devī saṃhārikā bhavet |
nābhi nyāsaṃ vijānīyāt mahākālīmapūrvakaṃ ||

nyāsaṃnitaṃvamadhyasthaṃ kurvītta parameśvari |
p. 10a) lokānāṃ pūrvavarṇe tu guhyesu kusumā yudhā(nyaset) ||

saṃvakaṃ śukramadhye tu śrukrā(sūkṣmā) devī vyavasthitā |
āṣāḍhīkrūrasaṃyuktā tatra tārāvyavasthitā ||

ūrū madhyagataṃ nyāsaṃ jānunyāsamataḥ śṛṇu |
nā(vā)mādivarṇamuddhṛtyajhaṇṭīśaṃ yatrasaṃsthitam ||

jñānaśaktiprayatnena vinyasedakṣajānuni |
ṛ antaṃ vāmajānusthaṃ kailāse yatpratiṣṭhitaṃ ||

kriyāśakti sthitā tatranyāsaṃkurvīta yatnataḥ |
sadyojātasya(stu) gāyatrī dakṣajaṃgheṣu saṃsthitā ||

sāvitrī vāmajaṃghāyā ṛkāraṃ tasya pūrvakaṃ |
dahanī dakṣiṇe pāde dakārāsanasaṃsthitā ||

vāmapāde phakārasya(tu phetkāri) nyāsaṃ kuryāt prayatnataḥ |
etaddeviparaṃguhyaṃ mālinī nyāsamuttamam ||

vinyastaṃ vidhiyogena paṃcapraṇavasaṃpuṭam |
bhūtapretapiśācāni yakṣarakṣamahoragāḥ ||

anyepi hiṃsakā ye ca saṃsthitāvaravarṇini |
tasya dṛṣṭi nipātena vidravanti diśodaśa ||

aśanīvajrapā(dhā)teṣu rājacaura bhayeṣu ca |
saṃgrāme jayamāpnoti śastraghātaṃ na vidyate ||

mahārakṣā bhavettasya sarvaśastreṣu caiva hi |
labhetsaubhāgya matulamāyurārogyasaṃpadaṃ ||

nyāsaṃkurvītayatnena smaśāne tiṣṭhate yadi |
bhūtapretapiśācaiśca jvalantaṃ dṛśyate tu saḥ ||

dadanti vipulāṃ siddhiṃ niśācāreṇasundari |
saptavārānkṛtanyāsā gajāśve tiṣṭhate yadi ||

p. 10b) athavābhūmipādena śastraṃsaṃgṛhyapāṇinā |
praviśyati mahāvīraḥ saṃgrāme yuddhakāṃkṣibhiḥ ||

dṛśyate paracakre tu ekeraunikarūpiṇaḥ |
paṃcāśadrūpapiṇḍasya ekosau devi dṛśyate ||

praharanśastrajālena pādeṣugajasaṃsthitaḥ |
nirjjitya parasainyāni āgachanti na saṃśayaḥ ||

mālinī nyāsayogasya sāmarthyaṃ īdṛśaṃ bhavet |
ante mukti mavāpnoti punarjjanma na vidyate ||

iti pārameśvarīmate mālinīnyāsasaptāviṃśatimaḥ paṭalaḥ ||




śrībhairava uvāca


devyā bhū?yaṃ paraṃguhyaṃ pravakṣyāmyanupūrvaśaḥ |
parā ca aparā caiva tathā caiva parāparā ||

nyāsaṃ kṛtvā tu deveśi sarvapāpaiḥ pramucyate |
pañcapraṇavamaṃtrāṇi ādisthāne bhavanti vai ||

parāvidyādisaṃyojya śrīkaṇṭhantadanantaraṃ |
śivaṃ ca pūrvamuddhṛtya sadyojātenasaṃyutam ||

jñānaśaktisamāyogāt bhujaṃgaṃ paṃcamaṃ vihuḥ |
navādi dīpinī varṇe prāṇantasyoparisthitaṃ ||

trimūrttena samāyuktaṃ lokādisaṃyutaṃ likhet |
punastu aṃbikā devī śukrabhairavasaṃsthitā ||

saptamaṃ ca mahābījaṃ lokāntaṃ purataḥ sthitaṃ |
pāvanī bījamuddhṛtya lohitaṃ yatrasaṃsthitam ||

bhujaṅgañca punardevi navamantasya agrataḥ |
mahākālodbhavaṃ varṇaṃ tasya agre punarlikhet ||

śivāyavarṇasaṃyuktaṃ(bhāgena) sadyojātenasaṃyutaṃ |
ṛ ādiścaiva yogena navāde *? gratasthitaṃ ||

dhruvādi āsanaṃ dadyāt lokādi mastake sthitaṃ |
dhruvāntasya adhaścaiva eṣuvarṇeṣu madhyagaḥ ||

dhruvānte antamante ca sadyojātavibhūṣitam |
caturdaśaṃ ca ityuktaṃ dīpinī tadanantaraṃ ||

kevalaṃ śūladaṇḍasthaṃ punastaṃ tasya agrataḥ |
dhruvānte āsanaṃsthaṃ ca ṣoḍaśaṃ bījamuttamam ||

jhaṇṭīśenasamāyuktaṃ lohitaṃ tadanantaram |
bhṛkhu?kaṃ bījamuddhṛtya ambikāmūrddhnisaṃsthitaṃ ||

pakṣāntāntaṃ śirase dattaṃ savisargaṃ samālikhet |
ṛ ādivarṇamuddhṛtya śivaṃ yatravyavasthitam ||

rudrāntenasamāyuktaṃ dīpanītadanantaram | 
anantena samāsaktaṃ? bījaṃ viṃśatimaṃ(bhujaṃgaṃ paṃcamaṃ) viduḥ ||

me ca madhyagataṃ devi nitaṃtvaṃ mālinī sthitam |
amarīśāsanārūḍhaṃ bījaṃ paramadurlabhaṃ ||

bhāradbhūti adho gṛhya pracaṇḍaṃ bhairavānane |
ṛ pūrvavarṇayogena bījaṃ dvātriṃśamaṃ(gupataraṃ) priye ||

ṛṣipūrvaṃ samuddhṛtya mālinījihvasaṃyutam |
kevalaṃ vainitaṃ vaṃca bhairave hṛdisaṃsthitaṃ ||

punastasyādi agre tu kāntaṃ tasyāgrato sthitam(likhet) |
umākāntaṃ samuddhṛtya jñānaśaktisamanvitaṃ ||

chagalaṇḍa bhairavaṃ yatravarṇaṃ tasyāgratasthitam |
mahāṃkālaṃ punaścaiva tasyāgre saṃsthitaṃ priye |

pāne(so)madhyagataṃ devi lohitaṃ sūkṣmasaṃyutaṃ |
snāyurudraṃ ca tasyāgre mālinī vaktrasaṃsthitam ||

p. 11b) punarettāni bījāni ubhau agre vyavasthitau(catvāryagre sthitāni ca) |
ubhau prāṇau(rakṣavarṇau)samuddhṛtya jhiṇṭiśenādisaṃyutaṃ ||

kevalaṃ caiva tasyāgraṃ aṃbikā yatrasaṃsthitam |
raktavarṇaṃ samuddhṛtya yugasthāneṣu sundari ||

dhruvādi āsanasthau cidvau varṇo pūrvasaṃsthitau |
tasyāgre saṃsthitau dvau tu kevalāni ca bhairavi ||

paṃcapraṇavādi antasthaṃ tasyāgre tu punarllikhet |
vidyayā viśvabījañca puratastaspadāpayet ||

bhūtādi varṇamuddhṛtya dīpinī saṃsthitaṃ kuru |
ṛpūrvaṃ śirase dattaṃ parasya(ṇyā) hṛdayaṃ viduḥ ||

asya bījasya yatpūrvaṃ te nedaṃ saṃpuṭaṃ kuru |
asyā vidyādi (vidyāyāviśva) bījaṃ ca purata(sta)spadāpayet ||

eṣā vidyā parā devi triṣu lokeṣu durlabhā |
catuścatvāri bījāni jīvabhūtāvyavasthitāḥ ||

arddhaṃ caḍī?ni(arddhaṃ ca trīṇi)varṇāni aparā ca tataḥ śṛṇu(ante ca niyojayet) 
|
praṇavādi dvitīyaṃ ca prathamaṃ bījamuttamaṃ ||

bhujaṃgāsanamāruḍhaṃ harādyenavibhūṣitam |
ṭha ādyena śirākrāntaṃ bījaṃparamadurlabhaṃ ||

vakārorddhaṃ samādāya *(?)vāntena vibhūṣitam |
śukrā devī śirākrāntaṃ bījaṃ suravarārcitaṃ ||

dhruvāntā sa(na)mārūḍhaṃ prāṇaṃbindu(ṇabīja)yutaṃ kuru |
mālinī vāmapādañca kevalaṃ ca tataḥ punaḥ ||

dhruvādikhaṇḍamāruḍhaṃ arddhendupuratasthitam |
aparā ca samākhyāttā sarvavignapraṇāśanī ||

yakṣāparāparādevyā tasyo dvāraṃ vadāmyahaṃ |

sarvasiddhipradāsākṣādbhuktimukti pradāyikā |5????

p. 12a)

dhruvānte adhasaṃgṛhyakṣāntantasyoparisthitaṃ |
śuklavarṇasamārūḍhaṃ savisargasamālikhet ||

praṇavādi punaḥ kṛtvā vidyānāma parāparā |
nyāsaṃ sarvāṅgadeheṣu kurvītavaravarṇini ||

dharmakāmārthamokṣañca labhate nātrasaṃśayaḥ |


śrībhairava uvāca


mahānyāsaṃpravakṣyāmi aghorasyāṣṭakaṃ paraṃ ||

praṇavādi samuddhṛtyaṃ śrīkaṇṭhantadanantaram |
apūrvamo(ma)ṣṭasaṃyuktaṃ saṃsthitaṃ gandhamādane ||

tṛtīyaṃ ca samākhyātaṃ śūladaṇḍaṃ caturthakaṃ |
apūrveśa(ṇa)śirobhinnaṃ caturthaṃ bījamuttamam ||

tasyānte aṃbikā devī saṃsthitaṃ chāntamandire |
viśvamālādisaṃyuktaṃ raktasthaṃ murddhnibindukaṃ ||

paṃcamaṃ ca mahābījaṃ śukracaivāgra(visarga)saṃyutaṃ |
aghoryāśca śironyāsaṃ ṛtuvarṇaṃ patiṣṭhitaṃ ||

praṇavādi ādi bījaṃ śūladaṇḍaṃ dvitīyakaṃ |
amarī āsa(śa)nasaṃyuktaṃ bindumastakabhūṣitaṃ ||

pāvanīvarṇamuddhṛtya kevalaṃ ca tṛtīyakaṃ |
caturthaṃ kevalandevi bhujaṃgaṃ bhairavālayaṃ ||

tasyāgre mālinīvarṇannitambaṃ kevalaṃ sthitaṃ |
tasyānte(gre) śivamityāhu rudrāntena vibhūṣitaṃ ||

jhaṇṭīśena śirasthena raktavarṇaṃ samālikhet |
chakārāntaṃ samuddhṛtya amarīśāsanasthitam ||

śrakā devī śirākrāntaṃ saptamaṃ parikīrtitam |
parāyā viśvabījaṃ ca aṣṭamaṃ tasya dāpayet ||

visargena samāyuktaṃ bhṛguvarṇaṃ tadagrataḥ |
p. 12b) etāni navayogāni paramaghoreṇasaṃyutāḥ ||

vakranyāsaṃ prakurvīta kaṇṭhanyāsamataḥ paraṃ |
ṛ antamādi muddhṛtya śirobindu vibhūṣitaṃ ||

tasyāgre raktamityāhurdvitīyaṃ varṇasaṃsthitaṃ |
ṛpūrvamoṣṭhasaṃyuktaṃ bhujaṃgaṃ tadanantaraṃ ||

dhruvānte raktamārūḍhaṃ pāditasyāgratasthitaṃ |
śire tasya ṛpūrvaṃ ca ṣaṣṭhamaṃ bījamuttamaṃ ||

aṃbikā ca punarddevi chā(ḍhā)ntavarṇe pratiṣṭhitam |
ekādaśe caturthaṃ ca śukramadhyagataṃ likhet ||

ghorarūpaṃ ca tasyāgre nyāsaṃ kaṇṭhasya madhyataḥ |
jhaṇṭīśaṃ varṇamuddhṛtya jñānaśakti vibheditam ||

navādi varṇaṃ tasyāgre dvitīyaṃ bījasaṃsthitaṃ |
ṛpūrvamoṣṭhasaṃyuktaṃ tṛtīyaṃ bījamuttamaṃ ||

anante na samāyuktaṃ bhujaṃgaṃ tasya agrataḥ |
dhruvādi āsanaṃ dadyāt me ca madhye samālikhet ||

(pra)caṇḍavarṇaṃ samuddhṛtya (ḷ) pūrveṇa(ca) samanvitam |
śukramadhyagataṃ varṇaṃ savisargaṃ tadagrataḥ ||

ghorā tasyāgrato dadyāddhṛdinyā saṃpratiṣṭhitam |
jñānaśaktiṃ samuddhṛtya anantaṃ śirasi sthitaṃ ||

ṛkṣādi varṇamuddhṛtya visvādi saṃyutaṃ kuru |
tatraiva ṛṣimālāyāṃ bhe ante saṃsthitaṃ ca yat ||

kriyāśaktisamāyogāttṛtīyaṃ bījamuttamaṃ |
śukraṃ visargavarṇaṃ (śuklavarṇaṃ visargaṃ) ca puratastasya dāpayet ||

bhīmavarṇenasaṃyuktaṃ nyāsaṃ (bhū?)da(ra)madhyataḥ |
mālinī vāmajānusthaṃ jñāna(kriyā)śakti samudbhavaṃ ||

p. 13a)

krūravarṇaṃ śire(ve)dattaṃ prathamaṃ bījamuttamam |
adha(pa)rasya dvitīyaṃ ca tasyāgre saṃsthitaṃ kuru ||

ṛṣyādi varṇamuddhṛtya viśvādi śira(va)bhūṣitaṃ |
kāntaṃ varṇaṃ ca tasyāgre svetarudrālayaṃ paraṃ ||

umākāntodbhavaṃ varṇaṃ jhaṇṭīśenasamanvitaṃ |
śikhā(sāsi)madhyagataṃ gṛhya bhairavaṃ śukrasaṃbhavaṃ ||

lokāntena samāyuktaṃ bhīṣaṇaṃ(ṇaḥ) pa(pu)rataḥ sthitaṃ |
nābhimadhyagataṃ devi nyāsaṃ kurvīta yatnataḥ ||

paṃcapraṇavādi taṃ? bījaṃ tasyāgre aṃbikāṃ kuru |
mālinī vaktravarṇantu mandare bhūtasaṃsthitaṃ ||

mahākālaṃ punarddevi kevalaṃ ca samuddharet |
tasyāgre śuklabījaṃ ca bhṛgukaṃ yatra bhairavaṃ ||

paṃcamaṃ ca mahābījaṃ visargasaṃhitaṃ likhet |
vamanīṃ agrata kṛtvā guhye nayāsaṃprakalpayet ||

kriyāśaktiṃ samuddhṛtya bindumastakabhūṣitaṃ |
mālinī vāmapādaṃ ca ādisthaṃ tadvarānane ||

arddhenupuratastasya dhruvādi khaṇḍasaṃyutaṃ |
yādisthaṃ rudramālāyāṃ sūkṣmantu śirasi sthitam ||

vāme madhyagataṃ gṛhya snāyu rudrālayaṃ param |
śukramadhyagataṃ devi sa visargaṃ samālikhet ||

pavanīṃ purataḥ kṛtvā gudanyāsaṃ prakirttitaṃ |
vajradehamidaṃ caiva nyāsaṃ parama durlabham ||

asādhyaṃ sura daityānāṃ nyāsaṃ yasya śarīragam |


iti pārameśvarī mate vidyātrayamaghoryāṣṭakoddhāro aṣṭāviṃśatimaḥ 
paṭalaḥ ||





p. 13b)

śrībhairavauvāca 


parā dvādaśakaṃ nyāsaṃ pravakṣāmyanu pūrvaśaḥ |
na kāle nāpamṛtyuśca na vighnaṃ tasya jāyate ||

sarvavyādhi vinirmukto rudrasya sadṛśo bhavet |
paṃcapraṇavādi yogena śukravarṇaṃ samuddharet ||

bhṛgu bhairava bījaṃ ca tasyādho aṃbikāṃ kuru |
uddhṛtavyaṃ prayatnena sthānāni dvādaśāni ca ||

dvādaśasvarabhedena bheditavyaṃ prayatnata |
pūrvaṃ pūrveṇa saṃyuktaṃ dvitīyena dvitīyakaṃ ||

tṛtīyena tṛtīyaṃ ca caturthena caturthakaṃ |
paṃcame pañcamaṃ yojyaṃ ṣaṣṭe ṣaṣṭhaṃ niyojayet ||

saptame saptamasthaṃ ca aṣṭame aṣṭasaṃyutaṃ |
tasyāgre ca punardevi navame navamaṃ kuru ||

daśame daśamaṃ caiva rudramekādaśena ca |
dvādaśena punarddevi visarga sahitaṃ kuru ||

sarva bindusamāyuktā dvādaśaite mahābalāḥ |
aṃbikā ca mahādevi svarairebhiḥ prabheditam ||

uccāraṃ praṇavādiśca sakārasahitena ca |
siddhiḥ prathama uccāraṃ ṛddhināmā dvitīyakaṃ ||

dyutināma tṛtīyaṃ ca lakṣmīnāmā caturthakam |
pañcamī dīpti mālī ca śivā ṣaṣṭhi prakīrttitā ||

sumukhī saptamī devyā mālinī cāṣṭamī bhavet |
nandā vai navamī proktā haya vegā tadagrataḥ ||

rudre mahāmukhī caiva viśve śā ca(yī) tadagrataḥ |
bījadvayasamāyuktaṃ pañcapraṇavapūrvakaṃ ||

ekaikasya tu karttavyaṃ nyāsaṃ sarvāṅga(rtha)siddhidam (pādau jānūru? guhye 
ca nābhau hṛtkaṇṭhataḥ mukhe nāsā puṭe caiva karṇe maṃtre śire nyaset) |
dvādaśāḥṅgamidaṃ nyāsaṃ ṣaḍaṃgaṃ ca ataḥ śṛṇu ||

bhṛgubhairavamuddhṛtya pañcapraṇavasaṃyutaṃ |
mālinī mukhavarṇaṃ ca vajriṇī yatrasaṃsthitā ||

raktabhairavamūrddhasthā ekapādaṃ tadagrataḥ |
sama(dharma)varṇasamāruḍhaṃ tṛbījaṃ hṛdaye nyaset ||

namontoccāraṇaṃ tasya saṃsthitaṃ hṛdi madhyataḥ |
kailāse śikhare devi śrīkaṇṭhaṃ yatrasaṃsthitam ||

pūrvavarṇaṃ vijānīyāt mahākālaṃ tadagrataḥ |
ṛkārakhaṇḍamārūḍhaṃ dvitīyaṃ varṇamuttamam ||

nāga(ma)madhyagataṃ gṛhya ārūḍhamātmanasya ca |
ṛpūrvaṃ śirasi dattaṃ tṛtīyaṃ bījamuttamam ||

dvijamadhyagataṃ devi mālinī śūlamuttamam |
sadyojātenasaṃyuktaṃ caturthaṃ ca samālikhet ||

viśvamālāntu yogena nitaṃvaṃ devi paṃcamam |
mandare lokamadhyasthaṃ tasyāgre tu samālikhet ||

adhasthaṃ varṇaṃ saṃgṛhya āṣāḍhī bhairavasya tu |
maṃdarāntaṃ śire dattaṃ saptamaṃ bījamuttamam ||

grahamālā ṛtu sthāne kailāsavarṇabhūṣitaṃ |
khaṇḍapāvakamārūḍhaṃ aṣṭamaṃ ca samālikhet ||

pānomadhyagataṃ gṛhya rudreṣu grahamandire |
āṣāḍhī bhairavāruḍhaṃ kaivalyaṃ varṇabhūṣitaṃ ||

śiro nyāsaṃ prakurvīta svāhā kārāntasaṃyutam |
mālinīkaṇṭha(mukha)varṇañca snāyū bhairavamandiraṃ ||

prathamaṃ bījāmityāhurnāmādi śira(va)bhūṣitam |
kā ū (u; rdra; di)madhyagataṃ devi rudradī(drāṇī)pa dvitīyakam ||

punarasyaiva agre tu pūrvavarṇenasaṃyutaṃ |

p. 14b)

dahanī varṇamuddhṛtyamālinī pādasaṃsthitaṃ |
mālinī mūrddhni varṇaṃ ca kaivalyaṃ varṇabhūṣitam ||

kailāsa śikharāntasthaṃ agratastasya dāpayet |
meṣavarṇaṃ punarddevi anantenasamanvitam ||

saptamaṃ ca samuddhṛtya aṣṭamaṃ kāntasaṃsthitaṃ |
sūkṣmabhairavasaṃyuktaṃ navamaṃ ca tataḥ śṛṇu ||

maṃdare bhūtamadhyasthaṃ pṛṣṭhe rudrālayaṃ param |
rudrāntena samāyuktaṃ navamaṃ bījamuttamam ||

dudhe(tvavi)madhyasthitaṃ varṇaṃ mīna rudrālayaṃ paraṃ |
daśamaṃ ca vijānīyād vauṣaḍantā ca yā śikhā ||

mālinī mukhavarṇañca bhūtavarṇe vyavasthitam |
sthāpitaṃ pūrva varṇaṃ ca japantīṃ ca samālikhet ||

śūladaṇḍāsanaṃ tasya bhujaṃgaṃ yatra saṃsthitam |
mandarāntena saṃbhinnaṃ umākāntaṃ tṛtīyakam ||

tasyasūkṣmaṃ śire(ve)dattaṃ kaivalyaṃ yadvyavasthitam |
pṛṣṭha(pūṣṇi)bhairavamuddhṛtya caturthaṃ varṇamuttamam ||

jayantī raktamārūḍhaṃ tasyānte paṃcamaṃ viduḥ |
anantena samāyuktaṃ mīnavarṇantadagrataḥ ||

bhujaṃgabhairavaṃ varṇaṃ prathamaṃ(saptamaṃ) ca samālikhet |
bhūṣaṇantasya dāttavyaṃ śirasthaṃ gandhamādane ||

vāgīśaṃ ca tato devi aṣṭamaṃ kevalaṃ bhavet |
mālinī kaṇṭhavarṇasya ārūḍhaṃ bhṛgujaṃ(kaṃ) kuru ||

na ūrddhe grahamālāyāṃ tādivarṇaṃ vyavasthitaṃ |
sopi binduyutaṃ kāryā tadvarṇantasya agrataḥ ||

sopi raktāsanaṃ kuryād rudra bījaṃ vyavasthitam |
kevalaṃ(kavacaṃ) tadvijānīyāt huṃkārāntaṃ vyavasthitam ||

p. 15a) bhūtamadhyasthitamvarṇaṃ mālinīmukha(kaṇṭha)saṃsthitam |
prathamaṃ tadvijānīyāt saṃvarttakaṃ tadanantaraṃ ||

dhruvānte adhaḥ saṃgṛhya chahū(bhūja)madhye vyavasthitam |
ūrddhamanu(ruñcā)grahīśena krūreṇa ca samanvitam ||

bhūṣitañca varārohe tṛtīyaṃ bījamuttamam |
śaṃvakasya tu varṇasya ūrddhavarṇamvyavasthitam ||

tasyādiṃ gṛhya medhāvī dhruvānte āsana sthitam |
lokāntaṃ śirase dattaṃ caturthaṃ bījamuttamam ||

visargenasamāyuktaṃ śukravarṇaṃ tadagrataḥ |
kailāsa śikharasthañca varṇādi (cā)grataḥ sthitam ||

dhruvādi āsanasthañca piśitaṃ bhairavaṃ kuru |
saptamantadvijānīyāllohitaścāṣṭamaṃ bhavet ||

āṣāḍhī rudravarṇena āruḍhaṃ nātrasaṃśayaḥ |
navakeśāṃvakaṃ gṛhya veśomadhye vyavasthitam ||

kevalantadvijānīyānnavamaṃ nātrasaṃśayaḥ |
krodhabhairavamūddhṛtya nāgamavyāsanaṃ kuru ||

sūkṣmarudraṃ śire dattaṃ daśamaṃ bījamuttamam |
paṃcapraṇava yogena vaṣaḍaṃ netramuttamam ||

jakāraṃ yasya urddhantu sa bījaṃ yatrasaṃsthitam |
ekādaśe caturthasya piśitasyāsanaṃ kuru ||

viśvamālādi saṃbinnaṃ krūraṃ śirasisaṃsthitam |
pūrvavarṇaṃ vijānīyāt pāvanī tasyāgrataḥ sthitā ||

śukravarṇaṃ punarddevi kṣe(nā)ntena vibhūṣitam |
kailāsa śikharasthaṃ ca caturthaṃ varṇamuttamam ||

meṣarudrodbhavaṃ varṇaṃ lokāntaṃ śirabhūṣitam |
p. 15b) paṃcamaṃ bījamityāhuścāṣāḍhī tadanantaraṃ ||

saṃvakaṃ ca punarddevi saptamaṃ ca samālikhet |
ekādaśādi cāgre tu āṣāḍhī āsanasthitam ||

mandarānte na saṃbhinnaṃ aṣṭamaṃ bījamuttamam |
ṣaḍaṃgasya idaṃ devi phaḍantaṃ astramuttamam ||

ṣaṭkauśikamidaṃ piṇḍaṃ śivaśaktisamudbhavaṃ |
śuṭhyaṃ te nātrasaṃdehaḥ saptajanmāntikaṃ malam ||

punaranyaṃ pravakṣyāmi granthi nyāsaṃ yathā bhavet |
sarveṣāmādi bījāni ye bhūtā kathayāmite ||

akārādi visargāntā ye varṇā mātṛkodbhavāḥ |
ekaikaṃ teṣu pūrvasthaṃ granthi nyāsaṃ sadā kuru ||

ādi yuktena prathamamanantamaṅgulīṣu ca |
dvitīyena samāyuktaṃ kālagranthiṃ varānane ||

adhoṅguṣṭha(gulpha)sya karttavyaṃ nyāsaṃ parama durlabhaṃ |
tṛtīyenādi yuktena jyeṣṭhagranthirudāhṛtam ||

nyāsaṃ jaṃghāsumadhyasthaṃ karttavyaṃ varavarṇini |
raudrigranthi caturthena ādibhūtena jānuni ||

paṃcamena samāyuktaṃ vāmadevaṃ prakīrttitam |
nyāsaṃ kṛtvā prayatnena sthicābhyāṃ devi yatnataḥ ||

ṣaṣṭhaṃ gudasya madhyasthaṃ pūrvoccāraṃ jagasya ca |
brahmagranthiṃ punarddevi brahmasthā teṣu vinyaset ||

saptamena tu pūrveṇa svareṇa varavarṇini |
aṣṭamaṃ guhyamadhye tu kāmagranthimudāhṛtam ||

aṣṭamena tu bījena ādavuccāraṇaṃ kuru |
navame bījayogena somagranthiṃ ca vinyaset ||

adho nābhisthitaṃ sthānaṃ saṃpūrṇaṃ candravadyathā |
p. 16a) sūryyagranthi punardevi vinyasennābhi madhyataḥ ||

daśamena tu pūrveṇa kurvīta varavarṇini |
ādi ekādaśaṃ bījaṃ sthitamudaramadhyataḥ ||

nyāsaṃ kurvīta yatnena prāṇagranthiṃ sureśvari |
hṛdaye jīvagraṃtthi ca dvādaśenasamanvitam ||

viṣṇugranthiṃ vijānīyāt kaṇṭhasthāne trayodaśa |
nyāsaṃ kurvīta yatnena rudragranthiṃ caturdaśaḥ ||

caturddaśena bījena saṃsthitaṃ tālumadhyataḥ |
īśagranthiṃ kṛtaṃ nyāsaṃ bījaṃ pañcadaśena tu ||

lalāṭasthaṃ vijānīyāt tasyorddhe ṣoḍaśīṃ kalāṃ |
sadāśivasya devasya granthimūrtte vyavasthitā ||

ṣoḍaśī kalabhedena granthinyāsaṃ prakīrttitam |
punardvādaśakaṃ caiva aṃbikānyāsamuttamam ||

tadahaṃ saṃpravakṣyāmi mahāsiddhikaraṃ param |
śukrabhairava muddhṛtya śūladaṇḍāsanasthitam ||

tasyorddhve aṃbikā devī kuṭabījamidaṃ mahat |
uddhṛtavyaṃ prayatnena sthāneṣu dvādaśeṣu ca ||

dvādaśasvarabhedena bheditavyaṃ (pra)yatnataḥ |
ekaikena svareṇaiva akārādi visargajāḥ ||

prathame bījayogena siddhināmā prakīrtitā |
nyāsaṃ pādeṣu karttavyaṃ sarvasiddhipradāyakaṃ ||

dvitīyena tu bījena ṛddhināmā mahābalā |
atranyāsaṃ prakurvīta siddhyate nātrasaṃśayaḥ (jānu nyāsaṃ prakartavya 
mṛddhi hetornasaṃśayaḥ) ||

rā(vā)mabījena deveśi pūrveṇa cakra(ūrū)madhyataḥ |
a(ghu)tinyāsaṃ ca vakṣyāmi sarvākṛṣṭikaraṃ param ||

p. 16b)

caturthaṃ gudamadhyasthaṃ yatra lakṣmīrvyavasthitā |
nyāsaṃ kurvīta yatnena mahālakṣmī prasādhanaṃ ||

pañcamena tu bījena nābhinyāsaṃ vyavasthitam |
dīptināmādharā devyā jñānadīpapradāyikā ||

hṛdimadhyagatā devi ṣaṣṭhabījādi saṃsthitā |
māyānāma parāsūkṣmā sarvamāyā prabodhanī ||

saptamaṃ bījamāśritya śivākaṇṭhasya madhyataḥ |
śivāśānti karī sā tu vinyastā devi yantataḥ ||

sumukhi nāmayā devyā(vī) aṣṭame bījasaṃsthitā |
mukhamadhyakṛtaṃ nyāsaṃ vācāṃ siddhikaraṃ param ||

navamena tu bījena ādi bhūtena cātmanā |
purapraveśaṃ kurute nāsikā nyāsamuttamam ||

netrābhyāṃ nandinī devī sarvānandakarī subhā |
daśamena tu pūrveṇa vinyastā dūradarśanī ||

ekādaśena bījena harikeśī mahābalā |
durāśravaṇā ca kurute vinyastā karṇamadhyataḥ ||

mūrdhnisthaṃ dvādaśe naiva mahākeśī pratiṣṭhitā |
dharmmakāmārtha mokṣaṃ ca nyāsaṃ sarvārtha siddhidaṃ ||

iti pārameśvarīmate dvādaśāṅga ṣaḍaṅga aṃbikānyāsamudhāramekona 
triṃśatimaḥ paṭalaḥ ||





śrībhairava uvāca


punaranyaṃ pravakṣyāmi tadgraha(ddhṛtaṃ)nyāsamuttamam |
yena vinyasta mātreṇa sarvasiddhiñca jāyate ||

śrīkaṇṭhaṃ nādinī devī parā bījaṃ tṛtīyakam |
anantaṃ grasanī bījaṃ tṛtīyaṃ parasambhavaṃ ||

p. 17a)

sūkṣmarudraṃ nivṛttīśca vidyā bījāntasaṃsthitam |
pratiṣṭhāyai trimūrtādi ante bījaṃ parasya ca ||

amarīśādi vidyāyai tṛtīyaṃ parapañcamam |
arghīśaṃ śānti pūrvasthaṃ māyā bījaṃ tṛtīyakaṃ ||

bhārabhūtiśca cāmuṇḍā parasya saptamaṃ likhet |
atithiṃ pūrvataḥ kṛtvā madhyasthaṃ priyadarśanī ||

tasyānte aṣṭamaṃ bījaṃ lohitaṃ ca samuddharet |
nārāyaṇī punardevi sthāṇvīśamādi(saṃsthi)tam ||

tasyānte raktamityāhuḥ paraspaparamañca yat |
hareśaṃ mohanī bījaṃ makāraṃ purataḥ sthitam ||

jhaṇṭīrudrodbhavaṃ varṇaṃ prajñā(jā)yai madhyasaṃsthitā |
tasyānte (aṣṭamaṃ bījaṃ lohitaṃ ca samuddharet) vadātavyaṃ rudrabījaṃ 
parasya ca ||

bhūtipūrvaṃ vijānīyād guhyaśaktistadagrataḥ |
parābījaṃ likhet prājño dvādaśañca na saṃśayaḥ ||

sadyojātaṃ punarddevi vajrinī madhyasaṃsthitā |
tṛtīyañca punarddevi parābījaṃ trayodaśaṃ ||

pūrve anugrahīśaṃ ca madhye kālī vyavasthitā |
tasyānte paramaṃ bījaṃ bījaṃ deyaṃ caturdaśaṃ ||

krūravarṇaṃ punardevi kapālī madhyasaṃsthitam |
parāpañcadaśaṃ bījaṃ punastaṃ tasya dāpayet ||

śivāmadhyagataṃ kuryāt mahāsenañca pūrvagam |
parāṣoḍaśamaṃ bījaṃ tṛtīyaṃ parataḥ sthitam ||

pūrvaṃ krodhaṃ samuddhṛtya ghorāṃ ca tadanantaraṃ |
saptadaśaṃ punardevi parābījaṃ tadagrataḥ ||

p. 17b)

pracaṇḍabhairavaṃ pūrvaṃ dvi?rvarāmadhyasaṃsthitā |
aṣṭādaśatṛtīyañca parābījaṃ samudbhavam ||

pracaṇḍabhairavaṃ pūrvaṃ madhye sarasvatīṃ viduḥ |
tasyānte tu punardevi (parāva?)jaṃ samuddharet ||

śivamādisamālikhya vā gegī?madhyasaṃsthitā |
parābījaṃ tṛtīyañca puratastasya dāpayet ||

ekapādaṃ mahārudraṃ madhye tu śikhivāhinī |
tasyānte caiva dātavyamekaviṃśatimaṃ parā ||

kūrmabhairavapūrvasthaṃ bhīmā ca madhyasaṃsthitā |
parābījaṃ tṛtīyaṃ ca nyāsaṃ kurvīta yatnataḥ ||

ekahastaṃ(netraṃ) punardevi vāyuvegasya pūrvaka(ga)m |
tṛtīyaṃ caiva dāttavyaṃ parābījaṃ suniścitaṃ ||

nāmā ca cakra madhyasthaṃ parābījaṃ tadagrataḥ |
caturānanena saṃyuktaṃ ādisthaṃ nyāsamuttamam ||

vāmakaṅgaṇasaṃbhūtaṃ bhairavaṃ ādi saṃsthitaṃ |
devī vināyakī nāma agratastasya dāpayet ||

parābījaṃ punaryadvattṛtīyaṃ saṃsthitaṃ kuru |
sarmmabhairavapūrvasthaṃ pūrṇimāmadhyasaṃsthitaṃ ||

parāvidyā samutpannaṃ (puratasthaṃ tṛtīyakaṃ) |
pūrve someśvaraṃ deyaṃ madhye jhaṃkāri(ṇīṃ) viduḥ ||

tṛtīyaṃ bījaṃ dātavyaṃ parāvidyāsamuddhṛtam |
lāṃgalīśaṃpunardevi kurdanyā pūrvasaṃsthitaṃ ||

parābījaṃ tṛtīyaṃ ca tasyānte ca samālikhet |
dārubhairavapūrvasthaṃ dīpinī madhyasaṃsthitā ||

uddhṛtavyaṃ prayatnena parābījantadagrataḥ |
arddhanārīśvaraṃ devaṃ jayantī pūrvasaṃsthitaṃ ||

p. 18a) parāvidyo(bījo)dbhavaṃ varṇaṃ tṛtīyaṃ ca samālikhet |
pūrvabījaṃ samuddhṛtya umākāntaṃ ca bhairavaṃ ||

kapālī madhya deśe tu tṛtīyaṃ ca parodbhavaṃ |
āṣāḍhībhairavaṃ pūrvaṃ pavanīmadhyasaṃsthitaṃ ||

parābījaṃ tṛtīyañca uddhṛtavyaṃ suyatnataḥ |
aṃbikā yā tu pūrve tu sthāpitaṃ diṇḍibhairavaṃ ||

aṃbikānte tṛtīyaṃ ca parābījaṃ samuddharet |
dhātrīśaṃ bhairavaṃ pūrvaṃ mahāsenaṃ ca (icchāśaktiśca) madhyagām ||

parābījaṃ tṛtīyaṃ ca ālikhedvaravarṇini |
mīnarudraśca pūrve tu sarmmarudro mahābalaṃ ||

udhṛtavyaṃ tṛtīyaṃ ca mahābījaṃ parasya ca |
meṣabhairavapūrvasthaṃ chāgalīmadhyasaṃsthitaṃ ||

tṛtīyaṃ ca parābījaṃ sthāpitavyaṃ tadagrataḥ |
lohitaṃ bhairavī devi puta (nāpārśva) pūrvagaṃ ||

ante bījaṃ tṛtīyaṃ ca parādevyā samudbhavaṃ |
śikhivāhī mahārudraṃ āṣāḍhī madhyasaṃsthitā ||

tasyānte paramutpannaṃ sthitaṃ bījaṃ su(du)rlabhaṃ |
chagalaṇḍaṃ punardevi tasyānte laṃvinīṃ viduḥ ||

parāvarṇaṃ(para) vijānīyāt tṛtīyaṃ nātrasaṃśayaḥ |
dviraṇḍaṃ bhairavaṃ hutvā madhye saṃhāriṇī bhavet ||

parābījaṃ tṛtīyaṃ ca uddhṛtavyaṃ prayatnataḥ |
pūrve rudraṃ mahākālaṃ mahākālī ca madhyagā ||

uddhṛtavyaṃ tadante ca parābījaṃ na saṃśayaḥ |
vāgī(lī)śaṃ bhairavaṃ pūrvaṃ ante kusumāyudha sthitaṃ ||

uddhṛtavyaṃ prayatnena ante bījaṃ parasya ca |
bhujaṃgabhairavaṃ pūrvaṃ devī madhye tu saṃsthitaṃ ||

p. 18b) parābījaṃ tṛtīyaṃ ca uddhṛtavyaṃ prayatnataḥ |
pūrve vināyakītyāhuḥ madhye tārā vyavasthitā ||

parābījaṃ tṛtīyaṃ ca tasyānte saṃsthitaṃ kuru |
khaḍgīśaṃ bhairavaṃ devi jñānaśaktiśca pūrvagaṃ ||

tṛtīyaṃ ca samuddhṛtya bījaṃ caiva parasya ca |
saṃvakañca samuddhṛtya kriyāśaktiśca madhyagā ||

parāyāśca tṛtīyaṃ tu tadante tasya dāpayet |
śvetabhairavapūrvasthaṃ gāyatrī madhyasaṃsthitā ||

aparasya tṛtīyaṃ tu tasyānte kuruyatnataḥ |
sāvitrī madhyadeśe tu bhṛguḥ pūrve vyavasthitaḥ ||

aparasya tṛtīyaṃ tu tasyānte ca samālikhet |
lakulīśaṃ bhairavaṃ pūrvaṃ dahanī madhyasaṃsthitaṃ ||

uddhṛtavyaṃ prayatnena tasyānte tu parāpunaḥ(parāṃ) |
saṃvarttakaṃ mahārudraṃ pūrvabhāge vyavasthitaṃ ||

madhye phetkāriṇī devī trividyāntaṃ tṛtīyakaṃ |
devī yajño pavītañca nyāsaṃ sarvāṅgasiddhidaṃ ||

sarvavighnopaśamanaṃ kurvītavaravarṇini |
akārādi kṣakārāntā ye varṇā rudra saṃbhavāḥ ||

ekaikaṃ tasya pūrve tu uccāraṃ devi saṃsthitaṃ |
nakārādi phakārāntaṃ madhyasthaṃ mālinīṃ kuru ||

parā ca aparā caiva tathā caiva parāparā |
etāṣu triṣu vidyāṣu bījaṃ paṃcāśatāni ca ||

mālinyodbhavavarṇānāmekaikaṃ puratasthitaṃ |
śabdarāśi kṛtaṃ nyāsaṃ varṇabhairavasaṃyutam ||

mālinī ca punardevyā vinyastā yoginī saha |
parā ca aparā devī tathā nyāsa parāparā ||

p. 19a) pareṣu sarvamantreṣu nyāsaṃ pūrvaṃ ca yatkṛtam |
tadgrahena vinā devi tatsarvaṃ nisphalaṃ bhavet ||

vinyastaṃ vidhiyogena devī yajño pavītakam |
sabījaṃ bhavate sarvaṃ pūrvamāse ca yatkṛtam ||


iti pārameśvarīmate tadgrahoddhārastriṃśatimaḥ paṭalaḥ ||




śrībhairava uvāca


athātaḥ saṃpravakṣyāmi ślokāni dvādaśāni ca |
yena vijñāna mātreṇa sarva pāpaiḥ pramucyate ||

arghīśamandire devi adhovarṇaṃ pratiṣṭhitam |
pūrveniṣa ca (saṃ)sthaṃ ca śire(va) tasya pradāpayet ||

devī kusumāyudhā varṇaṃ tasya ūrddhe tu dāpayet |
ādibījaṃ vijānīyāt bhujaṅgasthaṃ na saṃśayaḥ ||

ekādaśe ṛṣisthāne mālinī kaṇṭha ūrddhataḥ |
uddhṛtavyaṃ prayatnena ubhausthāne tadagrataḥ ||

punastaṃ bījamagre tu pāvakāsanasaṃsthitam |
adhastasya pradātavyaṃ śṛmadhyordhasaṃyutaṃ ||

mālinī saṃbhavaṃ śukraṃ śirasi tasya dāpayet |
mandire bhūtamālāyāṃ pūrve varṇaṃ vyavasthitaṃ ||

rikārā(navakā)di samārudhaṃ ṛvaṇaṃ ca śire sthitam |
lokādi varṇabhedena bhūṣitaṃ paṃcamaṃ bhavet ||

hu aṃtaṃ ca samuddhṛtya lokāntena samanvitam |
rudrasaṃvarttakaṃ nāma bhūtabījāntasaṃsthitam ||

eṣāṣaḍakṣarī vidyā ādibhūtāa kulāgame |
atramadhye samutpannā ślokāni dvādaśāni ca ||

p. 19b)

paṃcāśadvarṇabhedena yathā bhavati tachṛṇu |
vidyā bījaṃ dvitīyasya pūrve pūrvaṃ vyavasthitam ||

tasyānte ca punardevi tadvarṇaṃ saṃsthitaṃ kuru |
etāni ca suvarṇāni yugasthāneṣu kārayet ||

mātṛkodbhavavarṇaṃ ca ekaikanteṣu dāpayet |
dvitīyañca tṛtīyañca vidyā bījāni yāni ca ||

pūrve pūrve ca kartavyaṃ ekaikaṃ mātṛkākṣaraṃ |
akārādi sakārāntā pāvanī pūrṇatāṅgatā ||

iti vai dvādaśaślokāḥ pūrvamīśena bhāṣitāḥ |
yaḥ paṭhed dīyate yasya karṇaḥ karṇo padeśikāḥ ||

guptāguptatarā hyete pustakena ca saṃlikhet |
eṣā ṣaḍakṣarī vidyā na deyā yasya kasyacit ||

adīkṣitena dātavyaṃ kulamārgasya nindake |
pramādāhadatte yastu siddhihānistu jāyate ||

nyāsaṃ kurvīta yatnena mekhalā kubjikasya ca |
bhūtapretapiśācāśca duṣṭadaṃṣṭra(dasyuṃ)bhayānakāḥ ||

yamaścitro vicitraśca teṣu dūtā mahābalāḥ |
sa jīvojīva hīnasya sa pāpe nirmalasya ca ||

na śaknoti varārohe vaktraṃ tasya nirikṣituṃ |
nirjjityayamadūtāni rudralokaṃ sa gacchati ||

athānyaṃ saṃpravakṣyāmi śarīre dvādaśāni ca |
ākramyamūlakaraṇairadhorddhvaṃ ca vidyadhṛyet? ||

śaktidīpaśikhākārā sphurannāḍī niveśayet |
brahmarandhraṃ tu tatprāpya viṣṇurudreśvaraṃ śivaṃ ||

vijñānamamṛtaṃ divyaṃ caturvargaphalapradaṃ |
p. 20a) nāsikārandhra ūrddhe tu yāvadvyomavilāśrayaṃ ||

urddhasthā paramāśaktirvarṣanī paramāmṛtam |
candrabimbakalākāntaṃ dhautacāmīkaraprabhaṃ ||

dhyāyeccātmani saṃlīnamāpādatalamastakaṃ |
nāsikāgre gatā dṛṣṭiramṛtaṃ tasvādayet paraṃ ||

kākacaṃcu puṭākāramauṣṭau cākṛṣya sadbhutam |
ātmā samarasaṃ devi ādisthāne parāmṛtam ||

samādhireṣā dehasya samalaṃ ca prayacchati |
sarvāṅgamamṛtaṃ sicya tatrasthaṃ dehavigrahaṃ ||

tadāvarṇapadāvasthāṃ sa bāhyābhyantaraṃ priye |
(ahanya hani vinyasya) dharmādharmo samepade ||

yojayeccātmano dehe yogaṃ mṛtyuvināśanam |
āpādatalamūrddhāntaṃ nādāntaṃ tatvamuttamam ||

candrasūryasamaikatvaṃ yaḥ paśyati samucyati |
utpatennipateccaiva punarārudreśvare pade ||

nādo padeśaṃ vettā ca labhate śāśvataṃ padam |
karaṇadvayasaṃkṣobhāt somārkkātalamadhyagau ||

yadābindu viniṣkrāntantadātachreyatāṅgataḥ |
parabinduviniṣkrāntaṃ nityāditya pravāhakam ||

ye pivanti ca muktāste te yānti paramāṅgatiṃ |
ete ca dvādaśaślokāḥ pūrvaṃ devi mayā śrutāḥ ||

namayā bhāṣitā devi svayamevavinirgatāḥ ||
paṃcaratnasya saṃbaṃdhe daśaślokā udāhṛtāḥ ||

nirālambapade dve tu iti dvādaśakīrtitāḥ |


śrībhairava uvāca


athānyaṃ saṃpravakṣyāmi paṃcaratnāni yāni ca |
p. 20b) pañcasthānaviśudhyarthaṃ kṛtaṃ nyāsaṃ sureśvari ||

tato madhyagataṃ gṛhya graheṣu bhūtamaṃdire |
ekādaśe caturthe tu śikhā madhye vyavasthitam ||

dvitīyaṃ ca samuddhṛtya pavanī tasyā(pā)gratasthitam |
mahākālodbhavaṃ varṇaṃ puratastasya dāpayet ||

mālinī mūrdhnivarṇantu paṃcamañca na saṃśayaḥ |
etāni pañcavarṇāni lokādi bhūṣitaṃ kuru ||

(lokamadhyagataṃ vīkṣya adhasteyāṃ niyojayet |
arghīśena samārūḍhā lokāntena vibhūṣitāḥ ||)

bhrumadhyagataṃ varṇaṃ pinākī bhairavasya ca |
tatrāruḍhāni sarvāṇi arghiśāsanasaṃsthitāḥ ||

etāni paṃcaratnāni paṃcasthāneṣu vinyaset |
prathamaṃ murdhnimadhyasthaṃ saṃsthitaṃ candramaṇḍalaṃ ||

ṣoḍaśāraṃ mahāpadmaṃ adhomukhavilaṃbitam |
kundendu tu hinābhāsantasya madhye tu karṇikā ||

tatra madhyagataṃ varṇaṃ prathamaṃ ratnamuttamam |
akārādivisargāntaṃ ṣoḍaśāni kalāni ca ||

saṃsthitāni dalāgreṣu saṃpūrṇaśaśiraśmayaḥ |
śvetavarṇāmṛtenaiva varṣantaṃ cintayedbudhaḥ ||

tasya paṃkajamadhyasthaṃ ātmānaṃ cintayet tataḥ |
prathame ratnayogena saṃlīnaṃ dīpavadyathā ||

anenā bhyāsa yogena ṣaṇmāsātpalita |
kṣayādivyatejo mahākāyo abdamekena jāyate ||

varṣaiḥ ṣoḍaśabhirdevi khecaratvaṃ na saṃśayaḥ |
prathamakramayogena ye siddhāḥ kathayāmyaham ||

catuḥṣaṣṭistu koṭīnāṃ siddhānāṃ yoginī saha |
adhikāraṃ prakurvanti gaganasthā varānane ||

p. 21a)

dvitīyaṃ hṛdimadhyasthamaṣṭapatraṃ suśobhanam(sakarṇikam) |
śvetaṃ pītaṃ ca varṇābhaṃ karṇikā madhyasaṃsthitaṃ ||

tatra madhyagataṃ dhyāyet dvitīyaṃ ratnamuttamam |
śabdaḥ sparśañca rūpañca raso gaṃdhañca pañcamaḥ ||

manobuddhirahaṃkāra ekaikaṃ ca daleṣu ca |
madhyasthaṃ kuruyatnena ātmānaṃ varavarṇini ||

abdamekena deveśi utpadyante ca ye guṇāḥ |
dūraśravaṇavijñānaṃ durādarśanameva ca ||

anenakramayogena abhyāsena na saṃśayaḥ |
varṣadvādaśabhirdevi siddhyaṃte nātrasaṃśayaḥ ||

paṃcāśatkoṭiyoginyaḥ siddhaistu sahitaḥ purā |
adhikāraṃ mayā dattaṃ svargasthāḥ krīḍayaṃtitāḥ ||

punarnābhigataṃ cakraṃ ṣaṭpatraṃ paṃkaje sthitam |
raktaśvetamayaṃ sarvaṃ madhye ratnatṛtīyakaṃ ||

piṇḍasthañca vijānīyāt saṃsthitaṃ viṣamadhyataḥ |
tatra madhyagataṃ jīva(bījaṃ) dīpākāraṃ vicintayet ||

tāvattaccintayeddevi yāvattallayatāṃ gataḥ |
ṣaṇmāsābhyāsayogena vāgīśo nātrasaṃśayaḥ ||

vadate sarvaśāstrāṇi adṛṣṭā (nya) śrutāni ca |
parakāyapraveśañca kurute nātrasaṃśayaḥ ||

saptaviṃśati varṣaistu kathitāsiddhikhecarī |
dvātriṃśatkoṭi siddhānāṃ yoginyā sahitāni ca (yoginyaḥ siddhaiścaiva saha 
sthitā) ||

atrasiddhāvarārohe pātāle adhikāriṇaḥ |
yā sā śaktiḥ parāsūkṣmā susuptabhujagopamā ||

tasya madhye trikoṇañca sindūrāruṇasannibham |
p. 21b) yonisthaṃ paramaṃ divyaṃ yathotpannaṃ carācaram ||

tasyamadhyagataṃdhyāyeccaturthaṃ ratnamuttamam |
mahādīptikaraṃ divyaṃ bhāsayantaṃ carācaraṃ ||

ātmānaṃ sūkṣmarūpeṇa tatrasthāne layaṃ kuru |
tasyaivābhyāsayogena nirmalatvaṃ ca jāyate ||

dhūnanaṃ kaṃpaṃnā veśaṃ ānandaṃ surmi(ghūrṇa)meva ca |
divyabhāṣāgamannidrāṃ viṣuvecāraṃ saṃsthitam ||

satatābhyāsayogena vācāṃ siddhiśca jāyate |
nigrahānugrahaṃ caiva rūpasya parivarttanam ||

dṛśyo adṛśyatāṃ yāti abdadvādaśakena tu |
khecaraiḥ sahasāmānyamicchāsiddhiśca jāyate ||

anenābhyāsayogena siddhirvā kathayāmyaham |
aṣṭādaśānikoṭīnāṃ siddhānāṃ yoginī saha ||

adhikāraṃ prakurvanti martyaloke vyavasthitāḥ |
punaścādhāramadhyasthaṃ sthitaṃ cakraṃ maheśvari ||

tridalaṃ karvurākāraṃ svetaraktaṃ ca paṃkajaṃ |
tasyamadhye samutpannaṃ vaṃśāṃkuranibhaṃ priye ||

raktavarṇaṃ mahātejaṃ tasyānte saṃsthitā śikhā |
aṃguṣṭhaparvamānena dīpākārāvyavasthitā ||

tasyamadhye sthitaṃ devi paṃcamaṃ ratnamuttamam |
ātmabījenasaṃyuktaṃ tatrasthaṃ sūkṣmarūpiṇaṃ ||

tāvaccintayate devi yāvattallayatāṃ gataḥ |
ānandaṃppūrmi(ghūrṇa)māvejāyate maṇḍukaplutiḥ ||

yoganidrāsthito yogī paśyate avanī talaṃ |
divyabhāṣāgamaṃ caiva parakāyapraveśanaṃ ||

valīpalitanirmukto mṛtyustasya na jāyate |
abdaiḥ ṣoḍaśabhirdevi siddho bhavati niścitam ||

p. 22a)

atra cakre tu ye siddhā aṣṭakoṭimahābalāḥ |
yoginī sahasiddhānāṃ nāgalokādhikāriṇaḥ ||

etāni paṃcaratnāni kulamārge vyavasthitāḥ |
mahāsiddhi karāhyete na deyā yasya kasyacit ||

kulamārgābhiṣiktasya anuṣṭhānaratasya ca |
anekajanmayogena labhate nātrasaṃśayaḥ ||


śrīdevyuvāvāca 


śrutaṃ devatva śeṣastu nyāsaḥ paramadurlabhaḥ |
idānīṃ śrotumicchāmi ṣadūtīnyāsamuttamam ||

yena vinyasyamātreṇa ṣoḍhānyāsaphalaṃ labhet |
tanme kathaya deveśa yadi tuṣṭosi śaṃkara ||


śrībhairava uvāca


sādhu devi mahāprājñe yattvayā pṛṣṭamādarāt |
tadahaṃ saṃpravakṣyāmi tava bhaktyā na saṃśayaḥ ||

na deyaṃ yasya kasyāpi yataṃ krūrāstu mānavāḥ |
ādeśena vinā devi yo dadyājjñānamuttamam ||

bhakṣayanti ca yoginyo ḍā?dyāḥ kvaddhānasaṃśayaḥ |
dhanaṃdhānyaṃ kalatraṃ ca putrapautrādi saṃtatiṃ ||

nighnanti 'sataśaḥ kruddhā bhairavādeśataḥ sadā |
tasmādbhaktaṃ dṛḍhaṃ guptaṃ samayācārapālakam ||

kulīnaṃ sattvasampannaṃ * * * * * * * * |
* * * * * * * * * * * * * * * ||

* * * * * taṃ bījaṃ chaṅgamadhyoparisthitam |
sakhimadhyaṃ gatodhastā deyaṃ bījaṃ khageśvaram ||

bhūtādi mandarasyevadayavājavaraparam |
* * * * * * * * * * * * vibhūṣitam ||

p. 22b)

bindumastakasaṃbhinnaṃ kālikā hṛdayaṃ param |
hādibījaṃ samuddhṛtya tasyāgrerīlu madhyagam ||

viśvādimastake bhinnaṃ dvitīyaṃ varṇamuttamam |
nubhā * * punardevī hyanantenasamanvitam ||

ṣādivarṇaṃ punardevi anaṃtenasamanvitam |
pinākivarṇamuddhṛtya bhe?sayormmadhyasaṃsthitam ||

viśvādināsamāyuktaṃ vamuvarṇe prakīrtitam |
me? ca madhyagataṃ bījaṃ ṛ antenasamanvitam ||

bindunā śirasākrāntaṃ bhṛguvarṇannikevalam |?
śāstraṃ bījaṃ samuddhṛtya * * * * * * * * ||?

* * ? śirasi tasyaiva nāgamadhyagataḥ param |
bhe? ādi ṛṣimālāyāṃ sadyo jātenasaṃyutam ||

jacchīmadhyaṃ punarvarṇaṃ nogūmadhye tadagrataḥ |
trimūrttinā śiro bhinnaṃ varṇaṃ candra kalāṃkitam ||

javamadhyaṃ tridhā devī deyaṃ bījaṃ varānane |
bhujaṃgāsanamāsīnaṃ tadbījaṃ kārayet tridhā ||

prathamasya tvanantena dvitīyasya trimūrtinā |
arghīśena tṛtīyasya śiro deśaṃ vibhūṣayet ||

krūreṇaśirasākrāntaṃ bījatrayamudāhṛtam |
raktavarṇe punardevi krodhavarṇaṃ tadagrataḥ ||

ṭhīramadhyena saṃbhinnaṃ bījamekona viṃśakam |
punaḥ krodhaṃ samuddhṛtya kha urdhve adhasaṃsthitam ||

kīramadhyasthitaṃ varṇamumākāntaḥ śiraḥ sthitam |
jhaṃṭīśena samāyuktaṃ vajriṇīvarṇamagrataḥ ||

p. 23a)

viśvādinā śirobhinnaṃ bījaṃ syāt saptaviṃśakam |
mahākāladvayaṃ devi anantena vibhūṣitam ||

bindumastakasaṃbhinnamekamekonaviṃśakam |
lohitaṃ varṇamādāya pāno madhye vyavasthitam ||

veśamadhye samuddhṛtya vālīśāsanasaṃsthitam |
śirasikrūrasaṃyuktaṃ māṣāḍhantasya cāgrataḥ ||

daśaviṃśatimaṃ bījaṃ dvyadhikaṃjantasaṃsthitam |
vesomadhyaṃ punardevi tasyāṣāḍhī mukhe sthitam ||

bhujaṃgāsanasaṃsthaśca vajriṇī bījamagrataḥ |
ḷ antena samāyuktaṃ bījaṃ ṣaṭviṃśakaṃ varam ||

ante ca praṇavo deyo vilomāt pūrvasūcitā |
eṣādevī mahākālī kālakālānala prabhā ||

sarvajñādi guṇairyuktā bhuktimuktiphalapradā |
na deyā devadeveśī yasya no bhairavī tanuḥ ||

mayāpi gopinā pūrvaṃ brahmaviśveṣṇīśvādiṣu? |
devadānavasiddheṣu yakṣagandharvakeṣu ca ||

nāgeṣunāgapatnīṣu gopitābhuvidurlabhā |
yo dadāti imāṃ devī gurutastasya jagattraye ||

nādeyaṃ viyute tasya ātmadārādikaṃ ca yat |
idānīṃ devi te guhyā guhyāpi prakaṭī kṛtā ||

gopanīyā tvayā devi sarvathā sarvadā priye |
dattvādau praṇavānyaṃ ca tataḥ kūḍhaṃ * * * * ||

u ādimaṃ samuṃdhṛtya ga anteyadvyavasthitam |
tasyādho raktabījaṃ ca pūtanā tadadhaḥ sthitā ||

krodhabījāsavo dadyāttajvalamadhya madhaḥ punaḥ |
p. 23b) chaṅgamadhyamadhastasmāddhruvāntāsanasaṃsthitaṃ ||

tasyādho raktabījaṃ ca pūtanā na dadhaḥ sthitā |
a aṃtena tu saṃyuktaṃ nāma bindukalāṅkitam ||

kūṭarājamidaṃ devi deva dānavanāśakṛt |
śākinīhṛdayaṃ guhyaṃ tava bhaktyā mayoditam ||

yena japtaṃ hutaṃ dhyātaṃ lakṣāṇāṃ rasasaṃkhyayā |
tasya saṃkalpamātreṇa nigrahasaṃpravartate ||

meṣarudraṃ samādāyamahākālaṃ tadagrataḥ |
sadyojātenasaṃyuktaṃ bījarājaṃ dvitīyakam ||

dviraṇḍabhairavaṃ gṛhya pracaṇḍaṃ tasya cāgrataḥ |
vajriṇī bījamantre tu āṣāḍhī tadanaṃtaram ||

viśvādināśiro bhinnaṃ bījarājaṃ rasānvitam |
ṝ e madhyaṃ samuddhṛtya kaṣāntaṃ saṃsthitaṃ puraḥ ||

someśvarāsanasthaṃ ca vasu saṃkhyaṃ prakīrttitam |
kūrmarudraṃ tato dadyādanaṃtenasamanvitam ||

nādabinduśirobhinnaṃ ṭhī aṃtaṃ tasya cāgrataḥ |
ṛ aṃtena samāyuktaṃ pinākī tadanantaram ||

viśvādinā samāyuktama svaraṃ rudra * * kam |
bujaṃgabhairavaṃ gṛhya dhruvādyāsanasaṃsthitam ||

mīnarudraṃsamādāyamandarasthena maṇḍitam |
mālinīśūladaṇḍaṃ ca mahākālaṃ tadagrataḥ ||

anaṃtaṃ bindusaṃyuktaṃ tasya mūrdhne niveśayet |
bhṛṃgabhairavakaṃ paścāt kevalaṃ tasya cāgrataḥ ||

dviraṇḍaṃ bhairavaṃ tasmāt samvartaṃ tadanaṃtaram |
ikāreṇasamāyuktamumākāntamatogrataḥ ||

krodhabhairavamādāya o ādiṃ tasya cāgrataḥ |
dudhe madhyamumākāntāṃ neṣaḍhasthenabhūṣitam ||

p. 24a)

lakūlīśaṃ tatoddhrtya meṣarudraṃ tadagrataḥ |
dvidhākāryaṃ varārohe dahanī tasya cāgrataḥ ||

lakulīśaṃ punardadyāntaṃ dvidhā ca sureśvari |
(* * * * samuddhṛtya akāreṇa samanvitam ||

bhujaṃgabhairavaṃ tasya mandarasthena bhūṣitam ||)???

mīnarudraṃ tato dadyāt mahākālaṃ tadagrataḥ |
dvidhākāryaṃ mahādevi lohitaṃ kūrmameva ca ||

grasabīja dvayaṃ dvedhā huṃ phaṭ svāhānta saṃyutā |
draṣṭavyāsaṃpuṭenaiṣā sākinītantranāyikā ||

śmaśāne caikavṛkṣe vā nadītīretha saṃgame |
lakṣamekaṃ japedyastu vidyāṃ vīravrate sthitaḥ ||

tasya vidhvaṃ samāyānti jvararogādi śatravaḥ |
tīvrājñā jāyate nūnaṃ nigrahānugraheṣu ca ||

kimanyaducyate devi devyā sāmarthyamadbhutam |
yasyāḥ smaraṇamātreṇa jvarabhūtādināśanam ||

anyacca kathayāmyasyāḥ sānnidhyakaraṇaṃ param |
ghṛtaplutaṃ mahāphalgu viśeṣeṇa tu homayet ||

eṣā devi tridhā khyātā śivodūtīṃ samuddharet |
na deyā yasya kasyāpi yo na vīrāṃśabhāgbhavet ||

* * * paṃcakaṃ cādau datvā devi tatoddharet |
praṇavādisamuddhṛtya āṣāḍhī tadanaṃtaram ||

tadadho devī dātavyaṃ sadyojātenasaṃyutam |
pūtanā bījamādāyamandarasthena bhūṣitam ||

anaṃtamagratasyeṣāsī madhyaṃ ca tato likhet |
ṛkāreṇa śirobhinnaṃ paṃcamaṃ bījamuttamam ||

p. 24b)

mahākālaṃ tatoddhṛtya somaṃsomāsanasthitam |
ṛkāreṇa śirobhinnaṃ aṃte ṛ aṃtasaṃsthitam ||

lakulīśaṃ punarbhadre vajriṇī ceśvare sthitā |
bhṛguvarṇaṃ binduyuktaṃ chagalaṇḍogrataḥ sthitam ||

someśvaramathoddhṛtyamandarasthena bhūṣitam |
anaṃtamante dātavyaṃ binducandrakalāṃkitam ||

dahanībījamādāya niṣaḍhasthena bhūṣitam |
ṛ aṃtamante dātavyaṃ binducandra kalādhikaṃ ||

* * * * grato devi śṛṃgamīśvarasaṃyutam |
dhruvādi vāṃ samālikhya pinākī tadanaṃtaram ||

jhaṃṭīśaṃ mastake dattaṃ tadagre ṛ adhaḥ sthitam |
mandarasthe na tadbhinnaṃ mante cānantasaṃyutam ||

pūtanordhvaṃ mahābījaṃ mantevajrā yudhā punaḥ |
tasyā dhastākādattaṃ kāntaṃ dhruvādi saṃyutam ||

ṭhakārasya adhodadyāt pūrṇimāṃ sarvasiddhidaṃ |
bhamamadhyaṃ samuddhṛtya pūtanāmagrataḥ sthitam ||

ravīrce bindumādāya tasyādhastātta deva tu |
mandarasthena saṃbhinnamante cānantasaṃsthitam ||

devatāpaṃcakaṃ cānte vilomenapradāpayet |
eṣā devī mahāguhyā bhellakhīnāmaviśrutā ||

mohanīsarvasatvānāṃ tailayāgaprayogataḥ |
sa rahasyā mahākhyātā na deyā yasya kasyacit ||

pūrṇimā dvitīyaṃ cādau tasyānte rakta mālikhet |
krodhabījaṃ samādāya āṣāḍhī hyāsana sthitam ||

jhaṃṭīśena śirobhinnaṃ vedavarṇamudhāhṛtam |
p. 25a) mahākālaṃ samādāya aṃbikātadanaṃtaram ||

anaṃtenasamāyuktaṃ rasabījaṃ prakīrtitam |
bhujaṃgaṃbījamuddhṛtya krodhabījamataḥ param ||

āṣāḍhī hyāsanārūḍhaṃ jñānaśaktivibheditam |
the ca madhyagataṃ gṛhya mahākālaṃ tato yataḥ ||

dhruvādyāsanamārūḍhaṃ śirasikrūradūṣitam |
ṭhī dūmadhyasthitaṃ gṛhya jhaṃṭīśenasamanvitam ||

bhṛguvarṇaṃ samādāya vajra bījāsanasthitam |
ra la madhyagataṃ gṛhya vālīśaṃ bījamagrataḥ ||

ṛ ādinā śiro bhinnaṃ bījaṃ bhuvanasaṃkhyayā |
eṣā vidyā mahādevī netra pūtīti kīrtitā ||

paṃcapraṇavayogena ādyantena varānane |
samuddharedimāṃ vidyāṃ sarvakāmārthasādhanīm ||

athātaḥ saṃpravakṣyāmi sarvakarmmaprasādhanīm |
mahāvidyā mahādevi astradūtīti nāmataḥ ||

taṇomadhyagataṃ gṛhya prādhānyāsanasaṃsthitam |
tasyāgre cāmbikā devī mātmāmadhyāsane sthitam ||

tasyāgre krodhabījaṃ tu ekāroparisaṃsthitam |
vajrīṇībījamādāya kī ja madhyasthitaṃ priyeḥ ||

mandarasthenasaṃbhinnaṃ caturthaṃ bījamuttamam |
ekādaśādi saṃgṛhya jñānaśaktyāvibhūṣitam ||

chaṅgamadhyagataṃ gṛhya dhruvādyāsanasaṃsthitam |
nādabindukalāyuktaṃ bījaṃ trailokya ḍāmaram ||

mālinīvāmapādaṃ tu ṛṣirādi sthitaṃ punaḥ |
p. 25b) etattu patitaṃ kuryāt saptamādakṣarātparaṃ ||

mahākālaṃ dvayaṃ gṛhyamīṣāmadhyagatoddharet |
cāve madhye tato gṛhya bhujaṃgena tu bhāṣitam ||

sadyojāte sa saṃbhinnaṃ bījamīkharasaṃjñakam |
pānomadhyagataṃ gṛhya dahanī tasya cāgrataḥ ||

bhujaṃgaṃ bhairavārūḍhaṃ viśvavarṇaṃ prakīrtitam |
snāyuvarṇaṃ samuddhṛtya anaṃtenasamanvitam ||

atomadhyagataṃ gṛhya śūladaṃḍoparisthitam |
tatastārāṃ samuddhṛtya kusumāyudhabhūṣitam ||

punaḥ pūrvākṣaraṃ datvā varṇānāṃ viṃśatiḥ |
smṛtāḥ manomadhyagataṃ gṛhya dhruvāntāsanasaṃsthitam ||

padhamadhyamadho gṛhya rephākrāntaṃ tu mastake |
pavanībījamuddhṛtya bhujaṃgāsanasaṃsthitam ||

pāmomadhyagataṃ gṛhya sadyojātenasaṃyutam |
khīnūmadhyagataṃ tasmādanaṃtenasamanvitam ||

dahanībījamādāya mandarāsthenamaṃḍitam |
krodhabījaṃ tatoddhṛtya krūreṇa parikalpitam ||

vevūmadhyagataṃ grāhyaṃ ṛśāṃsenavibhūṣitam |
meṣavarṇamataḥ paścāt krodhavarṇānalānvitam ||

tārakābījamuddhṛtya binduyuktaṃ cakārayet |
dakārādyaṃ samādāya ḷ urddhe yadvyavasthitam ||

thasyādhasthamathoddhṛtya anantenasamanvitam |
thaghomadhyagataṃ gṛhya mandarasthenamaṃḍitam ||

paṃcatriṃśatimaṃ bījaṃ sā ca tārā sa bindukā |
ekādaśādyamuddhṛtya bhujaṃgenadathānvite ||

* * * * * * * * hyadhovālīśasaṃyutam |
p. 26a) krūreṇamastake bhinnamaṣṭatriṃśatimaṃ smṛtam ||

nārādvinavamuddhṛtya īkārāsanasaṃsthitam |
kaṣāmadhyamatonyastvā hyadho * * * saṃyutam ||

krūreṇamastake bhinnamaṣṭatriṃśatimaṃ smṛtam |
tārādvitīyamuddhṛtya īkārāsanasaṃyutam ||

meṣavarṇaṃ tato datvā patitaṃ kārayet priye |
bhṛṃgabhairavabījaṃ ca tasyāgre vajriṇi bhavet ||

anaṃtenasamākrāntaṃ bhujaṃgena tu bhūṣitam |
mālīnyā śivāgre tu patitaṃ taccakārayet ||

harabījadvayaṃ deyaṃ rudravarṇaṃ tatolikhet |
karāline padaṃ datvā bījatrayamathoddharet ||

cāmuṃḍā hṛdayaṃ mārgān mudrārājastato bhavet |
kubjikāpādamādāya guhyapūrvaṃ ca sarvadā ||

huṃphaṭ svāhāntasaṃyuktaṃ vidyārājñī prakīrtitā |
yastvimāṃ japate vidyāṃ juhuyācca viseṣataḥ ||

trailokyavijayī devi bhavet satyaṃ na saṃśayaḥ |
praṇavanyāsaṃ prathamaṃ navāatmānaṃ dvitīyakam ||

trikhaṃḍāyāstṛtīyaṃ tu samayātmaṃ caturthakam |
paṃcamaṃ śabdarāśi mālinyā ṣaṣṭhamaṃ smṛtam ||

saptamaṃ ca trividyāyā hyaghoryāṣṭakamaṣṭamam |
navamaṃ dvādaśāṃgaṃ tu ṣaḍaṃgaṃ daśamaṃ smṛtam ||

ṣoḍaśagraṃthisaṃyuktaṃ nyāsamekādaśaṃ smṛtam |
p. 26b) dvādaśe dvādaśaṃ jñeyaṃ tadgrahasya trayodaśaṃ ||

ślokadvādaśekā nyāsaṃ bhuvanākhyaṃ prakīrtitam |
ratnapaṃcakavinyāsaṃ paṃcādhikadaśaṃ smṛtam ||

ṣaḍdūtyaḥ ṣoḍaśaṃ jñeyaṃ ṣoḍhā nyāsamiti smṛtam |
trisandhyaṃ vinyased yastu śuddhakāyo dṛḍhavrataḥ ||

tasya dṛṣṭi nipātena sphuṭaṃti girayopi ca |


iti pārameśvarīmate dvādaśaślokapaṃcaratnoddhāraḥ ekatriṃśatimaḥ paṭalaḥ 
||





devyuvāca


dvādaśāni ca ślokāni sa bāhyābhyantarāṇi ca |
paṃcaratnopadeśaṃ ca śrutaṃ nirmmalaṃ tāṃ mayā ||

adhunā śrotu michāmi kramasya pūjanaṃ yathā |
yena vijñāna mātreṇa ājñā siddhiḥ pravarttate ||

yā sā śaktiḥ parāsūkṣmā jagat mātā vyavasthitā |
ekamātrā dvimātrā ca trimātrā ca kathaṃ bhavet ||

arddhamātrā parāsūkṣmā kasyavarṇārdhasaṃsthi(rṇe vyavasthi)tā |
bhagākārā kathaṃ deva arddhaliṃgā vyavasthitā ||

p. 27a)

nirvāṇapadabhedena mathitā bhairaveṇa tu |
ku(i)kārākhyā mahā(parā)devyā tatra madhye vyavasthitā ||

ājñāsanena rūpeṇa rudraśakti samudbhavā |
ṣaṭprakāraṃ kathaṃ siddhaṃ kathaṃ pīṭha catuṣṭayaṃ ||

pīṭha devyaḥ kathaṃ deva siddhiśca sahitāḥ sthitāḥ |
kasmin syāne samutpannā brahmagranthiḥ kimucyate ||

p. 27b)

sakalaṃ kīdṛśaṃ deva niṣkalaṃ ca kathaṃ bhavet |
muktibhedaṃ kimākhyātaṃ astināsi vivarjjitam ||

śabdaḥsparśañca rūpañca rasogaṃdhaṃ ca pañcamaḥ |
ṣaṭcakrapiṇḍamadhyasthaṃ ceto bhūtaṃ vyavasthitam ||

indriyāṇāṃ guṇaṃ vetti keṣusthāne pṛthak pṛthak |
saptaviṃśati bhedāni(dena) agra(hyatra)madhye kathaṃ sthitā ||

nāḍī saṃcārabhedena ṣaṭprakāreṇa bhairava |
cakre cakre sthito devo jīvākhyaḥ paramaḥ prabhuḥ ||

tasya caṃcakimākhyātaṃ vaṃdhe muktiḥ kathaṃ bhavet |
vṛkṣatrayaṃ samākhyāhi sa bāhyābhyantareṣu ca ||

mudrāsī?so? mahādeva khecarādyā yathā bhavet |
samayamudrā parādevyā kathaṃ jānāmi tatvataḥ ||

gṛhaṃ pūrvaṃ kimākhyātaṃ paścimaṃ ca kathaṃ bhavet |
etad bhedaṃ paraṃ guhyaṃ mamākhyāhi maheśvara ||

sthitiṃ kasya vijānīyāllayaṃ kasya pratisthitam |
ubhayoścaiva yogena kiṃ jātaṃ parameśvara ||

mātṛkāvarṇabhedena ṣaṭprakārāḥ kathaṃ sthitāḥ |
saptaviṃśati bhedena maṇḍalāni vadasvame ||

kālajñāna(no) padāñcaiva(padeśeṣu) udbhavāstamaneṣu ca |
viparītaprayogena(ca) vaṃcanaṃ(rvaṇaṃ) ca vadasvame ||

trividhamadhikāraṃ ca samaya(tathā)saṃketamaṇḍalaṃ |
anenaiva śarīreṇa kathaṃ muktirbhaviṣyati ||

gamanaṃ triṣulokeṣu piṇḍapātaṃ na vidyate |
etat sarvaṃ samāsena prasādaṃ kuru bhairava ||


śrībhairava uvāca 

p. 28a)

kathayāmi na saṃdeho guptaṃ gopyaṃ surakṣitaṃ |
ādibhūtaṃ kulāmnāyaṃ tanmayā gopitaṃ purā ||

uttaraṃ dakṣiṇe mūlaṃ dakṣiṇaṃ paścimasya ca |
paścimamuttare yojyaṃ parāśaktirvyavasthitā ||

bhairavākhyaṃ dvitīyaṃ tu yasya(syo) pūrvaṃ vyavasthitam |
tasyārddhe vāmabhāgena ūrddhe caiva pralaṃbitam ||

pūrve mūlaṃ prakurvīta dvau śāṃkhau vāmadakṣiṇau |
ante savyāpasavyena tṛtīyaṃ ca niyojayet ||

yā sā śakti parā(ramā)sūkṣmā arddhavarṇā vyavasthitā |
tasyānte pūrvaratnasya dakṣiṇāvartta yojitā ||

bhairavasya kalāhyeṣā liṃgabhūtā vyavasthitā |
rudraśaktiriyaṃ devi atrotpannaṃ carācaraṃ ||

madhye manthāna yogena bhairavaṃ śaktibhīḥ saha |
avyaktāvyakta rūpeṇa utpannā parameśvari ||

ājñānāmaparāsiddhiḥ kukārākhyā vinirgatā |
eṣā sā vyaktarūpeṇa śakti rūpā tvadhaḥ sthitā ||

nirvāṇapadarūpeṇa tasyorddhe bhairavaḥ sthitaḥ |
ṣaṭkauśikamidaṃ piṇḍaṃ kulakaulaṃ vyavasthitam ||

yojanāti varārohe sa bhavet kulayoginaḥ |
atra pūjāprakarttavyā ṣaḍvidhañca kulakramam ||

oṃkāramadhya deśe tu jālākhyaṃ dakṣiṇena tu |
pūrṇapīṭhaṃ sthitaṃ vāme agre śrīkāmarūpakam ||

rakāraṃ ca mikāraṃ ca paṃcapraṇavasaṃyuta(puṭa)m |
madhyasthaṃ pūjayeddevi oṃkāra trayasaṃsthitam ||

oṃkāravarṇasaṃyuktaṃ paṃcapraṇavasaṃpuṭaṃ |
pūjitavyaṃ prayatnena pīṭhe jālandhareṣu ca ||

p. 28b)

cakāraṃ ca ṣakāraṃ ca devī devādi saṃsthitā |
pūrṇapīṭhasya madhyasthaṃ pūjitavyaṃ prayatnataḥ ||

ukāraṃ devi pūrvasya cakāraṃ siddhipūrvakam |
agre pīṭhe mahādevi pūjayet praṇavasaṃpuṭaṃ ||

etat pīṭhacatuṣkaṃ tu śaktikoṇe prapūjayet |
paṃcaratnāni madhye tu yaduktaṃ nyāsa me purā (yaduktaṃ te mayā purā) ||

vikāraṃ prathamaṃ pūjya akārantadantaram |
makāraṃ ca tṛtīyaṃ ca svākāraṃ ca caturthakam ||

ākāraṃ paṃcakasthaṃ ca punastat ṣaṣṭhameva ca |
rudraśaktiṣu madhyasthaṃ sṛṣṭiyogena pūjayet ||

jñāna ṣaṭkamidaṃ śuddhaṃ catuṣkaṃ ca ataḥ śṛṇu |
ākāre siddhanāthaṃ ca svākāraṃ dvitīyakam ||

tṛtīyaṃ cakra kāreṇa makāreṇa caturthakam |
ādi siddhāśca catvāri pīṭhasthāne prapūjayet ||

devyāpaṃcakamadhyasthaṃ kulamārge vyavasthitam |
mākāre prathamā devyā sakāreṇa dvitīyakā ||

tṛtīyā ca ūkāreṇa caturthī ca ca pūrvikā |
kukāre paṃcamā devyā saṃsthitā varavarṇini ||

ete mahalli(hātmi)kā paṃcamadhyasthaṃ pūjanaṃ kuru |
ākāraṃ ca akāre ca ukāraṃ ca tṛtīyakaṃ ||

rudrasya śikhe sthāne trikaṃ caiva prapūjayet |
catuṣkaṃ paṃcakaṃ ṣaṭkaṃ catuṣkaṃ pañcakaṃ trikam ||

ṣaṭprakāramidaṃ devi uktaṃ saṅketakena tu |
saptaviṃśati bhedāni sa bāhyābhyantareṣu ca ||

kuladīkṣābhiṣiktena jñātavyaṃ kulayogibhiḥ |
asyorddhe pūjayed devi gurupaṅktiḥ krameṇa tu ||

p. 29a) tasyāgre kulavṛkṣāṇaṃ trayamekatrapūjayet |
kadāhaṃ vāmakarṇe tu karajjaṃ dakṣiṇe tathā ||

kadambaṃ nāsikāgre ca vṛkṣa trayamudāhṛtam |
ānandastu visargāntaḥ kādiṭhāntastathā vilaḥ ||

ḍādihāntaḥ prabhurjñeyo yogī salājñādi lāntakaḥ | 
kādikṣāntastathā tītaḥ pādaḥ smādakṣa varṇakaḥ ||

ānandaścā baliścaiva prabhuryogī tathaiva ca |
atī taścaiva pādaśca ṣaṭprakārāṇi pūjayet ||

prabhālokā lalitā utpalā pratimā śaṃkhāsiṃdyā |
śivāmanmathā sadyā puruṣā śāṃtyā(martyā)bhairavā ||

raktā sarvāpuṣpā kamalā khakha(padmā)gāgagaṇāghorā |
eka caryā saṃpūrṇā sūryā khaḍgā devā aśvā candrā ||

pūrṇā ḍamarukā vinayā ātmā tatvāsthavirā dhanuṣā |
pādā pavanā śabdā brahmā bhāvā yogā svagā raktā ||

tejā ātmanā utpalā bhuvanā kulā saṃkṣepā piśācā |
etāni siddhanāmāni ṣaḍvidhāni bhavanti ca ||

mātṛkākṣarayogena ādi bhūtānikārayet |
pūrvaṃ varṇaṃ samuddhṛtya ante ānandasaṃsthitam ||

ṣaḍvidhasthāna bhedena pūjitavyaṃ prayatnataḥ |
viśuddha ṣoḍaśairbhedaiḥ kaṇṭhasthāne prapūjayet ||

akārādi visargāntaṃ pūjitavyaṃ prayatnataḥ |
atramadhyagataṃ varṇaṃ yasya nāmasya pūrvakaṃ ||

sopi nāmā bhavet tasya kaulikasya varānane |
te naiva dhyāna yogena siddhyate nātrasaṃśayaḥ ||

punastaṃ hṛdimadhye tu dvādaśāramanāhatam |
kaṃṭhamadhyagataṃ vidyādatramadhye samudbhavam ||

nāmavarṇādi yogena yoginastu na saṃśayaḥ |
siddhyate nātrasaṃdeha asyānyāsarataḥ sadā ||

p. 29b)

nābhimadhyagataṃ devi urumadhye vyavasthitam |
daśabhistu maṇirbinnaṃ svādhisthānaṃ ṣaḍātmakam ||

vala(para)madhyagataṃ vidyālliṅgasthāne prapūjayet |
valamadhyagataṃ devi ādhāre varṇasaṃsthitaṃ ||

pūjitavyaṃ prayatnena tatra siddhacatuṣṭayam |
ājñā sthāne punardevi bhrūmadhye akṣaradvayaṃ ||

dvau siddhau pūjayen devi ājñāsiddhipradāyakau |
yasya cakrasya(siddhaya)dvarṇanāmapūrve vyavasthitam ||

ante ānandayogena tannāmaṃ tasya kīrttitam |
yā yasya stha(nu)kulaṃ cakraṃ nāmabhedenasaṃsthitam ||

tasya dhyānaprayogena siddhatenātrasaṃśayaḥ |
yāvat svakulanāmaṃ ca na jñānaṃ kulacintakaiḥ ||

tāvante darśane bāhyā na pūjā naiva pūjakāḥ |
sampūjya(rṇa)maṇḍalasthaṃ cakramaṃ saṃpūjyayatnataḥ ||

tamahaṃsaṃpravakṣyāmi maṇḍalasya yathāvidhi |
kuṃkumaiścandane naiva rakte na alināpi vā ||

ghṛtaṃ ca payaḥ pānīyaṃmadhulākṣārasena vā |
gorocanā ca karpūraṃ paṃcāmṛtamathāpi vā ||

sindūramathapiṣṭena athavā bhasmaśobhanaṃ |
viṣṇunāṃ gomaye naiva yathālābhena kārayet ||

maṇiratnamaye pīṭhe sūryasomānta(bha)saṃbhave |
sphaṭike nṛpāvartte kā ca pīṭhe suśobhane ||

viṣāṇekarisaṃbhūte kāṃcane rājatepi vā |
tāmrapīṭhethavādyoṣe śrīkhaṇḍe candanodbhave ||

raktacaṃdanapīṭhe vā aśokamadhukodbhave |
paṭṭe pāṣāṇamadhyasthe mṛtpīṭhe bhūtalepi vā ||

p. 30a)

pīṭhacatuṣkamadhyasthaṃ pāṇimānenamaṇḍalaṃ |
karttavyaṃ vāmahastena mekhalātrayasaṃyutam ||

tatramadhyagataṃ kūryāt ṣaṭcakraṃ śobhanaṃ priye |
śivaśaktikalairyuktaṃ pūrvoktaṃ yādṛśaṃ mayā ||

tatra madhye ṣaḍā(vratā)raṃ ca siddhapīṭhacatuṣṭayam |
saptaviṃśatibhedaiśca pūjitavyaṃ prayatnataḥ ||

jātīpuṣpakadaṃvaiśca pārijātakapāṭalaiḥ |
sapatraiḥ kundakairvilvaiḥ rmarukavīraiśca yūthikaiḥ ||

mallikāraktapadmāni ra?kapuṣpotpalāni ca |
sugandhīnyapi puṣpāṇi keśaraḥ kuṃkumasya ca ||

karttavyaṃ sādhakendreṇa yathālābhena pūjanam |
dhūpaṃdīpaṃ ca naivedyaṃ tarpaṇaṃ cāsavena tu ||

phalguṣaṃ trividhaṃ dadyādante jāpaṃ samārabhet |
dvātriṃśadakṣarī devyāḥ paṃcapraṇavasaṃpuṭā ||

sahasrañca sahasrārddhaṃ śatārddhārddhārddhameva ca |
mudrābandhaṃ paraṃ kṛtvā praṇipatyanivedayet ||

ekāṃgaṃ caiva paṃcāṃgamaṣṭāṃgaṃ sarvameva vā |
namaskāraṃ prakurvīta carukaṃ prāsanaṃ kuru ||

phalguṣaṃ dīpanaivedyaṃ āsavaṃ purataḥ sthitam |
prāsitavyaṃ prayatnena praṇipatyavisarjjayet ||

ṣoḍhānyāsamidaṃśuddhaṃ krameṇasahitaṃ yathā |
ākhyātaṃ devadeveśi pāraṃparyakramāgatam ||

yaḥ karoti idaṃ nyāsaṃ trikālaṃ ca divāniśi |
bhūtapretapiśācāni(śca)yakṣakuṣmāṇḍarākṣasāḥ ||

paśyaṃti pāvakaṃ rūpaṃ ye cānye hiṃsakāḥ priye |
satatābhyāsayogena ṣaṇmāsenavarānane ||

p. 30b)

dahate sarvapāpāni brahmahatyādi yāni ca |
punaranyapra(ni)vakṣyāmi kulaparvāṇi yāni ca ||

ṣaṣṭhī bhādrapade māse kṛṣṇāśvini caturdaśī |
kārttikenavamīśuklāmārgekṛṣṇācaturdaśī ||

pauṣe ca pūrṇimā devi māghevaracaturthikā |
phālgunenavamīśuklā caitrekāmaṃ trayodaśī ||

vaiśākhe ca tṛtīyā ca jyeṣṭhe ca daśamīsitā |
āṣāṭhaikādaśīśuklā amāvāsyā ca śrāvaṇe ||

vijñeyāraṇyaṣaṣṭhī ca paṃcamī dakṣiṇāyaṇe |
dīpotsavaṃ rajotsargaṃ karttavyaṃ cottarāyaṇe ||

etāni deva pūrvāṇi kathitā vīravandite |
yaḥ parva pū(ja)yennityaṃ viśuddhenāntarātmanā ||

abdairdvādaśabhirddevi khecaraiḥ sahamodate |
anenaiva vidhānena kramapūjāṃ sadākuru ||

śarīrasthaṃ punardevi pūjitavyaṃ prayatnataḥ |
sidhyate nātrasandeho bhāvayukto padā bhavat ||

vāṃchante yasya devāva(sta) caraṇapu(mā)rādhanaṃ bhktimantaḥ 
saṃsārachedahetorniyativasugatāḥ | puṇyapāpairvimuktāḥ ||

nirvāṇaṃ yena nītaṃ kṣaṇahṛtakalusaṃ candrasūryābhiṣiktaṃ taṃ vande(deva) 
ādināthaṃ vimalapadagataṃ nityamevaṃ namāmi || 1 ||

yo sau siddhā ca yādyaḥ suravaranamito yogibhirvandyamānabhyatkā śrī 
uḍḍiyāṇaṃ vividhaguṇagaṇālaṃ kṛtaṃ divyavṛndaiḥ ||

kṛtvākhyāti mahoccaissuravarabhuvane yogivṛndasya madhye tasyāhaṃ 
pādayugmaṃ stutiśaraṇagato nityamevaṃ namāmi || 2 ||

yena vyāptaṃ ca(śva) pīṭhaṃ girivaraśikharālaṃ kṛtaṃ gahvaraiśca 
śrīmajjālandharākhyaṃ vijitaguṇanidhiṃ yogīnendreṇa 
(kṛtaguṇanidhināyogināyena nityam) devaṃ(vandyaṃ) ||

p. 31a)

gatvā pātālamūle viṣadharavividhā marditā yena daityā |
taṃ śrīkhecakranāthaṃ(ścakrīśaspārthasiddhya)praṇama satataṃ 
pātumāṃ(śraddhayā)pādayugamaṃ || 3 ||

yaḥ śrīnāmnā kuraṅgī śivabhuvanagataiḥ krīḍate kāmabhogaigaṃ tyaktvā 
śrīpūrṇapīṭhaṃ bahulalitapadaiśca carccitaṃ divya vṛndaiḥ ||

bhuktvādīpānaśeṣā namaraguṇagaṇālaṃkṛte divyavṛndaistasyāhaṃ
pādayugmaṃ yu(stu)tiśaraṇagato nityamevaṃ namāmi || 4 ||

tyaktvā śrīkāmarūpaṃ niśiniśibhavane yoginī prārthyamāno gatvā 
kailāsadivyaṃ(pīṭhaṃ) suravaraśikhare toṣito yena śa(mbhuḥ) ||

laṅkāyāntatkṣaṇārddhe punarapi ca gato laṅkanāthābhiṣiktaḥ 
śrīmātalaṅkanāthaḥ surayuvati gaṇairarccitaṃ pāda(nu)yugmaṃ || 5 ||

kṛtyānekādhikāraṃ vividhajanabudhā(padya)marditā yena siddhā 
nānātīvraistravedhaiḥ suravarabhavane yojitā yena satvāḥ ||

tīvrājñābhuktabhogān bhramaṇamati kṛtaḥ kṣetrapīṭhepapīṭhaiḥ vajrasphoṭaṃ 
kṛtātyaṃ praṇamatasatataṃ paṃcamaṃ caryanāthaṃ || 6 ||

nāmnā yo vajra bodhervratacaraṇaratorādhito yena rudrakṛtvā vidyāvrataṃ yaḥ 
suravarabhavane śaktikhaṭvāṃga mudraṃ |
kṛtvā tatrādhikāraṃ suravaragurūṇā sobhiṣiktaḥ prayatnā devaṃ 
sadyoginendraṃ sakalaguṇanidhiḥ pātumāṃ ṣaṣṭhanāthaḥ ||7 ||

evaṃ siddhānvaye yā sakalaguṇanidhi mantramārge prasiddhā svarge brahmādi 
deṣaiḥ? kramacaraṇaratairmatyaloke munīndraiḥ |
pātāle cāntarīkṣe viṣadharavividhairarcyamānaḥ samastaistatrasthairdevadivyai 
stavacaraṇagataḥ kunnamāmi || 8 ||

ityetairaṣṭabhirvṛttaistrivāraṃ stauti yaḥ svayaṃ tasya devyā 
prasādenasiddhājñā saṃpravartate |

naśyanti vighnasaṃghātā divyajānodayo bhavet | 

hṛtpadmastha manantapīṭhanilayaṃ bindu trimātrānvitaṃ tejo 
maṇḍalamadhyamaṃ pravilasaddivyāṅganālaṃ kṛtaṃ ||

oṃkārākhyamanekasiddhanikaraiḥ saṃsevyamānaṃ jagat |
khyātaṃ nityamahaṃ namāmi vipulaṃ tadbhuktimukti pradaṃ || 1 ||

jñānāgni jvāladīpaṃ sakalamalaharaṃ nābhimadhye stha(pra)saṃsthaṃ 
dignāgā veṣṭitaṃ ca tribhuvanabhuvanaṃ dakṣavāmorddhamadhyaṃ |
tatsthānaṃ jñānarūpaṃ ramati parakalā netracaṃdrārddha yuktaṃ 
tachrījālandharākhyaṃ viṣamasamapadaṃ divyapīṭhannamaste || 2 ||

svādhiṣṭhānorddhavāsaṃ sakalaguṇamayaṃ 
piṇḍamadhyestha(pra)saṃsthaṃsaṃpūrṇapūrayantaṃ 
taralataḍibhaṃśodhayantaṃ suśudhaṃ ||

kaṃde kubjāṣṭa(sva)rūpā taduparibhuvanaṃ madhyagranthi prabhinnaṃ 
triṣkoṇaṃ tatpradeśaṃ raviṃśaśi nirayaṃ pūrṇapīṭhannamaste || 3 ||

utpannaṃ yatra piṇḍaṃ sakalaguṇamayaṃ bhūtasaṃghāta viśvaṃ 
traiguṇyākāśaliṃgaṃ yugakuṭilagataṃ bunduyaṃtrāvatāraṃ ||

brahmākālasya madhyo kru?ratimaṇirivayogibhistaṃ svapakṣaṃ siddhaṃ 
sidhāvatāraṃ tribhuvananamitaṃ kāmarūpaṃ namaste(namāmi) || 4 ||

p. 32a)

uktāni pīṭhasūtrāṇi catvāri varavarṇini |
jñātavyaṃ cakrabhedena śarīrasthaṃ susiddhidaṃ ||

adhunāsaṃpravaksyāmi trikhaṇḍā yādṛśo bhavet |
pūjitavyā prayatnena sarvasiddhiphalapradā ||

ḥmanamo narāyaṇāgatṛmāmo na phrṁlirubhulirubhuphreṁ ribhuribhu ||
phreṃ ri he ri he || phreṃ tivajīsaṃnivajīsaṃ || phreṃ ṇi bhakṣoṇi bhakṣo ||
phreṁ ṇisrāṇi ramā || phreṁṇivadrāviṇi vaḍrāvi || phreṁ ṇimabhrāṇi mabhrā ||
phreṁ nisatrāni satrā || phreṁ ribhiribhi || phreṁ rivirivi || phreṃ ṭatru ṭatru ||
phreṁ danu danu || phreṁ nāṃ nīrttava ripasrahasaparūkyalotrai yāmā ||
phreṁ tyanṛ tyanṛ || phreṁ saha saha || phreṁ rāda rāda ||
phreṁ ye privasarāsutaṇiśosamāṃ || phreṁ ddhekru ddhekru || phreṁ ṣṭraṃdaṃ takṛ 
vi 2 || phreṁ vebhru laṅgapi phreṁ kṣikāratā phreṁ hūṁ jjidtuvi phreṁ kheśitaṃlijva 
phreṁ śekerrddha-ū phreṁ ṇḍemucā || phreṁ hsrāṁ phreṁ śrīṁ hrīṁ aiṁ aiṁ hrīṁ śrīṁ 
phreṁ hsrauṁ ḥmanacceviniśa-ī ṇḍemucā ḥmanacce viṇindrā iṇḍemucā  

ḥmana ccevi riśvageyo ṇḍemucā ḥmana ccevi hirāvāṇḍemucā ḥmana ccevi 
viṣṇavai ṇḍemucā ḥmanacce virimākauṇḍemucā ḥmanacce viriśva hemā
