cavaṇāṃtṝ mā phreṁ raṃka ṣṭipra phreṁ raṃkaddhi  

p. 32b)

ṛ phreṁ raṃkaddhisi phreṁ raṃka dacchermmakarapa phreṁ yaṃdahṛ hyagu tṛmārvasa 
phreṁ nāṃnīrtavaripa rmakastama masanalanmūnodacchrāṇorakaśīvatvasarvasa 
phreṁ nāṃnīrttavaripaparūsvasva phreṁ nāṃtīgatahati pratrārvasa phreṁ 
ṇāṃrīmarāja-a phreṁ nāṃkīdhasārthamākārvasa phreṁ nāṃnīmigāśakā 
āmana phreṃ ṇḍemucā ḥmana || phreṁ yadrāru tevagabha mona oṃ phreṁ || 

asyā vidyādi ante ca antaṃ pūrvaṃ vyavasthitam |
jñātavyaṃ padabhedena vilomena yathābhavet ||

samayācārahīnasya pramadā laṃghane kṛte |
ajñānādathavā mohāt sarvapāpapramocanī ||

kramapūjāvasāne ca japtavyā parameśvarī |


śrībhairava uvāca


yogaviplavakānāṃ ca hāsyaṃ kurvanti ye narāḥ |
yoginī samayacakrāṇi(yoktāni) vratacaryāṇi yāni ca ||

vīramelāpakaṃ maṃtra māgamaṃ śivabhāṣitam |
dūṣayanti durācārā vighnaṃ kurvanti ye sadā ||

nigrahaṃte pravakṣyāmi ṣaḍvidhaṃ ca yathā bahvet |
mātṛṇāṃ ca ṛtu sthāne dārubījaṃ vyavasthitam ||

uddhṛtya ṣaḍvidhandevi sarvabindu yutaṃ kuru |
anante na samāyuktaṃ pūrvavarṇaṃ ca kārayet ||

mālinī jihvasaṃyuktaṃ dvitīyaṃ vīravandite |
dhruvāntasthaṃ tṛtīyaṃ ca bhūtīśena caturthakam ||

khe(kṣe)ante paṃcamaṃ yojya ṣaṣṭaṃ lokāntasaṃsthitam |
paṃcapraṇavapūrvasthaṃ mantraratnaṃ śirodbhavaṃ ||

p. 33a)

praṇavañca tṛtīyaṃ ca bindūhīnaṃ samālikhet |
etāni navabījāni viśuddhaṃ parataḥ sthitam ||

tasyānte ḍākinīnāma gṛhatvacaṃpunaḥ |
svāhāntaṃ pūjayed devi viṃśavarṇā trayodhikāḥ ||

mālinīśūladaṇḍaṃ ca navake pūrvasamsthitam |
ṣaḍvidhaṃ taṃ vijānīyād yādṛśaṃ pūrvasaṃsthitam ||

ri (sa) antaṃ bindusaṃyuktaṃ sthāpitaṃ kuruyatnataḥ |
ante ratnaṃ dvitīyaṃ tu lakṣmībījantadagrataḥ ||

anāhṛtaṃ samuccārya rākṣaśī tadanantaraṃ |
tasyānte raktavarṇāni śoṣayeti punaḥ punaḥ ||

svāhāntaṃ yojayed devi dvitīyaṃ nātrasaṃśayaḥ |
navake ca tṛtīye ca pinākī bhairavaḥ sthitaḥ ||

bheditaṃ ṣaṭprakāreṇa yathāpūrvantataiva hi |
tṛtīyaratnayogena pūrvaṃ pūrveṇasaṃyutam ||

uddhāraṃ devi kurvīta tadantaṃ maṇipūrakaṃ |
tasyānte lākinīmāṃsaṃ gṛhṇagṛhṇeti paṭhyate ||

svāhāntaṃ pūjanaṃ kuryānnābhisthaṃ yoginīṃ viduḥ(kuru) |
saṃvakaṃ varṇamuddhṛtya ṣaṭprakāreṇa pūrvavat ||

saptānte ratnasaṃyuktaṃ caturthena varānane |
punastaṃ pūrvabījaṃ ca ratnānte ca niyojayet ||

svādhiṣṭhānaṃ tadagre tu śākinīnāmasaṃyutam |
śaṃkārūpena ityuktaṃ gṛhṇagṛhṇeti payate ||

snānyu svāhāntasaṃyuktaṃ pūjitavyaṃ prayatnataḥ |
śikharasthaṃ bhuje vāme mālinī varṇamārūtam ||

saḍvidhaṃ taṃ vijānīyād yathā pūrvaṃ vyavasthitam |
aṣṭamaṃ pañcamaṃ ratnaṃ navamaṃ pūrvavat priye ||

p. 33b)

ādhāra yakṣiṇī nāmatasyānte saṃsthitaṃ kuru |
jṛṃbhaye iti (yadbhavanti ca) varṇāni dvisthāne saṃsthitāni ca ||

a(sva)stisvāhānta yogena pūjanaṃ paṃcamasya ca |
krodhabhairavamuddhṛtya ādi ekādaśe sthitam ||

pūrvaiḥ svaraprayogena ṣaḍete bheditāni ca(stathā) |
māyā bījāntayuktena pūrvabījaṃ tadagrataḥ ||

ta(pa)syānte kākiṇī devī ājñāpūrvaṃ samuddharet |
tasyānte kravyamā maṃtrāsaṃhareti padadvayaṃ ||

gudaṃ svāhāntayogena pūjanaṃ ṣaṣṭa kasya ca |
nigrahe ṣaṭkamityuktaṃ trailokyanigrahe kṣaṇāt ||

sādhitavaṃ bidhānena sadyaḥ pratyayakārakam |
ekaikasyā yutaṃ jāpyaṃ karttavyaṃ varavarṇini ||

homaṃ caiva daśāṃśena yogasya saṃgrahe sthitam |
prathamasya tvacaṃ kuryāt pauruṣaṃ ghṛtamiśritam ||

nṛraktaṃ ghṛtayogena dvitīyasya varānane |
mahāmāṃsaghṛtaṃ kuryāt deyaṃ devi tṛtīyake ||

caturthaṃ snāyuṃ homaṃ ca ghṛtena sahasaṃyutam |
hutaṃ devi sahasraikaṃ paṃcamaṃ cāsthisaṃbhavam ||

taccūrṇaṃ ghṛtasaṃyuktaṃ agnikuṇḍe tu homayet |
ayutasya daśāṃśena majja(hya)homaṃ tadagrataḥ ||

ṣaḍvidhaṃ ca idaṃ homaṃ kurvītavaravarṇini |
siddhaṃ caiva vijānīyāt paścāt kanyāṃ prapūjayet ||

siṃdūraṃ raktavastrāṇi kuṃkumaṃ kajjalānvitaṃ |
tilakaṃ puṣpadhūpañca āsanaṃ vividhaṃ priye ||

matsyamāṃsavicitrāṇi ṣaḍrasaṃbhojanaṃ śubhaṃ |
tṛptiṃ kurvīta yatnena dakṣiṇāṃ ca samarpyayet ||

p. 34a)

namaskāraṃ prakurvīta prārthitavyaṃ prayatnataḥ |
yatmayā sādhitaṃ kāryaṃ tasyājñā mukti me bhavet ||

anenaiva vidhānena nigrahaṃ sādhitaṃ yathā |
manasākurute yogī nigrahaṃ yasya kasyacit ||

ekaṃ daśaśataṃ caiva sahasraikamathāpi vā |
mārayennātrasaṃdehaḥ saṃkruddhenāntarātmanā ||

athavā bhūpateścaiva nigrahaṃ kartumicchati |
vidhānantasya vakṣyāmi yenā sau mriyate dhruvaṃ || 

pretakeśanivastraṃ ca cūrṇaṃ kṛtvā citāgninā |
sāditaṃ nararaktena niśārddhe maṇḍalaṃ kuru ||

kṛṣṇāṣṭamyāṃ caturdaśyāṃ saṣṭcakraṃ tasya madhyataḥ |
pūjayet ṣaṭka yogena dakṣiṇābhi mukhaḥ sthitaḥ ||

dakṣiṇāvartta yogena ekaikāmuttareṇa tu |
devadattasya nāmaṃ tu madhyasthaṃ kuru yatnataḥ ||

cintitaṃ sādhakendreṇa jīvahīnaṃ suvihvalaṃ |
amukaṃ ca mayādattaṃ gṛhṇagṛheti kathyate ||

ṣaṭsaptamadine pūrṇe mriyate nātrasaṃśayaḥ |
māraṇi bhūpatīnāṃ ca vidhānaṃ samudāhṛtam ||

vighnannaivaprakurvaṃnti āśramaṃ pālayanti ye |
darśanānāṃ ca sarveṣāṃ bhaktiṃ kurvanti ye sadā ||

idaṃ tasya na yuṃjīta lubdhe naiva kadācana |
ājñāhāniḥ paratrañca narapatenātrasaṃśayaḥ ||

tasmāt sarvaprayatnena kṣudre kṣudraṃ niyojayet |
jighāṃsantaṃ jighāṃsīyānnantena brahmahā bhavet ||

nigrahaṃ ca samākhyātaṃ na deyaṃ yasya kasyacit |
brāhmaṇeṣu kumāriṣu tathā vai liṅgadhāriṇe ||

p. 34b)

nigrahannaiva karttavyaṃ aparādhaśatairapi |


iti pārameśavarīmate kramapūjādhikāre nigrahe dvātriṃśatimaḥ paṭalaḥ ||





devyuvāca


śrutaṃ nāthamayāsarvaṃ pāraṃparyaṃ suniścitam |
ṣoḍhānyāsenasaṃyuktaṃ nigrahānugrahe yathā ||

adyāpi saṃśayo smākaṃ varttate hṛdi madhyataḥ |
tvayā pūrvaṃ samākhyātaṃ nirvāṇapadamuttamam ||

mudrāmantravihīnañca vratacaryāvivarjjitam |
pāraṃparyakramaṃ divyaṃ śarīrasthaṃ vadasvame ||

kathaṃ jīvaḥ samutpanno bhūtānāṃ caiva lakṣaṇaṃ |
kaḥkālograsate tasya kasmin kāle vyavasthita ||

dinaṃdinārddhamityāhustrīṇipaṃcāṣṭameṣu ca |
pakṣe māsaṃ ca ṣaṇmāsamabdai kasya ca lakṣaṇaṃ ||

cihnāni vada deveśa apamṛtyuryathā bhavet |
dinasaṃkhyādi naikena saṃkrāntiṣu yathā bhavet ||

viṣuvaṃgrahayogāni rāśinakṣatrayāni ca(lakṣaṇam) |
grahaṇaṃ candrasūryāṇāṃ cakrabhedaṃ vadasva me ||


śrībhairava uvāca


yo sau aciṃtyamityāhuḥ śivaḥ paramakāraṇaḥ |
tasyecchā nirgatāśaktiḥ prasuptabhujagopamā ||

taḍitpuṃjasahasrābhā sahasrakiraṇojjvalā |
hṛdaye kṣipyasāmāyā veda(devi)siddhāntamātṛkā ||

suptāvasthā gatā sā tu(sūkṣmā) gurvājñāyāḥ prabodhane |
utpannā tatra madhye tu tritā(vi)dyāstrīṇi devatāḥ ||

brahmāviṣṇustathārudrastriguṇaṃ bhuvanāntare |
p. 35a) triraṃśakamidaṃ piṇḍaṃ triṣaṇḍe āyusaṃsthitam ||

aṃśakāstrīṇi kālasya bhuṃjate ca pṛthak pṛthak |
brahmāṃśaṃ viṣṇuraśaṃ ca rudrāṃśaṃ trividhapumān ||

trayastriṃśati varṣāstute tribhāgā vyavasthitāḥ |
prajāpatiścaredvāyuḥ praviṣṭo bhūtapaṃjare ||

yāvadbrahmā śarīrasthaḥ kālarūpeṇa bhuṃjati |
tāvadbrahmādi yogena jñātavyaṃ kālalakṣaṇaṃ ||

yadā vāstamane brahmā udayaṃ kurute hariḥ |
calate kālarūpeṇa sṛṣṭirudrasya madhyataḥ ||

tenāpi viṣṇuyogena jñānasya vaṃcanaṃ bhavet |
gato brahmā gato viṣṇuḥ sṛṣṭibhogāḥ kṣayaṃ gatāḥ ||

bhakṣitāste varārohe garbhe rudrasya saṃsthitāḥ |
yadā tu dayate rudrastṛtīyāvasthasaṃsthita ||

tadā rudrasya yogena kālacihnāni paśyati |
yadākālagataṃ śeṣaṃ sṛṣṭirūpaṃ maheśvaraḥ ||

sa tatra dṛśyate kiṃcit pānīyaṃ kardame pi vā |
saṃhṛtyasarvarudreṇa jīrṇaṃ kandaṃ kṛtaṃ yadā ||

nistejañca bhavennālaṃ padmasya patanaṃ bhavet |
yadā biṃduṃ mahāpadmaṃ sa nālaṃ kandaśekharaṃ ||

tadā binduṃ vijānīyādasthi abhyantare sthitam |
ājya(aja)majjālayaṃ snāyuṃ māṃsaṃraktantathā tvacaṃ ||

ṣaṭdhātuṃ vidhyate rudraḥ pacyate romasaṃkulaṃ |
śabdahīnau tadākarṇau ghrāṇahīnā ca nāsikā ||

jyotirhīnābhavedṛṣṭi rasahīnāsarasvatī |
kaṃpate ca mahāmerurvṛṣaṇo mūlavarjitaḥ ||

p. 35b)

saṃjīrṇena ca te(ajīrṇaṃ bhavati) staṃbhaṃ yadā rudrodayo bhavet |
sakalākālasaṃyuktā vika(kā)lākālavivarjjitā ||

yā kalādarśayet kālaṃ sā kalākālameva ca |
lakṣitavyaṃ prayatnena sā kalākālavāsinī ||

kālasya vaṃcanaṃ kuryād yo yasya aṃśake sthitam |
etāni trīṇi bhedāni tribhirdevaiḥ samudbhavāḥ ||

brahmāviṣṇuśca rudraśca jñātavyaṃ mantramadhyataḥ |
yo yasya aṃśake āyu(prāpta)stattasya cakramadhyataḥ ||

paśyate jñānayogena susupta svapnagepi vā |
svapne vā yadi pratyakṣaṃ samādhiguṇayogataḥ ||

vivarṇāṃ paśyate chāyāṃ abdamekaṃ sa jīvati |
uttarābhimukhobhūtvā paśyate yadi dakṣiṇaṃ ||

diṅmohaṃ ca samākhyātaṃ saptamāsaṃ sa jīvati |
śuddhanirmmalamādityaṃ viraśmiṃ paśyate yadā ||

varṣadvayaṃ manuṣyāṇāṃ mṛtyurātmani viṃdati |

(arundhatīṃ dhruvaṃ caiva somacchāyā mahākhyātaṃ saptamāsaṃ sa jīvati ||
śuddhanirmmalamādityaṃ viraśmiṃ paśyate yadāvarṣadvayaṃ manuṣyāṇāṃ 
mṛtyurātmaniviṃdati ||)

arundhatīṃ dhrūvaṃ caiva somacchāyā mahāpathaṃ ||

yo na paśyati deveśi ahadhikanna jīvati |
madhye caṃdraṃ sa cchidraṃ ca adhobhāgena paśyati ||

jānīyādvatsaraikena tasya mṛtyurnnasaṃśayaḥ |
bhagnaśākhādrumaṃ caiva gandharvanagarantathā ||

paśyetpretapiśācāni daśamāsaṃsajīvati ||

p. 36a)

yasya vaisrātamātrasya hṛdi pādau ca śuṣyate |
dhūmo vā mastake nāsti saptamāsaṃ sa jīvati ||

agrataḥ pṛṣṭhato vāpi khaṇḍaṃ yasya padaṃ bhavet |
pāṃsau ca kardame vāpi saptamāsaṃ sa jīvati ||

raktamālyānu lepāni raktakṛṣṇāni vāsasī |
labhate svapnayogena ṣaṇmāsāni sa jīvati ||

parāsarasya kālasya tatvajñānaṃ vadāmyaham |
lekhanyādi prayogena vidhiyogena bhāvayet ||

mārgaśiṣasya māsasya kṛṣṇā ca paṃcamī payā||
saṃcā(hā)raṃ jñānayogena(gaṃ ca) rātrau jāgaraṇaṃ yajet ||

āharennirvraṇaṃ bhūrjje rocanā sṛkta kuṃkumam |
likhetpūrvamukhobhūtvā dvādaśā akṣarāḥ śubhāḥ ||

mātrābindusamāyuktā darśayitvā punaḥ punaḥ |
saṃvṛtyaśuklasūtreṇa japa vidyāṃ samālabhe(likhe)t ||

sitacandananaivedyairjjātīpuṣpairmmanoramaiḥ |
pūjayitvākramāmnāyaṃ vāmādyāmantrasaṃyutam ||

śarāvapuṭamadhyasthaṃ jātīkusumamadhyagaṃ |
sthāpayitvā japenmantraṃ yāvadrātrikṣayaṃ gatā ||

vimale suprabhāte ca kramaṃ saṃpūjya yatnataḥ |
kumārīṃ ca tataḥ pūjya vidyāladdhāmataḥ śṛṇu ||

hrīṃ hrūṃ svleṃ mahāpataye rakṣarakṣa māṃ amṛtodbhave |
hrīṃ hrūṃ svleṃ vicce saṃpuṭitvā japenmantraṃ devyāḥ kṣobhaṃ japena tu ||

kathyate nātrasaṃdehaḥ śubhaṃ vā śubhameva vā |
na ca kṣubhyati sā devyā japtavidyā ca saṃpuṭā(sāṃpratam) ||

p. 36b)

darśayanti mahāhāniṃ bhraṣṭosau yoginī kule |
evaṃ kṛtvā yadā paśyed bhūrjasthānekṣaṇbhu? śubhāt ||

vācā payannirūpecca samāniviṣamāni ca |
akṣaropyadhiko yatra tatra rājyaṃ vidhiyate ||

mātrāyābhyadhike(dhikelabhel)lābhaṃ same ārogyavatsalaṃ |
binduhīnaṃ yadāpaśyet tadāhāniṃ vinirdiśet ||

mātrāhīne bhavedvyādhirmṛtyuḥ syādakṣaraṃ vinā |
vāmādi kramayogena pūjayedupadeśataḥ ||

śuṣkatāluka-oṣṭhaṃ ca akasmāddhūsācchavi |
śuṣka ca(bha?)bhagnamāyāti ṣaṇmāsaṃ tasya jīvitaṃ ||

sunīlaṃ maṇḍalaṃ vyomni paśyate ca dine dine |
śitaṃ haritakṛṣṇaṃ ca vatsarāṃrddha sa jīvati ||

ātmīyāmaśirāṃ chāyāṃ tathā kṛṣṇāśca tārakāḥ |
yadā paśyati deveśi ṛtumekaṃ sa jīvati ||

tailābhyaṅgantathā pānaṃ raktaṃ caiva vilepanaṃ |
raktāṃvarāṇi kṛṣṇāni svapne ca  paśyate yadā ||

rākṣasairbhūtavetālaiḥ śvānaśūkaragardabhaiḥ |
gṛdhraikaṃ kairulūkaiśca mahiṣairuṣṭrameva ca ||

yadā paśyati cātmānamebhi parivṛtto naraḥ |
svapne ca paśyate yo vai abdamekaṃ sa jīvati ||

śaṃkhāvartte dhrūvormmadhye gulpheṣu marma saṃśru?ṣu |
yo na jānāti tattvajñaḥ spaṃdanaṃ ca muhurmuhuḥ ||

netrābhyāṃ karajaiḥ krā(jihvā)ntaṃ somasūryaṃ na paśyati |
dhūmaṃ ca mastake nāsti māsamekaṃ sa jīvati ||

p. 37a)

tālumadhyagataṃ dhūmaṃ mahājñānaṃ taducyate |
jihvācāruṃdhatī proktā nāsāgraṃ dhruva ucyate ||

rasanāśuṣkamāyāti hikakā ca vahate sadā |
śuṣyanti sarvagātrāṇi ṛtu(yā sa)mekaṃ sa jīvati ||

lohadaṇḍadharaṃkṛṣṇaṃ sanmukhaṃ paśyate yadā |
samādhi svapna yogena aṣṭamāsaṃ sa jīvati ||

tārāgaṇaṃ na paśyeta sthūlakāyātmakaṃ yadā |
dīpagandhaṃ na jighrāti abdamekaṃ sa jīvati ||

niśicāpaṃ divā ulkā ameghe vidyu darśanaṃ |
digmoho duṣṭadeśe ca ṣaṇmāsān mriyate tataḥ ||

cakṣurbhyāṃ śravate nityaṃ na śṛṇoti anāhataṃ |
dīpagandhaṃ na gṛhnāti varṣārddhe mriyate naraḥ ||

raktapadmopamaṃ vaktraṃ jihvākṛṣṇā ca jāyate |
gātre varṇānyanekāni māsamekaṃ sa jīvati ||

binduṃ yo hita paśyeta netrābhyāṃ purataḥ sthitaṃ |
tasya mṛtyuṃ vijānīyāt māsamekaṃ sa jīvati ||

(binduyoni na paśyeta netrābhyāṃ purataḥ sthitaṃ |
tasya mṛtyuṃ vijānīyāt māsamekaṃ sa jīvati ||)

śītalatvaṃ gato bhānuruṣṇatvaṃ ca niśākaraḥ |
dadāmṛtyuvijānīyādarddhamāsenasaṃśayaḥ || iti ||


devyuvāca


yā sā vidyā mahātejā kālāgni rudrasaṃbhavā |
kālasaṃkarṣanī nāma sarvasiddhi pradāyikā ||

tamahaṃ śrotumichāmi prasādaṃ kuru bhairava |


śrībhairava uvāca


śṛṇu devi mahāguhyā kālasaṃkarṣaṇī parā |
p. 37b) siddhyate yatprasādena trailokye sa carācaraṃ ||

ādeśaṃ kurute kālaṃ mārayed rakṣayed yathā |
sarvasiddhikarī hyeṣā kathayāmi tava priye ||

la(na)kūlīśaṃ mahārudraṃ dhruvāntamadhaḥsaṃsthitam |
navādi āsanārūḍhaṃ niṣavyantena bhūṣitam ||

nādabinduśiredattaṃ prathamaṃ akṣaraṃ viduḥ |
maṃdare bhūtapūrve tu vāmādrimālinī tu yā ||

navādi āsanārūḍhaṃ jhaṇṭhīśenasamanvitam |
ḷpūrveṇasamāyuktaṃ gandhaśikharabhūṣitam ||

(dvitīyaṃ bījamuttamam ||)???

cāmuṇḍāhṛdayaṃ proktaṃ sarvagrāhakaraṃ paraṃ |
kṣe pūrvaṃ tu samuddhṛtyasāvitrī bhūṣitaṃ kuru ||

bindūnādaśivākrāntaṃ nārasiṃhaṃ mahābalaṃ |
mātṛmaṇḍalavarṇaṃ ca ṣaṣṭhaṃ kṣe antasaṃyutam ||

sopi binduyutaṃ kuryādbījaṃ trailokyaḍāmaraṃ |
tadbījaṃ tasya agne tu anantenavibhūṣitam ||

krūravarṇaṃ śire(ve)dattaṃ mohanaṃ paṃcaṃmākṣaram |
ekādaśādi saṃhṛtya(gṛhya)sadyojātenasaṃyutam ||

śūladaṇḍāsanārūḍhaṃ aṃkuśaṃ bindusaṃyutam |
ekādaśe caturthe tu kevalaṃ saptamaṃ likhet ||

ca pūrveṇa mahākālaṃ rudraṃ saptamaṃ bhūṣitam |
tasya varṇaṃ caturthaṃ tu śirasī dāpayed budhaḥ ||

tasyāgrecchāntavarṇastu aṃbikā yatrasaṃsthitā |
rudrāṇā saptamaṃ gṛhyaviśvādi bhūṣitaṃ kuru ||

ṛṣīṇāṃ vedavarṇañca bindumastakabhūṣitam |
mātṛ ṣaṣṭhe tadagre tu ṛṇṣṭīśenasamanvitam ||

p. 38a)

tasyāgre rudrapūrvaṃ tu navamālā tṛtīyakaṃ |
ekādaśe caturthantu vyaṃjanaṃ tasya dāpayet ||

ekādaśādi saṃgṛhya tasyāntamagratasthitam |
śūladaṇḍaṃ śiredattaṃ grahamālā tṛtīyakam ||

viśvamālādi saṃbhinna devī aṣṭadaśākṣarī |
namonte jayakāle tu svāhāntaṃ havane bhavet ||

samasamānivarṇāni nyāsaṃ kuryāt tu ṣaḍvidhaṃ |
mahāpretā sanāruḍhaṃ bhūtamaṇḍalamadhyagaṃ ||

japedekāgra cittastu kṛṣṇāṣṭamyāṃ tu sarvarīm |
pratyakṣā bhavate devyā yatkāmaṃ taddhruvaṃ labhet ||

velā vratenasaṃyuktaḥ paṃcamudrādharaḥ śuci |
aṣṭottarasahasrantu japtvā homaṃ samarpayet ||

nṛmāṃsaṃ madirā yuktaṃḥ ghṛtamiśraṃ tu guggulūm |
ṣaṇmāsāt siddhimāpnoti sūrasāmānyatāṃ vrajet ||

kālasaṃkarṣaṇī nāma na deyā yasya kasyacit |
kulamārgābhiṣiktasya pāraṃparyaratasya ca ||

tasya deyamidaṃ jñānamanyathā narakaṃ vrajet |


iti pārameśvarīmate kālajñānasaṃkarṣaṇī trayastriṃśatimaḥ paṭalaḥ ||





devyuvāca


kālodayaṃ mayājñātaṃ aṃśakāni viśeṣataḥ |
adhunāśrotumichāmi vaṃca(dha)naṃ ca yathā bhavet ||

viparīta na cārayoge ātmānaṃ saṃkramate yathā |
cakrasthaṃ taṃ bījānīyād dhyānadhāraṇayogataḥ ||

p. 38b)

śrībhairava uvāca


yatnāt pūrvaṃ mayākhyātaṃ śaktyorddhaṃ aṃśakatrayaṃ |
mahānilayaṃ tasyorddhe (tasyorddhe tu mahānīlaṃ) tripathaṃ varavarṇini ||

pacya(kha)te tatra madhye tu viṣṇurudraprajāpatiḥ |
utpannaṃ tatra madhye tu bījabhūtaṃ varānane ||

pṛthivyāyastathā tejo vāyurākāśa niṣkalaṃ |
bījamadhye sthitā hyete pṛthag bhinnā krameṇa tu ||

yadāsau nirmale kṣetre patitaṃ bīja rohaṇaṃ |
tadābhidyanti bhūtāni svakīyaguṇapañcakaiḥ ||

tatratripathamadhye tu sūkṣmabhūtāvyavasthitāḥ |
ūrṇātantu nibhākārā gatāsānābhi maṇḍale ||

caturaṅgulamānena aṣṭadhā kuṭilaṃ gatāḥ |

prasūtā nābhi madhye tu śākhaiḥ śākhamanekadhā |
daśanādvyāvṛ(dhṛ)taṃ cakraṃ nābhisthāne pratiṣṭhitam ||

iḍā ca piṃgalā caiva suṣumnā ca tṛtīyakā |
urddhabhāge sthitā hyetā tribhirdevairvyavasthitā ||

gāndhāri hasti jihvā ca pūṣā caiva varānane |
adhomārga gatā hyetā vṛhat śākhā pralambitā ||

yathā(jayā) alaṃvuṣā caiva dakṣiṇasthā bhavanti hi |
vāmamārga prayogena saṃsthitā kuhuśaṃkhinī ||

nābherūrddha gatānāḍī ekātritayarūpiṇī |
sā prasūtā varārohe brahmāvartenasaṃsthitā ||

nāḍī saṃcārayogena saṃsthitaṃ padmayā dṛśam |
tamahaṃsaṃpravakṣyāmi śṛṇuṣvekāgramānasaḥ ||

paṃcapaṃca punaḥ paṃca paṃcapunaḥ punaḥ pañca |
paṃcamālāsamākhyātā saṃsthitā paṃcaviṃśakam(tiḥ) ||

p. 39a)

granthisthānaprayogena nāḍīsaṃcārasaṃthitā |
pūrvamālādi utpannaṃ dvitīyasya dvitīyake ||

viśrāntā tatra madhye tu gatāpūrva tṛ(dvi)tīyake |
pūrvabindu dvitīyasya prathamasya dvitīyakaṃ ||

granthitaṃ nāḍiyogena tṛtīyasya dvitīyakam |
dvitīyasya dvitīyena tṛtīyādi vibhedayet ||

tṛtīyasyya dvitīyena caturthe ādiyojitam |
tṛtīyādiṃ caturthasya dvitīyaṃ granthitaṃ kuru ||

caturthasya dvitīyena paṃcamādinniyojayet |
(paṃcamasya dvitīyena caturthāpi yojayet ||)

caturthasya dvitīyena paṃcamasya tṛtīyakam |
graṃthitaṃ pūrvayogena nāḍīsaṃcārabheditam ||

caturthasya tṛtīyaṃ ca paṃcamasya dvitīyakam |
paṃcamasya caturthe ca caturthe pañca yojitam ||

caturthasya caturthena paṃcamaṃ tu prayojitam |
paṃcamānta caturthasya caturthasthāna yojitam ||

(pañcamasya caturthena caturthāntaṃ niyojayet |
varānane pūrve caturtha yogena dvitīyānte vibheditam |
dvitīyasya caturthena dvaya(paṃca)masya tṛtīyakam |
bheditaṃ nātrasaṃdeho yathā pūrvantathaiva ca ||
tṛtīyena dvitīyasya)????? 

caturthāntaṃ tṛtīyasya caturthasthāna yojitam |
caturthasya catuḥsthānaṃ tṛtīyāntenasaṃyutam ||

tṛtīyāntaṃ dvitīyasya caturthasthānasaṃsthitam |
tṛtīyasya caturthena dvitīyāntaṃ niyojayet ||

pūrvāntena caturthaṃ ca dvitīyasya prathamasya caturthakaṃ |
graṃthitaṃ pūrvavadvevi ata urddhamataḥ śṛṇu ||

prathamasya dvitīyena dvitīyasya tṛtīyakam |
ekatra graṃthitaṃ kuryād ye cānye dve tathaiva hi ||

p. 39b)

nāḍīcakramidaṃ śūkṣamaṃ maṣṭa(sapta)patraṃ vyavasthitam |
śūnyarūpaṃ sthitaṃ tatra madhyasthaṃ parameśvaram ||

tadbāhye ṣoḍaśākāraṃ ṣoḍaśakalasaṃyutam |
candrādityamayaṃ sarvaṃ nāḍīcakraṃ vyavasthitam ||

eṣā prakṛtirākhyātā paṃcaviṃśasamudbhavā |
atramadhye sthitaṃ devaṃ jīvākhyaṃ parameśvaram ||

satvaṃrajastamaiścaiva veṣṭitaṃ trividhena tu |
aṣṭapatrasya madhyasthaṃ daleṣu yādṛśaṃ śṛṇu ||

indro agniryamaścaiva nair-ṛtyaṃ varuṇānilaṃ |
kauberamīśaparyantaṃ daleṣusaṃsthitāni ca ||

śabdasparśañca rūpaṃ ca rasogaṃdhaṃ ca paṃcamaḥ |
manobuddhirahaṃkāro daledhaḥ saṃsthitāni ca ||

kāmaṃ krodhantathā lobhaṃ madaṃ mātsaryameva ca |
mohaṃ daṃbhaṃ ca paiśūnyaṃ ekaikasya pṛthak pṛthak ||

yo sau aciṃtyamityāhuḥ sarvajñaḥ parameśvaraḥ |
madhyasthaṃ taṃ vijānīyād rūpamavyaktasaṃsthita ||

prerakantasya deveśi manonāmakamātmanaḥ |
yena yena hi bhāvena manaḥ saṃyujyate nṛṇāṃ ||

tasya tanmayatāṃ yāti viśvarūpo maṇiryathā |
caturviṃśati tattvāni patre patre vyavasthitāḥ ||

prakṛtiṃ ca vijānīyāt puruṣe bandhanātmikāṃ |
muktintena vinā devi niṣkalaṃ ca tadā bhavet ||

sakalaṃ tatra madhyasthaṃ niṣkalaṃ tena varjitam |
atra madhya gataṃ vidyāt kālaṃ kālodayāni ca ||

prakṛtiṃ yona jānāti caturviṃśātmarūpiṇīṃ |
vṛthāpariśramaṃ tasya muktisthānaṃ na gacchati ||

p. 40a)

athātaḥ saṃpravakṣyāmi tithisaṃkhyā yathā bhavet |
dinamekena deveśi ye dināḥ kāya saṃbhavāḥ ||

prāṇā pānasya cāreṇa dinaṃ rātrirvidhīyate |
ekaviṃśasamākhyātāḥ sahasrāḥ ṣaṭcchatādhikāḥ ||

saptaviṃśottarājñeyā saṃkrāntiṣu śatāni ca |
viṣuvāḥ ṣaṣṭi samākhyātā(vijñeyā) amāvasyā ca pūrṇimā ||

yo sau trikālarūpena aṣṭasaṃgrahasaṃsthitam |
viṃśottaraśataṃ yāvad bhramate candramaṇḍalam ||

yadājānāti tattvajñaḥ somagrahamupāgatam |
amṛtāśanasthito yogī pūjayet tattvanāyakam ||

sarvadeva mayaṃśaṃbhuṃ sarvaviśveśvaraṃ prabhuṃ |
jyotirūpaṃ paraṃśātaṃ avyaktaṃ nirmmalaṃ ca yat ||

jīvākhyaṃ paramaṃ śuddhaṃ nirmale malanāśanaṃ |
tasya pūjāprakarttavyā plāvitamamṛtena tu ||

yegatā(paśuyo)tiryagyonīṣu * narake cāti dāruṇo |
uddharet tat kulaṃ sarvaṃ daśapūrvaṃ daśāparam ||

rasmijvālā kule dīptaṃ bhānuvivaṃsunirmalaṃ |
graṃthi(grasi)taṃ paṃcavārāṇi ahorātreṇa dṛśyate ||

pitṛyānasya pūrveṇa yā kalādaivikī mṛtā |
tāṃ kalāṃ tarpayed devi sūryagrahasu pā gate ||

devayā tasya pūrveṇa pitṛyānasya cottare |
setubaṃdhasya madhye tu bhūtvā bhūyo na jāyate ||

pūjitavyaṃ prayatnena hṛdisthaṃ parameśvaraṃ |
divyadhārāmṛtenaiva bhānugrāsaṃ yadā bhavet ||

p. 40b) devadānavasiddhānāṃ gandharvo ragarākṣasāṃ |
tṛpyanti sarvabhūtāni vikalā tarpitā yadi ||

yadā bhavati saṃkrāntirviṣu vā piṇḍamadhyataḥ |
turyasthānasamārūḍhaḥ pūjayet tattvanāyakam ||

gurupadeśayogena sthānaṃ paramudurllabhaṃ |
tvaritaṃ tadvijānīyācchīghraṃ siddhyati mānavaḥ ||

yenajñātaṃ sureśāni tūryasthānasya tarpaṇaṃ |
aśvamedhasya yāgasya kṣipralabhati tatphalam ||

na yogāt sadṛśaṃ mantraṃ na yogāt sadṛśaṃ tapaḥ ||

na yogāt sadṛśaṃ tīrthaṃ triṣulokeṣu dṛśyate |
brahmāviṣṇuśca rudraśca īśvaraśca sadāśivaḥ ||

tadbhāvabhāvitātmānaṃ cintayanti parāparaṃ |
indrādyā devatāḥ sarve candrasūryādayograhāḥ ||

maharṣayaḥ saptapūrvaṃ mavādaya aśeṣataḥ |
aṣṭāśīti sahasrāṇi vā lakhitpādayopi ca ||

anenaiva tu yogena siddhāste varavarṇinī |
itikhalupuṅgalākāraṃ nāḍīsañcāramaṇḍale ||

mukhyaṃ kathitamihasiddhi hetorboddhavyaṃ yatnataḥ |
siddhiḥ eta devi paraṃguhyaṃ nirvāṇaṃ paramaṃ padam ||

śarīrasthaṃ paraṃ divyaṃ na deyaṃ yasya kasyacit |


devyuvāca


naiva jānāmyahaṃ nātha udayāsta manaṃ yathā |
viṣuvaṃ naivajānāmi grahaṇaṃ candrasūryayoḥ ||

rāśisaṃkramaṇaṃ caiva sakalaṃ niṣkalaṃ kathaṃ |
ko vetti atramadhyasthaḥ sukhaduḥkhāni yāni ca ||

kasya dharmmañca pāpaṃ ca ko gataḥ kotra tiṣṭhati |
p. 41a) candrasūryādi bhedena kathamātmāpratiṣṭhitaḥ ||

jñāyate kena yogena indriyaṃ viṣayāni ca |
anītā nāgataṃ caiva varttamānārthameva ca(bodhakam) ||

animādi guṇopetaṃ sādhanaṃ ca kathaṃ bhavet |
nirvāṇaṃ mokṣamārga ca vividhaṃ kālavaṃcanam ||

anenaiva śarīreṇa yathābhavati tadvada |


śrībhairava uvāca


yo sau devi mayākhyātaṃ cakraṃ prakṛti saṃbhavaṃ ||

tatramadhyagataṃ devaṃ sarvajñaṃ parameśvaram |
tadbrahma ca mahādevaṃ tadviṣṇuṃ paramaṃ padaṃ ||

taccandrasūryamityāhurindu varuṇameva ca |
taṃ kālaṃ bhavamityuktaṃ taṃ vāyuṃ taṃ khageśvaram ||

sarvadevamayā so hi tat śūnyaṃ bharitaṃ sthitam |
caitanyaṃ sarvagaṃ nityaṃ sarvaprāṇi hṛdi sthitam ||

viṣayasthamatītaṃ ca vāyusaṃyogasaṃsthitam |
bhramate cakramadhyasthaṃ manaḥ pavanasaṃyutam ||

dakṣiṇacāruyogena piṃgalaṃ bhānumārutam |
prāṇāpāṇaprayogena nīyate ca śanaiḥ śanaiḥ |

yasya patrastha madhyasthaṃ tiṣṭate paramātmanaḥ |
tasyatanmayatāṃ bhāvaṃ dhārayennātrasaṃśayaḥ ||

anya patrasya madhyasthaṃ yadāsaṃkramate tu saḥ |
saṃkrāntiṃ tadvijānīyāt taiḥ ṣaḍbhiścottarāyaṇaṃ ||

viṣuvaṃ dvādaśaṃ caiva yā rāśiṃ saṃkrameṣu ca |
abdairdvādabhiścaiva grahaṇo bhāskarasya ca ||

ṣaṇmāsadinayogena candrasya grahaṇaṃ bhavet |
p. 41b) samādhistho mahāyogī ravicandrasya madhyagaḥ(ta) ||

pratyakṣaṃ dṛśyate grā(śvā)saṃsthānabhedena yā dṛśāṃ |
candramaṇḍalamadhyasthaṃ ātmānaṃ kurute yadā ||

grahaṇaṃ tasya jānāmi somasthaṃ paśyate dhruvaṃ |
candrasūryasya cakrāṇi sṛṣṭisaṃhārakārakāḥ ||

pāraṃparyakrameṇaiva guruvaktrāttu labhyate |
yasya cakrasya madhyasthaṃ ātmānaṃ kramate layaṃ ||

candrasūryaprayogena jñāyate nātrasaṃśayaḥ |
dharmādharmaṃ ca mokṣaṃ ca jñānaṃ vairāgyameva ca ||

kāmaṃ krodhaṃ ca lobhaṃ ca yatra(cakra)sthaṃ kurute dhruvam |
marutasthaṃ vijānāti saṃyoge vāmadakṣiṇe ||

prāṇāpānasya madhyasthaṃ nāsikāgre vyavasthitam |
ūrddhaṃ hyagni dadhaścāpatiryagaśca prabhañjanaḥ ||

madhye tu pṛthivīṃ viṃdyānnaptaścaiva tu sarvagaḥ |
bhūtānte viṣuvaṃ cāraṃ suṣumnābhinnamastakaṃ ||

yadā bhavati madhyastha mātmānaṃ varavarṇini |
vahatinātrasaṃheho yogaṃ viṣuvamūttamam ||

kolacihnāni sarvāṇi uktāni yanmayā purā |
vaṃcanaṃ teṣu deveśi yathā bhavati tacchṛṇu ||

candrāditya mayaṃ sarvaṃ trailokyaṃ sa carācaram |
mārgaṃ ca tadvijānīyāda mārganteṣu varjitam ||

saṃcāraṃ kathayiṣyāmi prakṛtistasya cātmanaḥ |
vṛṣabhaṃ tatra madhye tu maithunasthaṃ ca vāyasaṃ ||

rudramānaṃ sadātiṣṭhet mahāśabdamanāhatam |
satvaṃrajastamaścaiva tasya vaṃdhatrayaṃ bhavet ||

p. 42a)

somasūryāgni cāreṇa tripādāstatrasaṃsthitāḥ |
adhastāt tu vijānīyād brahmādi aṃśakeṣu ca ||

pṛthivyāpastathā tejaścaturthaṃ vāyusaṃsthitam |
ūrddhabhāge sthitā hyate(kṣetre) śṛṃgāścatvāri yāni ca ||

sārddhaṃ trīṇi sthitā vāme punarettāni dakṣiṇe |
nāḍīsañcārabhedena asthirūpā vyavasthitāḥ ||

etāni saptahastāni dvauśīrṣau candrasūryayoḥ |
yo sau madhyagato devi racate ca anāhatam ||

maithunena vinā tasya naśabdo naiva jīvitam |
tasmāt sarvaprayatnena maithunasthaṃ sadākuru ||

maithune mokṣamāpnoti maithunamāyuvarddhanam |
sarvasiddhikaraṃ devimaithunaṃ paripaṭhyate ||

tasya devādhi devasya prakṛtisthasya sundari |
mocanaṃ saṃpravakṣyāmi ātmano vaṃcanasya ca ||

utpattistatramadhye tu kālaṃ kālodayeṣu ca |
ete trīṇi samākhyātā vṛṣapādāṃśakeṣu ca ||

tatrasthaṃ kāraṇaṃ yojyaṃ nīyate ca śanaiḥ śanaiḥ |
saṃkocya saptahastāni nāḍīsthaṃ yatsamīraṇaṃ ||

dve śīrṣe catuḥśṛṅgāni tribandhaṃ prakṛtitā saha |
guruvaktra śate naiva karaṇe varavarṇini ||

savyasthaṃ kramayogena caturviṃśātmakaṃ kuru |
saṃkocamiti vikhyātaṃ piṇḍasthaṃ ca tadā bhavet ||

hṛdi hyākāśamadhyasthaṃ dīpākāraṃ vyavasthitam |
dahyate hṛdimadhyasthaṃ indriyā viṣayāni ca ||

p. 42b)

sāvitrī yasya kāyasthāḥ kālānalasamaprabhā |
yakṣadīpti gato vahniḥ śuṣkamārddhaṃ ca nirdahet ||

tathā dahati karmāṇi yogiśo atra saṃsthitaḥ |
yadā sau śikhirūpeṇa sarvaṃ saṃkocyatiṣṭhati ||

tadāmuktaṃ samākhyātaṃ sarvamārgeṣu gacchati |
pitṛyānasya pūrveṇa devayānasya cottare ||

setubandhapūrve tu nīyate ca śanaiḥ śanaiḥ |
yā sā vāyu(ma) kalāsūṣmātribhirdehairvyavasthitā ||

ekāsaṃvaraṇīnāma dvitīyā ca visarjanī |
kalā caiva tṛtīyā ca anteṣṭirnāmaviśrutā ||

visarjanīṃ samāśritya abhyāsena muhurmuhuḥ |
nīyate baṃdhayogena sā ca saṃvaraṇī kalā ||

anteṣṭi pada(kṣa)madhyasthaṃ vilomena praveśayet |
brahmasthānaṃ parityajya viṣṇurudreśvaraṃ padam ||

śaṃkhiṇī dvārabhedaṃ ca kuṃciko dadyāṭayedvilam |
yā sā nāḍī parāsūkṣmā suṣumnā nāmaviśrutā ||

vibhidya mūrddhnimārgantu pratiṣṭhā tatra madhyataḥ |
mārgena tena gantavyaṃ sthānaṃ caiva nirāmayaṃ ||

prathamaṃ ca dvitīyaṃ ca suśiramasthimadhyagam |
tṛtīyaṃ ca samāśritya layaṃ kṛtvā suyatnataḥ ||

vaṃcanaṃ ca samākhyātaṃ kālasya varavarṇini |
na tatra bhidyate janma na kālakṣayameva ca ||

dharmādharmau parityajya līyate parame pade |
tatrasthāne yadālīnaṃ prāṇāpānaḥ kṣayaṃ gataḥ ||

āgataṃ ca gataṃ caiva nosvāsaṃ svāsameva ca |
p. 43a) na śṛṇoti ca śabdāni muktā caiva manāhatam ||

rūpaṃ caiva najjānāti varṇāni yādṛśāni ca |
navasaṃsā te jihvā śītoṣṇaṃ sparśameva ca ||

ghrāṇañcaiva na gṛhṇāti gandhaṃ durgandhameva ca |
buddhi dṛśyamidaṃ sarvaṃ trailokyam sa carācaram ||

ya sa? cāhya?manebuddhistadā dvaitaṃ na vidyate |
ekībhūtaṃ yadātmānaṃ sarvendriya vivarjitam ||

caitanyaṃ cetanaṃ cetā na kālakṣayameva ca |
etaddevi paraṃbrahma nirvāṇaṃ paramākṣaram ||

jñāyate guruvaktrasthaṃ muktisthānaṃ sunirmalam |
anyatpurapraveśaṃ ca kurute sādhakeśvaraḥ ||

yā sā devimayākhyātā kalānāmaviva(sa)rjjanī |
hastamātrapramāṇena puṣpaṃ karatale sthitam ||

ante vaṃdhaṃ prakurvīta kalāsaṃvaraṇī tu yā |
nītaṃ puṣpasya madhyasthaṃ mārutaṃ ca śanaiḥ śanaiḥ ||

taṃ puṣpaṃ calitaṃ dṛṣṭvā dviguṇaṃ ca samabhyaset |
dvihastamātrayogena puṣpasaṃkramaṇaṃ yadā ||

sidhayate nātrasaṃdehaḥ paṃcahastapramāṇataḥ |
puṣpaṃ ca calitaṃ dṛṣṭvā vajrakṣīraṃ vibhedayet ||

vibhedite vajravṛkṣe parakāyalayaṃ kuru |
parasya hṛdimadhye tu paṃkajasthaṃ yadā bhavet ||

tadā bhidyanti bhūtāni svakīya guṇapaṃcakaiḥ |
rūpe rūpaṃ vijānāti śravaṇe śabdaṃ śṛṇoti ca ||

nāsikāgrasthito vāyuḥ prāṇaṃ gṛhṇāti yā dṛśam |
sarvaṃ jānāti caitanyaḥ śītoṣṇaṃ sparśameva ca ||

p. 43b)

sthāne sthāne visarpanti indriyā viṣayāni ca |
pūrvoktā yādṛśī vidyā tatsarvaṃ vadate dhrūvam ||

parakāyapraveśaśca uktaṃ devi sudurllabhaṃ |
dvipade catuṣpade caiva apade padasaṃkale ||

saṃkramennātrasaṃdeho asya yogaprabhāvataḥ |
atha utkāntimicchanti vairāgyo pahatā narāḥ ||

tasahaṃ saṃpravakṣyāmi sadya utkramaṇaṃ yathā |
pūrvaṃ saṃkocayogena layaṃ yāti pare pade ||

utkramennātrasaṃdehaḥ punarjanma na vidyate |
sarvaṃ devimayākhyātaṃ yatsurairapi durllabhaṃ ||

ātmasaṃvinnibhedena kimanyat paripṛcchasi |


iti pārameśvarīmate guhye ātmasaṃvittibhedaścatustriṃśatimaḥ paṭalaḥ ||





devyuvāca


atraiva merumadhye tu yā sthitā kulanāyikā |
tripurāmantravikhyātā vaktrāṃśābāna(gādiṣu)saṃyutā ||

saptaviṃśati yoginyaḥ paṃcamudrā tathaiva ca |
vaktumarhasi deveśa sāmarthaṃ pūjane vidhiṃ ||

catuṣke ca mahātantre uttare ṣaṭkakeṣu ca |
rudrayāmalamadhye ca pūrvoktaṃ tanmayā śrutam ||

viśeṣena punardeva yathā bhavati tadvada |


śrībhairava uvāca


kathayāmi na saṃdehaḥ pāraṃparyaṃ yathāsthitam |
brahmaṇo viṣṇutejācca rudratejātsamudbhavā ||

tripurā ca mahāvidyā bhuktimuktiphalapradā |
tasyoddhāraṃ pravakṣyāmi merumadhye yathāsthitam ||

p. 44a)

mālinīvāmakarṇasya dhruvānte bhūṣaṇaṃ ca yat |
adhastasya samuddhṛtya lakulī yatrasaṃsthitam ||

adhordhveta(arddhe arddhe)dvijānīyācchaladaṇḍāsanasthitam |
sarasvatī samopetaṃ krūraṃ śirasisaṃsthitam ||

vāgbhavaṃ ca mahādevi brahmatejasamudbhavam |
asya bījaprabhāvena sṛṣṭi rūpaḥ pitāmahaḥ ||

karālīvarṇamuddhṛtya śveta rudrādi saṃsthitam |
tatraiva vāmanetrasthaṃ bhairavena yutaṃ kuru ||

śukrabhairava ūrddhaṃ vai pūtanā yatra saṃsthitā |
āsanaṃ tadvijānīyādambikāmūrddhnisaṃsthitam ||

ayaṃ binduyutaṃ kuryāt kāmarājaṃ pratiṣṭhitam |
viṣṇutejaḥ samudbhūtaṃ mahākṣobhakaraṃ viduḥ ||

satatābhyāsayogena māhāsaubhāgyadāyakam |
jalamaddhyagataṃ devi bhairavaṃ retaḥ saṃbhavam ||

bhairavaṃ tasya deveśi adharasthaṃ niyojayet |
mahāsenodbhavañcaiva puratastasya dāpayet ||

trāṇaṃ mūrddhni gataṃ kuryāt tadbījaṃ rudrasaṃbhavam |
etāni trīṇi bījāni tribhirdevi samudbhavāḥ || (imenavāstra yo bījā deva traya 
samudbhavā ||)

suṣuptāḥ saṃsthitā hyete prabuddhāphaladāyakāḥ |
tatahaṃsaṃ pravakṣyāmi guhyādguhyataraṃ sthitam ||

vidyātrayaṃ paraṃ guhyaṃ dīpanaṃ tripurasya ca |
chagalaṇḍabhairavaṃ yatra dahanī yatra saṃsthitaḥ ||

uddhṛtyavidhiyogena ubhau varṇau punaḥ punaḥ |
anantena samāyukta vajriṇī tasya cāgrataḥ |

pañcārlaṃ?bhairavaṃ yatra tadvarṇamagrataḥ sthitam |

p. 44b)

jñānaśakti samudbhūtaṃ śirasthaṃ tasya dāpayet |
tasyāgre saṃvakaṃ bījaṃ devīkhaḍgīśamāsanā(gāminam) ||

bhairavaṃ raktasaṃbhūtaṃ guhyaśakti samanvitam |
aṣṭākṣarī mahāvidyā brahmabījapradīpinī ||

krodhavarṇaṃ samuddhṛtya pinākī āsane sthitam |
vāmanetrodbhavaṃ sarvaṃ(rudraṃ) saṃyogaṃ tasya dāpayet ||

mālinī prathamaṃ bījaṃ jhaṇṭīśena samanvitam |
tṛtīyaṃ pūtanārūḍhaṃ varṇaṃ krodhasamudbhavam ||

jñānaśaktisamārūḍhaṃ dhātrīśamagrataḥ sthitam |
tasyāgre meṣavarṇaṃ ca guhyaśakti vibhūṣitam ||

mahāvarṇāni tasyāgre uddharedvaravarṇini |
varṇasaṃvarttakonāma gāyatrī bhūṣitaṃ kuru ||

dakṣiṇasya bhuje mūle yā bhīmā śikhare sthitā |
tāṃnvā? binduyutaṃ kuryādūddharedvaravarṇini ||

tasyāgre tu karālī ca dhruvādi āsanasthitā |
tasyāgre raktasaṃbhūta makārasanaṃ saṃsthitaṃ ||

mahākṣobhakarīhyeṣā vidyā ekādaśākṣarī |
kāmarājādi saṃbhūtā viṣṇutejaḥ prabodhanī ||

dhruvamādai samuddhṛtya tṛtīyasya sureśvari |
mahākālaṃ samuddhṛtya sadyojātenasaṃyutam ||

dvitīyañca samākhyātaṃ ātmabījaṃ sudurllabham |
devīṃ saṃghā(hā)tikāṃ nāmabindumastakabhūṣitam ||

amare śāsanaṃ datvā krodhavarṇaṃ samālikhet |
bhujaṃgaṃ ca tato devi ukārāsanasaṃsthitam ||

eṣā paṃcākṣarī vidyā mukti bījaprabodhanī |
vidyāṅgeṣu ṣaḍaṅgeṣu vaktreṣu pūjaneṣu(prapūjayet) ca ||

p. 45a)

vāgbhavaṃ kāmarājasya muktidaṃ parameśvari |
yā yasya dipinī vidyā uccārantasya pūrvikaṃ ||

anenaiva vidhānena sa jīvā nikhilā bhavet |
tribhistejaiñca saṃbhūtā tripurā tejadīpiṇī ||

jñātavyā purabhedena sarvasiddhi pradāyakā |
nyāsamātre kṛte devi trailokyaṃ vaśamānayet ||

tamahaṃ saṃpravakṣyāmi nyāsasthānāni yāni ca |
mūrddhni sthānaṃ ca prathamaṃ vratatejo vyavasthitam ||

punarnārāyaṇantejaṃ guhyasthāne ca vinyaset |
hṛdaye rudratejena nyāsaṃ kṛtvā tu yatnataḥ ||

vāgbhavaṃ dakṣanetrasthaṃ vāme caiva janārdanaḥ |
lalāṭe śivamityāhurvinyased varavarṇini ||

dakṣiṇeśravaṇe nyastaṃ vāgbhavaṃ devi yatnataḥ |
kāmarājasthitaṃ vāma śravaṇe nātrasaṃśayaḥ ||

rudra tejo mahādevi nyasedvaktrasya madhyataḥ |
dakṣiṇe tu bhujā daṇḍe punardevaḥ pitāmahaḥ ||

keśavasya sthitaṃ tejaṃ bhuje vāme pratiṣṭhitam |
vinyased vidhiyogena rudraṃ pṛṣṭhasya madhyataḥ ||

vāgbhavaṃ dakṣajaṃghāyāṃ vāme kāmaḥ pratiṣṭhitaḥ |
nābhimadhyasthitaṃ rudraṃ punasta.km sarvasaṃdhiṣu ||

eṣā vidyātmako nyāsaḥ ṣaḍaṃgañca ataḥ śṛṇu |
māyā vāgeśvaī nāma hṛdisthāne niyojayet ||

kāmarājaṃ śiredattaṃ śikhāsthāneṣu muktidaṃ |
vāgbhavaṃ kavace yojyaṃ netrau kāma pratiṣṭhitau ||

nyasyāstraṃ rudrabījena diśāsu vidiśāsu ca |
p. 45b) ṣaḍaṃgañca idaṃ devi vakranyāsamataḥ śṛṇu ||

vāgbhavaṃ pūrvavaktrasthaṃ tejo vidyā samanvitam |
vāme nyāsaṃ prakūrvīta kāmarājaṃ sureśvari ||

tasyāpi dīpinīvidyā nyāsaṃ vaktrāntare sthitam |
rudrabījaṃ nyaseddhīmān vaktre caiva tu dakṣiṇe ||

pūrvavidyādi yogena vaktraṃ nyāsaṃ sudurllabhaṃ |
vāgbhavaṃ pañcavāṇāni cāpena sahitāni ca ||

tamahaṃ kathayiṣyāmi yathā nyāsaṃ vyavasthitam |
rudramālādi vedā(devā)nāṃ mandire mīnasaṃsthitam ||

tristhāne saṃsthitā hyete ālikhedvaravarṇini |
amarīśāsanārūḍhaṃ madhyasthaṃ kuru yatnataḥ ||

trirakṣaramidaṃ cāpaṃ vāgbhavaṃ saṃsthitaṃ yataḥ |
bījāṅge pañcavāṇāni ye bhūtā kathayāmyaham ||

śūladaṇḍaṃ samuddhṛtya navake pūrvasaṃsthitam |
sarvagabhairavaṃ vāṇaṃ nyāsaṃ guhyasya madhyataḥ ||

ekādaśa ṛṣisthāne ccha(cā)ṅgamadhye vyavasthitam |
aṃbikā varṇamuddhṛtya varṇānāṃ sṛṣṭikārakāḥ ||

dvitīyaṃ vāṇamityāhuḥ saṃsthittaṃ nābhimaṇḍale |
punastaṃ hṛdimadhye tu varṇaṃ saṃdīpanaṃ param ||

tṛtīyaṃ vāṇamityāhustrailokyaṃ yatra saṃthitam |
nābhi mālādi saṃbhūtaṃ śikhare niṣada(dha)sya ca ||

sarasvatī samudbhūtaṃ sarvavāgeśvaraṃ viduḥ |
caturthaṃ ca mahāvāṇaṃ nyāsaṃ vaktrasya madhyataḥ ||

ṭhe-ā(lokā)dipaṃcamaṃ caiva krūraṃ yatra vyavasthitam |
mūrddhni nyāsaṃ mahādevi sarvasaṃjīvanaṃ paraṃ ||

etāni paṃcavāṇāni brahmabijāṅgasaṃbhavaḥ |
p. 46a) vācā siddhikarāstvete vinyastā piṇḍamadhyataḥ ||

kāmarājāṅgaṃ saṃbhūtaṃ paṃcavāṇaṃ vadāmyaham |
vilumadhyagataṃ gṛhya navake vāma mandire ||

pūrvavarṇaṃ vijānīyānmāṃsasya bhairavā śrayaṃ |
rudramālā dvitīyaṃ ca ūrddhe yasya vināyakī ||

anantenasamāyuktaṃ tadvarṇaṃ biṃdu bhūṣitam |
raktabhairavamārūḍhaṃ dvitīyaṃ varṇamuttamam ||

tadvarṇaṃ tasya agre tu śūladaṇḍāsanaṃ kuru |
tṛtīyaṃ bījamityāhurvaktraṃbhairavabhūṣitam ||

cakārāntaṃ samuddhṛtya aṃbikā yatra saṃsthitā |
mālinīkaṃṭha(mukha)varṇaṃ vai caturthamakṣaraṃ viduḥ ||

ya varge ādi saṃbījaṃ paṃcamaṃ ca samuddharet |
punaretāni bījāni trīṇi agre vyavasthitāḥ ||

prathamaṃ kāmarājasya vāṇaṃ śirasi vinyaset |
aṣṭākṣaramidaṃ vāṇaṃ raktavarṇaṃ tu saṃsthitam ||

karālī varṇamuddhṛtya ādi ekādaśe sthitam |
dvitīyasya sthitaṃ pūrvaṃ dahanī tasya agrataḥ ||

navakādi sthitaṃ varṇaṃ tatrārūḍhaṃ sureśvari |
viśvamālādi varṇena bhūṣitaṃ bindusaṃyutam ||

vaktrasthaṃ bhairavaṃ tasya tṛtīyaṃ tasya agrataḥ |
caturthī aṃbikā devī snāyu rudrāsanaṃ kuru ||

viśvamālāntasaṃyuktaṃ meṣaṃ tasyāgrataḥ sthitaṃ |
vāgīśa agratastasya ṣaṣṭamaṃcanasaṃśayaḥ ||

punaretāni bījāni catvāraḥ purataḥ sthitaḥ |
daśākṣaramidaṃ vāṇaṃ nyasedvaktrasya madhyataḥ ||

kakulīśaṃ punaścaiva tṛtīye pūrvasaṃsthitam |
p. 46b) makāraṃ yasya ūrddhe tu cukāramagrataḥ sthitaḥ ||

pṛṣṭhabhairavavarṇaṃ cari aṃtamāsanaṃ kuru |
lokādi śirasedattaṃ arghīśasya dvitīyakam ||

punaḥ saṃvarttakaṃ gṛhya rudramālāntasaṃyutaṃ |
tṛtīyaṃ bījamityuktaṃ tasyāgre bhīmamālikhet ||

paṃcamaṃ vāyuvegasthaṃ vāmasya śikhare sthitam |
punarettāni bījāni tasyāgre trīṇi ālikhet ||

tṛtīyaṃ ca mahāvāṇaṃ vasuvarṇaṃ hṛdisthitam |
trimūrttibhairavaṃ pūrvaṃ lasthaṃ krodhasamudbhavaṃ ||

mālinī jihvavarṇena bheditaṃ bindusaṃyuttam |
tṛtīyaṃ dīpanī bījaṃ jñānaśakti vibhūṣitam ||

tārāmadhyagataṃ gṛhya meṣasyo parisaṃsthittam |
caturthabindusaṃyuktaṃ āṣāḍhī nāmanāmataḥ ||

mahākālaṃ punardevi dhruvādi saṃyutaṃ kuru |
krūravarṇaṃ śiredattaṃ pañcamaṃ bījamuttamam ||

devī netraṃ tṛtīyaṃ ca anantena samanvittam |
lohitaṃ saptamaṃ bījaṃ dakṣakukṣisamudbhavam ||

vāgīśaṃ cāṣṭamaṃ yojyaṃ punaścatvāri agrataḥ |
devīvarṇākṣaraṃ vāṇaṃ caturthannābhimadhyataḥ ||

krūraṃ pūrvasamuddhṛtya sambakaṃ tasya agrataḥ |
lokāntaṃ purantastasya dvitīyamakṣaraṃ viduḥ ||

punastaṃ saṃvakaṃ bījaṃ anantena vibhūṣitam |
śūladaṇḍāsanārūḍhaṃ caturthaṃ snāyusaṃbhavaṃ ||

paṃcamaṃ vāyuvegaṃ ca trīṇye tānyagrataḥ punaḥ |
vargasaṃkhyāni vijāni paṃcamo bāṇasaṃsthitaḥ ||

p. 47a) yonimadhye kṛte nyāso mahāsiddhikaro bhavet |
etāni paṃcavānāni kāmabījasamudbhavāḥ ||

vinyasedvidhiyogena pūjākāle tu yatnataḥ |


devyuvāca


kāmabījāni vāṇani aṅgavaktrāṇithāni ca |
śrūtaṃ devamayāsarvamuddhāraṃ tripurasya ca ||

adhunāśrotumicchāmi pūjanasya yathāvidhiḥ |


śrībhairava uvāca


tristhānaṃ tripuraṃ vidyā dāsanatrayasaṃyuttam ||

pīṭhatrayaṃ tathā bījaṃ rūpatrayamudāhṛtam |
caryātrīṇisamākhyātā uccāraṃ trividhaṃ bhavet ||

layaṃtrividhamityāhuḥ sthāne sthāne pṛthak pṛthak |
ekaikasya tu bījasya nava bhedāḥ prakīrtītāḥ ||

bhede bhede sthitā devyā svasthāna nāmasaṃyutā |
pūjitavyā prayatnena ādibījenasaṃpuṭam ||

ekānte vijane sthāne paśu dṛṣṭi vivarjjite |
śobhane gandhayukte ca pūjayet tipurabhairavī ||

digvāsā muktakeśaśca netre kajjalabhūṣite |
pūjākāle ca kartavyaṃ sarvasiddhipradāyakaṃ ||

trivarṇaṃ paṃkajaṃ devi pūrṇapīṭhe vyavasthitam |
taptahemapratīkāśaṃ sa bāhyābhyantareṣu ca ||

āsanaṃ tadvijānīyāt puraṃ brahmasya kīrtitam |
tatra madhyagataṃ bījaṃ vāgbhavaṃ surapūjitam ||

svamātrā veṣṭitaṃ kuryāt triḥprakārārddhameva ca |
suśuddhahemasaṃkāśaṃ visphurantaṃ vicintayet ||

tasyāṅge(nte) nava yoginyaḥ pañcavāṇastathaiva ca |

p. 47b)

vācāṃ siddhi karaṃ divyaṃ pūjitavyaṃ prayatnataḥ ||

punarjālandhare devi ṣaṭpatraṃ paṅkajaṃ śubhaṃ |
yathābāhye tathā dehe raktavarṇaṃ vicintayet ||

tattrimātrārddhasaṃyuktaṃ kāmarājaṃ sureśvari |
javāpuṣpanibhākāraṃ bhramantaṃ cintayed budhaḥ ||

navadūti samopetaṃ paṃcavāṇasamanvitam |
mahākṣobhakaraṃ devi paraṃ viṣṇoḥ prakīrttitam ||

uḍḍiyānaṃ punardevi aṣṭapatraṃ suśobhanaṃ |
kundendu tu hinābhāsaṃ puraṃ rudrasya kīrtitam ||

kāmorddhe ca mahābījaṃ paṃkajasthaṃ tṛtīyakaṃ |
nirvāṇaṃ ca samākhyātaṃ nirvāṇa pada dāyakam ||

navadevyā samopetaṃ pūjitavyaṃ prayatnataḥ |
mahāśāntikaraṃ divyaṃ muktidaṃ vighnanāśanam ||

pītai raktaiḥ sitaiḥ puṣpaiḥ karpūrādi sugandhibhiḥ |
phalguṣaṃ trividhaṃ deyaṃ tribhi(triṣu)sthāneṣu yojayet ||

dhūpaṃ dīpaṃ ca naivedyaṃ tarpaṇaṃ ca yathāvidhiḥ |
sarvaṃ saṃpūrṇataḥ kṛtvāmudrayāmudritaṃ kuru ||

athātaḥ saṃpravakṣyāmi mudrābaṃdhaṃ ca yādṛśaṃ |
sarvasiddhikaraṃ divyaṃ nirvighnaṃ vighnanāśanaṃ ||

karamadhye mukhaṃ vāmaṃ dakṣiṇaṃ na bhasannibham |
pṛṣṭhe pṛṣṭhayutaṅakuryāt tarjjanī kanyasāntare ||

dakṣiṇesya kṛtaṃ vāme vāmākhyāṃ dakṣiṇasya ca |
anyonya graṃthitaṃ kuryādaṃ guṣṭhenadānāmikāṃ ||

gṛhītvātatprayatne na vāmena dakṣiṇena tu |
jyeṣṭhāṃgulīṣu karttavyaṃ aṃkuśaṃ adha ūrdhataḥ ||

eṣā sā drāviṇī mudrā vāṇapūrvāṃ ca darśayet |

p. 48a)

tarjjanīvāmahastasya jyeṣṭhā tasyoparisthittā ||

nāmikākanyasā ruḍhā aṃguṣṭhaṃ tatra madhyataḥ |
sparśaṃ caiva prakurvīta tarjjanyā kanyasāṅgulī ||

kṣobhanī caiva vikhyātā mahākṣobhakarī viduḥ |
darśitavyā prayatnena dvitīya vāṇasaṃyutā ||

mahāsiddhikarājyeṣā trailokya kṣobhakārikā |
dvauhastau saṃpuṭau kṛtvā anyonyagrathitāṃgulī |

tarjjanyādakṣahastasya vāmekare anāmikā |
grathitavyaṃ prayatnena vāmasya tarjjanī tu yā ||

tenāpi savyahastasya gṛhītvā tu anāmikām |
punareva hi karttavyaṃ ddhordhau madhyamāṅgulī ||

aṃguṣṭhaṃ vāmahastasya hastamadhye niyojayet |
yonimudrā tviyaṃ devi sarvasiddhikarī parā ||

tṛtīyavāṇayogena yoni sthāne pradarśayet |
saṃkocya aṃgulī triṇi aṃguṣṭhe na nirodhayet ||

tarjjani vāmahastasya vāmakarṇe niyojayet |
tarjjayantīṃ diśāṃ sarvāṃ bhrāmayen netra saṃyutāṃ || 

caturthavāṇasaṃyuktāṃ mahāvighnapraśanīm |
muṣṭibaṃdhaṃ ca karttavyaṃ karevāme ca dakṣiṇe ||

saṃyogaṃ teṣu karttavyaṃ tarjjanyau ca prasārayet |
aṃguṣṭhayugalaṃ caiva teṣu mūle niyojayet ||

nirālaṃbā mahāmudrā paṃcame vāṇasaṃyutā |
darśitavyā prayatnena bhuktimukti phalapradā ||

kāmarājasya ye devi paṃcavāṇavyavasthitāḥ |
teṣu bījaṃ dvitīyaṃ ca ekaikasya pṛthak pṛthak ||

p. 48b) tatpūrvaṃ darśayenmudrāṃ caturvargaphalaṃ labhet |
puṣpaṃ dhūpaṃ ca naivedyaṃ jāpyaṃ pūjāṃ tathaiva ca ||

tejaṃ gṛhṇanti deveśi bhūtāśca rākṣasādayaḥ |
mudrayā rahitānāñca prabhavanti na saṃśayaḥ ||

tasmāt sarvaprayatnena mudritaṃ sa phalaṃ bhavet |
mudritena sthitaṃ tejo mudritaṃ ca sthiraṃ vratam ||

mudritena sthirāpūjā mudrite na sthiraṃ paraṃ |
mudrayāsiddhimāpnoti yatkāmaṃ taddhruvaṃ labhet ||

mudrayā rahitā devi tripurāvighnadāyikā |
tasmāt sarvaprayatnena pūjānte mudritaṃ kuru ||

yenavaivaddhamātreṇa dravate kāminī janā |
drāvaṇī sā samākhyātā mahāprīti karī śubhā ||

svargge martye ca pātāle yāstriyo madagarvitāḥ |
tāsāṃ kṣobhakarīmudrā kṣobhaṇīkṣobhakārikā ||

siddhagandharvakanyānāṃ devāsura sa pannagāṃ |
ānayennātrasaṃdeho yonimudrā prabhāvataḥ ||

duṣṭanārī kurājānāṃ śatrūṇāṃ ca viśeṣataḥ |
tarjjanyātrāsitrāssarve mohaṃ gacchanti tatkṣaṇāt ||

dharmmārtha kāma mokṣaṃ ca rājyaṃ caiva viśeṣataḥ |
labhate nātrasaṃdeho vadhvā caiva nirāmayā ||

mudrittasya guṇā hyete kathitāni mayā tava |
drāvaṇī yonimudrā ca kṣobhaṇī tarjjanī tathā ||

mudrā catuṣṭayaṃ baddhā nirālaṃvā ca paṃcamī |
sarvaṃsaṃpūṃrṇataḥ kṛtyā pūjānte japamārabhet ||

pūrvaṃ madhyagataṃ kuryyādubhau ante niyojayet |

p. 49a)

nītvā trīṇi pare tatve kāmasthā triprabhedinī(reśvarī) ||

kūṭaṃ dīpaśikhākāraṃ layaṃ kṛtvā muhurmuhuḥ |
punastrīṇi ubhau hyekaṃ sthāne sthāne vilomataḥ ||

eṣa jāpyavidhiḥ prokto rahasyaṃ prakaṭī kṛtam |
brahmacārī jitakrodhī nakta bhojī dṛḍhavrataḥ ||

anenaivavidhānena lakṣajāpyaṃ karoti yaḥ |
homaṃ caiva daśāṃśena tvāmaṃtraḥ sthiro bhavet ||

brahmavṛkṣodbhavaiḥ puṣpai karavīrairjātīkādibhiḥ |
kṣaudraṃ lākṣārasaṃ kṣīraṃ agnikaṇḍe tu homayet ||

sāmarthaṃ tasya deveśi yadbhavet kathayāmyaham |
ṛgvedothayajurvedaḥ sāmavedo-atharvaṇaḥ ||

itihāsapurāṇāni tarkavyākaraṇāni ca |
āgamāni aśeṣāṇi paṃcavaktrodbhavāni ca ||

gītaṃ vādyaṃ tathā nṛtyaṃ kavitvañca manoramaṃ |
ye bhūtā ye bhaviṣyanti vidyāsyānāni yāni ca ||

pratyakṣaṃ bhavate sarvamasyā vidyā prabhāvataḥ |
yasyakasyāyi mūrkhasya strīkumārasya kanyakā ||

vāgīśo bhavate sākṣādbhujāgraṃ yāvanmastake |
rājā ca rājapatnī ca rājakanyā tathaiva ca ||

brāhmaṇī kṣatriṇī vaiśyā śūdrī vā varavarṇinī |
kṣubhyanti sādhakendrasya dadanti vipulaṃ dhanam ||

āvarttyasaptavārāṇi yasya nāmavidarbhitam |
viṃśadgavyūtimānena ānayennātrasaṃśayaḥ ||

mriyatenātrasaṃdeho yaditena na kāmyate |
lakṣamekena japtena homaṃ caiva yathāvidhi ||

p. 49b) vāgīśo bhavati sākṣādrūpeṇamakaradhvajaḥ |
bhuṃjate vipulānbhogānasyā vidyāprabhāvataḥ ||

lakṣadvayena japtena siddhe homakṛtena ca |
bhavate nātrasaṃdeho bhūpatiḥ sādhakeśvaraḥ ||

yadā japati catvāri lakṣāni sādhakeśvaraḥ |
ājñāsiddhiḥ pravarttena pātālaṃ sphoṭayet tu saḥ ||

viladvāraṃ samāśrityaśatamaṣṭottaraṃ tu saḥ |
siddhārtharājikānāñca viladvāraṃ tu tāḍayet ||

kṣubhyaṃti nātrasandehaḥ kanyāḥ pātālavāsinīḥ |
nāgarākṣasadaityānāṃ yā sthitāvarayoṣitaḥ ||

āgacchanti na saṃdeho yatrāsau sādhakeśvaraḥ |
namaskāraṃ prakurvanti ādeśaṃ yā ca yanti ca ||

svargapātālamārgeṇa praviśya sādhakeśvaraḥ |
yadā na rocate devi aṣṭalakṣajapaṃ kuru ||

devadānava siddhānāṃ svargapātālavāsīnāṃ |
mahākṣobhaṃ prakurvanti dadanti kāmikaṃ phalam ||

navalakṣakṛte jāpe homena sādhitena ca |
koṭī caturdaśānāñca vidyādharapatirbhavet ||


iti pārameśvarīmatemerumadhye tripuroddhāraḥ paṃcatriṃśatimaḥ paṭalaḥ ||




devyuvāca


pūjājapavidhānaṃ ca mudrāsāmarthayādṛśaṃ |
tripurasya mahādeva nikhilaṃ ca śrūtaṃ mayā ||

yā sā saṃjīvanī vidyā jarāmṛtyuvināśanī |
yatprasādeva(na) devāśca(veśa) amarāḥ parikīrtitāḥ ||

kathaṃ sā merumadhye tu varṇabhūtāvyavasthitā |
uddhāraṃ kīdṛśaṃ tasyāstanme vada mahāprabho ||

yasya jāpyena deveśa amaratvaṃ ca jāyate |
p. 50a) uthānaṃ mṛtakānāṃ ca yasyāvidyā prabhāvataḥ ||

śrībhairava uvāca


śṛṇu devi parāṃ guhyaṃ vidyāparamadurlabhāṃ |

indrādyā devatāḥ | sarve āgatāḥ purato mama |


stutohaṃ vividhaiḥ stotrairdivyavarṣaśataṃpurā |

tato mayā su tuṣṭhena dattā amarasundarī |
teṣumadhye mahādevi śukreṇaṣvavatāritā ||

viṃśākṣarī mahāvidyā daśadūtī samanvitā |
saṃjīvinī vivikhyātā dvittīyaṃ nāmakīrtitam ||

amaratvaṃ dadecchīghraṃ merumadhye mayoddhṛtā |
dānavānāṃ hitārthāya śukreṇa dhāritā tu sā(krameṇa sādhitā purā) ||

devī sure mahāyuddhe ye mriyanti mahāsurāḥ |
teṣāṃ jīvapradaḥ śukre asyā vidyā prabhāvataḥ ||

tiṣṭhate yasya dehe tu vidyā dūtī samanvitā |
śatamaṣṭottaraṃ jāpyaṃ yaḥ karoti dine dine ||

pūjāvidhāna yogena tasya vakṣyāmi ye guṇāḥ |
bhūtapretapiśācāni apamṛtyurmahābhayaṃ ||

jvaraśūlaṃ tathā rogaṃ astrāṇi kuliśādayaḥ |
saṃgrāme japamāpnoti mahāvighnairnna bhūyate ||

siṃhavyāghrabhayaṃ nāsti yannageṣu tathaiva ca |
tamahaṃ saṃpravakṣyāmi uddhāraṃ yādṛśaṃ bhavet ||

brahmaṇe kṣatriye vaiśye śūdre vā varavarṇini |
parīkṣiteṣu dātavyaṃ kulamārgopadeśike ||

ekānte vijane sthāne paśu dṛṣṭi vivarjite |
catuḥśṛṃgaṃ mahāmeru mālikhedvaravarṇini ||

indrehyagniryamaścaiva nair-ṛtyaṃ varuṇā nilaṃ |
p. 50b) kauberamīśaparyantaṃ diśāsu vidiśāsu ca ||

paṃcaratnenasaṃpūrṇaṃ varddhanyāsahitaṃ ghaṭam |
śrīkhaṇḍacandane naiva śvetavarṇāni kārayet ||

divyāni puṣpavastrāṇi veṣṭayitvā punaḥ punaḥ |
maṇiratnamayaṃ kuṃbhaṃ abhāve kāṃcanaṃ śubham ||

athavaupyamayaṃ devi purastasya dāpayet |
puṣpairdhūpaiśca naivedyaiḥ pūjayitvā punaḥ punaḥ ||

uddhāraṃ tatra kurvīta vidyāṃ saṃjīvanīṃ parām |
merumadhyagataṃ yacca dhruvaṃ nāma(gha)pratiṣṭhitam ||

ādivarṇaṃ samuddhṛtyavarṇanāṃ jīvanaṃ param |
dhruvānte varṇaṃ arghīśamadhastasyavyavasthitam ||

ekahastāntasaṃgṛhya lokādi bhūṣitaṃ kuru |
dvitīyaṃ tu samākhyātaṃ bhṛgu varṇaṃ tṛtīyakam ||

punarbhṛguṃ caturthe ca śukravarṇena bhūṣitam |
mālinī dakṣiṇe pāde dhātrīśaṃ yatra saṃsthitam ||

adhastasya(syāṃ) sa(na)muddhṛtya caturānana bhairavam |
jihvāśakti samopetaṃ paṃcamaṃ bījamuttamam ||

mālinī kaṃṭha(mukha)saṃbhūtaṃ mahāsena(mokārasyeva)sya ūrddhataḥ |
ṛtuvarṇaṃ vijānīyānmālinī śikhameva ca ||

viśvādi varṇasaṃyuktaṃ saptamaṃ bījamuttamam |
punaḥ krūreṇasaṃyuktaṃ japantī aṣṭamaṃ bhavet ||

dhruvādi āsanantasya bījaṃ kāla nivāraṇaṃ |
punastaṃ varṇamuddhṛtya jayantī yatra saṃsthitā ||

sopi viśvādi varṇena trimūrti saṃyutaṃ kuru |
mālinī kaṇṭha(mukha)varṇaṃ ca kūrmāntādho vyavasthitam ||

sopi bindu yutaṃ kuryāt snāyu rudrālayaṃ paraṃ |
p. 51a) daśamaṃ ca samākhyātaṃ amṛtaṃ jīvalaksaṇam ||

lohittaṃ ca punarvarṇaṃ rudrānte pūrvasaṃsthitam |
śūladaṇḍe samārūḍhaṃ viśvāntena vibhūṣitam ||

umākāntaṃ punardevi dvādaśaṃ kevalaṃ(śavaṃ) bhavet |
mālinī yā śivādūtī grahamālā vyavasthitā ||

bhujaṃgaṃ tu samārūḍhaṃ pañcārtha(pracaṇḍa)bhairavaṃ |
tasya bindu śire caiva akṣaraṃ viśvavarṇakam ||

āṣāḍhī bhairavaṃ yatra tasyāntānte ca vyavasthitam |
caturdaśaṃ samākhyātaṃ sūkṣmarudreṇa bhūṣitam ||

bhṛgurudraṃ samūddhṛtya nāntasthaṃ bindu bhūṣitam |
tasyānte krodhavarṇaṃ ca pūrvamekādaśe sthitam ||

amarīśa rudramārūḍhaṃ bījaṃ ṣoḍaśamaṃ viduḥ |
śūlaṃ daṇḍaṃ punardevi oṃkārādi sthitaṃ punaḥ ||

punastaṃ bhṛgukaṃ rudra lokāntena vibhūṣitam |
śukramadhyagataṃ varṇaṃ yatra bhṛgurvyavasthitaḥ ||

snāyuvarṇasamārūḍhaṃ viśvantena vibhūṣitam |
aṃbikā tasya agre tu lakulī yatra bhairavaḥ ||

anante na samāyuktaṃ uktaṃ viṃśākṣarī bhavet |
asyāṅgāni punardevi ye bhūtā kathayāmyaham ||

nāmavarṇādi saṃgṛhyavidyāmūle tu saṃsthitā |
hṛdinyāsaṃ prakurvīta namontaṃ varavarṇini ||

tasyānte bhūtavarṇāni śiranyā pratiṣṭhitam |
svāhāntaṃ tadvijānīyādvedavarṇākṣarī śikhā ||

vauṣaḍantena saṃyuktā apamṛtyuvināśanī |
kavace trīṇi(ṣaḍ) bījāni huṃkārānte vyavasthitā ||

p. 51b)

dvau varṇo netramadhye tu tasyānte ye pratiṣṭhitaṃ |
vaṣaḍantaṃ vijānīyāt trīṇi astre(gre)vyavasthitaṃ ||

tasyānte ca phaḍityāhuḥ ṣaḍaṅgaṃ kālavaṃcanam |
asyadūtī pravakṣyāmi śṛṇuṣvaikāgramānasā ||

pūjitavyāprayatnena daśaitāḥ siddhidāyikāḥ |
prāṇādyā vayavaḥ paṃca hrīṃkārādi samuddharet ||

saṃjīvanī tadagre ca namontaṃ pūjanaṃ kūru |
caturdaśāni varṇāni aindrabhāge prapūjayet ||

nāgādyāsta punaścaiva tasyāgre vāyumuccaret |
saṃjīvanī ca tasyāgre dūtī caturdaśākṣarī ||

bhaktiyukto mahādevi agnikoṇeṣu pūjayet |
dharādyāḥ paṃcabhūtāni agre saṃjīvanīṃ kuru ||

hrīṃkārādi samuddhṛtya namontaṃ dakṣiṇe sthitam |
pūjitavyāṃ prayatnena dūtī caturdaśākṣarī ||

śabdādi indriyāṇāṃ ca māyābījena mohitam |
saṃjīvanī ca tasyāgre namontaṃ kuru nair-ṛte ||

namaḥ saṃjīvanī caiva māyābījādi muccaret |
namontaṃ nava bījāni paścimena prapūjayet ||

māyādi buddhiruccārya uktasaṃjīvanīṃ punaḥ |
navabījātmikā hyeṣā anilasthā prapūjayet ||

saṃjīvanīṃ punaścaiva ahaṃkārādi saṃbhavet |
māyādi pūjanaṃ tasya kaubere rudravarṇagāḥ ||

īśāne satva(nava)mityāhuragre saṃjīvanīṃ punaḥ |
pūrvantasya samuccārya māyābījaṃ sureśvari ||

asyāpi nava bījāni rajomadhyasthitaṃ kuru |
agre saṃjīvanīṃ caiva hrīṃkārādi namontikam ||

p. 52a) māyābījaṃ namoccārya punaḥ saṃjīvanī yutam |
madhyasthaṃ pūjanaṃ kuryāt padme vṛ(su)daleṣu ca ||

site pīḍhaṃ sitaiḥ puṣpairdhūpaṃ dīpaṃ sa candanam |
naivedyaṃ vividhañcaiva tasyāgre ca nivedayet ||

mudrābaṃdhaṃ prakurvīta jāpyaṃ ca tadanantaram |
asya mudrāṃ pravakṣyāmi sthairyī karaṇamuttamam ||

pañcavakrodbhavā vidyā yena kālo vaśī kṛtaḥ |
anyonya karapṛṣṭhāni aṃgulī graṃthitaṃ kuru ||

savyāṅguṣṭhenavāmasya kanya sā ca nirodhayet |
punareva hi kartavyaṃ vāmena daksiṇasya ca ||

vāme jyeṣṭhāṃgulī caiva tarjjanyā dakṣiṇena tu |
gṛhītvā tatprayatnena dakṣiṇasya tathaiva ca ||

madhye anāmike dve tu uddhṛtavyāsu yatnataḥ |
kālasaṃkarṣaṇī nāmapaṃcavaktrāvyavasthitā ||

anenaiva vidhānena pūjitavyā prayatnataḥ |
eṣā vidyā parāguhyā jīvākhyā mṛtyunāśanī ||

na tasyā jāyate mṛtyuryadā sādhayate tu saḥ |
vrata(dhruva)caryyā samopetaṃ brahmacārī dṛḍhavrataḥ ||

japate daśalakṣāṇi lakṣahomaṃ tu gugguluṃ |
śarīrasthā bhaved devi amaratvaṃ prayacchati ||

dīrghāyuḥ sa(ya)surāḥ sarve asyā vidyā prabhāvataḥ |


itipārameśvarīmate saṃjīvanīvidyā uddhāraḥ ṣaṭtriṃśatimaḥ paṭalaḥ |




devyuvāca


rurubhede mahātantre pūrvoktaṃ yat tvayā prabho |
uddhṛtā tatra madhye tu nityā ca madanotkaṭā ||

p. 52b)

vajraprastārabhedena kulanityā tathoddhṛtā |
punastasyaiva madhye tu vidyā ca dvādaśākṣarī ||

āsanaṃ paṃcavāṇāni sarvadūtī samanvitam |
karaṇavijñānayogaṃ samādhīryādṛśī bhavet ||

staṃbhanaṃ kīlanaṃ caiva māraṇoccāṭanantathā |
suvaśī karaṇaṃstrīṇāṃ saubhāgyaṃ yādṛśaṃ bhavet ||

kathaṃ kena prakāreṇa siddhyate ca kadācana |
kimarthaṃ tena siddhyanti sādhakānāṃ sureśvara ||

mahāsaṃdehamutpannammahābhrānti kṣayaṃ kuru |


śrībhairava uvāca


śṛṇu devi pravakṣyāmi yat tvayā paripṛcchitam |
varṇāni biṃdu mātrāṇi saṃpradāyantathaiva ca ||

kulamārge sthitaṃ śuddhaṃ jñātavyaṃ meru madhyataḥ |
anyeṣu sarva tantreṣu paṃcavakrodbhaveṣu ca ||

jīvavarṇavihīnāste siddhavidyā anekadhā |
sa jīvaṃ nikhilaṃ caiva bījasaṃkaravarjitam ||

saṃpūrṇasiddhidaṃ caiva merumadhye vyavasthitam |
tamahaṃ kathayiṣyāmi saṃpūrṇaṃ ca yathā bhavet ||

vajraprastārabhedena yā vidyā dvādaśākṣarī |
tribījarahitā sāsyādādimadhyāntameva ca ||

tasyoddhāraṃ pravakṣyāmi yanmayā gopitaṃ purā |
bhārabhūti sthitaṃ rudraṃ yasya pūrve sureśvari ||

vāmāntaṃ yadbhavedvarṇaṃ tadbījaṃ prathamaṃ bhavet |
uccāraṃ binduyuktaṃ ca tasyānte aṃbikā bhavet ||

raktavarṇasamārūḍhaṃ lokādi śirasi sthitam |
dhruvamālādi saṃbhinnaṃ dvitīyaṃ bījamuttamam ||

p. 53a)

urddhe yasya ṛkāraṃ ca svetaṃ rudraṃ puraḥstitam |
tadvarṇaṃ gṛhyamedhāvī māṃsavarṇāsanaṃ kuru ||

ṛpūrvaṃ ca śirodattaṃ guhyaṃtamagratasthitam |
vāmādi śirase tasya ṛ(a) ādi kevalaṃ viduḥ ||

paṃcamañca samākhyātaṃ bījaṃ varṇāsanaṃ paraṃ |
pūrvamekādaśe varṇaṃ udhṛtya varavarṇini ||

rudrāntaṃ śirase caiva dīpanī āsanasthitam |
ṛ(ḷ)pūrvaṃ śirasedattaṃ tat ṣaṣṭhamaṃ kuśākṣaram ||

asyāvidyādimaṃ bījaṃ aṃkuśāgre niyojayet |
saptamaṃ ca samākhyātaṃ bījaṃ paramadurllabham ||

āṣāḍhī yasya ūrddhe yā? romadhye vyavasthitam |
tadvarṇaṃ sūkṣmasaṃyuktaṃ aṣṭamaṃ cākṣaraṃ viduḥ ||

mālinī urumadhe tu yatra tārā vyavasthitā |
vāgīśamānārūḍhaṃ ṛpūrvaṃ ca śire(vyava)sthitam ||

navamaṃ bījamityuktaṃ nitaṃbaṃ kevalaṃ punaḥ |
ṛṣimālā tṛtīye tu tadvarṇaṃ saṃsthitaṃ viduḥ ||

phāntaṃ varṇaṃ samuddhṛtya chi(tri)-ūrddhe saṃsthitaṃ ca yat |
kevalaṃ tadbijānīyād bījamekādaśaṃ bhavet ||

punastaṃ raktamārūḍhaṃ dvādaśambījamuttamam |
ūrddhe ca aṃbikā devī vāme madhye vyavasthitā ||

trayodaśaṃ ca ityuktaṃ śire(ra) yasya ṛpūrvakam |
asyāvidyādi antasthaṃ tabījamagratasthitam ||

asyānte ca punardevi ādimaṃ ca niyojayet |
eṣā sā paramā vidyā nikhilāsiddhi dāyikā ||

utpannā merumadhye tu vidyāpaṃcadaśākṣarī |
p. 53b) punaranyaṃ pravakṣyāmi āsanasya yathāvidhi ||

ṛ antaṃ prathamaṃ bījaṃ mālinī kusumā yudham |
la(na)kulīśaṃ tadagre tu dhruvāntamadha(dhya)saṃsthitam ||

dīpanī āsanārūḍhaṃ viśvādi bhūṣitaṃ kuru |
krūreṇa śirasākrāntaṃ dvitīyaṃ bījamuttamam ||

ī-aṃte adhaḥ saṃgṛhyapūṃtanā tasya āsanam |
ṛpūrve śirasidattaṃ sopi binduyutaṃ kuru ||

śukramadhyoddhṛtaṃ devaṃ lokānte saṃsthitaṃ kuru |
caturthaṃ bījamityāhustasyā vidyādi agrataḥ ||

paṃcabījamayaṃ divyaṃ mūlavidyāsanaṃ bhavet |
paṃcavāṇāni ye proktā teṣubhūtākṣarāṇi ca ||

ekaikamāda-ūddhṛtya saṃpūrṇāśca bhavantite |
ekādaśādi uddhṛtya bhrāntāntaṃ tasya dāpayet ||

rudrādi antaṃ saṃgṛhya jhaṇṭīśenasamanvitam |
navake ṛtukoṣṭhasya hakāraṃ yasya ūrddhataḥ ||

caturthaṃ tadvijānīyāt snāyu rudrālayaṃ param |
navake nāmamadhye tu bilvamadhye vyavasthitam ||

āsanaṃ vedavarṇānāṃ kakārādi sthitāstu ye |
tṛtīyena vinā devi trimūrttiṃ trīṇi bheditā ||

sarve binduyutāḥ kāryāścatvāri akṣarāṇi ca |
paṃcamaṃ ādidātavyaṃ antaṃ biṃdu saṃyutam ||

bindumastaka yogena paṃcaite siddhidāyakāḥ |
etāni paṃcabījāni paṃcavāṇeṣu dīpakāḥ(nāḥ) ||

anenarahitā devi niṣphalāni bhavanti te |
āsanaṃ paṃcavāṇāni mūlavidyā tu dūtayaḥ ||

p. 54a)

yatkarmma atramadhye tu māraṇoccāṭanādayaḥ |
pūrvoktaṃ yanmayādevi rurubhede ca madhyataḥ ||

vajraprastāra bhedena uddhṛtā ye mayā purā |
anenaiva vidhānena saphalāni bhavanti te ||

nirbījā naiva jāyante saṃpradāyavivarjjitāḥ |
sa vighnāste samākhyātā nirbījannaiva pūjayet ||


devyuvāca


yā sā vidyā tvayā proktā trailokya kṣobha kārikā |
yasyāvidyā prasādena siddhosau makaradhvajaḥ ||

dvitīyā ca tato deva punastasya kṣayaṃkarī |
kīdṛśaṃ ca bhaveccāpaṃ vāṇāni kīdṛśāni ca ||

kīdṛśaṃ ca svaproktammūlavidyā tu kidṛśī |
sāmarthaṃ kīdṛśantasya kathaṃ caiva sa siddhyati ||

etat sarvaṃ mahādeva prasādaṃ kuru bhairava |


śrībhairava uvāca


atra madhye samutpannā vidyākāmāṅganāśanī |
aṣṭāviṃśati varṇāni kāmanirnāśanī parā ||

ṣaḍvarṇarahitā sā tu kāmasaṃjīvanī tu(pu) sā(rā) |
dhanuṣaṃ paṃca vāṇāni mūlavidyā samarpitā ||

etāni kramayogena trailokyaṃ mohayanti te |
ārādhitohaṃ pūrvaṃ tu viṣṇu putreṇa dhīmatā ||

tato mayāsutuṣṭena kāmavidyā samarpitā |
śarāṇi dhanuṣaṃ caiva mūlavidyā tu yādṛśī ||

etāni varṇabhedena maṃtrarūpā vyavasthitā |
jāpyahoma manuṣṭānaṃ mudrāpuṣpāṇi yāni ca ||

āsanaṃ vidhisāmarthyaṃ tatsarvaṃ prakaṭī kṛtam |
hemakūṭe gatā(tadā) devi gṛhītvā keśavastataḥ ||

p. 54b)

tatravarṣasahasārddhaṃ vidyārādhanatatparaḥ |
varṇalakṣapramāṇena pūrvaṃ vāṇajapaṃ kṛtam ||

kṛtaṃ homadaśāṃśena puṣpaistai parijātakaiḥ |
ghṛtenamadhusaṃyuktaṃ hutaṃ havyaṃ hutāsane ||

dvitīyaṃ ca punardevi tasyāpi ce dṛśī kriyāṃ |
varṇalakṣapramāṇena sopi japto na saṃśayaḥ ||

aśokakusumaiḥ kṣīraṃ homantasya samarpitaṃ |
jāpyaṃ kṛtaṃ caturtha(tṛtīya)sya caturllakṣapramāṇataḥ ||

karavīrakusumairaktairhutaṃ ghṛtavimiśritam |
jātīpuṣpaiścaturthaṃ ca jalamadhye japaṃ kṛtam ||

tasyāpi tādṛśī saṃkhyā paṃcamantadanantaraṃ |
sopi kṣīrasamudrasthaṃ varṇalakṣapramāṇataḥ  ||

kṛtaṃ japtaṃ vidhānena sidhyate varavarṇini |
cāpavadvartulākāraṃ latājāti samudbhavaṃ ||

siddhatasya na saṃdeho mūlavidyā japaṃ kṛtaṃ |
sma itvaṃtena saṃprāptaṃ asyāvidyāprabhāvataḥ ||

svarge martye ca pātāle bhūtagrāme caturvidhe |
dvi pade catuṣpade caiva apāde pādasaṃkule ||

adhikāraṃ mayādattaṃ kāmadevasya sundari |
praviṣṭaḥ sarva deheṣu sṛṣṭirūpāvyavasthitā ||

punaranyaṃ pravakṣyāmi uddhāraṃ yādṛśaṃ bhavet |
yādatā kāmadevasya śṛṇuṣvaikāgramānasā ||

pañcapraṇavādi uddhṛtya krodhavarṇaṃ dvitīyakaṃ |
sakārasya adhaścaiva āsanaṃ tasya dāpayet ||

dhruvamālā(nā)disaṃyuktaṃ aṃbikā tasya mūrddhagā |
lokādi śiraḥ saṃyuktaṃ dvitīyaṃ bījamuttamam ||

p. 55a)

navake vāma ūrddhe tu jukārasya adhasthitam |
nāgamadhyagataṃ devi āsanaṃ tasya dāpayet ||

trimūrtti sahitaṃ kuryā cchūladaṇḍāsanasthitam |
tṛtīyaṃ bījamityāhurbindumastakabhūṣitam ||

aṃ-ādivarṇaṃ (sa)muddhṛtya śiva(ra)sya adhaḥsaṃsthitam |
anantena samāyuktaṃ bindumastakabhūṣitam ||

arghīśasya adhaścaiva snāyu varṇorddhasaṃyutam |
āsanantasya deveśi bhṛguṃ ca bhairavaṃ kuru ||

dīpinī śaktimārūḍhaṃ kṣe-aṃtena tu bhūṣitam |
paṃcamaṃ bindusaṃyuktaṃ jīvākṛṣṭi karaṃparam ||

punastaṃ bhṛgukaṃ devi tasyāgre kevalaṃ viduḥ |
punastaṃ śūladaṇḍaṃ ca snāyu varṇāsanaṃ kuru ||

devaśukrasanoddhṛtya śūkṣmarudreṇabheditam |
śūladaṇḍasamārūḍhaṃ aṣṭamaṃ bījamuttamam ||

kakārāntaṃ samuddhṛtya kaivalyavarṇabhūṣitam |
mātarādi samārūḍhaṃ navamaṃ sṛṣṭi dāyakam ||

cāntasya(vāntaṃ) ava(tasya)saṃgṛhya bhūteṣu vedamandire |
trimūrttisahitaṃ kuryāt triśūlanteṣu agrataḥ ||

anantaṃ pūrvataḥ kṛtvā tasyānte krodhamālikhet |
krodhāntaraktamūrddhasthaṃ kakārāntaṃ niyojayet ||

tasyāgre tva ca bījaṃ ca pūrṇimāyāntatoddharet(vayaṃ) |
vidyādau paṃcabījāni ante kuryād vilomataḥ ||

dvātriṃśadakṣarī vidyā saṃbhūtā merū madhyataḥ |
eṣā vidyā parāguhyā paṃcavāṇasamanvitā ||

asyādhāraṇayogena siddhosau makaradhvajaḥ |
siddhavidyādharādaityā yakṣagandharva kinnarāḥ ||

p. 55b)

mānuṣāpannagāścaiva ye cānye sā kāḥ priye |
vidyārādhanayogena kāmadeva samāstute ||

yaṃ yaṃ spṛśati hastena yaṃ yaṃ paśyati cakṣuṣā |
saṃparkālāpayogena vihvalaṃ bhava(ja)te jagat ||

dravanti darśanāaddevi trailokye yoṣitastu yāḥ |
kṣobhayennātrasaṃdeho munīnāṃ ca mataḥ priye ||

pūjanāntena deveśi trailokyaṃ vaśagaṃ bhavet |
strīṇāṃ caiva viśeṣeṇa mahāsaṃmohakārakam ||

tiṣṭhate yasya dehasyā vidyāvāṇeṣu saṃyutā |
satataṃ japamānasya śatamaṣṭottarasya ca ||

kāmadevaḥ svayaṃ sākṣātmānuṣaṃ dehamāśritaḥ |


itipārameśvarīmate vajranityākāmadevyā uddhāre saptatriṃśatimaḥ paṭalaḥ ||




devyuvāca


pūrvoktaṃ yattvayādeva garbhayāgaṃ sudurllabhaṃ |
aputramānuṣīṇāṃ ca sutotpattiḥ kathaṃ bhavet ||

tanme brūhi mahādeva mānuṣāṇāṃ hitāya vai |


śrībhairava uvāca


yā sā vidyā mahāguhyā kāma devena sādhitā |
tenaiva vidhiyogena garbhadyāgaṃ vadāmyaham ||

sṛṣṭirbrahmamayāsāpi utpannāmerumadhyataḥ |
tamahaṃ saṃpravakṣyāmi vidhānaṃ yādṛśaṃ bhavet ||

suśuddhe bhūpradeśe ca kṛta(kṣa)valmīka varjjite |
praviśyabrāhmaṇīṃ bhūmīṃ śvetapītā caryā bhavet ||

kṣatriṇānṛpabhayāvaḥ(mityāhuḥ) pītavarṇā ca kevalā |
kiṃcidraktamayāpītā vaiśyā nāmavasundharā ||

raktakṛṣṇamayī yā tu śūdrī sā parikīrttitā |
p. 56a) yasya yasya hi varṇasya garbhayāgaṃ ca kārayet ||

tasyatasyāṃśake bhūmīṃ gṛhītvā phaladā bhavet |
anyavarṇānya bhūmīṣu sa vighnāni bhavanti ca ||

ācāryeṇa kṛte devi yajanaṃ niṣphalaṃ bhavet |
tasmādbhūmīṃ parīkṣeta yajanaṃ tu sadā kuru ||

śobhane bhūpradeśe ca hastamuddhṛtyamṛttikāṃ |
kuṭi?tavyaṃ prayatnena kṣīradārusamudbhavaiḥ ||

mudgaraiḥ śobhanaiścaiva iṣṭaṃ cūrṇena śobhitam |
aṣṭahastapramāṇena tatrasthānaṃ suśobhanaṃ ||

vedīṃ kṛtvā prayatnena kṣīradāru samudbhavam |
svetāni kuṃbhayuktāni catvāri toraṇāni ca ||

cūtapallavasaṃbhūtairmmārjjayitvā ca vedikāṃ |
tatramadhyagataṃ kuṇḍaṃ ṣaṭkoṇaṃ maṇḍalaṃ priye ||

koṇe koṇeṣu karttavyaṃ varddhanyā sahitaṃ ghaṭam |
paṃcaratnenasaṃpūrṇaṃ śuddha * *?samanvitam ||

puṣpavastrākṣatairdīpaiḥ paripūrṇāni kārayet |
māṅgalyaṃ gītavādyañca caturvedamayī dhvaniḥ ||

arasthaṃ pāvakaṃ gṛhyakṣīradāru samudbhavam |
prakṣiptaṃ kuṇḍamadhye tu indhanaṃ ca tatodbhavam ||

brahmāviṣṇuśca rudraśca īśvaraśca sadāśivaḥ |
uktaṃ manthāna bhedena amarāgndhoyaṃ mayā purā ||

kuladravyāni saṃgṛhya teṣu piṇḍasamudbhavaḥ |
ṣaṣṭitaṃ dunaprasthaṃ ca kṣatalīnaṃ sunirmmalam ||

gavāṃkṣīreṇasaṃyuktaṃ tāmrapātraṃ suśobhane |
kulācāryeṇa karttavyaṃ sādhanaṃ carukasya ca ||

mudrayā mudritaṃ kṛtvā caturdikṣuvaliṃ haret |
bhūmī kuṃbhākṣatā dīpāḥ kuṇḍāraṇihavistathā ||

p. 56b)

paṃcabhūteṣu saṃbhūtāḥ paṃcadravyāni yāni ca |
(pātraṃ udakādyāni yāni ca) mūlavidyā tu deveśi kuryāt saptābhi mantritā ||

pūjayitvā prayatnena agnisthaṃ carukaṃ kuru |
yāvat tatsidhyate devi tāvanmantraṃ manusmaret ||

āsanaṃ vāmasaṃyuktaṃ maunaṃ kuryātsuyatnataḥ |
asyamantraprasādena siddhyate nātrasaṃśayaḥ ||

avatāryasuyatnena yāvadbhavati śītalam |
ṛtukāle tu saṃprāpte caturthe paṃcame pavā ||

sugandhaiḥ śobhanairdravyaiḥ snānaṃ kṛtvā suyatnataḥ |
pīṭhe catuṣkamadhye tu upaviśya sukhāsane ||

namaskāraṃ prakurvīta bhakṣitavyaṃ prayatnataḥ |
dakṣiṇācārayogena puruṣaṃ(pumān?) bhavanirmitam(bhavati niścitam) ||

vāmekanyā samākhyātā ubhaye tu napuṃsakam |
sarveṣu dakṣiṇāndadyādvipreṣu sādhakeṣu ca ||

cāraṇeṣu kumārīṣu ye cānye liṅgadhāriṇaḥ |
ācāryaṃ pūjayed devi rājāśca ratnakāṃcanaiḥ ||

godānaṃ bhūmidānaṃ ca annadānaṃ tathaiva ca |
divyābharaṇavastrāṇi praṇipatyanivedayet ||

garbhayāgamidaṃ devi kulamārge vyavasthitam |
na deyaṃ paśulokeṣu āgama dveṣakārake ||

kaulikaṃ carukaṃ divyaṃ dīkṣitasya pradāpayet |
anyathā siddhihāniḥ syādbhavate nātrasaṃśayaḥ ||


iti pārameśvarīmategarbhayāge aṣṭatriṃśatimaḥ paṭalaḥ |




devyuvāca

pūrve mṛgāvatāre ca anuṣṭhānaṃ tvayā kṛtam |
yuṣmaddehe samutpannā yoginyaḥ ṣaḍvidhāni ca ||

p. 57a)

koṭīmekaṃ sa pādañca ugratejā mahābalāḥ |
teṣu(tāmis)tvaṃ prārthito deva ātmapiṇḍaṃ dadasva me ||

yusmatpiṇḍena bhuktena alināmiśratena ca |
sarvāstā tṛptimāyānti cakrasthāḥ krīḍayanti ca ||

tvayā uktaṃ punardeva gṛhṇagṛhṇeti bhāṣitam |
ātmarakṣākṛtā caiva vajra cakrasya madhyataḥ ||

mayāpi ī"dṛśī vārttā śrutā kailāsamadhyataḥ |
mahākrodhasamutpannā mamadehādvinirgatā ||

bhāsayantī jagatsarvaṃ dvādaśāditya sannibhā |
bhakṣayāmi na saṃdeho yoginī cakramuttamam ||

tato brahmādayo devā āgatāḥ purato mata |
stutohaṃ vividhaiḥ stotrairyoginīsaṃgharakṣitam ||

jñātvā tejaśca bījaṃ ca varṇa bhūtā tvayā kṛtaṃ |
trotalānāmavikhyātā daśadūtī kṣamanvitā ||

tasyāṅgāni ca cakrāni āsanaṃ mudrayā saha |
yuktā yuktāni karmmāṇi nigrahānugrahāṇi ca ||

saptakoṭyayutānāṃ ca pūrvavakreṇa bhāṣitā |
tatra madhye samutpannaṃ totalaṃ taṃtramuttamam ||

sa pādalakṣamānena yā vidyā tatra saṃsthitā |
kīdṛśaṃ tatra sāmarthyaṃ karmāṇi kīdṛśāni ca ||


śrībhairava uvāca


trotalaṃ ca mahātantraṃ pūrva vaktrātsamudbhavam |
atrasiddho mahādevi sūryavaṃśe prajāpatiḥ ||

tanāpi bhayabhītena tribhirmmantraistu mudritā |
ādimadhyāvasāneṣu varṇamātrādi mena hi ||

punareva tribhirmmudreruṭghāṭaṃ tasya kīdṛśaṃ |
p. 57b) mudrāstrisraḥ samākhyātā merumadhye vyavasthitā ||

pāraṃparyakrameṇaiva guruvaktrāt tu labhyate |
udghāṭarahitā vidyā siddhyate na kadācana ||

mahāvighnaṃ ca kurute sutadārārthanāśanaṃ |
vyādhiśca śokasaṃtāpa duḥkhāni vividhāni ca ||

tasmātsarvaprayatnena samyagudghāṭanaṃ kuru |
udghāṭitā mahāvidyā sa jīvāsaphalā bhavet ||

sidhyate nātrasaṃdehaścaturvarṇa(rga)phalapradā |
merumadhya gatā devi yathā bhavati tacchṛṇu ||

brahmāviṣṇuśca rudraśca īśvaraśca sadāśivaḥ |
kulācāryeṇakarttavyā guṭikāṃ śubhalakṣaṇām ||

punastāṃ mūlavidyāyā(dyayā) kuryādviṃśābhi mantritā |
pādādārabhyamūrddhāntaṃ vidyā(sthā)nāniyāni ca ||

karttavyaṃ kramayogena tilakaṃ sādhakena tu |
mahārakṣā samākhyātā trailokye sa carācare ||

vighnaṃ naivaprakurvanti nāgāvidyādharādayaḥ |
āsanaṃ ca tataḥ kuryāt siṃhaṃ ca vikṛtānanam ||

tasyoddhāraṃ pravakṣyāmi merumadhye yathāsthitam |
śivasya arddha(mukha)pūrvasthaṃ rudraṃ saṃvarttakaṃ viduḥ ||

krūrānandenasaṃyuktaṃ bījaṃ pañcānanasya ca |
saṃvarttakasya pūrveṇa dvitīyaṃ cākṣarā bhavet ||

tasya pūrve punardevi tṛtīyamakṣaraṃ bhavet |
vāme madhyagataṃ gṛhya navake snāyu bhairavam ||

caturthañca samākhyataṃ jayantī purataḥ sthitam |
bhujaṃgamāsanantasya paṃcamaṃ bījamuttamam ||

rudramadhyagataṃ gṛhya jñānaśakti vibhūṣitam |
tasyante aṃbikā devi saptamaṃ ca sadālikhet ||

p. 58a)

ṛkārādi samuddhṛtya dhruvādi āsanasthitam |
bhujaṃgañca punardevi ja ante āsane sthitam ||

punaretānivarṇāni ubhau agre niyojayet |
krodharudrodbhavaṃ varṇaṃ kṣe madhye vyavasthitam ||

trimūrttibhūṣitaṃ kuryāllokādi saṃyutaṃ kuru |
dvādaśaṃ ca mahādevi bījaṃ paṃcānanasya ca ||

venamadhyagataṃ gṛhyadhruvaṃ yasya adhaḥ sthitam |
viśvavarṇaṃ ca vikhyātaṃ tasyānte(tre) vālamadhyagaṃ ||

etadvai varṇayugalaṃ asyāgre tu punarlikhet |
tato madhyagataṃ devi khi(ā)kārasya adhaḥ sthitam ||

kevalaṃ ca samuddhṛtya bījaṃ saptadaśākṣaram |
mālinī vāyuvegā ca vāmeśikharasaṃsthitā ||

śirasi tasya dātavyaṃ bhujaṃgannātrasaṃśayaḥ |
punaretāni bījāni yugala tasya cāgrataḥ ||

prāṇava(ṇānta)rṇaṃ samuddhṛtya tasyānte purataḥ sthitam |
huṃ antaṃ ca punardevi anantenasamanvitam ||

krūravarṇaṃ śiredattamāsanaṃ samudāhṛtam |
paṃcānanaṃ samākhyātaṃ ugrabījaṃ mahābalam ||

sa dharmaṃ nārasiṃhaṃ ca viṃśadvarṇaṃ trayādhikam |
asya jāpena deveśi lakṣajāpena yadbhavet ||

ye ke cidga?jasaṃghātāstristhānamadavihvalāḥ |
tasya dṛṣṭi nipātena madahīnā bhavanti te ||

gandhena pralayaṃ yāṃti vidravanti diśodaśaḥ |
siṃhavyāghrāśca nāgāśca bhūtapretāśca rākṣasāḥ ||

asanīrvajrasaṃghātaṃ kṣudra karmāṇi yāni ca |
viṣayantraṃ(ntaṃ) na(tra) tasyāsti mahāvighnāni yāni ca ||

p. 58b)

āsanena tu siddhena mahārakṣā ca jāyate |
āsanena vinā devi mahāvighnairvibhūyate ||

athātaḥ saṃpravakṣyāmi mūlavidyā tu yādṛśī |
nikhilā vighnahīnā ca sa jīvā yādṛśī bhavet ||

prāṇavarṇaṃ samuddhṛtya sūkṣmarudreṇasaṃyutam |
lokādi śirasākrāntaṃ trimūrti saṃyutaṃ kuru ||

punastadvarṇamuddhṛtya dhruvānte āsanasthitam |
nādabiṃdu śiredattaṃ dvitīyaṃ bījamuttamam ||

bhārabhūtiradho gṛhya śikhare urddhvasaṃsthitam |
jhaṇṭīśenasamāyuktaṃ devī vaktrākṣaraṃ param ||

manomadhyagataṃ devī(gṛhya) kūrmarudrālayaṃ param |
rudrabījaṃ samākhyātaṃ kaṃṭhasthaṃ tvaritasya ca ||

siṃhamadhyagataṃ gṛhya dhruvasya adhaḥ saṃsthitam |
jñāna(kriyā)śakti śiredattaṃ hṛ(ta)dbījaṃ śaktisaṃsthitam ||

saṃvarttakaṃ samuddhṛtya sūkṣmarudreṇasaṃyutam |
nābhivarṇaṃ vijānīyāttrotalasya na saṃśayaḥ ||

bhṛguvarṇaṃ punardevisi pūrveyadvyavasthitam |
nāgamadhyāsanārūḍhaṃ ḷ ādi bheditaṃ kuru ||

ḷpūrvaṃ ca śiredattaṃ śūladaṇḍāsana sthitam |
viṣṇubījamidaṃ proktaṃ tadguhyaṃ tvaritasya ca ||

punaḥ saṃhāriṇī devī mālinī nābhimaṇḍale |
jñānaśakti samāyuktaṃ brahmabījaṃ sudurllabham ||

ūrubhyāṃ saṃsthitaṃ devi trotalasya varānane |
navakādiripūrvasthaṃ prāṇāntasyo paristhitam ||

biṃdumastakasaṃbhinnaṃ arghiśāsanasaṃsthitam |
sūryabījaṃ vijānīyājjātubhyāṃ devi saṃsthitam ||

p. 59a)

bhūtādi saṃsthitaṃ varṇaṃ vāmapāde ca(na) mālinī |
tasyāgre tu punardevi varṇaṃ someśvarasya ca ||

dhruvādikhaṇḍamārūḍhaṃ pādāni tvaritasya ca |
eṣā devi samākhyātā vidyā parama durlabhā ||

nikhilā ca samākhyātā siddhyaṃte nātrasaṃśayaḥ |


devyuvāca


pūrvaṃ deva tvayākhyātaṃ mudritātva(nta)ritā sthitā |
tena(tatra) jānāmyahaṃ nātha kīdṛśāni bhavanti te ||


śrībhairava uvāca


yo sau prajāpatiḥ siddho asyāvidyā prabhāvataḥ |
tenāpi(etāni)pūrvavaktre tu gṛhītvā trotalottaram ||

uddhṛtya tvaritā vidyā tribhirmudraistu mudritā |
tejohīnaṃ kṛtaṃ sūryaṃ mātrāhīnaṃ vihaṃgamam ||

śirevarṇavihīnaṃ ca ebhirmudraistu mudritam |
āsanasya tu deveśi tasyāpi īdṛśaṃ kṛtam ||

bījaṃ bījaṃ ca sāmarthyaṃ hutaṃ paṃcānanasya ca |
ādi madhyā vasāne tu trivarṇarahitaṃ kṛtam ||

mudritā varṇahīnādya svavarṇaphaladāyakāḥ |
udghāṭamīdṛśaṃ teṣāṃ merumadhye vyavasthitam ||

asyāpi nava(daśa)dūtīnāṃ vajratuṇḍādi saṃsthitaṃ |
vidyādi varṇasaṃyuktā nikhilāni bhavaṃti te ||

vidyāṅgāni karanyāsaṃ jāpyaṃ homaṃ tathaiva ca |
udghāṭasahitā devī sidhyate nātrasaṃśayaḥ ||

pūrvoktamantramadhye tu yatkarmaphaladāyakam |
staṃbhanaṃ kīlanaṃ caiva pararāṣṭrasya bhaṃjanam ||

vetāla sādhanaṃ caiva piśācarākṣasādayaḥ |
ghaṭaṃ kanyāvatārañca akāle meghavarṣaṇaṃ ||

p. 59b)

daityavidyādharānāṃ ca teṣu kanyā prasādhanam |
khaḍgamālājanaṃ śūlaṃ vivarāṇāṃ praveśa(sādha)nam ||

guṭikā pādukānāṃ ca parakāya praveśanam |
nāgānāṃ nāgakanyānāṃ puṣpadravyāni yāni ca ||

sidhyanti nātrasaṃdehaḥ sarvākṛṣṭikaraṃ param |
yakṣiṇī sādhanaṃ caiva khecaratvaṃ na saṃśayaḥ ||

bhūpateḥ sādhanañcaiva adṛśya(mudrisaṃ)padamuttamam |
tilakaṃ pādalepaṃ ca yoginīnāñca melakam ||

bhavedvidyādharāṇāñca cakravarttirnasaṃśayaḥ |
bhavet saubhāgyamatulaṃ tvaritā yasya dehagā ||

paṃcopacārasahitaṃ śuddhabhaktisamanvitam |
rājapatnī viśeṣeṇa yā tu pūjayate imāṃ ||

śataṃ sahasralakṣaṃ tu nārīṇāmuttamā bhavet |
puruṣopi labhedrājyaṃ pūjanena na saṃśayaḥ ||

eṣā vidyā mayākhyātā udghāṭasahitā priye |
asyāvidyā prasādena sarvakāmārthasādhakam ||

navalakṣeṇajaptena homena sādhitena ca |
asyāvidyāḥ prasādena vidyādharapatirbhavet ||


iti pārameśvarīmate tvaritānikhiloddhāra ekonacatvāriṃśatimaḥ paṭalaḥ ||




devyuvāca

tvayā pūrvaṃ pratijñātaṃ aghoraṃ maṃtramuttamam |
kasmin sthāne samutpannaṃ sāmarthyaṃ tasya kīdṛśaṃ ||


śrībhairava uvāca 


kathayāmi na saṃdeho yattvayāparipṛcchitam |
dvātriṃśadakṣaropetaṃ aghoraṃ nāmanāmataḥ ||

rurubhede mahātantre siddhayogeśvarī mate |
svacchandalalite ghore mārttaṇḍe śivasādhane ||

p. 60a)

trikhaṇḍe ca niśācāre yoginījālasaṃvare |
pracaṇḍa(pañca)muṇḍamālāyāṃ matsyodaryāṃ mahārṇave ||

eteṣu sarvatantreṣu jīvahīnaṃ prakāśitam |
śaktiprāṇavinirmmuktaḥ paṃcabījavivarjjitaḥ ||

sāmarthyarahito mantraḥ sidhyate na kadācana |


devyuvāca


yadā na siddhyate mantraṃ pratyayo naiva jāyate |
devadānavasiddhānāṃ bhrāmite kiṃ prayojanam ||


śrībhairava uvāca


yatpṛṣṭhaṃ kathayiṣyāmi bhrāntinirnāśanaṃ param |
aghorasya prasādena daityadānavarākṣasāḥ ||

jāpyena divyahomena te ca siddhāmahābalāḥ |
divya astradharādaityā divya yānairvyavasthitāḥ ||

indrādyā devatāḥ sarve kṛtāste hīnaśaktayaḥ |
svarge martye ca pātāle pratimallona dṛśyate ||

tatsarvaṃ(darthaṃ) ca mayā mantraṃ varṇakīlaistu kīlitaṃ |
hṛ(kṛ)tāni paṃca bījāni bhakāreṇa tu pūritam ||

dvātriṃśadakṣaraṃ mantraṃ bījahīnaṃ prakāśitam |
bhakārasahitaṃ mantraṃ namaste madhyasaṃsthitam ||

aṣṭakīlāmayādatatte nāsau naiva sidhyati |


devyuvāca


aghoraṃ ca mahāmantraṃ nikhilaṃ siddhidāyakam |
prasādaṃ kurudeveśa yadituṣṭosi me prabho ||


śrībhairava uvāca


sādhusādhu mahāprājñe sādhakānāṃ ca vatsale |
kathayāmi na saṃdeho yanmayā gopitaṃ priye ||

susame bhūpradeśe ca nimnonnata vivarjjite |
p. 60b) puṣpaprakaraśobhāḍhye gaṃdhadhūpādi vāsite ||

tatra sthāne śubhe ramye kuṃbhaṃ vārisamanvitam |
paṃcaratnenasaṃpūrṇaṃ dīpākṣatasamanvitam ||

śvetacandanaliptāṃgaṃ śvetavastrāvaguṇṭhitam |
caturdikṣu imāṃ kṛtvā madhye meruṃ samālikhet ||

paṃcopacārasaṃyuktaṃ āsavaṃ phalgunā saha |
pūjayitvā vidhānena uddharen mantramuttamam ||

kailāsaśikharāsīnaṃ ādau śrīkaṇṭhasaṃsthitam |
tasyāgre viśvamālāyāṃ dvitīyaṃ bījamuddharet ||

dakṣavāhusamudbhūtaṃ śivasya kaṃkaṇaṃ likhet |
sadyojātenasaṃyuktamakārapūrataḥ sthitam ||

śūladaṇḍaṃ tṛtīyaṃ ca navake ādi saṃsthitam |
jhaṇṭīśenasamāyuktaṃ tṛtīyaṃ bījamuttamam ||

bhūtīśaṃ varṇamuddhṛtya akrūreṇa samanvitam |
tasya bīja prabhāvena sṛṣṭirūpapitāmahaḥ ||

asyāgre grahamālāyāmaṣṭamena(saptame yad) vyavasthitam |
paṃcamaṃ tadvijānīyānmālinīṃ āsanodbhavam ||

ṛ-aṃtaṃ tasya dātavyaṃ puratastasya kaṃkaṇam |
sadyojātenasaṃyuktaṃ bhujaṃgaṃ tasya agrataḥ ||

ṛpūrveṇa śirobhinnaṃ saptamaṃ bījamuttamam |
aṃbikā bījamuddhṛtya navādi āsanasthitam ||

lokādi śirasākrāntaṃ trimūrttiṃ bheditaṃ kuru |
viṣṇubījaṃ vijānīyāt sarvasiddhiprakāśakam ||

p. 61a)

asya mantraṃ dvitīyaṃ ca bhujaṅgaṃ ca ataḥ paraṃ |
ubhau etāni bījāni puratastasya dāpayet ||

bhāga(va)medhyaṃ samuddhṛtya āṣāḍhī rudramuttamam |
ṛpūrveṇa samāyuktaṃ navādi agratasthitam ||

ṇādhavarṇaṃ(sohyādivarṇa) samuddhṛtyaṃ va(re)antaṃ yadvyavasthitam |
saṃvakaṃ rudramityuktaṃ asthimadhye vyavasthitam ||

navādi āsanaṃ dadyāt jihvāśakti prabheditam |
aṃkureṇaśirobhinnaṃ lakṣmī bījaṃ prakīrtitam ||

āsya jāpyena homena alakṣmīrnaivatiṣṭhati |
icchāśaktiṃ vijānīyādīpsitaṃ phalamāpnuyāt ||

punaḥ saṃvakamuddhṛtya caturtha ṛṣi āsane |
mālinī netramityāhustṛtīyaṃ yadvyavasthitam ||

ṣoḍaśaṃ ca samākhyātaṃ netraṃ kūrmmasamudbhavam |
ekādaśe caturthañca rudraṃ śukrasamudbhavam ||

bhṛguṃ nāmavijānīyāt ṣoḍaśānte samālikhet |
bhūtamadhya caturthañca rudraṃ pṛṣṭhasamudbhavam ||

navādiśirasākrāntaṃ āṣāḍhī tasya agrataḥ |
ḹ(ṛ) antena samāyuktaṃ tasyāgre saṃvakaṃ bhavet ||

viṃśavatadvijānīyānmaṃtra(dānta) śāntaṃ(treṇānte) punarllikhet |
punarbhṛgodbhavaṃ varṇaṃ tasya pūrvaṃ punarlikhet ||

jhaṇṭīśena samāyuktaṃ akṣaraṃ paramaṃ viduḥ |
vāmāṅgamālinī likhya bhūtādi yadvyavasthitam ||

śikhivāhī vijānīyād rudraṃ kukṣisamudbhavam |
śūladaṇḍāsanārūḍhaṃ ṛ-ādimūrddhnisaṃsthitam ||

aṃkrureṇa samāyuktaṃ cāmuṇḍā hṛdayaṃ param |

p. 61b)

taccaghorataraṃ bījaṃ jitakālānalaprabham |
asyasmaraṇamātreṇa brahmāṇaṃ parikaṃpati ||

raktabhairavamuddhṛtya atitheścaiva paścimam |
tasyamūrddhni pradātavyaṃ dhātrīśaṃ rudramagrataḥ ||

sopi raktasamārūḍhaṃ punaraktaṃ samālikhet |
dhruvāntamāsanasthaṃ(nta) ca(sya) saptaviṃśatimaṃ viduḥ ||

lohitaṃ rudramālāyāṃ navame yadvyavasthitam |
jhaṇṭīśena samāyuktammālinī hṛdayaṃ param ||

ekādaśe caturthaṃ ca śū(mū)ladaṇḍāsanasthitam |
cchāntaṃ mūrddhnipradātavyaṃ rudrānugrahabheditam ||

krūreṇaśirasākrāntaṃ bījaṃ caiva parāparam |
yasyoccāratāmātreṇa trikālaṃ ca divāniśi ||

viṃśajjanmakṛtaṃ pāpaṃ bhasmamāyāti tatkṣaṇāt |
saptamaṃ rudramālāyāṃ mālinī mūrddhnisaṃsthitam ||

kevalantaṃ vijānīyādvaṃśaṃ viṃśadaśākṣaram |
ṛṣimālā tṛtīyaṃ ca nitaṃvamālinīṃ viduḥ ||

rudraṃ hṛdayasaṃbhūtaṃ mahākālantadakṣaram |
ekādaśe caturthañca bhairavaṃ śukrasambhavam ||

nāgamadhyāsanārūḍhaṃ jhaṇṭīśaṃ mūrddhnidāpayet |
etanmantravaraṃ guhyaṃ dvātriṃśadakṣaraṃ bhavet ||

aghoraṃ ca samākhyātaṃ siddhisānnidhyakārakam |
svarge martye ca pātāle ye maṃtrā divyarūpiṇaḥ ||

tānyevasaptakoṭīnāṃ ayaṃ rājāsureśvari |
dṛśyate yasya pārśve tu catuḥśṛṃgo mahānagaḥ ||

uddhāraṃ mantra rājasya nāsta(deyasre)ki mataḥ param |
maṇirantaiśca saṃpūrṇamāsamudrāntamedinīṃ ||

p. 62a)

dakṣiṇāṃ guravedadyānnatasya nir-ṛṇī bhavet |
tathāpi saṃvṛ(bhṛ)tācārā gurupūjā prayatnataḥ ||

godānaṃ bhūmidānaṃ ca annadānantathaiva ca |
divyābharaṇavastrāṇi praṇipatyanivedayet ||

pūjayitvā prayatnena ājñāprārthayate tataḥ |
ājñālabdhena deveśi kṛtārthaṃ manyate tanuṃ ||

ādeśaṃ guruto labdhā tato yajanamārabhet |
saṃśodhyabhūmiṃ yatnena sarvalakṣaṇasaṃyutāṃ ||

āśramantatra kurvīta kṣetramālaṃbhanaṃ kuru |
aśvasthakīlakaṃ pūrvaṃ hastamātraṃ pramāṇataḥ ||

aṣṭottara śataṃjaptvā dūtī mantreṇasādhakaḥ |
nikhanedbhūmi madhye tu agnidūtīṃ tatorddhvataḥ ||

sopi tena vidhānena kīlakaḥ khadirodbhavaḥ |
dakṣiṇe devadāruṃ ca nair-ṛte tu vibhīttakam ||

auḍumbaraṃ tu vāruṇyāṃ vāyavye śiśayodbhavam |
uttarevaṭasaṃbhhutamaiśānyāṃ bilvasaṃbhavam ||

mahāpūjāprakurvīta sādhako mantra tatparaḥ |
paṃcamudrādharo vīro rakta kṛṣṇāmvarastathā ||

siṃhacarmāsane cāpi abhāve mṛgacarma ca |
bhasmasnānaṃ prakurvīta mantrasnānaṃ tatorddhvataḥ ||

kṛtvāyajñopavītāni darbhakārpāśakeśajāṃ |
aṃganyāsaṃprakurvīta mūlamantreṇasādhakaḥ ||

ekoccārṃ kareśuddhiḥ parasparavimārjitau |
hṛdaye ekamuccāramekenaivaśiro bhavet ||

śikhā ekena ityāhurekaṃ kavacasaṃsthitam |
trinetramekamuccāraṃ ṣadvidhaṃ maṃtrasaṃsthitam ||

p. 62b)

aṅganyāsaṃprakurvīta jātpaṅgasahitaṃ priye |
pūjitavyaṃ mahādevi pūrvaṃ gurumukhācchrutam ||

kuṃkumaṃ candanaṃ caiva gorocanamadhu tathā |
etāni sarvadravyāṇi raktena sahitāni ca ||

caturasraṃ maṇḍalaṃ kuryānmadhye padmaṃ samālikhet |
mantrādau vedavarṇāni avarṇa sahitāni ca ||

trayānte ca trayānte ca trayānte ca punaḥ punaḥ |
mantrabīja prabhedena vidarbhenasamuccaret ||

brahmendrasahitādūtī avatara-avatara namaḥ |
anena vidhinā devi pūrve diśi prapūjayet ||

punaścatvāri varṇāni kavargena vidarbhitāḥ |
māheśvarī agni dūti ca avatara-avatara namaḥ ||

āgneyāṃ diśi pūjayet |

ghore ghoreti varṇāni ca vargena vidarbhitāḥ |
kaumārī yamadūtī ca avatara-avatara namaḥ ||

dakṣiṇā diśi pūjayet |

punaścatvāri varṇāni ṭavargeṇa vidarbhitāḥ |
vaiṣṇavī rākṣī dūtī avatara-avatara namaḥ ||

nair-ṛtyāṃ diśi pūjayet |

paṃcame caturthavarṇāni tavargeṇa vidarbhitāḥ |
vārāhī vāruṇī dūtī avatara avatara namaḥ ||

paścimaṃ diśi pūjayet |

punaścatvāri varṇāni pavargeṇa vidarbhitāḥ |

aindrī vāyu dūtī ca avatara avatara namaḥ ||

vāyavyāṃ diśi pūjayet |

saptame caturthavarṇāni yavargena vidarbhitāḥ |
caṇḍikā yakṣa dūtī ca avatara avatara namaḥ ||

uttara diśi pūjayet |

ante catvāri varṇāni savargena vidarbhitāḥ |

p. 63a)

īśāne ca mahālakṣmī avatara avatara namaḥ |

īśānyāṃ diśi pūjayet |

madhye tu pūjayed devaṃ dvātriṃśadakṣaraṃ param |
gurupaṃktiṃ tataḥ pūjya kṣetrapālaṃ tatorddhvataḥ ||

puṣpaṃ dhūpaṃ ca naivedyaṃ āsavaṃ phalgukaṃ tataḥ |
sarvaṃ saṃpūrṇataḥ kṛtvā mudrayā mudritaṃ kuru ||

padmamudrākarālī ca bhairavī vikṛtā nanā |
mudrābandhaṃ prakurvīta paścājjāpyaṃ samārabhet ||

vidhānaṃ saṃpravakṣyāmi yanmayā gopitaṃ priye |
yā sā śaktirmahāmāyā kuṇḍālākhyā maheśvari ||

tatsthānaṃ asya mantrasya ekībhūtaṃ vicintayet |
tamuccāryakrameṇaiva dvādaśādityasannibhaṃ ||

bhāsayantaṃ svatejejana āpādatalamastakaṃ |
nābhibhedakrameṇaiva hṛdaye caiva tiṣṭhati ||

punastaṃ viṣṇumārgeṇa rudrabhedakrameṇa tu |
māheśamāsana(mīśasaṃ)sthānaṃ layaṃ kṛtvā punaḥ punaḥ ||

anenaiva prakāreṇa uccāraṃ vidhipūrvakam |
punaścaiva vilomeṇa uccaritvā praveśayet ||

tenaiva mārgayogena svasthānasahitaṃ kuru |
sakṛduccārametat tu nava devi prakāśitam ||

sphaṭikaṃ mauktikaṃ vātha maṇikaṃ putrajīvakam |
mahāśaṃkhodbhavaṃ caiva atharudrākṣasaṃbhavam ||

akṣamālāṃ prakurvīta aṃgulyā nāmikopari |
cārayenmerumārgeṇa yathā bhūmau na gacchati ||

japānte carukaṃ prāśya mantrāgre yadvyavasthitam |
yaccheṣaṃ samayīnāñca dātavyaṃ athavāgniṣu ||

p. 63b) brahmacārī jitakrodhaḥ paśusamparkavarjitaḥ |
dinānte bhojanaṃ kuryādbhūmiśāyī jitendriyaḥ ||

daśalakṣakṛtaṃ jāpyaṃ homaṃ caiva samārabhet |
śivaśaktisamudbhūtaṃ ṣaṭkoṇaṃ śobhanaṃ priye ||

hastamātrapramāṇena ānanaṃ cāpi kārayet |
araṇistha pāvakaṃ gṛhya bhūtadārusamudbhavam ||

tasya madhyasthitaṃ maṃtraṃ aghoraṃ ghorarūpiṇaṃ |
mahāsya mahika(siva)rṇañca tālupātāladāruṇaṃ ||

candrasūryāgnisaṃbhūtaṃ trinetrāni vicintayet |
paṃcamīcandrarūpeṇa daṃṣṭreṇi bhāṣayanti ca (saṃbhāvayet punaḥ) ||

lelihānaṃ mahājihvaṃ ūrddhavaktraṃ vicintayet |
viśantī tasya madhye tu āhutīṃ ca vicintayet ||

mudrāmṛgamukhī caiva gṛhītānena āhutīḥ |
ekoccāreṇa kartavyaṃ praviśabhairavānane ||

tato homaṃ daśāṃśena niścale nāntarātmanā |
tataḥ siddhiṃ vijānīyādyojayet sarvakarmasu ||

aṣṭapatre śubhe padme ekaikaṃ ca samālikhet |
mantraṃ catvārivarṇani ekaikasya pṛthak pṛthak ||

sthāpitavyaḥ prayatnena indrādyā īśagocare |
yasya yasya svavargasya tānyeva hi samālikhet ||

pūrvavat prathamaṃ gṛhya uttarasya dvitīyakaṃ |
tṛtīyamagnidigbhāge dvau pūrvaṃ varuṇasya ca ||

rākṣase pūrvamantastha me tanmantraṃ ṣaḍakṣaram |
śiṃśapādārusaṃyuktaṃ nṛmāṃsamadhyasaṃsthitam ||

homayed ghṛtasaṃyuktaṃ sahasramaṣṭasaṃyutam |
uttiṣṭhati na saṃdeho vimānamagni madhyataḥ ||

p. 64a)

tatrārūḍho mahāvīro bhuvanāni caturdaśa |
saptabrahmāṇḍaparyantaṃ bhramate nātrasaṃśayaḥ ||

yā dūtīpūrvadigbhāge ṣoḍaśākṣara saṃyutā |
homayedbrahmapuṣpāṇi brahmavaktrenasaṃyutā ||

svāhāntaṃ kuru uddhāraṃ brahmāstraṃ caiva dehi me |
aṣṭottarasahasreṇa dīptānalasutejasaṃ ||

uttiṣṭetkuṇḍamadhye tu sādhakasya kare sthitam |
brahmādyā devatāḥ sarvābhuvanāni caturdaśa ||

mārayennātrasandeho etanmantraṃ sudurjayaṃ |
yā sthitā agni digbhāge māheśī ca navākṣarī ||

lāṃgalī puṣpamādāya meṣaraktenasaṃyutam |
pāvakāstraṃ samuccārya dehi dehī vibhāṣayet ||

aṣṭottarasahasreṇa sidhyate nātrasaṃśayaḥ |
kālānala samaṃ tejaṃ duḥsahaḥ sarvadaivataiḥ ||

nirdahetsādhakaḥ kruddho bhuvanāni caturdaśaḥ |
yā sthitā yāmyadigbhāge kaumārī nāmanāmataḥ ||

kuṃkumasya prayogena māhiṣaṃ raktasaṃyutam |
aṣṭottarasahasraṃ tu homaye kuṇḍamadhyataḥ ||

svāhānte śaktiruccārya dehi me śakti ṣaṇmukhe |
uttiṣṭhati na saṃdeho amoghā?śaktidurjayā ||

yasya kruddho mahāvīro mārayennātrasaṃśayaḥ |
vibhītakasya patrāṇi ulūka(jalaukā)raktasaṃyutam ||

vaiṣṇavīdūtiyogena sahasreṇa hutena ca |
homānte dṛśyate devi candrahāsaṃ sudurlabham ||

tena khaḍgagṛhītena pratimallona dṛśyate |
aṣṭottarasahasrantu samidhāṃ varuṇodbhavāṃ ||

mīnasnehasamāyuktaṃ vārāhī mantramuccaret |
p. 64b) svāhānte prathamaṃ kuryād varuṇāstraṃ tu dehi me ||

dṛśyate kuṇḍamadhye tu sopi saṃgṛhyapāṇinā |
yadīcchetsādhako devi mahāvṛṣṭistu jāyate ||

punarvāyavyadigbhāge indrāṇī nāmanāmataḥ |
vajrīpatrasya homena tasyaiva kṣīrasaṃyutam ||

aṣṭottarasahastreṇa svāhānte homitena ca |
vajraṃ ca labhate vīro asyā devyā prabhāvataḥ ||

kruddha ca mārayet kṣipraṃ yadiśakrasamo bhavet |
uttare caṇḍikā devī aṣṭavarṇā vyavasthitā ||

gṛhītvā vaṭapatrāṇi śvānaśoṇitasaṃyutam |
aṣṭottarasahasreṇa gadāsiddhirbhaviṣyati ||

anantaṃ labhate vittaṃ nidhānaṃ kuṇḍamadhyataḥ |
akṣayaṃ tadvijānīyādīśānyāñca dyutā śṛṇu ||

aṣṭottarasahasraṃ tu bilvamādāyasādhakaḥ |
ghṛtayogena hotavyaṃ niścalenāntarātmanā ||

homānte dṛśyate devi śūlaṃ tridaśadurjayam |
mārayennātrasaṃdeho yadi brahmāsvayaṃ hariḥ ||

viparīta prayogena pūrvadūtī yadā hunet |
devadārusamudbhūtamaśvaraktenasaṃyutam ||

homayedvidhisaṃyogāt sahasrāṇi caturdaśaḥ |
rathaṃ me dehi uccāarya mantrānte sādhakottamaḥ ||

uttiṣṭhati na saṃdeho nandighoṣo mahārathaḥ |
śvetāśvaṃ śvetasaṃyuktaṃ patākā sahitaṃ priye ||

tatrāruhyamahāvīro utpatte nātrasaṃśayaḥ |
ajeyaḥ sarvalokānāṃ hartā kartā svayaṃ prabhuḥ ||
