viparītoccārayogena gugguluṃ ghṛtasaṃyutam |
homamaṣṭasahasraṃ tu kurute sādhakeśvaraḥ ||

p. 65a)

paṭaṃ pāvaka saṃbhūtaṃ labhate nātrasaṃśayaḥ |
punastre naivamantreṇa viparītaṃ varānane ||

sādhitāyamadūtī ca gopālena hutena tu |
paṭamadṛśya daṇḍaṃ ca labhate nātrasaṃśayaḥ ||

vaiṣṇavī ca vilomena nṛmāṃsaṃ ghṛtasaṃyutam |
aṣṭādaśasahasreṇa rākṣasīṃ vidhimāpnuyāt ||

māyā prapaṃca rūpāṇi rupāṇi ca na saṃśayaḥ(kurute sādhakeśvaraḥ) |
viparītena yogena yā dūtī paścimena ca ||

matsyamāṃsasya homenasahasreṇa varānane |
sarvākṛṣṭiṃ vijānīyāllabate nātrasaṃśayaḥ ||

indrāṇī vāyudigbhāge gavāṃkṣīreṇasaṃyutam |
homayet mṛgamāṃsaṃ ca sahasraṃ ca śatādhikam ||

guṭikāsidddhiṃ jānīyāllabhate nātrasaṃśayaḥ |
uttare yasya(kṣa) dūtī ca vilomenasamuccaret ||

gavājyena(gavāṃ sāreṇa) samāyuktaṃ vaṭapatrāṇi homayet |
akṣayaṃ labhate vittaṃ tasya īśāna bhāṣitam ||

mahālakṣmī vilomena bilvapatrājyasaṃyutam |
aṣṭottarasahasreṇa vīra(rasa)siddhimavāpnuyāt ||

etatkarma aghorasya uktaṃ ca parameśvari |
yadicchetsādhayetkarma(sarva) asyamaṃtra prabhāvataḥ ||

(yadi meruścaletsthānāt sāgaraṃ vā mahīśrayet |
sūyañcetpatito bhūmau etānmithyāma * *?ve ||

satyaṃ satyaṃ maheśāni nā dūnaṃ kathitaṃ mayā || iti pustakāntaredhikaḥ 
pāṭhaḥ| etāvānbhāgaḥ prācīna tāḍapatra pustakāt | kṣara likhitvāduddhṛtaḥ ? 
itaduttaraḥ ṣaṭpañcāśatpaṭalāntoya ca 1488 śabdālekhitā?nmadhyadeśī * * * * 
* * * * * * * *? pustakāntarā duddhṛtya * * *? | tasminpustakāntare puṣpikāsu 
kvacit pārameśvarīmate * *?tkubjikāmate ityu * *? dṛśyate)

iti pārameśvarīmate navakoṭisaṃhitāyāṃ caturāśītisāhasre 
aghoranirṇayonāmacatvāriṃśatimaḥ paṭalaḥ ||




p. 65b)

śrīdevyuvāca 


bhagavansarvamākhyātaṃ merumadhye yathāsthitaṃ |
uddhāraṃ sarva vidyānāṃ nāḍīsaṃcārabhedataḥ ||

vajraprastārikā vidyā brahmavidyā tathaiva ca |
dvātriṃśadvarṇa bhedena purāproktā tvayā nagha ||

yā vidyā bahudhā taṃtre pūjāmādātkulakrame |
vratasyoddhāra mātreṇa yathāpāpaiḥ pramucyate ||

uddhārakramayogena pūjādhyāna krameṇā tu |
varṇasaṃkhyā vrataṃ kaścit kathaṃ homaṃ ca tarpaṇaṃ ||

nyāsaṃ ca kīdṛśaṃ dayāt sādhanaṃ vā kathaṃ bhavet |
dravyaṃ ca kīdṛśaṃ caiva adhikāri viśeṣataḥ ||

ayagūḍhataraṃ deva prayuktaṃ yat sureśvara |
saṃjñātaṃ ca sureṃdrāṇāṃ tatsarvaṃ ca samāsataḥ ||

kṛpā parama kalyāṇa yadi me prītirūttamā |
asti cetkathayasvātra merumadhye sureśvara ||


īśvara uvāca 


yanna kasya cidākhyātaṃ devi gopyaṃ tathāpi te |
kathayāmi yadā vidyā sāmarthyaṃ vasusādhanaṃ ||

viṣṇanāthapurā devi daityendra vijayāya ca |
ārādhitastu śakreṇa tuṣṭo loka pitāmahaḥ ||

mayāpi vargaśo vidyā tvāṃśapeśvara sundari |
jānannapi mahādevi pṛchase ca punaḥ punaḥ ||

tathāpi kathayiṣyāmi sāvadhānaṃ kuruṣvatha |
susame(kānte)sthā kule dhūpadīpagaṃdhā sa vādibhiḥ ||

saṃbhṛte saṃbhṛtācāro paśudṛṣṭi vivarjitaḥ |
svayaṃ dīkṣāṃ gurorlabdhvā nadīparvatabhūgṛhe ||

sthitvaikaṃ vinyasennyāsaṃ maṃtranyāsaṃ tathaiva ca |
uddhareddhi vimuktātmā ekaṃ madhyagataṃ kramam ||

dvātriṃśadakṣaraṃ devaṃ kāryakāraṇa rūpataḥ |
p. 66a) dhruvaṃ nābhigataṃ vidyādādivarṇaṃ sureśvari ||

vāma madhye tu kukṣaṃtaṃ nivṛtti kṣatrasaṃsthitaṃ |
ḷ ādivarṇayogena dvitīyaṃ bījamuttamam ||

ekādaśe ṛṣisthāne aṃbikāprāṇasaṃsthitā |
akrūraṃ ca śivedattaṃ sādhimadhyagataṃ punaḥ ||

maṃdare e(ḷ) aṃtasthaṃ caturthaṃ varṇamuttamam |
bhāgamadhyagataṃ devi tāvaṃke yatrasaṃsthitā ||

pāvanī hṛdayaṃ cātha narake rudramaṃdire |
dhruvādi varṇayogena ṣaṣṭaṃ bījaṃ sureśvaram ||

narake ādivarṇaṃ na? mohinī bhūṣitaṃ kuru |
laṃbodarī śvetagehe sāmānī gaṃdhamādane ||

mātṛvarṇaṃ vijānīyādyāsāṃ madhyaṃ gururbhavet |
kāra madhye tu deveśi sūkṣmaṃ bhairavabheditaṃ ||

daśamaṃtraṃ vijānīyātkāsamadhyaṃ tu kālayuk |
ekādaśe ṛṣisthāne jhaṃṭīsena vibheditam ||

ravisaṃkhyākṣaraṃ devi māyābījaṃ trayodaśam |
kālāmoḍī gataṃ kṛtvā aṃbikā ca tathā bhavet ||

kāramadhye tu deveśi jhaṃṭīsenasamanvitam |
vajriṇī aṃbikādhasthā danantaṃ gaṃdhamādane ||

yāmamadhyagataṃ devi aṣṭādaśamudāhṛtam |
dhenu madhyaṃ tu saṃgṛhya maṃdare sūkṣmasaṃyutam ||

kālonāma mahārudro se? ca madhye vyavasthitaḥ |
ekādaśe ṛṣisthāne maṃdare sūkṣmasaṃyutam ||

ekaviṃśaṃ vijānīyādvāviṃśaṃ vādhamārabhet |
agamadhyaṃ kevalaṃ tu trayoviṃśākṣaraṃ punaḥ ||

palayormadhyagataṃ devi rudre tu vasumaṃdire |
caturviṃśākṣaraṃ bhadre tato rudrapadaṃ bhavet ||

bhūtādi raktasaṃsthaṃ ca pūrvamūrddhani saṃsthitam |
p. 66b) biṃdubhairavasaṃbhinnaṃ etatkālānalaprabham ||

aṣṭāviṃśākṣaraṃ devi ata ūrddhaṃ ca tacchṛṇu |
he he tasthādhavarṇaṃ ca śūladaṃḍāsanasthitam ||

kūrmonāma mahārudraḥ sadyonāthavibhūṣitaḥ |
ahamadhyaṃ kevalaṃ vadhātrīṃśo bhairavasmṛtaḥ ||

yāmamadhyagataṃ sūkṣmaṃ pālīśaṃ nāma bhairavam |
yugamadhyagataṃ kuryādanaṃtākrāṃtamaṣṭakam ||

aṃbikājīvamadhyasthaṃ kṣe aṃtaṃ svargabhūṣitam |
dvātriṃśadakṣaraṃ devamevaṃ varṇairvyavasthitam ||

svarge martye tathā devi pātāle pannagālaye |
nāradaścāṃgirāścaiva bharadvājotha gautamaḥ ||

viśvāmitra vaśiṣṭhādyā ye cānye devadānavāḥ |
asya vidyā prabhāvena caṃdrasūryādayo grahāḥ ||

jagatāṃ sthita ye devi saṃcaraṃti divāniśi |
makaradhvajopi deveśi ārādhya parameśvaram ||

sarvasaubhāgyasaṃpanne sva? bhavatsurasattamaḥ |
ṣaḍaṃganyāsayogena varṇanyāsakrameṇa ca ||

śuddhātmane bhavedyānaṃ taddhinyāsādi bhirbhavet |
nyāsaṃgurumukhe naiva jñātavyaṃ varavarṇini ||

ājñāguruprasādena siddhyate nātrasaṃśayaḥ |
vinyasya vidhivadbhūyo dhyāyenmūrtiṃ ca ciṃtayet ||

kuṃdasiṃdūravarṇābhā vāgaiśvaryapradāṃ smaret |
yāstā ekadhanurvāṇa pāśāṃkuśavarābhayaiḥ ||

bījapūraṃ tathārukṣe vāme pūrṇakapālakaṃ |
brahmāviṣṇuśca rudraśca īśvaraśca sadāśivaḥ ||

vaktraiḥ pītāruṇasyāmaśvetāṃjananibhāḥ priye |
caturddhāreṣu dvau dvau ca hetukādīn prakalpayet ||

p. 67a)

āṇavo raktena śāktena śāṃbhavenātha cākhilaiḥ |
samayā sādhakā cāryanirvāṇe kṛtadīkṣayā ||

śāṃbhavī cinmayī vidyā śaktiḥ syātriguṇātmikā |
gurūktamānavīdīkṣā karmmāneka vidhāstviha ||

āvāhyamaṃḍale devaṃ arghaṃ dadyāt sureśvari |
kṣīreṇa brahmaṇāṃstāvadājyena pṛthivī bhujaḥ ||

vaiśyāṃstu madhunā śūdrāṃstvaikṣavenā savena ca |
nitya pūjābhavenmukhyā yā ca parvaṇi parvaṇi ||

athavā mā?sikī pūjā nāto vicchedamarhati |
yodbhavo madhu hetuḥ syādāsavaḥ parikīrttitaḥ ||

caturdravyaṃ samāsādya caturbhirādhakā(savā)dibhiḥ |
mumūkṣūṇāṃ phalādhikyaṃ alpālpavṛtti pūjanāt ||

adhikā vṛttipūjāyāṃ ubhukṣoradhikaṃ phalam |
sarvāvṛttaṃtikāsāhīṃ pūjā syāt parameśvari ||

evametanmahāmaṃtraṃ bhukti mukti pradaṃ nṛṇāṃ |
athānyaṃ saṃpravakṣyāmi atraprāsaṃgikā gatam ||

idvāruktaṃ gamomamaṃkhamukhyalajasya savidedhiravadavidadhehemavidadhe |

dhruvādi varṇaṃ deveśi bhāgamadhya dvayaṃ punaḥ |
sūkṣmabhairavasaṃbhinnaṃ kā * * * * saṃ bhavet ||

navarudramālāyāṃ dhruvādyāsanasaṃsthitā |
navake ādivarṇaṃ tu dhruvādyāsanasaṃyutaṃ ||

śveto nāmamahārudro māyādevī svataḥ * * ||

jhaṃṭīśenānavodbhinnaṃ vāruṇaṃ sūkṣma bheditam |
krodhīnaṃ gulphaśaktyā ca viśvayuk navamaṃ smṛtam ||

sāsimadhyagataṃ devi saddhāsaṃ nāmanāmataḥ |
vārimadhyagataṃ binnaṃ lohitaṃ sūkṣmabheditam ||

laṃbodarī mahādevī guhyaśaktyā vibhūṣitā |
ravārto yadiśabdaḥ syādvako nāmaṃtvanaṃtaram ||

p. 67b)

krodhavarṇaṃ punardevi vidhāya vegāsanasthitaḥ |
mahākālaṃ kevalaṃ ca vakaṃ cāṣṭadaśaṃ bhavet ||

punaḥ krodhaṃ samuddhṛtya iṃduyuk bindusaṃbhavam |
dadhamanyaṃ mahādevi śive akrūra bhūṣitam ||

bhāgamadhyasthitaṃ varṇaṃ kevalaṃ tu punaḥ punaḥ |
bhṛgubhairava varṇaṃ ca dvāviṃśaṃ nātrasaṃśayaḥ ||

vārimadhyagataṃ devi vajriṇī akṣasaṃsthitā |
śive tu dīpanī yuktaṃ biṃdubhedita mastakam ||

vāruṇaṃ aṃśakaṃ devi kevalaṃ tu punarbhavet |
kālo nāma māhārudroṃ prāṃśukastavabhūṣitaḥ ||

gurormadhye kevalaṃ tu yāma madhye tathā punaḥ |
vahnijāyā samāyuktā dvātriṃśākṣarabhūṣitā ||

vidyāparamakalpāṇi sarvakāmaphalapradā |
imāmevapurā bodhya vidyāṃ trailokya mohinīṃ ||

tārakaṃ nāma daityeṃdraṃ jitavān rudradhvajaḥ |
lakṣajāpena deveśi sidhyate nātrasaṃśayaḥ ||

brahmaguhasamiddhomāñkṣīrājya madhuvarddhanaiḥ |
sarvakāmeṣu deveśi sarvakāmātvamāgatā ||

hāsvāyāṃtra ije yāne bhaṃjayatepadhi |

kṣayapāṇasotvamatattaṇi māmaṇi bhadrāyadgatasarvabhadrā padbhruveni 
mālinesivikuṃḍaline nareṃdrā yāvaveṃdrāyaṣaḍaṃgamevaṃ vinyasya 
lakṣamekaṃ svasamataḥ ||

bilvapuṣpasamidarka phalahome dhanapradaḥ |
śvetapatra sthito hāra kaṭakeyūrakuṃḍalī ||

raktākalpasmite divaḥ piṃgākṣastrimukhastripāt |
padmaṃ nāmakaraṃ vakṣye caṣakaṃ taddinaṃ dadhat ||

p. 68a)

kṛtvātaṃ maṃḍalaṃ kṛtvā vasvaraṃ veṣabhūṣitam |
pūrvabhadrāya pūrṇabhadrāya vanamāline ||

sanikuṃḍaline narendrā yaśarendrāya |
mātṛbhairavasaṃyuktāmarcayediṃdirā pradām ||

eka evasatanmaṃtraḥ kālādi guṇabhedataḥ |
saṃpade vipadaścaiva kuryādyasya parasya ca ||

nānārūpaprabhāvāḥ syuḥ kinnakuryurvijānatāṃ |
śoṣayaṃtyapivāṃbhodhīn brahmāṃḍaṃ sphoṭayaṃtyapi ||

vivarṇayaṃti cālokā saṃharaṃ yāvākhilam? |
mūkaṃ vā vākyatiṃ kuryurmūkaṃ kuryuśca vākpatiṃ ||

daridraṃ dhanadaṃ kuryuriktīkuryuśca vittapam |
dīrghāyuṣaṃ kṣaṇātkuryurjīveyuśca gatāyuṣaṃ ||

 svurāmānaṃ kṣaṇātkuryurbaddhaṃ ca mocayaṃtyapi |
maṃtrākṣarāṇi yāvanti tairyathāsaṃbhavena tu ||

paritkṛ?ptaśarīrāṃgamaṃtratejastu devatā |
evaṃ maṃtramayī yasmād devadehavirūpatā ||

maṃtāraṃ trāyate yasmāt tena maṃtramidaṃ smṛtaṃ |
akṣaraṃ kṣaraṇā bhāvānniraṃgatvāñca niṣphalam ||

mudakaroti devānāṃ drāvayantripurānanān |
drāvaṇānyāsajālasya tatparasya vyamohanāt ||

tanamudreti sā proktā taṃtresmin parameśvari |
nā putrāyanā śiṣyā ya deva vipragurudguhe ||

datte ca siddhihāniḥ syāddevatā syāni āpatet |
evametad gṛhasyārthaṃ tavādya prakaṭī kṛtam ||

kimanya tṛpṛcchase devi sarvatadvadasva me |


śrīdevyuvāca 


bhagavan bhūtabhavyeśa śrutaṃ tatpuruṣasya ca |
merumadhye kathaṃ devi caṃḍikā vyaktimāgatā ||

p. 68b)

varṇasaṃkhyāstu deveśa nyāsadhyānaṃ kathaṃ bhavet |
etat saṃkṣeparūpeṇa vadatasyāstu suvrata ||


śrībhairava uvāca 


taṃtre caivāgame sā tu vistareṇa prakāśitā |
tathāpi kathayiṣyāmi meruddhāra krameṇa ca ||

sarvalakṣaṇasaṃyuktaḥ sādhakaḥ sthiramānasaḥ |
ekānte vijane sthitvā ṛtulakṣāvadhirjapet ||

tāvatā yuta homena sarvānāpnoti yatnataḥ |
carutaṃdulakairājyabrahmapuṣpasamudbhavam ||

dhruvaṃnābhigataṃ devi devī bījaṃ tu agrataḥ |
kālamadhyagataṃ gṛhya dhruvādyāsanasaṃsthitam ||

2(ḷ) ādi varṇayogena tṛtīyaṃ bījamuttamam |
mohinī dhruva ādistha kevalaṃ bhagayukpunaḥ ||

guhyaśakti samopetaṃ paṃcamaṃ tu na saṃśayaḥ |
śvetabhairavamuddhṛtya ṭāṃ tasthaṃ lohitaṃ punaḥ ||

mohinī varṇayogena saptamaṃ tu na saṃśayaḥ |
navādi dīpanī devī amarīśenasaṃyutā ||

śvetabhairavamuddhṛ sa guhyaśaktyā vibhūṣitam |
ekādaśādi deveśi maṃku(da)re guhyaśakti yuk ||

yā si madhyaṃ tu saṃgṛhya vāme madhyenasaṃyutam |
lohitaṃ saṃgṛhyanigmasaṃkhyaṃ sureśvari ||

ekādaśe śvetarudraṃ maṃdare guhyaśakti yuk |
bhīṣaṇī ṛṣigehe tu vāyuvegāt tathā bhavet ||

mahākālaṃ te ca madhye kailāse bhagayugbhavet |
pāśimadhyaṃ kevale tu mahākāla dhruvādiśaṃ ||

lohitaṃ kevalaṃ devi vāme śaktistadagrataḥ |
dhenumadhyasamopetaṃ guhyaśakti vibhūṣitam ||

bhāgamadhye kevalaṃ tu biṃdubhairavabhūṣitam |
yāmamadhyaṃ tu deveśi dhātrīkaṃ guhyaśaktikam ||

p. 69a)

śarakaṃ narake vātha krodhīśaṃ vāyusaṃyutam |
bhedamadhye mahākāli mahākālenasaṃyutam ||

kuṃ antaścādhasavakaṃ śvetapūrvaṃ karālini |
krodhīśastatra deveśi yāmamadhyena bheditam ||

lokādiśirasākrāntaṃ dharmmabījaṃ tadagrataḥ |
agne vidyātṛtīyaṃ tu biṃdubhairavasaṃyutam ||

kuśamadhye punardevi tanormadhyaṃ tadagrataḥ |
śaṃḍīśena tataḥ kṛtvā dīpanīṃ mūrddhni vinyaset ||

ādau tu ṛṣimālāyāṃ bhīṣaṇī kevalaṃ bhavet |
bhāgamadhyaṃ tathā devī nāve madhyaṃ na saṃśayaḥ ||

tārākhyāgrahamālāyāṃ sūkṣmeśaṃ medaraṃ viduḥ |
vesvaṃtaṃ kevalaṃ tadvadbhe ca madhyagataṃ punaḥ ||

yāmo madhyaṃ tathā devi vahnijāyā tato bhavet |
vidyādau tritayaṃ bījaṃ matte vai lomato bhavet ||

śaktyante caṃḍikā yā ca bījaṃ madhyāṃtasaṃsthitam |
grahāṃte hṛdayaṃ deviśchrānte tu matirbhavet ||

jñānaśaktiryuta devi akṣuṇaṃ ca śoronnitam |
vasvantaṃ ca śikhā jñeyā kriyā * * tadagrataḥ ||

punastāvadbhirevānte raudraśaktyā samanvitam |
abhyartraiśca tathā netraṃ sarasvatyā ca vinyaset ||

bhūtāntairvastramākhyātaṃ cāmuṃḍāśaktiyuganatiḥ |
evamastrapadaiḥ kṛtvā maṃḍaletāṃ viciṃtayet ||

śaktauśaktiṃ ca saṃlikhya ṣaḍvarṇān vinyased vahiḥ |
caturaṣṭāṃtasaṃyuktaṃ vahirbhedāṣṭakaṃ bhavet ||

daśāṣṭadvaṃdvaśorājye saptatriṃśattathā bhavet |
p. 69b) catuḥṣaṣṭibhirāvīnāmekaikaṃ tu daśāgrahe ||

pariveṣṭasamāyuktaṃ caturdvārasamanvitam |
bhūbiṃbeśaktidurgāṃ ca cakraṃ vā śaktimālikhet ||

dūrvādalaśyāmalāṃgī keyūraghaṃṭikādibhiḥ |
virājamānā cakreṇa * * * sāpipāṇitā ||

bhīṣayatya surān sarvān trinetrān sebhru?mūrddhajān |
hṛdibrahmasamārudhāṃ jvalatpāvakasannibhām ||

paricakradharaṃ devaṃ prativaktraṃ trilocanam |
dhavalāṃvarasaṃvītaṃ dhavalā hi vibhūṣaṇaṃ ||

dvijā hi kuṃḍalābhyāṃ ca vāhūrājanyasaṃbhavau |
viśau tu mekhalādāmapadbhyāmanyaunu nūpurau ||

pustakākṣasrajaṃ śūlamaṃkuśaṃ nṛkapālakam |
saktipāṇā hi ṭaṃkākhyaṃ varadābhayaśobhitam ||

nīlajīmūtasaṃkāśaṃ rasanākalpamaḍitam |
anekavaktranayanaṃ karānekavikhaṃḍitam ||

sātvikaṃ mokṣadaṃ devi rājasaṃkāsya varddhanam |
śatrukṣayakaraṃ viddhi tāmasaṃ trividha bhavet ||

iṃdragopanibhaṃviṣṇuṃ maṃḍanānekamaṃḍitaṃ |
rathāṃgaṃkaṃvunalinaṃ gadāstrāsaṃkhyamālakam ||

pāśāṃkuśadharaṃ devaṃ bhīmākṣaṃśitadaṇḍinam |
kālameghanibhaprakhyaṃ jvalatraguṇānvitam ||

vahniṃ vamaṃtamajñāyavainateyoparisthitam |
nivṛtyādibhirāvīnāṃ sadyaprisamāvṛtām ||

aṣṭādaśaṃ tu paritaḥ utpādyāvarṇaśaktayaḥ |
vahniraṣṭāṣṭakaṃ devi devīnāṃ parito yajet ||

lokapālāṃśca tadvetī netī sarvamarcayet |

p.70a) āryyā bhadrā bhadrakālī aṃbikāvyāghramukhi siṃhamikhī jayā 
naṃdā ||

umārākāprabhāsaṃdhyā dhāriṇī dharaṇī prabhāḥ |
siṃhinī vajriṇī satyā samārādhyāśākaṃbharīti ||

viśvarūpā hariṇīvirupākṣādharā uṣātīgmā ṣaṣṭhīpuṃrūpiṇī |
rudravīryā yakṣiṇī kiṃkarī surāpi pāsinī ṣaṃḍāpaśuvatī medhā sarasvatī ||

mikhadiṇī pāvanī sthirā tārākṣinī payasvinīdidṛkṣā śaśirekhā kālarātrī 
mādhavī śaṃkhinī krūrddhinī kapardinī ||
huṃkārī durgā durvisahāgaganarekhābhayaṃkarī gāyatrī vāgeśvarī 
tiraskriyāsatī mahākālīdhamā hāriṇā oṃkārī yāmāpadmamukhī 
duṣṭasāriṇī || 50 ||

kālī vikarālī tapasvinī cchedinī kirātī kapardinī karālī anaṃ * gā caṃḍā 
virāṇī drurgāsarasvatī vyādhīghnī sṛṣṭisaṃhṛti sthitī kāntisau dāminī 
caṃḍāviṇī vināśinī tārākṣī tāriṇī kātyāyanī subhadrāsiddhānirvṛtī 
lakṣmī dīptī sumukhī nityāniraktinī ||


ānaṃdinī bhīmā ghorā devakī gaganākhaḍgīśaṃkhanīṭlinī vāmanī 
staṃbhanītrāsī mohinī asurāvinatāsu na tā smṛtirdhṛtiḥ kanakā 
ajñāśivāhuṃkārī kṣemaṃkarī vaṣaṭkārī sūryyā caṃdrāsatiratī durgā 
vasudhā vasudā kanakākhilavastrā yaṃtrekākhila kalpyate 
navaikapaṃcāṣṭadaśasaṃkhyātastu yathā kramāt ||

p. 70b)

madhyakuṃbhe samābāhya kalāsārddhaṃ sudevaki |

kalānāśakaneseti nigadyate | bhakṣatāṃ prajapettatra yathāpūrvopacārataḥ ||

alipaṃktyādibhiścālpa dravyairvā sādhu sādhayet |
sarvāsāṃ caiva devānāṃ tathā paṃcopacārataḥ ||

varṇānāṃ vyayaye nātha paṭhetpāda krameṇa tu |
tattattadvarṇaṃ deveśi yojayedupadeśataḥ ||

evamārādhyadeveśīṃ sādhayet sarvamātmanaḥ |
astulktasivailomājjñātavyaṃ guruvaktrataḥ ||


iti kubjikāmatetatpuruṣanirṇayonāma ekacatvāriṃśatamaḥ paṭalaḥ |




śrīdevyuvāca


evametattvayā proktaṃ puruṣasyo kṣame vidhiḥ |
sukṣepāyamaheśāna tathānyāntrapi koṭiśaḥ ||

tṛmuṃḍādi mahātaṃntre brahmataṃntre sureśvara |
noktamā asya kutrāpi vidhānaṃ śobhanaṃ śubhaṃm ||

sarvapāpaharodevo mokṣaikaphalade vibhuḥ |
śivaḥ svacchandabhedena sthitaḥ paramakāraṇaḥ ||

ābrahmastaṃ ca paryantaṃ careṣu sthāvareṣu ca |
vyāptaṃ sarvatradeveśaṃ carācaraguruṃ paraṃ ||

na jānāśi grahaṃ nātha kathaṃ kena kvacid vidhaṃ |
merumadhye yathādevaṃ tatsarvaṃ vaktumarhasi ||

varṇānāṃ ca yathā rūpaṃ sarvajayakaraṃparam |
nyāsadhyānasamāyogaṃ pūjanaṃ sarvadevatam ||

anyacca * * ṭghātaṃ devayathā yogyaṃ ca me vada |


śrībhairava uvāca 


sādhu bhāvini bhadrante yatpurā gopita mayā |
akathyamapi deveśi tava snehena suṃdari ||

p. 71a)

krūrāste mānavāḥ sarve mohayanti parasparam |
tvayāpi sarvadā gopyaṃ yatsamatvānnasaṃśayaḥ ||

āvayostu vibhāgaṃ ca kiṃcinmātraṃ na vidyate |
mamātyaṃta priyāsitvaṃ nānyaḥ priyatamo bhavet ||

adyame vadato vākyaṃ śṛṇudevi yathāsthitam |
pūrvokta kramato devi prastāryaṃ ca parasparam ||

sarvavidyālayaṃ sākṣāt pāraṃparyakramāgatam |
sarvakāmadutvaghaṃ devaṃ varṇarūpairvyavasthitam ||

sarvārthasādhakaṃ devamuddhare tvaṃ nageśvara |
vidhihīne bhavedvyādhirvidhihīnermṛtirbhavet ||

vidhyabhāve mahādevi sognirnānyo bhayasapi |
sarvathā vidhiyuktaṃ ca uddhare tvaṃ nageśvaram ||

ādau ca viśvamālāyāṃ dhrūvaṃ nāti pratiṣṭhitam |
ṛjumadhyaṃ tu saṃgṛhya dhruvādyāsanasaṃsthitam ||

krūraṃtaṃ śivedattaṃ sāpimadhyaṃ ca suṃvaka |
vedavantaṃ vijānīyāduttare niṣadhālaye ||

navakeśarakaṃ gṛhya gavāvaṃne?nabhūṣitam |
paṃcamaṃtra vijānīyādadenmadhyastu uttamam ||

sāsimadhyadvayaṃ devi kevalaṃ saptamaṃ bhavet |
dīpanī śūladaṇḍe tu carenmadhye tu vajriṇī ||

punaḥ sā vajriṇī devī sūkṣma bhairavasaṃyutām |
mudrīśaṃkātva madhye tu munīnāmattasaṃyutam ||

tāraguṇottamādāya āmoṭīkṣaramadhyataḥ |
ekādaśaṃ tu deveśi bhautīśaṃ krūramastakaṃ ||

māyā devīṃ kesare tu jaharaṃ niṣadhālaye |
veśyantaṃ navake devi vajriṇyāsanasaṃsthitam ||

navādi dīpanī devī daśapaṃca na saṃśayaḥ |
p. 71b) vasimadhye tvayāṃ caiva daśa saptasureśvari ||

navake caturvarṇaṃ ca dīpanyāsena saṃsthitaḥ |
bhīṣanāṃ ṛṣimālāyāṃ dhruvāṃtāsanasaṃsthitām ||

ṛtvavarṇaṃ gṛhamālāyāṃ tārākṣā dīrghasaṃsthitāḥ |
guṇomadhyaṃ tu deveśi anantāsanayugbhavet ||

rudraśakti samāyogāddevāviṃśannasaṃśayaḥ |
natorṣeruttavarṇaṃ ca brahmāsārthapatirbhavet ||

bhinnaśaktiśca saṃbhinnā aṣṭāviṃśannasaṃśayaḥ |
chagalaṃḍaṃ bhūtamadhyena bāhyāsanasaṃsthitam ||

akā madhyasthāne ca madhyāsanasaṃsthitā |
gaṇamadhye adhaścaiva dhenu madhyestu dhenukān ||

lohitaṃ narake devi kevalaṃ rudramaṃdire |
vāmorugāṃtatārākṣā bhagamadhye vyavasthitā ||

maṃdare sūkṣmasaṃyuktaṃ catustriṃśākṣaraṃ bhavet |
rudraśaktyā bhavantau dvau bhavetāmavicārataḥ ||

bhūtādi varṇaṃ deveśi navādyāsanabhūṣitam |
jhaṭīśenaśiro lokā śivādi śivabhūṣitam ||

śavakaṃ navake varṇaṃ guhyaśaktivibhūṣitam |
vāṃtaṃ na ca keservigāya ādi bheditaṃ kuru ||

me ca madhye mahākālaṃ jñānaśakti vibhūṣitam |
kailāse maṃdire devi deviñca kaṃṭhakevalaṃ ||

ekāḥ valavamaṃdirasthā bhūmīśo sasimadhyamaḥ |
tārākṣāsaṃyutaḥ sopi dhruvādisthāsana viduḥ ||

agnijyādhaśca dhṛgvaśīśaṃke aṃtabiṃdu bhūṣitam |
sāpi madhyakevalaṃ tu dhātrīśaṃ kāṃtu madhyataḥ ||

p. 72a) anantaṃ gaṃdhaśikhare bhinnā cātha sureśvari |
śaraṃ caiva * ke caiva sūkṣmabhairavabheditam ||

bhāvettaṃ caravaradbhyāśaṃ sadyojātenabheditam |
biṃdubhairavasaṃbhinnaṃ haṃsavarṇaṃ tato yataḥ ||

dhruvāntaṃ naivadeveśi mātṛkāsaṃkhyako bhavet |
tārāṃ te tu mahāśaktiṃ haṃsāt prāksaṃprayojayet ||

evametan mahāmaṃtraṃ tavasneho ca bhāvini |
prakāśitaṃ rahasyārthaṃ nyāsāṃścārtha nava priye ||

dvādaśaṃ *?śca ca hṛdayanivṛttiścātmane tataḥ |
navāṃtaṃ ca śivojñeyā pratiṣṭhājñānasaṃyutā ||

śikhā tato grahārṇaṃ syādvidyā caiva kriyāthavā |
varṇavadyakṣaraṃgāṃti svātmaśaktiśca bhairavi ||

ṛtivarṇaṃ sādyatītānetraṃ saṃjīvanaṃ putaḥ |
bhūtāṃte varṇamākhyātaṃ mokṣaśaktiṃ tataḥ param ||

mahālakṣmī yutaṃ kṛtvānyāsametaṃ samācaret |
cakre tvevaṃ prayuṃjīta yathāśakti krameṇa tu |

urddhādi kramato devi padanyāsaṃ tu kārayet |
vidyānyāsaṃ mātṛkāyāḥ śabdarāśiḥ mataḥ param ||

nyāsaṃ kṛtvā pureśāni dhyāyet taṃ cakramadhyagam |
śakti dvaṃ dvaṃ likhet pūrvaṃ tripurāśaktiyugbhavet ||

antare tu tayāyuktaṃ netraṃ bhrūmaṃḍalaṃ bhavet |
agneḥ śaktirvibhinnaṃ ca aṃteśaktiṃ niyojayet ||

svarāṇāmaṃtare devi jīvaṃ prāṇaṃ tu kārayet |
caṃdrabiṃbaṃ likhed dante śaktyāśaktiṃ niyojayet ||

śaktiṃ dve dve nyase cchaktiṃ madhye śaktiṃ dvitīyakaṃ |
svarāṇāṃ ca mahādevi nyasedvarṇānvibhāgaśaḥ ||

ādyadvitvaṃ ca śaktiḥ syātkramādakramayogataḥ |

p.72b)

vinyasenmātṛkāṃ devīṃ svaśireṇāgnidīrghayuk |
biṃvāṣṭake mahādevi caṃḍikāṃ vinyaset tataḥ ||

agnibījaṃ tato nyasyā madhye devaṃ vicintayet |
dhavalāsvarasaṃvītaṃ dhavalākalpabhūṣitam ||

dhavalaṃ dhavalābjasthaṃ dhavalāvāsamadhyagam |
dhavalairevamārgādyaistathā pūjyopakārakaiḥ ||

śūlaṭaṃkākṣavalayaṃ jñanamudrāmabhīṣṭadam |
dakṣevāme tathā devi ahipāśāṃkuśau tathā ||

pustakaṃ makarālaṃ ca dadhatpūrṇasamaprabham |
yathā vā devi deveśaṃ dakṣiṇāmūrtirūpiṇam ||

pūrṇāmṛtaghaṭaṃ corddhe vāme cātha supustakam |
akṣasraṃjaṃ dakṣa ūrddhe mudrāṃ jñānamayīṃ tathā ||

ekavaktraṃ trinetraṃ ca kuṃdasiṃdūrabhāsvaram |
itthaṃ vicintyadeveśaṃ pūjayedupacārataḥ ||

aṃgamūrtinivṛtyādi mātṛbhairavayogataḥ |
vidyeśanityamārādhyatadvetrīnparito yajet ||

indrādyā devatāḥ sarve vāhanaiḥ śastrasaṃyutāḥ |
vistareṇādya deveśi tavasnehāt prakāśitam ||

tvaṃ ca devi svarākhyā(tryā)nyānprakāśayana cānyathā |
anādi siddhisaṃsāre ajñānena vimohitāḥ ||

sakṛduccāramātreṇa saptajanma kṛtaṃ mahat |
pāpāni vilayaṃ yānti satyaṃ satyaṃ na saṃśayaḥ ||

lakṣamātrakṛte jāpe siddhinā mālayo bhavet |
naladurgeśi deveśi trailokyaṃ ca samālabhet ||

lakṣatraye kṛte jāpe sākṣādvai śravaṇo bhavet |
p. 73a) vedalakṣāntrivedīśo kartāhartā svayaṃ vibhuḥ ||

paṃcalakṣairmahādevi gāṇapatyaphalaṃ labhet |
lakṣairekādaśordevi sādhako matsamo bhavet ||

tatvalakṣāhasau devo mayā tulyaḥ sadāśivaḥ |
kulāntārayate spta daśapūrvādaśāparen ||

ito vaktuṃ na śaknomi satyaṃ satyaṃ varānane |
sūryo vā tapate bhūmau sāgarātā mahīścaret ||

maṃhīṃgachecca pātāle vidhurāgnirbhavedyadi |
bhagnaṃ parvatarājasya etanmithyā bhavisyati ||

brahmapusyasamiddhomaṃ tilataṃḍulakaiḥ kramāt |
lājāpūpaphalānāṃ tu madhuraiḥ saha bhāvini ||

jñātvā bhavo vimuktosau dvārāye parameśvari |
sarvairdravyaistathā devi sarvakāmāṃ navāpnuyāt ||

ityuktaṃ ca rahasyārthaṃ itaḥ kiṃ kathayāmya?ham |


śrīdevyuvāca 


bhagavansarvamākhyātaṃ vidhānaṃ śobhanaṃ param |
tatparasya tatheśasya devī sarvārtha siddhidaṃ ||

vidhānaṃ paramaṃ guhyaṃ devi siddhaḥ supūjitaḥ |
bhairavīti ca vikhyātā yā devī sarvasiddhidā ||

tasyāstu paramaṃ bījaṃ maṃtramaṃtrādi saṃyutam |
nyāsadhyānasamāyuktaṃ sāmarthyaṃ tasya kīdṛśam ||

bhairavī samayā devī yathāvat parameśvarī |
kathayasva prasādena yenajānāmi suvrataḥ ||


śrīmahādeva uvāca 


śṛṇu devi yathā sā tu pūjyate ca kulakramān |
kulakubjeśvarī devī kulācārakrameṇa tu ||

kulajā yoginī jātā yoginyaḥ siddhayoginaḥ |
ārādhyavidhinā devi prāpnuyustena saṃśayaḥ ||

p. 73b)

anyaiśca siddhivargaiśca bhairavīṃ bhavabhaktinīṃ |
nyāsamātre kṛte devi sarvapāpakṣayo bhavet ||

tārapaṃcaka mādyantaṃ yojayet sarvasiddhaye |
hṛdimadhyeṃḍaṃvikā devī ātmaśakti samanvitā ||

navādi dīpanī devī trimūrtyā bheditaṃ kuru |
lokādi śivabhinnaṃ ca devyābījaṃ sureśvari ||

haṃsavijau tu dīpanyā vāmāṃte bhautikena tu |
krūrabhairavasaṃbhinnaṃ dvitīyaṃ varavarṇini ||

sahāṃttau krodhasaṃruddhau vidhumadhyaṃ sureśvari |
meṣe nāma mahārudro guṇoṃtaḥ sūkṣmayugbhavet ||

tārākṣabhāvamadhyasthā yāmontena bheditam |
ekādasādi varṇaṃ ca villamadhye tu pūtanā ||

maṃdare sūkṣmarūdreṇa bheditenaiḍayukbhavet |
mahākālaṃbhe ca madhye kātramadhyaṃ narā bhavet ||

dhātrīśaṃ dīpanī yukta śaramdhye sureśvari |
rudrasaṃkhyāṃ samākhyātaṃ vidyāṃtegnahasaṃyutam ||

prathamaṃ hṛdayaṃ devi dvitīyaṃ śiva ucyate |
tṛtīyaṃ śiṣyasaṃjñeyā tatorddhaṃ ca catuṣṭayam ||

tasmātkāraṇasaṃkhyārṇairvīkṣaṇaṃ devi pūjitam |
devī bījena cāstraṃ syādumājyeṣṭāṃ ca raudrikā ||

vijayā maṃgalā caiva siddhidā sarvapūrvakā |
guhyādi padasaṃyuktāṃ devatātulamotthitāḥ ||

navādhāreṣu natvaṃ tu natvābhyāṃ vyāpyasamāhitāḥ |
bhūbiṃbe caṃdrabiṃbaṃ ca madhyabiṃbe sudhākaraḥ ||

karehya biṃbāni caṃdrasya caturddhāropi kalpitaḥ |
p. 74a) sādhyabījaṃ mahādevyā aṣṭāvaṣṭau tathāvahiḥ ||

vinyasenmātṛkaṃ tatra devībījaṃ tato vahiḥ |
madhyavivagatāṃ devīṃ vālārkakiraṇāruṇām ||

kurukuṃbhalatāṃgīṃ ca nānākalpavibhūṣitāṃ |
cāpavāṇavarābhīṣṭā karapallavaśobhitām ||

śoṇāradhauraktapātaḥ samyakkiṃjalka sasvaram |
dvipaṃca śobhanaṃ devī dhvāntādau sādhakeśvari ||

guṇalakṣakṛte jāpe home tveka guṇena ca |
palalātākṣahomena sādhayet sarvamātmanaḥ ||

māyā haṃsi siddhāsati umākāli sarasvati |
grāmaṇi bhairavi muktā(devi) ekavaktrāḥ caturbhujāḥ ||

kucalaṃbikā?digvāsāḥ sāvadhānāḥ sa dūṣaṇāḥ |
lokaśaktistathāsaṃghāḥ kucalaṃvivibhūṣaṇāḥ ||

pādukā paṃktisākṣāccanamaskārādi pūrvavat |
eva evaṃmabhyarcyanāṃ devī bhairavīṃ bhavaśātanīṃ ||

dhyānayetpādasaṃbhinnaṃ dhūpadīpādisaṃyutam |
ṣaṇmāsābhyāsayogena sādhakaḥ khecaro bhavet ||


iti śrīkubjikāmate śālanirṇayo nāmadvicatvāriṃśatitamaḥ paṭalaḥ |





śrīdevyuvāca 


devadevajagannāthalokānugrahakārakaḥ |
tvatprasādena śāstrārthaṃ śrutaṃ vistarataḥ prabho ||

adhunā śrotumicchāmi bhairavyāḥ kramamuttamam |
kalākāṣṭhādi rūpeṇanimeṣānmeṣa āgnataḥ ||

pakṣaśabde kṛte tatra kalidvāparayogataḥ |
anyānyapi ca yā devi śuktitehasurenyapi ||

ekadvitricatuḥpaṃcaṣaṭsaptāṣṭakaṃ navam |
daṃśekadvādaśatriṃśacaturdaśaṣupaṃca ca ||

p. 74b)

daśasapta tathātriṃśatsahastrakaḥ |
arddhalakṣaṃ tathālakṣaṃ catuḥpaṃca ca paṃca ca ||

koṭibhedena deveśa anaṃtaṃ trividhārṇavam |
jñānavijñānasaṃbhinnaṃ sarvaṃ sā sūjani sphuṭam ||

nyāsa dhyānaṃ tathā pūjā vakraṃsarvārthasiddhidaṃ |
brahmādistaṃ va paryantaṃ vyāptaṃ devyāstu tejasā ||

iti sarvaṃ sureśāna vistareṇa vada prabho |

śrībhairava uvāca ||

devi gopyamidaṃ jñānaṃ sarvaṃ yatparipṛcchati |
tathāpi sneha bhāvena vakṣyāmi surasuṃdari ||


śrībhairava uvāca ||


bhairavīṃti ca yā devī viddhitāṃ garuḍadhvajam |
yā devī harirityuktā sa devo bhairavaḥ smṛtaḥ ||

nakulīśī śaktiśca rephohatvaṃvaraṃ punaḥ |
nādabiṃdvātmakaiḥ sodrautvaṃ cāhaṃ viddhi pārvati ||

dṛgvalākulavāṃtāṃtaṃ śikhijyātena bheditam |
nādāadi saṃjñakaṃ kulīnadi sarvedabhūṣitam ||

sarvāmnāyakramaṃ caitajjñātavyaṃ varavarṇini |
pūrvavat samayaṃ devī saṃpūjya ca kulakramam ||

pādau ca viśvamālāyāṃ dhruvaṃ nāmapratiṣṭhitam |
devī bījaṃ tato devi bahumadhyaṃ prayāsināṃ ||

tristhāne dau samuddhṛtya āghaurutātta bheditau |
dīpanī śūladaṃḍaṃ ca navādyakrūrabheditau ||

dvitīyaṃ tu mahākālaṃ nikarānyāsanasthitam |
pūtanāśūladaṃḍaṃ ca tadadhaḥ kārayedbudhaḥ ||

vedavarṇaṃ punardevi uttareniṣadhālaye |
maṃdare krūrasaṃbhinnaṃ caturthaṃ tu na saṃśayaḥ ||

tṛtīyaṃ tu sureśāni navādi kṣetrabhūṣitau |
saṃhārikā tato devi dvekamadhye vyavasthitā ||

ekādaśaṃ tu dīpanyā bheditaṃ krūramastakam |
p. 75a) na savarṇaṃ punardevi guṇaitaṃ kevalaṃ bhavet ||

bhe ca madhye mahākālaṃ gāyatryā bhinnamastakam |
guṇeṃtaṃ nādinī devī anaṃtaṃ gaṃdhamādane ||

tadvallavādimuddhṛtya cātmamadhyaṃ tu kevalam |
nārāyaṇī mahādevī anaṃtena vibhuṣitā ||

vāyuvegā punardevi caṃḍikāntaṃ samuddharet |
punaryāgauriyadrājyaṃ triṣulokeṣu kathyate ||

mūkopi vākpatirbhūyādvratahīnopi vittapaḥ |
śītalenāsitulpaśca tejasā taraṇi prabhā ||

gurvākṣāyāṃ nutaireva sidhyate nātrasaṃśayaḥ |
nyāsaṃ tu kārayed yatnān sarvaṃsiddhikaraṃ param ||

devī bījaṃ maheśāni vācāṃte pūrvapatrake |
ulkaṃ caṃdra prabhaṃ kṛtvā bhairavī hṛdayaṃ hṛdi ||

devī kāmaṃ tathā devi mahā ulkaṃ tathā punaḥ |
dharā dau tripadaṃ prokṣa bhairavī hṛdayaṃ śivaḥ ||

devī tṛtīyasaṃbhinnaṃ vīkṣolkaṃ bhairavī tathā |
devīti prathamaṃ bījaṃ yuktaṃ tadvanniyojayet ||

kāmabījaṃ mahādevi sahasrolkaṃ ca pratimuk |
bhūyāhupūrvavadevamudyanīyāniyojayet ||

mahācakraṃ ca yadbhinnaṃ tripurāyāḥ sarvataḥ priye |
hṛdayaṃ vāstusaṃbhinnaṃ caṃḍikāṃtaṃ prakalpayet ||

vaktre cātha prayuṃjāna caṃḍikā bījamuttamam |
ante vācaṃ tataḥ kāmaṃ tārtīyaṃ ca punaḥ punaḥ ||

viṣṇuḥ sadāśive śasya rudro brahmāthavāraviḥ |
tato'ṣṭāṣṭakaṃ devi nyāsa yogaṃ śṛṇu priye ||

yāsi madhyesana kulīdaṃḍena bheditaṃ kuru |
aṣṭādini ca dīrghādi śirobhinnani kārayet ||

akrūreṇa tathā devī brahmāṇi asitāpnukam |
p. 75b) māheśīrurubhede ca kaumārī caṃdrasamyutā ||

vaiṣṇavī krodhasaṃbhinnā vārāhyunmatta bhairavī |
indrāṇī sakapālākhyā caṃdrikābhīṣaṇena tu ||

saṃhāreṇa mahālakṣmī yāmalaṃ vai prakalpayet |
bījaṃ tathā hrasvadīrghaṃ mātṛbhairavayogataḥ ||

keśavaṃ prathamaṃ devi nārāyaṇamanaṃtaram |
mādhavaṃ cāthagoviṃdaṃ madhusūdanameva ca ||

trivikramaṃ kubjāṃgavarasaṃbhairavīti ca |
vāsudevenasaṃbhinnaṃ viṃdyānte evameva hi ||

dumadhyaṃ dakṣavāhū ceva pārśvo pārśvādhārake |
asaṃpṛṣṭe paragalemastakeṣvevamācaret ||

aṣṭāṃ taṃ ca tathāyokṣa aṣṭabāhuṃ ca bhairavaṃ |
ādau tu mātṛkāṃ devīṃ prāgbhavāvakrameṇa tu ||

aṣṭābhuvaṃ cāṣṭāṃśo deśe hutvā bījāṣṭakaṃ tataḥ |
(nyāsamātre kṛte cātra sarvapāpakṣayo bhavet ||

lakṣmīsarasvatī prītiratiḥ kātiḥ sakaṃ tataḥ |)
nyāsamātrekṛtecātra sarvapāpakṣayo bhavet ||

lakṣmīsarasvatī prītiratiḥ kāṃtiḥ satī jayā |
maṃgalā sarva pūrvā ca keśavāṃte tu pūrvavat ||

tataḥ kurvīta deveśi maṃḍalaṃ hastamānataḥ |
sitakaiḥ kuṃkumaiścaiva siṃdūramṛganābhiḥ * * ||

liptvā tu pīṭhe paṭṭe vā hemarājatanāmrake |
sa sarvaṃ mohanaṃ cakraṃ nyāse tatra samāhitaḥ ||

śivaṃ śaktiṃ ca deveśaṃ anyonya mathanātmakam |
madhyasūtraṃ prakurvītamadhya cakraṃ yathā bhavet ||

prāgyoni ca samaṃbhitvā likhecchaktiṃ sa lakṣaṇam |
pūrvokta śivaśaktyagre nyaset saptārṇavaṃ vahiḥ ||

pariveśakrameṇātha veṣṭayedagramagnataḥ |
aṣṭāraṃ caṃdrabiṃbaṃ ca paribhrāmyātha vākyataḥ ||

ṭāṃtamākhaṃḍalaṃ bhinnamagreṇaguṇamālikhet |
madhye bījaṃ maheśānyāḥ sakalaṃ vinyasedbahiḥ ||

p. 76a)

mahābījaṃ tu paritaḥ saṃvarttaṃ śāgnisaṃyutam |
kṣemaṃtabhūmnisaṃbhinnaṃ aṃtarānte vidhānataḥ ||

vṛṃde pṛṣṭe aṣṭavarṇaṃ yathā pūrvaṃ na saṃśayaḥ |
ṭhakārān vinyased varṇā naṃte vidyāṃ sureśvarīṃ ||

praveṭenasaṃbhinne śriyāsaṃvartakaṃ nyaset |
tanmadhye tu sureśānaṃ bhairavī sahitaṃ smaret ||

vākśrutī tathātītau sadasadbhāvavarjitau |
sṛṣṭisthityaṃta rahitai sarvakāraṇakāraṇau ||

arddhanārī hitastau ca pūjyau mādhyaṃ ca tau sadā |
vāmadakṣe avivadau dakṣe vāmeṣu pustakam ||

nānāratnamayaiḥ kḷptaṃ sphāṭikīmakṣamālikām |
jñānamudrāmadho deśe vāme pūrṇakapālakam ||

vaṃdhakāsirasābhāsau sarvakalpavibhūṣitau |
nayāsoktavidhinā devī pūjanaṃ sarvadā kuru ||

brahmādyā devatāḥ sarve pādamūle vyavasthitāḥ |
aṃtye ca ṛṣayo devi ye cānye bhūtajātayaḥ ||

rudralakṣajapādīśaḥ svayaṃbhū kusumairhutaḥ |
bhavedācāraniyataḥ paśudṛṣṭivivarjitaḥ ||

sarvajanmakṛtaṃ pāpamuddhāreṇa kṣayo bhavet |
bahunoktena kiṃ cātrasarvaṃ sādhayate tu saḥ ||

etadvidyāddhivatkliṣṭe śiṣṭe deyo na cānyathā |
anyonyaṃ saṃpravakṣyāmi vidyāmaṃtraṃ sudurlabham ||

aṃbikābhṛgumadhyasthā śūladaṇḍāsanasthitā |
bhautikaṃ mūrddhivinyastaṃ ādāvaṃte ca madhyataḥ ||

evaṃ vā ciṃtayed devaṃ pāśāṃkuśadavāvikam |
akṣaśrakpustakaṃ cānyau ikṣukodaṃḍasāyakāṃ ||

p. 76b)

kaumodakīṃ ca jalajaṃ dhavalākalpamaṃḍitam |
cakramadhye sureśānyāmarkyedupadeśataḥ ||

prajāgaraṃ ca suraṃbāhye hrāsāṃte nu ṣoḍaśam |
tatvasaṃkhyaṃ daṃtapatraṃ pārthivaṃ cakrasaṃyutaṃ ||

tadbāhyemaṃḍalaṃ cāpi vidhidvāropaśobhitam |
aṣṭāvaraṇametaddhi cakraṃ caitatsureśvari ||

praṇavaṃ madhya deśe tu śrībījena puṭī kṛtam |
ṣaḍāra aṃśamaṃtrāṇi aṣṭāreśvarayugmaśaḥ ||

manmathaṃ dvādaśāre tu dvādaśāsura biṃdumat |
caṃḍikāṃ ṣoḍaśe nyasya caturviṃśe maṃtramālikhet ||

dvātriṃśe lipivarṇāni vajraṃ māhendramālikhet |
vāruṇaṃ vāruṇe caiva veṣṭayecchaktimaṃkuśe ||

purvavatpūjayed devīṃ vāgurīṃ madanāvatīṃ |
nārāyaṇīṃ ramāṃ devīṃ yāminīṃ nādinīṃ tathā ||

niravadyāṃ mahādevīṃ yamajihvāṃ tatorcayet |
devī bījaṃ maheśāni dīrghāṣṭaka vibhūṣitam ||

akṣasrak pustakaṃ tāsāṃ pāśāṃkuśavarābhayāṃ |
vajrasaṃkhyādikātāstu prauḍhāḥ sarvārthadāyikāḥ ||

sthitāstadagnato devi sahasraṃ cāṣṭakaṃ śataṃ |
akṣasūtraṃ samādāya ādikrātaṃ vibhūṣitam ||

athabāhurlipirvaistu muktādi maṇisaṃbhavaiḥ |
paṃcāśatīmokṣārthī saptaviṃśati pauṣṭike ||

triṃśatirddhanasaṃpattāpaṃcadaśyābhicārake |
mādhyaṃ ca śāṃtike jñeyaṃ arthalābhaṃ ca nāmike ||

kaniṣṭāyāṃ sarvasiddhiḥ syādetairjapalakṣaṇam |
caturasraṃ yonisaṃkāśaṃ trirasraṃ vartulaṃ subham ||

anyānyapi ca kuṃḍāni kartavyāni parameśvari |
home mudrā trayaḥ proktā mṛgīhaṃsī ca sūkarī ||

p. 77a)

mudrāhīnaṃ ca yaddhomaṃ taddhomaṃ niṣphalaṃ bhavet |
śūkarīkarasaṃkocī haṃsīyuktakaniṣṭikā ||

mṛgīkaniṣṭatarjanyā mudrātrayamudāhṛtam |
eṇī ca śūkarī devī grathinī muṣṭiraṃjinī ||

abhicāre śūkarī syāditaraṃ śāṃtikādiṣu |
kaniṣṭhāṃgulimārabhya prāgdakṣiṇakrameṇa tu ||

anāmikāṃtaṃ vai devi tatkoṭiguṇitaṃ bhavet |
kamalākṣe ca rudraśca palāśaṃ ca japārṇavam ||

hotavyaṃ madhunā vātha phalgunī alimiśritam |
evamārābdha deveśaṃ tribhirvarṣaiḥ susaṃyataḥ ||

śrīyādhanadatulyosau vācāvāgīśvaro bhavet |
kīrtyā ca viṣṇunā tulyaḥ svargārthaṃ sthitaiṃ mamaḥ ||

bahunoktena kiṃ vāsau mayā vaṃdyo maheśvaraḥ |


iti kubjikāmatebhairavīnirṇayonāmacatvāriṃśatitamaḥ paṭalaḥ ||





śrīdevyuvāca ||


yayātu dharmakāmārthaṃ kaivalyaṃ ca prapadyate |
jñātṛprasādarūpeṇa sā sadānaṃdarūpiṇī ||

aṇusthūlādi bhedena sarvaṃ sāsṛjanisphuṭam |
amartyaḥ pūrvadevoyaḥ agastyādyāḥ sutāpasāḥ ||

bhajaṃti sudhi yo nityamanekopāyasaṃkulaiḥ |
varṇavidyārthabhūyastvāha hatvādbrahmagadyate ||

vidyābrahmasamatvācca brahmavidyā tu sā smṛtā |
tasmād vidyottamāṃ devīṃ sarvasaubhāgyasaṃpadāṃ ||

tvantaḥ paramakalyāṇa nānyo vettājagattraye |
tāmarādi mahātaṃtre āmnāyairvāmanādiṣu ||

uktaṃ ca bhavatā sarvaṃ na jñātameva sādhyataḥ |
kathayatvaṃ samāsena sāṃgopāṃgāṃ trayī mayīṃ ||

p. 77b)

śrībhairava uvāca ||


sādhu sādhu mahāprājñe gūḍhametadatīvatu |
yāmale yāmale devi navakoṭi pramāṇataḥ ||

umeti kubjikā paṃca uktā sā vistareṇa hi |
atrāpi śṛṇu deveśi yathāpūrvaṃ mayoddhṛtā ||

jayadrathe paṃcakīlītriḥ kīlīyāmale mate |
rājarājeśvare paṃca ṣaṭkīlāskaṃdayāmale ||

kālavedhaṃ ca jānadbhirvaddhaḥ sattaśca nirmalāḥ |
taṃtre taṃtre mahāvidyā jīvahīnāḥ prakāśitāḥ ||

na siddhyaṃti varārohe kīlitā vighnadāyikā |
samayaṃ tu purādatvā śrīkramāvalipūjane ||

kulācāryaḥ susaṃpannaḥ śāstravitsarvalakṣaṇaḥ |
gaṃdhaṃ puṣpaṃ sudhūpaṃ ca dīpaṃ naivedya karma ca ||

aśeṣaiḥ saṃbhṛtā cāraḥ paśudṛṣṭi vivarjitaḥ |
kasyatārasahasreṇa palabhāraśatairapi ||

na tṛpyāmi mahādevi paṃcakīlāḥ prakīrtitāḥ |
tadyathā maraṇaṃ vidyāttarayā vargaridratā ||

śāśimadhyagataṃ devi kāruṇyaṃ dīpanī yutam |
śrotuṃ kātvamadhorūpe yāme yodhyanta yānugam ||

paṃcāntakaṃ tatottasthaṃ maṃdare sūkṣmagaṃ punaḥ |
guṇontādbhu tathā yojye phaṭkārā dīpanī yutā ||

rūdyādi varṇayogaṃ caru aṃtaṃ biṃdumastakam |
viṣṇupadbhyāṃ samāyojya sarvāntāṃ siddhimātṛkāṃ ||

caṃḍikārākumārdevi nityonitya naṃditā |
caturthyārathasaṃbudhyā śaktibījaṃ tu kevalam ||

karālikāputanā ca kulacakrapadaṃ bhavet |
nāyikā yai bhagavatī caṃḍaṃ cātha karālinī ||

anaṃtaṃ gaṃdhaśikhare mālinī ca maheśvari |
p. 78a) aṃbikārājasaddīrghatadvanmartyā vibheditam ||

tadevaṣaṣṭasvarayuk caṃḍikāṃ caivameva hi |
siṃhādau tu sa caṃḍā ca aṃkuśaṃ kāmaśaktidam ||

pāvanīśūladaṃḍaṃ ca bhe ca madhyaṃ tu haṃsinīm |
nirvāraṇaṃ ca daruṇaṃ * * * maṃdareṇa tu ||

kāṃtaṃ sāṃtaṃ sa valāntaṃ pūrapraśama*?ni ca |
śaktiḥ krodhaḥ pūtanā ca tvaritāṣṭa kleśataḥ ||

dahanīkātṛmadhye narake pūtanāni ca |
sarvāṃbhodhi śaktirbrahmā śacīpatiḥ ||

śakraprathamanī devī yugehasallayuk |
anamedhyaṃ  kevalaṃ tu śūlaṃ daṃḍaṃ tathaiva hi ||

amṛgā durbalā devī bhakṣiṇī kālayuk punaḥ |
svaśatru yujgrāhirajābhiṃdichiṃdi yukpunaḥ ||

asinātaṃ japedvaidvaṃ cchedayeti tathaiva ca |
bhairavo bhairavī devī cāmuṃḍā caṃḍasaṃyutā ||

mahākālaṃ mahākālyāḥ sakalaṃ ca manorathān |
sādhayadvitayaṃ devi pāvanī śūlakālayuk ||

kāruṇike bhagavati kālahā bhairavaṃ punaḥ |
rūpadhāriṇi deveśi tridaśaṃ vāruṇaṃ bhavet ||

śūladaṃḍāguṇoṃ tavamaṃtre śaktikamānataḥ |
maṃtrastu jananī devī rudrāntena ca maṃdire ||

bhujaṃgabhairavasthaṃ ca rādhādināgamadhyataḥ |
biṃdu madhye japatyākhyā guṇoṃtaṃ kevalaṃ viduḥ ||

vatsale devadeveśi mahākālī tadagrataḥ |
rudrāgniṃdibilvamadhyaṃ tu * * padamuddharet ||

vidyādau coddhṛtā bījāḥ prasīdamadanānarī |
kubuddhiṃ kuru deveśi guruścakra sutā priye ||

kumārīṃ tu samāyojya devī maṃkuśabhūṣitāṃ |
aṃbikāṃ kuru madhyasthā ṣaṣṭaśūlaṃ sa padma phaṭ ||

p. 78b)

tato arghyāttapanīsyāt prajānāṃ gatiravyayaḥ |
jagatyatītaṃ deveśi udgatā sarvakāmadā ||

eṣā pratyaṃgirā devī nāmnā trailokya mohinī |
anayā vidyayā devī trailokyasya janārdanaḥ ||

sargasaṃsthiti saṃdevī karotveṣā jaganmayī |
rājavidyā mahādevī rājaguhyā puretamā ||

rājottamonta māsā tu pāvanānāṃ ca pāvanī |
davodāve mahādāve avivyālāgni janādiṣu ||

āhave jayamāpnoti parthivatsahibhūpati |
yamādi sādhanaṃ devi aṇimādi guṇāṣṭakam ||

aṃjanaṃgrasakalpā ca maṃtrāṇyanyāni mudrayā |
rūpabījā kṛśānanyā tapanī pramukhā api ||

itthaṃ prabhāvo devyāstu siddhyante te tu siddhayaḥ |
dvāviṃśadakṣaraṃ devi kalpāṃte tu pradāpayet ||

aṃganāṃte purākṛtvā aṃge bījadvayaṃ likhet |
śaratkāle tu saṃprāpte nirāhāro jiteṃdriyaḥ ||

dinaṃ prati yathākhyātaṃ sahasraṃ ca dine dine |
navarātre tu saṃprāpte yathāvibhavavistaraiḥ ||

arādhyaparameśānīṃ sa bhavet sāmara priyaḥ |
mayāpi yaddhatastena sukhamāsyāmi nityaśaḥ ||

daśagnīvaḥ purādevi darpato madanugrahāt |
tasya jyeṣṭo mahābhāgo matsakhastu parājitaḥ ||

sa evaṃ dana?rājyaṃ ca sa tu hīnaṃ sataḥ * * |
samuyāgatya śaraṇaṃ prasannaḥ sa dhaneśvaraḥ ||

ādiṣṭhātāṃ mayā tasya tathāsaubhāgyato bhavat |
daśakaṃdho nālalokān jitavān vijiteṃdriyaḥ ||

p. 79a)

jayadrathaḥ kāmadevo brahmā ca vinatā prabhuḥ |
sa vaikuṃṭhothaduścarya itarāṇi api vādirāṭ ||

kurvantvete mahādevyā ādeśaṃ sarvadā priye |
kṛtvādau mātṛkāṃ devīṃ nadiphānta krameṇatām ||

śrīkaṃṭhanādinīṃ devīṃ ādikhyāṃta sa nādavān |
parānāthaṃ ca arvā ca pādukāsthānapothitāḥ ||

paramātmā aṃbikāṃ ca pātasaṃdīpanaṃ tataḥ |
ka ādibhinnaṃ deveśi dvitīyaṃ tadva deva hi ||

jāpanīṃ śūladaṃḍaṃ ca mahākālaṃ sa haṃsiniṃ |
punastāṃ dīrghasaṃbhinnaṃ sarvatrābhirniyojayet ||

sarvajñāṃ cāthadebhinnāṃ hṛdayaṃ cātra pārvati |
nirvāṇapadmade devīṃ tṛptāṃ ye ca śivo bhavet ||

vāruṇyasūkṣmasaṃbhinnaṃ śvetaṃ kālaṃ ca yāṃtagam |
pāvanī plavasaṃbhinnā narake pāvanī punaḥ ||

navādi dīpanī yuktaṃ śavakaṃ kālabhairavam |
nārāyaṇī mahādevī maṃdare sūkṣmasaṃyutā ||

caturthānādi bodhāṃ tu devī bījaṃ ca kalpayet |
devī bījaṃ samuddhṛtya krodhaṃ ca bilvamadhyagam ||

tato bhave maheśāni haritā kliṣṭake śataḥ |
dalanībhirmahādevīṃ svataṃtrā patanacchadam ||

huṃgurvā pāvanī devī dhātrīśo dīrghabiṃdumat |
bhīṣaṇīṃ rāṃtasaṃbhinnājalajaṃ sūkṣmabheditam ||

taraṇābhiḥ punastāṃ ca śrīkaṃṭholuptaśaktikaḥ |
nikhilaṃ padamuddhṛtya avipraśamanīti ca ||

deveṅgaṃ vyastrasamāpokṣya pannagaṃ śaktiśāstra phaṭ |
guruṇāṃ pādukā paṃktiṃ jñātavyaṃ copadeśataḥ ||

padanyāsaṃ varṇabhedairanyaiścāpi sureśvari |
nyāsānanyānvidhātavyā guruvaktravinirgatān ||

p. 79b)

anekavaktranayanāmanekāṃtu karāṃbujām |
aneka pāṇi caraṇāṃ netrāneka vibhūṣitām ||

anekabharaṇasaṃdīptāṃ nīlakuṃtalamurddhajām |
dhyāyentāmatha deveśīṃ niścale nāṃtarātmanā ||

udayārkasamaprakhyāṃ kiraṇānekasaṃkulām |
vaṃdhūkapuṣpasaṃkāśāṃ siṃdūrasadṛśopamām ||

paṃcavaktrāṃ daśabhujāṃ pīnonnatapayodharām |
sarvābharaṇasaṃbhinnāṃ sarvalakṣaṇalakṣitām ||

tripaṃcanayanāṃ saumyāṃ muktakeśīṃ hima prabhām |
siddhalakṣmī mahādevīṃ siddhimuktipradāyinīm ||

nityaprasiddhayogeśīṃ kulākula maheśvarīm |
akṣamālāṃ kapālaṃ ca * * * khaḍgasaṃyutām ||

muṃḍakhaṭvāṃga hastāṃ ca varadā bhaya pāṇinīm |
pāśāṃkura dharāṃ devīṃ pretasyoparisaṃsthitām ||

pāṭhakramagatairvarṇairvinyasedaṃ kuśadvayam |
viṣṇubījaṃ nyaseddevī dalāgre ca punaḥ kramāt ||

dalapaṃcakasaṃyuktaṃ rekhāsaptadalāvṛtam |
aṣṭādaśorddharekhāṃkiṃ bhūtirāgrahamuttamam ||

śikhāgre triśikhāṃ pūjyamaṃḍalākhaṃḍalasya ca |
madhyādi śarapatreṣu yāvatkoṣṭhāśca bāhyataḥ ||

kaṃṭakādi nyasedvidyāṃ yuvatiḥ ṣoḍaśaiḥ kramāt |
teṣu ṣodaśakoṣṭheṣu mājīnamadhyagai saha |

mukhāgraṃ śarapatrāṇi sāgraṃ rāṣṭasapatrakam |
ṣoḍaśāraṃ mahādevī dvātriṃśadgaṇaśobhitam ||

brāhme ṣaṣṭicatuḥpūrvaṃ pararekhā trayānvitaṃ |
p. 80a) bhūviṃvai parisaṃyuktaṃ vīthī dvāropaśobhitam ||

devī bījaṃ vasaṃlikhya patreṣvapi pṛthak pṛthak |
devī devau ca deveśīṃ madhyage padasaṃyute ||

hākinyā tu yathāṣaṭko aṣṭotaranavātmakam |
vasubhistu śivobhinnaṃ ṣoḍaśe tu navātmakam ||

dvātriṃśe tu mahādevīṃ vidyārṇāṃ ca yuthā kramāt |
catuḥṣaṣṭi yutāṃ devīṃ vivekānu vilomataḥ ||

vinyasenmātṛkāṃ yuktāṃ bāhyetāṃ kevalaṃ bhavet |
bhūbiṃbe caṃḍikāṃ devīṃ vajrebhubhā?gurīmapi ||

dvātriṃśena tu varṇāni vākye catvāri vāni ca |
brāhmī kṣātrī tathā vaiśvā śūdrīkaṃṭṭakīnīti ca ||

rajakī mocakī paścāccaṃḍālī ca yathākramāt |
vaṭkāṣṭaketu vaṭkībhīryojayenmātṛbhairavāt ||

bījāṣṭakaṃ tathā cādau guruvakropadeśataḥ |
kāmarūpī kolagiriḥ sauvārāvyālakau tathā ||

malayaṃ śrīkulāṃtaṃ ca jālaṃdhaṃ devi koṭakam |
pīṭheśvaryo mahādevyaḥ kāmākṣāṃ ca yā tathā ||

devī pūrvaṃ tu kolā yai sauvārāyāṃ tathā punaḥ |
tajjāyaṃ tu tathā devī samalāṃgaṃ tu pūrvavat ||

kulāyāṃ ca tathā vāpīṃ jalāyāṃ tadvadeva hi |
devakāyāṃ nivāṃge pūjāapāyāti bhūya ca ||

tvaṅmāṃse raktapittāsthi majjāśukra sa jīvakāḥ |
pīṭhādau yojayedetattvagādyābījapūrvikāḥ ||

evamaṣṭāṣṭakaṃ devi pūrvanyāsakrameṇa ca |
sadyojātaṃ tu pūrveṇa vāmadevaṃ tu dakṣiṇe ||

aghoraṃ paścime nyasya puruṣaṃ cottare viduḥ |
jñānāpūrve pravinyasya tatkālābhiśca tāṃ tyajyet ||

p. 80b) ikāraṃ prathamaṃ pūjyajñākāraṃ va dvitīyakam |
kriyākhyāṃ ca devīti pātvekāmāṃsasaṃyutām ||

brahmāviṣṇustathā rudra īśvaraśca sadāśivaḥ |
pretamadhye maheśānyā āsanaṃ muṃḍasaṃyutam ||

dharmajñānaṃ ca vairāgyamaiśvaryaṃ dikpunaḥ punaḥ |
ākāraṃ prathamaṃ devī muḍaviśveti saṃjñitam ||

rudaṃ ca valamadhye tu arcayet surapādapān |
kaṃdanālaṃ tathāparṇaṃ mātṛkāṃ pūrvavat priye ||

guṇaruṣkaṃ na dharmādīn tatra pretāsanaṃ bhavet |
caṃḍikādyaṃta madhye ca caturbhyāṃ ca parā parā ||

aparā tu tathā devī mahāpretaṃ ca taṃyutam |
padmāsanaṃ mahādevyā umāmācalanirbhavet ||

siddhilakṣmyādi mūrtyante devī yuge tu yukpunaḥ |
maṃḍalesmin mahādevī sānnidhyaṃ kuruyugmakam ||

āraṃbhāni maheśānimarcayedupadeśataḥ |
saparyāsādhanaṃ vakṣye vāme tvarya sa hetukam ||

yathāvidhānaṃ saṃśodhya rūpasaṃkhyopa cārataḥ |
caṃḍikāśaktimatpūrvaṃ tukārapīṭhaye saha ||

na caṃḍikā bījamuddhṛtya vase heti ca caṃḍikā |
caṃḍaṃ ca bhairavaṃ devaṃ pādukā paṃktisaṃyutā ||

ānaṃdaṃ ca nivṛttiti kalādevī punaḥ punaḥ |
jakāraṃ pīṭhaṃ śrīpūrvaṃ umāṃ tāṃ ca karālinīm ||

padmapūrvaṃ tu cāmuṃḍāṃ brahmānaṃdaṃ tathaiva hi |
pratiṣṭhāpyasamākhyātā tribījaṃ punareva hi ||

pūrṇapīṭhaṃ tu deveśi giri cāmuṃḍacāṃḍaca |
nāthetiṃ saṃvarāṃte syāmāṃtāṃ cātha ca caṃḍrakam ||

p. 81a) vidyākalādi devīti pādukāṃ pūrvavatpunaḥ |
bījānimāni deveśi caṃḍeśaṃ puraśaṃvake ||

sapheśakti mahābīja trimalāmata dvisaṃyutaḥ |
pheśrīrudranāthānte gālaṃ tathā yāṃtikā devī ||

kalādi pūrvoktena yathāvat * saṃprayojayet |
ārapūrveṣu pādeṣu brahmaviṣṇvaṃśa bhedataḥ ||

jyeṣṭhā vāmā ca raudrī ca devīnāṃ purataḥ kramāt |
ṣaṭpaṃcāśatpadaṃ jñeyaṃ dvādaśaṃtaṃ dvitīyakam ||

tṛtīyaṃ śiśuvarṇāni yathā devī purārcitā |
surāṇāmaṃtare devyāḥ pūjyāḥ ṣaṭsuyathā kramāt ||

ṣaḍdīrghabheditāṃ kṛtvā prāṇajīvaṃ ca mādanam |
tato bhaven maheśāni aidro rephaṃ sa dārukam ||

kṣakāraṃ kālamārūḍha madhasthā caiṃdravāruṇam |
dīpanī śūladaṃḍaṃ ca dhruvāṃtenavibhūṣitam ||

bījamenaṃ maheśāni sarvatraivā parājitam |
namodikṣu caturthyantā mahāpūrṇitalocanā ||

ṭaṃkakṣoṇikāḥ sarvāvaradābhaya śobhitāḥ |
ātmānamasūtāṃnuktaṃ bhūdharaṃ madanāvatī ||

śūlinaṃ dhṛtisaṃyuktaṃ vidyātmānaṃ sa bhūṣayā |
sitikaṃṭhaṃ ceṃdirāṃ yuktamaparaṃ ca kapardinam ||

viṣāpalalitāṃ devīṃ sarvajñaṃ prajanāṃ priye |
nīpaṭyuk satī devī pinākī sahajādgavīm ||

varadāya virūpākṣaṃ vareṇyāyasubhadrikām |
śūrāyā thasatīṃ devīṃ smarāya priya darśinīṃ ||

aghorāya subhīmāya kumārāya parājñayā |
pāśāṃkuśadharānetrā rakṣasūtrakamaṃḍalūn ||

p. 81b) devyastā aṃgapīṭhasthā jāyākalpavibhūṣaṇāḥ |
pānapātraṃ ca sarvāsāṃ vāme dakṣe ca mṛṇmaye ||

śrīkaṃṭhādeḥ padaṃ devī hetukaṃ tripurāṃtakam |
agnivetālasaṃgaṃ ca kṛtānāṃ ca karālakam ||

ekapādaṃ mahādevī bhīmākṛti sa bhīmakam |
aghore paraurudrau syātāṃ bhairavāya sa bhīmakam ||

ete rudrā mahāvīryā ghṛtaśūlakapālakāḥ |
akṣasūtraṃ sudhākṛṣṭaṃ madaprauṭheṃdra śekharaḥ ||

etārudrāṃgapīṭhasthāḥ siṃdūrāruṇavigrahāḥ |
raktotpalābhyā * * * malaṃkṛtakarāṃbujāḥ ||

dakṣeṇāgniṣṭaramaṇā vāme śūṇimadālasāḥ |
staṃnāranatāvālāḥ kṛtacaṃdrāvataṃsakāḥ ||

muktakeśyā mahodbhūtāḥ sarvāyauvanagarvitāḥ |
kakudaśca mahādevyaḥ pūjanīyāḥ prayatnataḥ ||

ādityayoginī devī śrīmahāyoginī tathā |
śrīsiddhayoginī caiva śrīgaṇeśvara yoginī ||

niśācarī ca pretāśī vāgīśī kālarātrikā |
ṭhaṃkāra yoginī devī raudrī vetālikā tathā ||

haṃkārayoginī caiva raktavarṇāsuvartulā |
ūrddhakeśī virūpākṣī sūkṣmāṃgī ūrdhanetragā ||

navabhojanī ca phaṭkārī vīrabhadrāṃti mukhyakā |
kalahapriyā mahāyogyā rākṣasī ghoranetrikā ||

siddheśvarī ca pheṭkārī cāmuṇḍā vīrabhāsurā |
raudrī hūṃkārīṇī kṛṣṇā bhīṣaṇā ca sura priyā ||

durmukhā bhoginī caiva kharvāṃgī pādukāṃ tathā |
dīrghālaṃvauṣvikā caiva māriṇī maṃtrayoginī ||

p. 82a)

kalahākṣī grāmaṇī ca cakrīkaṃkālikā tathā |
bhuvaneśī kaṭaṃkārī ḍāṭakī pādukī tathā ||

kāmāthadūtikā caiva kākadṛṣṭiradho mukhī |
dhūrjaṭā vikaṭā caiva īśvarī viṣakaṃṭhinī ||

dvi ṣoḍaśā mahādevī pūjyāpuṣpaśarāśanam |
kodaṃḍapūnayāvarada vibhrāṇāmadamaṃdagā ||

ratāyāruṣamāriṣṭā aśliṣṭānya karotpalā |
kāmadevā parādyante kāminī prīti me? punaḥ ||

kāmahā yathā deveśi kamalāṃtenatirbhavet |
kāmacāravilāsinyaḥ karmaṇaḥ kalpakaṃ punaḥ ||

kāmakeśyāvavarṇāyai kāmamarddhe śuci smite |
rāmāya vismitāṃ devīṃ viśālākṣīṃ ca kāmakam ||

ramakaṃ lelihāyuk ratīnāthaṃ digaṃvarām |
ratipriyāṃ priyāyuktaṃ rātrināthaṃ sa kubjikām ||

ratikāṃtāṃ dharāyai ca ramaṇaṃ ca praṇiṃsakām |
niṣācaraṃkaṣadyāsau lagnāpathavimohinīm ||

naṃdakāyamahāyaitranaṃdanāyasurottamām |
naṃdetāyasulāvaṇyaṃ niśācārāya mardinīṃ ||

kalahapriyārati sukhamekākṣaṃ puṣpadhanvane |
sumukhe tu mahādha * * bhrāmarāpāṭanyatha ||

bhramamāṇasumālinyāṃ bhramā * * śikhā bhavet |
bhrāntāyāthasusuśrāpairāśrama vāsine ||

bhṛgasyāthabhramāyai ca mohanaṃ dīrghajihvikām |
ekapāśāya vārāyai namoṃtā bījapūrvikāḥ ||

sakraṃdanāgni paramā * pituṃ ca niśācaram |
p. 82b) pureteraṃ ca śākhāgraṃ jñānapadmaṃ ca cakriṇam ||

īśāgne ca vidhātavyā yathāyogā sureśvari |
graharāśi tathā homairmātṛkā karṇayogataḥ ||

krodhinī staṃbhinī caiva śaṃkhayā munidhī punaḥ |
gaṇeśaṃ kṣetrapālaṃ ca durgāṃ caiva sarasvatīm ||

airāvataḥ puṃḍarīko vāmanaḥ kumudoṃ janaḥ |
puṣpadaṃttaḥ sārvabhaumaḥ supratīkaśca diggajāḥ ||

nakṣatrayātparaṃ ṣaṣṭi yoginyaḥ parivārikāḥ |
nānārūpadharāḥ sarvāḥ kāmanamrāṃgayaṣṭayaḥ ||

kaṃṭhalaṃvitadigvāsā madāghūrṇitalocanāḥ |
dīpakhaḍgadharāḥ kecinnāsāpānaratāḥ parāḥ ||

karaṃkadaṃvakāṭhinyaścāmarā dalaśobhitāḥ |
tālavṛṃtaṃ tathācchatraṃ vividhā yudha bhūṣaṇāḥ ||

daṃbholī śaktidaṃḍaṃ ca khaḍgapāśaṃ śūlīmapi |
kaumodakī gadāṃbhoja sudarśanamanukramāt ||

ṛgvedothayajurvedaḥ sāmavedopyatharvaṇaḥ |
purāṇaṃ dharmaśāstraṃ ca mīṃmāsānyapi vāskalam ||

lā?syairgānaiḥ sa vādyaiśca veṇuvīṇā manoramaiḥ |
prīṇayanto mahādevī gaṃdharvākṣarakinnarāḥ ||

nāgāsurāmanuṣyāśca svamūrtyāḥ siddhacāraṇāḥ | 
ṛṣayaśca vaśiṣṭādyā nāradoṃgirasastathā ||

atrirvaśiṣṭo bhagavān pulastyaḥ pulahaḥ kratuḥ |
viśvāmitrabharadvāja bhṛgugautamakaśyapāḥ ||

anye vālmīki pramukhāḥ prīṇayaṃto maheśvarīṃ |
sūktibhiḥ prastutibhiśca śāstrairvedāṃgasammitaiḥ ||

p. 83)

evamārādhyavidhivajjape sthitvā tadagrataḥ |
śataṃvāthaśatārddhaṃ vā sahasraṃ vā tadarddhakam ||

lakṣaṃ tadarddhakaṃ vāpi rudralakṣe śivo bhavet |
kaluṣaṃ trividhaṃ devī hotavyaṃ kasya sammatam ||

madhvaktaḥ madhubhiḥ paṃkai ravairutpalairapi |
brīhitismacaruḥ siddhaṃ panasaṃ nārikelakam ||

devī maṃtre ca raudre ca svayaṃbhuḥ kusumaiḥ saha |
tarpaṇādau ca home ca vidheyamaviśaṃkitam ||

samāsenātra deveśi tavasnehena bhāṣitam |
prakāśitaṃ rahasyārthaṃ satyame tatra cānyathā ||


iti śrīkubjikāmate catuścatvāriṃśatitamaḥ paṭalaḥ |




śrīdevyuvāca ||


sarvametadbhavānāha tattvayāme va bhāṣitam |
sūcitā vistareṇātra vidyā pratyaṃgirā subhā ||

nā kulaḥ śivabhedena upāsyaḥ śivayogināma |
maṃtrapakṣābhidhānaṃ ca yogadhyānaṃ susiddhayaḥ ||

yathāvedmi sureśāna viśvamākhyā hi yatnataḥ |


śrībhairava uvāca ||


viśvāmitraḥ purādevi vāśiṣṭhena tiraskṛtaḥ |
tapastepetadābhīmmamaruvikrama cetasā ||

varṣāṇāṃ dvādaśairdivyairnirāhāro jiteṃdriyaḥ |
tuṣṭoha tapasā devi bhasmādbhagamanusmaret ||

ādiṣṭau tu mahāmaṃtrau dikasaṃkhyā cākṣatau bhavet |
tābhyāṃ brahmatvamāpanna gupte vakṣyāmi suṃdari ||

śaivanā kulataṃtrebhyo yamau dūṣṭau sanātanau |
pūrvoktaṃ samayaṃ devi vijñātavyaṃ guruvaktrataḥ ||

rasabhūtāṃtamadhyasthaṃ kevalaṃ prathamaṃ bha * t |
p. 83b) meṣonāma mahārudraḥ prāṇāṃtaṃ bījamuttamam ||

mahākālaṃ punardevi maṃdaresena bhūṣitam |
vāntonterddhacchamadhyasthattasyādhonuṃtamālikhet ||

trimūrtyā śivayā bhinnaṃ tṛtīyaṃ saptaśūnyakam |
tadagre ca mahādevi viṣṇorjāyaṃ samālikhet ||

tāttīparasavaṃ bhinnamastirudraṃ punarbhavet |
guhyaśaktyā ca mūrddhnisthaṃ vaktravarṇaṃ cakaṃ likhet ||

tasyamūrddhni pradātavyamanaṃtaṃ gaṃdhamādane |
vāṃtāṃtaṃ na pureśāvayāmomadhyaṃ na saṃśayaḥ ||

asya jāpena homena sādhakaḥ sarvamāpnuyāt |
śṛṇu devi pravakṣyāmi anyathā saṃpradāyakaḥ ||

pūrvoktamaṃtrabījā nāmādau varṇatrayaṃ likhet |
bījaṃ ca vanitā śaṃbho nā likheti puṣeṇaham ||

adhastāvatpaduktaṃ gaṃdhanekrūramastakam |
caturānanā trimadhyasthā vāgīśvaryā ca bheditaḥ ||

sāpi madhyagataṃ śukraṃ guhyavarṇena bhūṣitam |
dhūhyārudantaṃ sūkṣmeśaṃ kāraṇe varavarṇini ||

senonāma mahārudro viṣṇoḥ śayyāsanasthitaḥ |
savyabāhusthaśikhare madvarṇaṃ kevalaṃ bhavet ||

arddhaniśyāṃ mahādevi susupte pāśabījane |
dravyāṇi vidhivatsādhya maṃḍalaṃ hastamānataḥ ||

tripurāṃ sādhakastatra dhyāyedīpaśitamaṃtrataḥ |
raktābharaṇasaṃdīptamaruṇaṃ viśvavigraham ||

saṃdigdho daṃḍasaṃbhinnaṃ pītaraktāśilāśitaiḥ |
āsyaiścaṃdrāṃśusaṃkāśaistadviṃvenavinirmitaiḥ ||

caṃdrārkavahninetrābjairāśīnaṃ padmaviṣṭareḥ |
vargapādasya mūle tu devasiddhairūpasthitam ||

akṣasrakpustakaṃ pāśamaṃkuśaṃ varadā bhayam |
p. 84a) mṛgaṃ ratnasaṃhṛṣṭaṃ devyāvāmoka?siṣṭayā ||

pānapātraṃ maheśāna devyālokana tatparaṃ |
īśānamīśatulyābhaṃ dhyāyet kuṃkumasannibhaṃ ||

dhruvaṃnāsi gataṃ kṛtvā madhye devaṃ vidhānataḥ |
pātreṣvarṇānvasumitānsurān keśarasaṃdhiṣu ||

va?ṭkaṃtaduhyasedbāhye kugehaṃ na cchipet punaḥ |
vipatsāṃ mātṛkāṃ devī yathoktanyasane purā ||

vinānyāsena deveśi yo maṃtrān japate punaḥ |
japantanniṣphalaṃ tasya vadhire karṇajāpavat ||

śabdarāśividhāne ca samāhita phalapradam |
sarvaśaktisamaṃyuktamādyatrayamanaṃtaram ||

hṛdinyāsaṃ ca deveśi kartavyaṃ ca vidhānataḥ |
niyāṃ śaktiyutāṃ devīṃ śiranyāse prakīrtitam ||

pṃcapaṃca mahādevī śaktyādi na śivā smṛtā |
śaktistu tatra saṃbhinnaṃ ṣaṣṭha * * tanucchadam ||

saptame vīkṣaṇe devi śrīkaṃṭhaṃ luptaśaktitaḥ |
bījatrayaṃ tathā cānyamanaṃtaṃ śaktisaṃyutam ||

nyasetsaśabdaṃ deveśi astraṃ sarvatra rakṣitam |
navādhāreṣu tadvarṇāndaśamaṃ mūrti vinnyaset ||

saṃpuṭi kṛtyamaṃtreṇa mātṛkaṃ sa navātmajam |
japādau tu viśeṣeṇa tena niṣkalato bhavet ||

devī maṃtreṇa devena tatvayā dravya lakṣaṇam |
tataḥ saṃpūjayed devi niścalenāṃtarātmanā ||

ātmayāgaṃ purākṛtvā gaṃdhapuṣparasādibhiḥ |
umāvākprathamaṃ devi vravīddīrghaṃ tu deśikaḥ ||

vittādi viśvadiksaṃsthā devatāḥ pūjaye kramāt |
p. 84b) mātaro digvidiksaṃsthā bhairavāḥ pūrvavatpriye ||

vāsudevāḥ kugehastāḥ śatadhā ca sukhān punaḥ |
karālī vasatī devī sumukhā durmukhā punaḥ |

krodhinī staṃbhinī caiva sarasvatyā ca dhūrjaṭī |
cāpabāṇadharāścaitā aruṇāruṇabhūṣaṇāḥ ||

ya * * viṣivaddevi śrībījenavadīrghakaiḥ ||

vyāghrājinādi viṣṭare viṣṭolakṣacaṃdraṃ japedvaśī |
juhuvaḥ svarasāhasraṃ madhubhiḥ kṣīrasaṃbhavaiḥ ||

samidbhiśca galakṣākṣaiḥ sādhakaḥ khecaro bhavet |
athānyaṃ saṃpravakṣyāmi śṛṇu devi prayatnataḥ ||

pratyaṃgireti vikhyātā nāmnā trailokya mohinī |
sādhakānāṃ hitārthāya tvayi snehāt sureśvari ||

sunī kṛtī samābhinnāmādāvaṃte ca kārayet |
bhujairaṣṭādaśairbhinnāmekavaktrāṃ trilocanām ||

arjunovātha deveśi uditārka samaprabhām |
cāpavāṇaukhaḍgakhaṭṭau śaṃkhāsinalinaṃgadām ||

sūṇipāśā hi ghaṃṭāṃ ca varadā bhaya śobhitām |
daṃḍāśani kapālaṃ ca mātuluṃgaṃ ca vibhratīṃ ||

triśūlaṃ muṃḍasaṃyuktaṃ muḍasraṅnaṃḍitāṃ śivām |
dhyātvaināṃ prajaped vidyāṃ lakṣamātraṃ sureśvari ||

vrīhībhiḥ puṃḍarīkaiśca lājābhiḥ keśarodbhavaiḥ |
sāṃprataṃ śṛṇu deveśi vidyārūpābhigarbhitām ||

śūnyādau vā ca mokṣa aṃtevāraṃ niyojayet |
namasānaṃtaraṃ devi hṛṣṭāṃte vā samuddharet ||

śaśimadhyagataṃ devi jvālaśaktivibhūṣitam |
vedyantaṃ ḍāgamadhyatatyāsi madhyaṃ sahāṃta yuk ||

p. 85a)

paramātmikāṃ mahādevīṃ bhujaṃgāsanasaṃsthitām |
karālikāṃ samuddhṛtya rudrāṃ tena vibhūṣitām ||

kapālinīṃ mātṛgehe maṃdare sūkṣmabhūṣitām |
dakṣe * ca tato devi cakriṇī gaṃdhasaṃyutām ||

ekādaśe ṛṣisthāne kevalaṃ nādinī punaḥ |
vāmādi varṇayogena sāsimadhyaṃ punastathā ||

ekādaśe dvi gaṃ bījaṃ pannagāsanabhūṣitam |
vadane ca mahādevi ratna śrīkaṃṭhayukpunaḥ ||

rudrasamutyanarakaṃ rāśinerṇān punarlikhet |
aṣṭādaśabhujairdevi tathā pratyaṃgire padam ||

parasainyaṃ parakarmavidhyaṃsini padaṃ punaḥ |
maṃtravicchedinī paścā sarvaśatruccāṭanī punaḥ ||

sarvabhūtaśca damanī sarvadevāśca vaṃdhayuk |
sarvavighnāṃśca cchiṃdhiyugaṃ guṇontaṃ sūkṣmamūrddhniṣu ||

krodhaṃ ca avivarṇena bhāgamadhyaṃ tu kevalam |
sarvaduṣṭān bhakṣayuga jvālā jihve karālayuk || 
 
vakre ca sarvapātrāṇi sphoṭayaṃ dvitayaṃ bhavet |
śṛṃkhalāṃ troṭayadvaṃdvaṃ vādhāṃ caiva sureśvari ||

mahāvāraṃ samuddhṛtya no aṃtaṃ pannagāsanam |
tarumadhye ca tārā * yāmo madhyena bheditam || 

paṃcāntakaṃ tanontasyaṃ sūkṣmabhairavabhūṣitam |
navādi varṇamuddhṛtya vāmādi varṇake kuru ||

dvā viṃśatyakṣarairdevi ātmaśaktyā hṛdi nyaset |
daśabhūtā suraiścaiva jñānaśaktyā śivonnatam ||

triṃśadbhistu śikhā proktamicchāśaktyā ca vinyaset |
caturdaśaikavarṇaistu raudra śaktyā tanucchidam ||

vidaśaistu mahādevi kriyāśaktyā ca locanaiḥ |
daśāṣṭasaṃkhyakaṃ caiva śastraśaktyā ca vinyaset ||

p. 85b) mūrddhnibhrūmadhye nayane śrotrayoḥ karṇayornnasoḥ |
āsyekaṃṭhe ca dormūle kūrpare hṛdi vinyaset ||

nābhipārśvadvayormūle kaghāṃtālu dvaye punaḥ |
pādayośca mahādevi vinyasedupadeśataḥ ||

pūjāṃ kṛtvā vidhānena pūrvoktāṃgāramāntaḥ |
iti sarvaṃ sureśāni samānilagamasyataḥ ||

pādauprasārjyana japedutkaṭāsanavarjitaḥ |
paścārddheśamane vāpi rathyāyāṃ nīcasaṃnidhau ||

na japedriktabhūmyāṃ ca khaṭvāyāṃ harmmyamadhyataḥ |
avīkharamārjāra aṃgāroṣṭre tu reṇakam ||

keśadhvajanakhānāṃ ca snānavastragatodakam |
aśrīkaraṃ manuṣyāṇāṃ aśuddhaṃ saṃspṛśedyadi ||

eteṣāṃ saṃbhave vāpi kuryātsuryyāgni darśanam |
ācamya vājapeccheṣaṃ kṛtvā vā prāṇasaṃyamam ||

tuṣṭīravīkaṃ ca kīlagno muktakeśo gaṇairvṛtaḥ |

apavitro niṣiddhaśca vācāṃ viglāṃpanaṃ nava |
krodhomadaḥ kṣudhātaṃdrīniṣṭīvanavijṛṃbhanam ||

haṃsavāgeśvarībījaṃ sarvatraivātrasaṃpuṭaṃ |
vinyastavedikā madhye śaṃkustatrāvalaṃbanam ||

śaṃkusūtrāgrasamiti sūtrayogāditaḥ kramāt |
prākpratyaksaṃsthitaṃ sūtraṃ dakṣiṇontaragaṃ tathā ||

varttulāgraṃ tu saṃsthāpya lāṃkayet koṇamatsyakam |
ṣaṭsūtranyāsamārgeṇa tatsūtraṃ caturāśritam ||

sūtrāṇyā sphālayet tatra aṣṭāviṃśatisaṃkhyayā |
prāgagrāṇi udagrāṇi sūtrāṇyaṃ gulamānataḥ ||

koṣṭha saptaśataṃ nūnaṃ navaviṃśatikottaram |
aiṃdramaḍalamadhyasthaṃ bāhye paṃkti dvayaṃ nyaset ||

p. 86a)

dvāraśobhopaśobhā ca catuḥpaṃkti catuṣṭakam |
mahāvīṃthyā catuṣkaṃ syāt pārśvayorubhayorapi ||

tanmadhyeropavīthyaṃtaṃ paṃktidvayamudāhṛtam |
tanmadhye paṃcadaśabhiḥ paṃktibhiḥ kamalaṃ bhavet ||

navapadmamidaṃ proktaṃ navavīthi vibhūṣitam |
ṣaḍbhiḥ ṣaḍbhiḥ padairdvāraṃ dvārāṣṭakasamujvalam ||

caturbhiśca caturbhiśca śobhāścaivopaśobhayā |
koṇekoṇapadaiḥ ṣaḍbhiḥ paṃktidvayasamākṛtiḥ ||

uktamārgamanenaiva bāhyevalayamālikhet |
caturbhirvahulairbhāgāṃ cchitvācchitvātha vartayet ||

prathamaṃ karṇikā kṣetraṃ dvitīyaṃ keśarāśriyam |
tṛtīyaṃ dalasaṃdhī syāt patrāṇi ca caturthake ||

tatrāṣṭasūtrayogena vartayedaṣṭapatrakam |
catuṣkoṇeṣu sarveṣu vajrāṣṭakaṃ samālikhet ||

mātṛṇāmapi sarvāṃ sāṃ devabhadragaṇeśayoḥ |
navapīṭhamidaṃ proktaṃ sadyaḥ pratyayakārakam ||

vyomarekhābhavettāsāṃ padmānāṃ vāyusaṃsthitāḥ |
evaṃ likhitvā patreṇa rajasā pūrayecchubhām ||

karṇikāpītavarṇena rekhā sarvāḥ sitena ca |
rakta pīta bahiḥ śuklaṃ keśarāṇi prapūrayet ||

patrāṇi raktavarṇena śyāmavarṇenasaṃṣikam |
padmabāhyaṃ tu yatkṣetraṃ tatkṛṣṇena prapūrayet ||

dvārāṇi śuklavarṇena śobhāṃ raktena pūrayet |
upaśobhāñca śuklena koṇān śyāmena pūrayet ||

balibhiścalitābhiśca vīthikāṃ paribhūṣayet |
pītavarṇena rajasā aiṃdraṃ tu paripūrayet ||

rajasā kṛṣṇavarṇena ante śvetena pūrayet |
evaṃ maṃḍalamākhyātaṃ sarvāmnāya kramāgatam ||

p. 86b)

maṃḍalesmn mahādevī pūjayet sarva devatāḥ |
tasyamāyārcitāḥ sarvā tārcitā sādhakena tu ||

abhiṣekaṃ prakurvīta pāraṃparyakramāgatam |
tāvatsekaṃ prakurvīta yāvatpratyayatāṃ brajet ||

pratyayastu mahādevī gurorājā prabhāvataḥ |
madhulubdho yathābhṛṃgaḥ puṣpātpuṣpāṃtaraṃ vrajet ||

jñānalubdhastathā śiṣyo gurorgurvantaraṃ vrajet |
śaktihīnam guruṃ prāpya śiṣye muktiḥ kutaḥ priye ||

mūlacchinne kṛte vṛkṣe kutaḥ puṣpaphalādikam |
ekānekavibhinnena kalaśādyairmakārajaiḥ ||

makāra rahite devi na tṛptiṃ yātitāḥ kramāt |
kuṃḍe dvayastu sammiśraṃ mayādau viniyojayet ||

tenasarvatra saṃprokṣyasaṃtarpya ca kulakramam |
saṃtarpyabhairavaṃ bhaktyā bhairavī bhavaśātanī ||

tadrūpadhārako devi svayaṃ svīkṛtya yatnataḥ |
bhairavohamiti jñātvā mudrāṃ badhvā tu dakṣiṇe ||

vāme coktitaṃ kṛtvā maṃtraṃ kuru śivātmakam |
cakramelapakaṃ tatra namaskṛtya vidhānataḥ ||

snāpayedyoginī cakraṃ yathā devī jayaṃ kuru |
abdairdvādaśabhirdevi sarvadā siddhimāpnuyāt ||

ityetadviśvamākhyātaṃ vidhānaṃ surapūjitam |
paścimāmnāya hīnaiśca aprāpyaṃ deśikottamaiḥ ||


iti śrīkubjikāmatenavakoṭisaṃhitāyāṃ caturaśītisāhasre 
bhairavīnirṇayonāmapaṃcacatvāriṃśatitamaḥ paṭalaḥ || 0 ||




śrībhairava uvāca ||


ekānekavibhinnastāḥ pūrvoktā varavarṇini |
varṇamabhyāsataḥ pūrvānavakoṭyāsamāśritān ||

p. 87a)

saṃhitādhyāya kalpaṃ ca taṃtraṃ trividhamucyate |
saṃhitā cottamaṃ devi tathā dhyāyaṃ madhyasaṃsmṛtam ||

kalpaṃ tu kalpasaṃjñoyaṃ saṃhitā vismṛtaṃ bhavet |
tato dhyāyaṃ samākhyātaṃ saṃhitā tu pratiṣṭhayā ||

adhyāye nārcane devi kalpe maṃtra japādiyam |
guṇāndoṣānparā mṛṣyameṣa teṣu viśeṣataḥ ||

vargavarṇātmakaṃ piṃḍaṃ prākṛtaṃ devasaṃbhavam |
nava praṇavasaṃbhinnamayaśāni ca varjayet ||

vārāha nṛsiṃhāka dhrūvaprāsādameva ca |
adau madhye tathā cāṃte bhārayuktāḥ krameṇa tu ||

strīpuṃ napuṃsakā jñeyā vauṣaḍantāḥ striyo matāḥ |
ṣaṭphaḍaṃtāḥ puruṣāstritattvā napuṃsakāḥ ||

mātṛkāsarvavidyānāṃ maṃtrāṇāṃ jananī parā |
uddhārastatra kurvīta yasrāsaddhato naraḥ ||

kuhūmadhye kaḍadhvaṃ ca jīvaṃ prāṇaṃ samuddharet |
kailāsamaṃdire devi yadvarṇaṃ karṇapūrakam ||

tadaṃnādhaḥ sthitaṃ varṇaṃ tṛtīyaṃ caṃḍanāyakam |
mālinīvāmapāde ca śikhāsaṃbhūtamaṃdire ||

bhujaṃga bhairavīṃ devīṃ kialāse vāmamaṃdire |
akrūraṃ ca śivedattaṃ bījaṃ kīlānalaprabham ||

asyajāpanahomena daitya dānava rākṣasāḥ |
siddhāṃśenātrasaṃdeho devāścāpi tapodhanāḥ ||

aghorasya prabhāvena devi kiṃ khathayāmite |
asya bījaprabhāvena padmanābhaḥ pratāpavān ||

sutī sutī ca varteti prāṇāyāmaṃ niyojayet |
tato nyasaṃ pravakṣyāmi asyaghorasya suṃdari ||

ādau tu viśvamālāyā manaṃtaṃ gaṃdhamādane |
p. 87b) śive akrūrasaṃbhinnaṃ uttare niṣadhālaye ||

māyā devī samāpekṣya dhruvāṃtaṃ krūramastakam |
dvitīyaṃ ca tṛtīyaṃ ca anaṃtaṃ prathamaṃ viduḥ ||

bījānyetāni deveśi tristhāne ghoramastake |
brahmaviṣṇuśivāścaitā icchājñāna kriyāśca te ||

evaṃ tu tatsamāyojya japtakaṃ copadeśataḥ |
mālinī svāṃtamadhyasthaṃ maṃḍare sūkṣmagaṃ punaḥ ||

kailāsamaṃdire devi jhaṃṭīśaṃ mūrddhni dāpayet |
punareva maheśāni hū aṃtaṃ mūrdha tadbhavet ||

karālī tṛcayādistā vajriṇī * * madhyagā |
krodharudraṃ samuddhṛtya jhaṃṭīśenavibhūṣitam ||

tristhānaṃ bījamuddhṛtya anaṃtā krūramadhyagam |
prathamaṃ maddhyamaṃ devi trimūrtinniṣadhe sthitam ||

prajñāṃ ca vāmakarṇasthā madho deśe pratiṣṭitāṃ |
paṃcavargānya varṇāni teṣāmagre pradāpayet ||

tataḥ pūrvokta vidyāyā varṇāśca tridaśā smṛtāḥ |
māninyā bhāvanetrāṃ tu dārukaṃ mātṛmaṃdire ||

dvibiṃdumastake devi caṃḍiśaṃ vāmamaṃdire |
agne catvāri varṇāni pūrvoktāyā yathā kramāt ||

dakṣajānusthitaṃ varṇaṃ lokādi śirasi sthitam |
tadadhaḥ kārayed devi prāṇaṃ jīvaṃ ca mādanī ||

yaidbhānaṃ tena saṃbhinnaṃ dvitīyaṃ ca tataḥ śṛṇu |
ādi bīja dvaye devi vāmāntaṃ mūrddhnivinyaset ||

guṇavarṇaṃ punarlikhya tribījaṃ pūrvameva hi |
kailāsamaṃdire devi śrīkaṃṭhaṃ cātrasaṃsthitam ||

ekādaśaṃ caiva gāyatryā * * bhūṣitaṃ kuru |
navake dīpakāṃ devi īśenāthavibhūṣitāṃ ||

p. 88a)

punareva maheśāni ubhauvarṇautadagrataḥ |
bhūyaśca varṇayugalamuddharedupadeśataḥ ||

anaṃtānantasaṃbhinnaṃ ṭhaṭhamadhyaṃ tadagrataḥ |
ekanetrabhavaṃ devi dīpanyānaṃtabheditam ||

agnetaṃ tu punarlikhya jihvāgreṇorddhataḥ sthitam |
tena mūrddhni gataṃ kṛtvā tadvatkrodhaṃ sakāṃtakam ||

bhūyastau aśvadeveśa vāruṇaṃ sūkṣma bheditam |
ṛṣimadhye tu cāamuḍāṃ vāmādi mastakaṃ vrajet ||

evameṣā mahāvidyā devī mūrddhā ca siddhidā |
brahmāṇaṃ pādayordevi guhyadeśaṃkayāmatam ||

udare ca tathā rudraṃ kaṃṭhe vai īśvaraṃ nyaset |
sā śivaṃ ca bhruvormmadhye tadūrddhaṃ ca paraṃparam ||

dviraṃḍabhairavaṃ devi ṛṣimadhye tu kevalam |
śivāyāṃ tu tanoṃtaśca vāme madhye ca cakriṇaṃ ||

urumadhyeṃtarālaṃ ca merorddhe saṃvyavasthitam |
mūrddhni tasyāstu deveśi kalāṃ devīṃ pravinyaset ||

saṃdhye caṃḍe tathā aṃbe nitye klinne dureśvari |
rakte ghore viśālākṣī sarvatraivaṃ niyojayet ||

vidye bhadre ca ādyante aṃte ādau ca madhyataḥ |
madyādau ādi madhyānte bījānāṃ vyatya yena tu ||

aṃtarānaṃtamātmānamante aṃte ca nātmikīm |
vīṇāpaṃcakamevādau ramame tu vilomataḥ ||

māninīgrathanaṃ devi guṇontakālamagrataḥ |
lokāṃtaṃ mūrddhni vinyasya punaścatvāri agrataḥ ||

padmodbhavaṃ hasānaṃtaṃ lokādiṃ mastakaṃ nyaset |
graho tu magrataḥ kṛtvā caturthyāṃ caturo bhavet ||

p. 88b) māyādevī tridhānyorkva vāraṃ cāpi navātmakam |
tatopyevaṃ samuddhṛtya krūrmeśāṃtaṃ sureśvari ||

prathamaṃ kṛtuvarṇāni nakulīśaṃ dīpanaṃ yutam |
bhāgamadhyaṃ ca krodhīśaṃ kālaṃ ca kṛtumadhyagam ||

anaṃtaṃ gaṃdha śikhare yāmo madhyaṃ ca padmajam |
hṛdinyāsaṃ prakurvīta bījapaṃcaka melanāt ||

karālikaṃ samuddhṛtya dhruvādye nāsanaṃ kuru |
pūtanāmagrataḥ sthāpya mudrīśaṃ kātakohajam ||

maṃdarasthaṃ vijānīyāt pāvanyā mūrddhnisūkṣmajaṃ |
atrīśaṃ maṃdirādau ca pāpāyavaṃ padmayoninā ||

śivanyāsaṃ prakarttavyaṃ guṇavaṃtaṃ tadagrataḥ |
vajriṇā ca dvidhā kṛtvā bhujaṃgaṃ mūrddhni dāpayet ||

bhujaṃgaṃ bhairavaṃ devi agrataḥ kevalaṃ likhet |
śikhādūtīṃ pravinyasye cchikhāyāṃ ca na saṃśayaḥ ||

bhūtayugmāṃtakairdevi dūtyāa ca kavacaṃ nyaset |
lokādi varṇaṃ ca yadbījaṃ bhūtamaṃdire * * ||

kevalaṃ tatsamādāya hatyāttaṃ agrato nyaset |
raktabhairavamuddhṛtya vāmāṃguliṣumadhyataḥ ||

vedādyamakṣaraṃ devi amarīśaṃ purataḥ sthitam |
pāvanyā hṛdi yaṃtasthaṃ taṃ * * * * * * * ||

nāsāgre saṃsthitaṃ devi mūrddhni tasyāstu dāpayet |
rudrādityapadmayoniśca vāyavye viniyojayet ||

urddhvaṃ catvāri varṇāni kālaṃ prāṇastha biṃdukam |
tārikā hetumuccārya netra dṛtyāsavinyaset ||

karālībhipātasaṃbhinnā hṛdyaṃta syāstu dāpayet |
krodhabhairavamuddhṛtya umākāṃtaṃ tadagrataḥ ||

lokādi maṃdire devi purato biṃdubhairavam |
p. 89a) anaṃtenāthacakri ṅettaṃ cāstraṃ samuddharet ||

sarvatraivaṃ prayuktāni vidyāprāṇā na saṃśayaḥ |
vaktranyāse yathāpūrvaṃ vidyāṃ cāthasureśvarīm ||

kalānyāsaṃ prakartavyaṃ śikhāgrāccaraṇāvadhiḥ |

cā uṃ daitye bhagavatiṃgya kuleśvarīṃ krīṃ hrīṃ hūṃ hūṃ āṁ kṣīṃ haṃ 
kṣeṃ hauṃ uṃ iṃ ṇa na mekṣaḥ śvaṁ śrīṁ prauṃ prīṃtyaghorāmukhi muktikā 
paṃcāṃ aghore aghore yaṃ raṃ laṃ vaṃ haṃ saṃ kli lokini mahākili viddhe ||

catuḥṣaṣṭikrame vidye mūrddhni deśa vidhānataḥ |
kalānte vaistu sā jñeyā pūjāyāṃ ca viśeṣataḥ ||

sānnidhyaṃ kurute sātra nityā devi kulārṇave |
śaktayonaṃgarūpāḥ syuścāpesu namitaṃ kuśam ||

veṇī vajrā viśālākṣā vāmāvarta paribhramāt |
akṣoḍaṃ ca śikhāgre tu paścimena tu vāgbhavam ||

ṛkṣakarṇīṃ śikhāmadhye śikhāmūleṣu vā nyaset |
kṣaṇadāṃ ca śiro deśe veṣṭanekṣayatāṃ punaḥ ||

piṃgākṣīṃ tālumadhye ca kodaṃḍeṣu kṣayāvatīṃ |

nīlā dakṣiṇanetrasthā vāmanetrelupaṃ nyaset |

viśālāṃ cibuke nyasya tārāyāṃ dakṣakarṇagam |
vaḍavāṃ vāmakarṇe ca tāravāṃ śrī ca karṇagāṃ ||

mahākrūraṃ kaṃṭhadeśe krodhāṃgāṃkukṣakakṣagām |
bhayānakāṃ vāmakakṣe bījānāṃ melanaṃ yathā ||

p. 89b)

sarvajñāṃ ca karālīṃ ca stanayorvāmadakṣayoḥ |

karṇe dakṣe vinyasyed bāhau dakṣasya madhyataḥ |
haste ca meghanādāṃ ca caṃḍāgreṣṭhaṃgulīṣu ca ||

dakṣahastanakhāgreṣu kālakaṃṭhaṃ tu vinyaset |
navaprasādī vinyasyedvāmakaṃṭhe vilocane ||

bāhau haste cāṃgulīṣu caṃdrāṃ candrāvatīṃ punaḥ |
prapaṃcaṃ vinyased devi vāmahasta nakheṣu ca ||

pralāyāṃtāṃ picuvaktra dvaye pārśve pravinyaset |
piśācīṃ vāmapārśve ca piśitāśīṃ dakṣapṛṣvataḥ ||

vakṣau ca lolvayājñeyā vamaṇāṃ dakṣajānuni |

vikṛtaṃ vinyased devi vāmorau viśvarūpiṇīm ||

tadagre yamajihvāṃ ca vāmajānusthale sthitām |
japaṃtīṃ vāmajaṃghāyāṃ durjarāṃ kāmagulphake ||

pādāṃgulīṣu deveśi yamāṃti kāṃcanasaṃśayaḥ |
yamadvāre viḍālīṃ ca vegagāṃvāmapādagām ||

vāmapādatale devi pūjanāṃ sa varṇāṃ nyat |
p. 90a) evaṃ nyāse kṛte devi yoginīnāṃ priyo bhavet ||

sarvasiddhikaraṃ devi sarvādyavinivāraṇam |
evametā mahādhipa devī sarvārthasiddhidā ||

arcitāḥ sādhakasyaitāḥ prakurvaṃti yathepsitam |

japaṃ vinā na siddhyeta na kutaḥ phaladāyakā |

tiṣṭhan gacchan śayāno vā maithune bhojanepi ca |
mūṣāṃ vā ca vinā jāpyaṃ vidhātavyaṃ prayatnataḥ ||

iti śrīkubjikāmateṣaṭcatvāriṃśatitamaḥ paṭalaḥ || 0 ||

p. 90b)

brahmādayastu śakreṇa muktibhistasya tuṣṭuvuḥ |

dvitīyaṃ ca tṛtīyaṃ ca mahādevī samudbhavam ||

rudrāṇāṃ maṃdare cātha utanorddhatasaṃsthitam |
tasyamūrddhni pradātavyaṃ gatvānaṃtaṃ na saṃśayaḥ ||

vinatāṃ gāṃttamuddhṛtya tasyādhaḥ kālamālikhet ||

p. 91a)

prāṇarudraṃ samuddhṛtya madhye ca aṃbikām |

rudrāṃtaṃ hṛdi vinyasya tadūrddhe ca śiro dvayam |

anenaivaṃ prayuṃjītadaśaikena maheśvari ||

p. 92a)

tadagrādagraparyantamaṃgulīmūlamagnage |

nādiphāntaṃ smṛtaṃ vargaṃ aye devatāpatī ||

nirgataṃ dvādaśaṃ rudraṃ khamapuṣpa tu dvayena tu |
sūtraṃ saṃtānakaṃ prajñā gūḍhārthena vravīmyaham ||

saṃvartamaṃdalodyānaṃ śūnyagrahavinirmitam |
iti siddhadvayaṃ tatva guhāṃ tuṣṇīṃ samādhiṣu ||

pratyayājñā bhavet tatra vṛkṣaṃ caiva kadaṃbakam |
saptāviṃśatibhiścchedairbhinnaṃ maṃthāna bhairavam ||

śivaśaktisamācāramadvaitā cāragotrakam |
pīṭhanāmadvayaṃ devi yathoktaṃ tava gahvare ||

nyāsa yogaṃ ca uddhāraṃ tathā yadapi śaṃkaram |
piṭhādau varṇamālikhya paṃcakai ślokaṃ navātmakam ||

kāmarūpaṃ hākaḍokaṃ nepālaṃ pauṃḍravarddhanam |
purasthiraṃ kānyakubjaṃ pūrṇāṃgiryaṃ tadārbudam ||

p. 94b)

āmnātakeśvaraṃ kāstraṃ triśrotaṃ kāmakoṭaram |
kailāsaṃ bhṛgu nagaraṃ kedāraṃ caṃdrasūryakam ||

śrīkaṃṭhaṃ kākiṇījālaṃ kalutaṃ ca punaḥ punaḥ |
herikoṭaṃ ca gokarṇaṃ vinyasen merukeśvaram ||

aṭṭahāsaṃ ca virajaṃ rājaveṇyaṃ mahāpathaṃ |
kollagiryaṃ kollagiriyaṃ saurājaṃ ca jayaṃtikāṃ ||

ujjayinyākhyāṃ caritraṃkiri hastināpuraṃ |
iṃdukeśaṃ prayāgākhyaṃ puṣṭaṃ māyāpurīṃ tathā ||

jarasāṃ kalayaṃ caiva śailabhekaṃḍameva ca |
mādreṃdraṃ vārukaṃ caivaṃ hiraṇyapurameva ca ||

mahālakṣmītanodyāna cchāyācchatramataḥ param |
ete pīṭhāḥ samākhyātāḥ paṃcāśat parikīrtitāḥ ||

sṛṣṭinyāyena saṃjātā vāmāvarttaparibhramāt |
vāmādyā ca śaktīnāṃ madhyagre kūṭamaṃḍale ||

udayaṃ svaravarṇānā muktaṃ vai pīṭhavigraham |
mudrāmaṃḍalamadhye tu māyayāgahanī kṛtam ||

svaravarṇavibhāgaṃ ca meru śrīśaṃkara kramam |
ādyaprastāvamākhyātaṃ saṃtānaṃ siddhisādhanam ||

ānaṃdavimalaṃ jyotiḥ śaktiḥ kuṃḍalinī kalā |
umāvismṛti taptā vā mukhyaikaścaika pādakam ||

vāguraṃpadyayoniśca tripāde brahmasāgaraḥ |
vārṇe dvayaṃ mahāratnaṃ galitastapta cāmaram ||

tāṃḍavaṃ ca mahāhāraṃ sthalamunmattasiṃhakam |
japānaścaiva saṃvarttiḥ kaṃkālaśca trilocanā ||

vāsudevo virūpaśca kamalodaravāhanaḥ |
dhanurddharo jihvajaśca dvijovārastathaiva ca ||

p. 95a)

amṛtaṃ bhadrakālaśca māruto hyagnipārthivaḥ |
varuṇaḥ śaktipālaśca pavanāmā tathaiva ca ||

sṛṣṭiśca bhairavī devī kubjiketi prakīrtitā |
anulomena vinyāsaṃ vilomenasamuddharet ||

ete vai śabdanāmāni ghaṇṭādau pratiropitāḥ |
catuḥśṛṃgo mahāmeruḥ pūrvoktavidhiyuktitaḥ ||

kālikā tu yathāproktā tadūrddhena vadāmyaham |
adhaḥ kālāgnirudraṃ ca tadūrddhaṃ tu trayodaśa ||

daṃḍayaśca samākhyātārugādyā ūrddhaṃ gātradhaḥ |
punarurddhaikādaśārudrā maṃthāne adhikāriṇaḥ ||

navasiddhāstadūrddhe tu punarurdddhe saptayoginī |
paṃcamaṃḍala ūrddhe tu aḥ ṣaḍādyā krameṇa tu ||

śaktitrayaṃ tadūrddhe tu tatrordde nāthaciṃcini |
kālāgnyādi hi nāthāntaṃ uktante kurutrayam ||

gahvarārthasya ca dehe tu siddhasaṃtānavarjitam |
na pūrvaṃ paścimācāryaṃ ityuktaṃ kādi bhedi hi ||

varṇanyāsaṃ maheśāni śṛṇu prastāramuttamam |
akāraṃ cādināthaṃ ca merurddheśaṃvyavasthitam ||

akāraśca jakāraśca olitritaya devatāḥ |
bhakāraṃ ca makārañca takāraṃ ca ikārakam ||

yathaite saṃsmṛtābhūtā devadevo maheśvaraḥ |
cavakārasakāraṃ ca heyahākārameva ca ||

saptaita mātaro devi lokānugraha kārakāḥ |
japhakārapakāraśca śavalāgniśca lāṃgaliḥ ||

ṭakāraṃ ca maheśāni grahāste ca na saṃśayaḥ |
nyapakāraṃ ca nadhakādakāraṃ rathakārakam ||

tanakāraṃ ca dakāraṃ ca ṭatakāraṃ tathaiva ca |
p. 95b) ete rudrāḥ samākhyātāḥ santānenādhikāriṇaḥ ||

ṛ ṝ ḷ ḹ kāraṃ e oṃ tukārameva ca |
oṃkāraṃ biṃdusargaṃ ca kakhagāṃtān maheśvari ||

ājñāsiddhāstu vikhyātā ete rudrāḥ prakīrtitāḥ |
ciṃcinī nāthanāmānaṃ kathākhyāmi maheśvari ||

sarvaśūnyaṃ tu madhye tu kārmukoparisaṃsthitam |
evametan mayākhyātaṃ pūrvasaṃketakaṃ dvidham ||

tritaye ca mahādevi puṣpaṃ tripuravācakam |
yena vijñānamātreṇa saṃtāna kramiṇo bhavet ||

sādha ūrddhagataṃ piṃḍamādāvaṃtavibhūṣitam |
ādyā yāvadbhave sthāne parṇātmāsanarūpiṇī ||

ādyāmeva hi vidyāyā madimadho ca yojayet |
ūrddhe bījatrayasyāsya vidyāmaṃtrāsanasthitā ||

gṛhītvā varṇamekaikamādyāmādyā tṛtīyayoḥ |
apye tu mūrddhni sādhena vidyā cakrāsana sthitā ||

viparītastu taurārau sorddha ūrddhaṃ tvanā dṛtau |
datvā tṛtīyavidyāyāṃ kāmajāvāstu deśataḥ ||

sā tu siddhāsanagatā sapta vidyā prakāśitā |
dharmādharmagatiṃ jñātvā śivaśaktiṃ niyojayet ||

vahninā dīpitā vidyā sphurantīm sphurā bhavet |
datvā ca bījatrayasyorddhe varṇye yā sādhayaṃ aṃtagau ||

yāṃtasthā ādyavighnau ca ācāhanavidhau parā |
karpūrāṃtaṃ mahābījaṃ śāṃtāṃtaṃ dadho nyaset ||

mudrayā śirasā krāntaṃ sādhake norddhabhūṣitam |
eṣa netro mahāmaṃtraḥ sarvakāmaphalapradaḥ ||

uttamāṃgaṃ śunāśīraṃ adhaḥ śvāsā niyojayet |
p. 96a) ādyaṃ caturthasaṃyuktaṃ biṃdunorddhavibhūṣitam ||

tṛtīyamakṣaraṃ caiva devyā vai parikīrtitam |
nāṃterāmiṃdrasaṃyuktamādyāturyeṇabheditam ||

navenaśirasāyuktaṃ tṛtīyaṃ bījamuttamam |
eṣā kuleśvarī vidyā samayācārapālinī ||

japapūjāvidhānena akṣaraḥ sarvasiddhidaḥ |
athātaḥ saṃpravakṣyāmi mūlavidyāṃ varānane ||

ya yā vijñātayā samyak sarvametat prasiddhyati |
athamadhyagataṃ guhyagraumadhyagataṃ kuru ||

tasyā madhye nyaset paścādakārorddhaṃ vyavasthitam |
kāṃtasādhanayakārāṃta māyā biṃdu nirodhanaḥ ||

vāguroyaṃ samākhyātaḥ samyak pratyayakārakaḥ |
trayāṇāṃ sārdvavarṇanāmekaikasyāsane nyaset ||

ādyayorddhnaṃ pakārāṃtamevādvarṇanāṃvitam |
kāmabījaṃ maheśāni sa rahasyaṃ prakāśitam ||

yasyoccāraṇamātreṇa jagatkṣobhaḥ prajāyate |
tṛtīyaṃ saṃpravakṣyāmi śaktibījaṃ mahādbhutam ||

thādhaṃ nādhaṃ samāyuktaṃ gādyaṃ nādha sa yo ciradhaḥ |
aḥpūjyadūrddhaṃ saṃyuktaṃ śaktibījaṃ samuddhṛtam ||

anenajñānamātreṇa kuryātsaptaviṣakṣayam |
eṣāvidyāsamākhyātā sarvāścarya karīvarā ||

(eṣāvidyāsamākhyātārpyakarīvarā |)?????

anayā vidyayā samyak nāsti nadyatasādhayet |
kākaro modano jñeyaḥ saṃketo biṃdu mālinī |

śaktibījaṃ sakāraśca śeṣāvarṇāyathā sthitāḥ |
hakāro bhagavānkālaḥ surāpe caiva vāhitā ||

p. 96b) akāraḥ praṇavaḥ sākṣāt somasūryātmakaḥ prabhuḥ |
saṃkocaśca vikāraśca hasa ityakṣaradvayam ||

prathamaṃ ca kakārākhya hakāraṃ ca dvitīyakam |
śaktibījaṃ tṛtīyaṃ tu oṃ i oṃ vyatyayena tu ||

tatopari mahāyoni kuṃḍalākhyā visarpiṇī |
mahāmāyā mahāghorā caturthā ca sureṇa tu ||

guhyaśaktiḥ svarūpeṇa jñākārākhyaṃ mahājvalaṃ |
rudraśaktiḥ kakārākhyā kuṃḍalākhyā hareṃdrukam ||

tanmadhye homayecciṃttaṃ kusumaṃ brahmavṛkṣakam |
brahmasthāne tu yatpuṣpamuditaṃ viśvamāyayā ||

tatpuṣpaṃ homayetkuṃḍe na puṣpairanyajānibhiḥ |
majjāmedastathā reto yathaitānmadhuratrayam ||

raktaṃ medastathā reto śiva ekāranirgatam |
śatamaṣṭadale devi japedatha pratarpayet ||

vedyāsenpātrayatproktaṃ guruvaktrāt tu labhyate |
caturasraṃ tato datvā kuryādvāracatuṣṭayam ||

catuṣkoṇasamāyuktaṃ evaṃ syānmaṃḍalottamam |
aṣṭāpade cāṣṭāśīti genācāsu sarvadā ||

aṣṭāṣṭakapadasyāpi sagururbhairavā game |
aṣṭānāmadhurārūḍhaḥ padavyaktikrameṇa yaḥ ||

aṣṭadhā kurute sādhu sagururbhairavaḥ śive |
ṣoḍaśādhāra cakreṇa ṣoḍaśaḥ svarapālakaḥ ||

ṣoḍaśāṃtakanādhāri * * * * * * * * |
caturdaśapadāntastu caturdaśavidhāyataḥ ||

caturdaśabhihīnaḥ sargādaśakauleśvaro dhīmān daśadigjanabhairavaḥ ||
pāśāśvamedhamadhyasthaḥ śastā caturddhā krama vijñāni caturdhāsaṃtato 
yathā ||

p. 97a) yaḥ sadādrodhacaṃḍeśa samūrtistānbiṃdu lakṣasthā trihomānte ca 
cārakaḥ |
trinābhiścakrakuṃḍasya sagnā ekākī vinayatthaṃ yaścaikapadaḥ nāyakaḥ ||

ekākṣaraika cakrasthaḥ sa gururbhairavāgame |
ṣaṣṭi ṣaṣṭirbhavet sūtraiḥ saptasaptakrayojitam ||

caturasraṃ puraḥkṛtvā caturviṃśāṃgulaṃ śubham |
jāpātpaścimagaṃ sūtraṃmagnipaścimagaṃ punaḥ ||

pūrvasūtraṃ tu saṃsthāpya śeṣāṇi trīṇi kopayet |
tṛdaṃḍā tu smṛtā yonistriyāmāsā tridevatā ||

ekaikaṃdaṃḍake prāhuḥ ṣaṭṣaṭsūtrāṇi pādayet |
yonayo yenayā caṃte samastāḥ samasaṃdhayaḥ ||

koṣṭhakānpūnapaṃcāśadbhagarūpāṇi tāni ca |
īśādakāramārabhya vāruṇāṃtaṃ pravinyaset ||

vinyasyen mātṛkān sarvān viṣṇordiśi ca paścimām |
etaccakrāṇi likhetpaṭṭaistadbhedārumaye dṛte ||

trikoṇe ca tripāde ca varṇai kāṇi ca lakṣaṇe |
ṣaṇmāsājjāyate siddhiḥ pūjājapaparāyaṇe ||

ato maṃtrasamuddhāro yathādevi tathā śṛṇu |
ujamadhyagataṃ varṇaṃ ūrddhvena śirasājatam ||

athamadhyagataṃ varṇaṃ paramadhyāsanasthitam |
dvitīyametatkathitamātamadhyasabiṃdukam ||

rahaṃ madhyaṃ adhisthaṃ aṃkamadhyena nā * taṃ |
prathamā akṣarī vidyā karaśuddhi karī smṛtā ||

sakalaṃ ca kalākhyaṃ ca kevalā hlādanaṃ priye |
dvayoḥ kṛtvā viparyāsaṃ pūrvabījena vairiṇā ||

aṃganyāsa vidhauvidyā dvitīyā ca kṛśodarī |
tatra prakaṭa yoginyaścakre trailokyamohane ||

p. 97b)

aṇimādyā mahādevi siddhayoṣṭau vyavasthitāḥ |
tathaiva guptayoginyaḥ sarvāśāparipūrake ||

kāmākarṣaṇarūpādyā devatāḥ parikīrtitāḥ |
guptaguptatarāśaurisarvasaṃkṣobhakārake ||

anaṃgakusumādyāśca śakradevyā vyavasthitāḥ |
saṃpradāyakramāyātā cakre saubhāgyakārake ||

sarvasaṃkṣobhiṇī pūrvāsaṃsthitā purasuṃdarī |
kulakaulinayoginyaḥ sarvasiddhiḥ pradāyakāḥ ||

sarvasiddhipradādyāstu cakre sarvārthasādhake |
aṣṭasiddhikrameṇaiva sarvasiddhiphalapradā ||

nigarbhadevatāścakre sarvarakṣākare pare |
sarvajñādyā mahādevi saṃsthitā vīravaṃdite ||

rahasya devatāścakre sarvarogahare priye |
vaśinyādyā mahādevyaḥ saṃsthitā paramārthataḥ ||

sarvasiddhimaye cakre paramābhi rahasyakāḥ |
sthitā kāmeśvarī pūrvā mṛtayuḥ pīṭhadevatāḥ ||

sarvānaṃdamaye cakre paripūrṇe sureśvarī |
saṃsthitādyā maṃtramayī mahātripurasuṃdari ||

kaulātīta svabhāvāntaprāntakramasamanvitā |
paramā dvaitaṃ bhāvasthā mahāpūjā parā priye ||

parāyāpyaparāgaurī tritayā ca parāparaḥ |
tasyā nityoditā pūjā tribhirbhedairvyavasthitā ||

tatrādi bhedastripurā dvitīyā tripureśvarī |
tṛtīyāpurasuṃdaryā caturthāpuravāsinī ||

paṃcame tripurasiddhāśca ṣaṣṭe tripuravāsinī |
saptame tripurāsiddhiraṣṭame tripurāṃbikā ||

navame tu maheśāni mahātripurasuṃdarī |

athāpyanaṃtādikapīṭhamadhye madhye gudakṣodagudaprakoṇe ||

ṣaḍādikānapi parāṇi catuṣṭayaṃ ca |

p. 98a) tanmadhyayonerapi cāṣṭayoneḥ santarpayet śrīrurupāṃdukāṃ ca ||

viśvamadhye ca bhrāṃtaṃ dharmamāyendusaṃyutam |
sadadarśaśivaṃ prārābhagevahni dedīpya sarvasaṃ ||

mālinī prathamaṃ kālaṃ śivaṃ * nta vibhūṣitam |
caturthānte ca hṛdayaṃ mahāśī bhavamūrti yuk ||

anena devi kurvīta bhairavyāḥ pīṭhamardanam |
tato rakṣyāmyaśeṣeṇa tastubhaṃ cakramuttamam ||

sṛṣṭirūpaṃ sthitisthaṃ ca saṃhārakramato matam |
tridhāyasthaṃ trikardākhyaṃ vipravāhaṃ trivetanam ||

paṃcāśadbījakhanitā tridhāhintā mahānaye |
yena jñāyeta vai samyak sarvamaṃtra svabhāvataḥ ||

paramārthatve sārabhūta * * * midaṃ mama |
kathayāmāsamāsena cakrasya parame vidhiḥ ||

cakrācāraṃ tu kartavyaṃ tasmin pinyavase priye |
tato nityoditātīma maṃtra * * * * * * * * ||

ādyaṃ karṇaṃ vahiḥ kāryaṃ praṇamya ca muhurmuhuḥ |
uttarābhimukho bhūtvā ālikheccakramuttamam ||

ṣoḍaśāre mahādīpaṃ sṛṣṭirūpaṃ svatejasām |
karṇikāre śvatthayuktaṃ mahātīkṣṇaṃ suśobhanam ||

tasya brāhme tu vai rudraṃ nimiṃ datvā viśeṣataḥ |
ālikhet tu paraṃ divyaṃ cakraṃ ca paramaṃ mahat ||

tatvānnityaparaṃ devī nimiṃ datvā prayatnataḥ |
ālikhet tu tṛtīyaṃ vai cakraṃ saṃsārarūpakam ||

etaccakratrayaṃ ghoraṃ sṛṣṭisthitilayaṃ karam |
tatrasthā mātṛkā devī tridhā kuryād dvidhā tathā ||

kārayet sarvathā nādhe pāraṃparyavyavasthitam |
janyadā tu parityajyavarṇau dvau ghoradarpitau ||

p. 98b)

anacchādyā tu deveśi vāgurādyaharatpayān |
sṛṣṭicakrasya ādau tu ṣoḍaśāni samaṃ tataḥ ||

varṇāni kīlakādīni purvādīśānikaṃtamam |
athavā vinyaset prājñaḥ śūnyaṃ kuryāṇi vai tataḥ ||

tatastiṣṭhati cakrasya varṇānyanyāni ṣoḍaśa |
pūrvādīnigajākāṃta māśānyāṃ tu manu kramāt ||

tato nyāni tu varṇāni ṣoḍaśāni varānane |
saṃhāracakra bhāvajño surāsuraniyojayet ||

pūrvādīśānakaṃ yāvad vā gurāṃ tāni suvrate |
acalasyodarastūrṇaṃ kuryāccakreśvarīṃ śubhām ||

etaccakra sā vinyāsaṃ kārayeccakrasaṃkramam |
sṛṣṭicakrasya yadvarṇaṃ prathamaṃ śaktirūpakaṃ ||

sthitinnavarasairyuktaṃ prathamaṃ varṇanāyakaṃ |
viddhi bhairava tatvena sarvasaubhāgyadāyakam ||

sthiti cakrasya bhāvajñā dvitīyaṃ cānu bhāvakam |
sṛṣṭi dvādaśasaṃyuktaṃ kuruteṃdrenanāyakam ||

dvitīyaṃ varṇasaṃyogaṃ viddhisarvavibhāvakam |
lakṣmībījaṃ tato devi tṛtīyaṃ parikīrtitam ||

sthiti cakrasya deveśi daśamastu samuddharet |
caturthaṃ tu vijānīyāt sarvaisvaryaprakīrtitam ||

sṛṣṭiprathamakaṃ grāhyaṃ saṃhārasyāṣṭamenatam |
paṃcamaṃ tadvijānīyād vidyā paṃcākṣarā bhavet ||

eṣā vidyā tu mūrtīnāṃ sarvathā kālikā krame |
etat te kathitaṃ rudramaṃtroddhāraṃ paraṃ śubham ||

caturthaṃ tu paraṃ jñeyaṃ kramasantānapuṣpakam |
uttarasthaṃ mahādevi yaduktaṃ kālikākrame ||

atra te darśitaṃ mārgaṃ samāsātpaścimakramaṃ ||

p. 99a)

iti śrīkubjikāmate aṣṭacatvāriṃśatitamaḥ paṭalaḥ || 0 ||



śrīdevyuvāca ||


cakrasaṃketakaṃ devamudrāsaṃketameva ca |
pīṭhasaṃketakaṃ cānyadvistareṇavadaprabho ||


śrībhairava uvāca ||


yaduktaṃ sarvasaṃketaṃ sarvāmnāya kramāgatam |
guhyajaṃ kathitaṃ devi parāśaktistu mātṛkā ||

mātṛṇāṃ pūrvataḥ sāra tena sā mātṛkā smṛtā |
rudraśaktistu sā proktā rudrāṇāṃ mātṛkā yataḥ ||

aṣṭau gaṇeśvarā hyatra vargastāte tavākhyate |
aṣṭau tā mātaro devyo yoginyo bhūyaśastathā ||

sthitāśca sarvadā sarve dṛṣṭā dṛṣṭaphalapradāḥ |
tejobalaṃ prasādaśca rūpaṃ jñānaṃ viśuddhitā ||

parāmumudyamaśceti sapta sarvottamā guṇāḥ |
idamatrotamaṃ kṛtsnaṃ jāyeta bhuvanatrayam ||

yadi śabdā dvayaṃ jyotirāsaṃ sāraṃ na dīyate |
evaṃ kramāt samuddiṣṭaṃ rudrāṇāṃ lakṣaṇaṃ mahat ||

te nugrahakarāḥ sarve parādyete karāḥ sphuṭāḥ |
tairvyāptaṃ ca jagat sarvaṃ śabdārthajñānarūpakam ||

yoniruṣmā tathā labdhaṃ ṣaṭkoṇaṃ kuṃḍalī kulam |
mayavaṇo yaśo haṃso aṣṭadhā vāgbhave smṛtam ||

prathamaṃ vāgbhavaṃ cātha suvarṇaṃ lokaviśrutam |
dvitīyaṃ haṃsadevaṃ tu tṛtīyaṃ vahniromaṇam ||

caturthaṃ viśrutaṃ māyā paṃcamaṃ praṇavaṃ bhavet |
catuṣkalaṃ bhavet saṣṭhaṃ saptamaṃ ceśvara priyam ||

tadatra samavāyena vahnisaṃyogajaṃ param |
dīpakaṃ kulasāraṃ yat tat proktaṃ vakūraṃ sadā ||

kaṭāhastu brahmavilaṃ kaṭāhaḥ śaktimaṃdiram |
kālarudreti saṃyojya saṃhārastu mahātmani ||

p. 99b)

sa śivo bhūtale sākṣāt sa vijānī sa mocakaḥ |
sudīptaṃ maṃḍalākāraṃ biṃdudvayasamaṃkitam ||

īkāredetimārūḍha mahāśikhi śikhāsanā |
īṣatvaṃ hi yadāṃtasthā paramānaṃdarūpiṇī ||

ānaṃdarasasaṃproktā nityānaṃda yathoditā |
vāguraṃ prathamaṃ tat tu pavarṇaṃ cāṃvaraṃ yataḥ ||

imā yā vaikatāṃ prāptā prakāśārkāgnisomagā |
tadā trikoṇago yatra makāraṃ lokaviśrutam ||

somārkānalarūpaṃ hi svaroccāramakārajam |
yasmādidakāramekāraṃ caikoccāraṇasaṃsthitam ||

ojāpūkakrameṇaivaṃ kāpūjā-okrameṇa tu |
eṣa kramaḥ samākhyātaḥ sṛṣṭisaṃhāragocaraḥ ||

prakāśayati mārgo 'yaṃ vyaktāvyaktapade sthitam |
dharmārthakāmamokṣāṇāṃ jñānato daṃḍadhārakāḥ |

kruddhatyarthagrahī siddhā preryaprerakarūpataḥ |
caryādharme ca piṃḍasthaṃ ṣaṭpādasvākṛto bhavet ||

rūpasthaṃ kāmamokāramavanīmatra maṃḍalam |
catuṣkaṃ guruvaktrasthaṃ māṣāvadhimanukramāt ||

devyādiyugadaṃ cāthamūrddhni madhyā ca * * * |
teṣu vā gurupaṃktistu yeṣāṃ janma tu kārate ||

te pūjyāḥ pūrṇagiripīṭhe kāmarūpāṃtamadhyataḥ |
mokṣājñāyāṃ prathamo nātha kāmājñāyāṃ dvitīyakaḥ ||

tṛtīyaṃ cāthasaṃpattau dharmājñāyāṃ caturthakam |
liṃgamekaṃ tridhābhinnaṃ trigyoti dvādaśānimān ||

svaracakraṃ svare caikaṃ dvitīyaṃ kāditāntakam |
pathādimāntaṃ tṛtīyaṃ tu liṃge tāni traya smṛtāḥ ||

liṃgatrayasamāyogācchlokāni dvādaśāni tu |
p. 100a) sthitāni jñānarūpeṇa karṇāt karṇagatāni tu ||

akārādivisargāntaṃ jyotikāsvaraveṣṭitam |
trikūṭasthaṃ mahalligaṃ svaravarṇaṃ svayaṃbhuvat ||

kaṃdasthānasthitaṃ tiṣṭhet tatrasthaṃ vāguraṃ japet |
tatpaścādādikāṃtastu āvṛtastu trikūṭagam ||

paramāṃtaragatābhāsairhṛdaye ca virājate |
etat te vāṇaliṃgākhye nityānaṃdoditaṃ param ||

tatrasthaṃ kāmarājānaṃ lakṣamekena tiṣṭhati |
tadūrddhe turyasaṃsthānaṃ ekāraṃ bhuvanāṃtare ||

dhādi sā tā vṛtte sakāra mūrddhānte itaraṃ smṛtam |
tatrasthaṃ golakaṃ tiṣṭhellakṣamekena niścalam ||

svayaṃbhurvāṇaliṃgaṃ ca itaraṃ? cāpi suvrate |
teṣu lakṣatrayaṃ kṛtvā bījatritayamabhyaset ||

pīṭhaṃ tu mātṛkā proktā tadīśo 'haṃ sa ucyate |
nādākhyaṃ tu paraṃ rūpaṃ visargākhyaṃ parāparam ||

krameṇa kulamāveśa krameṇaiva tu kālikam |
niḥśeṣajaṃ tu cakreṇa tyaktādoṣapalālavat ||

parasaubhāgyamādhatte tenaḥ nityā prakīrtitā |
trikoṇamekaṃ daśamaṃ vahnigehaṃ ca yonikam ||
