śṛṃgāṭakaṃ ca ekāraṃ nāmabhiḥ parikīrtitam |
yadatra samavāyena narasaṃyogajaṃ param ||

dīpaṃ ca kulasāraṃ ca tat proktaṃ vāguraṃ sadā |
padāni caiva varṇāni satyāsatyāni suṃdari ||

vyaktadhyānādibhūtaṃ hi pātrāmasmin vyavasthitam |
jvalaneḥ saṃsthitastasminnadho devo varānane ||

tāvatī yajñasvadhya saṃpāśamasmin vyavasthitam |
sāvādyāskaparityaktamucyate dehasaṃkṣayāt ||

p. 100b) atrāpyadhipatirjñeyo rudro devo hutāsanaḥ |
tadicchayā maheśani bhāratīyaṃ pravartate ||

vāmitri ye tathā vahni dhātā vāgbuddhidāyakaḥ |
athātaḥ saṃpravakṣyāmi cakrasaṃketanirṇayam ||

tadarthaṃ devi kurvīta trihastaṃ maṃḍalottamam |
tasya kuryāt tribhirbhāge karṇikāṃ cakracihnitām ||

padmadvayaṃ tadarddhena dvārakṣetraṃ ca tatsamam |
brahmasūtraṃ purā datvā karṇikātrayamadhyataḥ ||

yāmyāṃ saumyāyataṃ dadyāt tatrasthaṃ saptasūtrakam |
turīyasūtraṃ tato luptvā madhyāṃśaśca vibhāgaśaḥ ||

tṛtīyaṃ ca samairbhāgaiḥ kṛtvā aṃśavivarjitaiḥ |
tena mārgeṇa ca punarmadhyabhāgaṃ vivarddhayet ||

tṛtīyasya dvitīyasya * * * * * * * * |
prasārayet tvadhodastaṃ tat tat sūtraṃ dvayaṃ dvayam ||

tameva saptake sūtre brahmasānevaśaṃgatam |

(āpaṃcamādadhasthānāṃ praṇavāṃtāṃ nama yojayet ||

tameva saptake sūtre brahmasāneva saṃgatam |)

āpaṃ ca mādhasthānāṃ praṇavāntāṃ tama yojayet ||

tameva saptake sūtre viśāṃtiśca tṛtīyake |
sūtradvayaṃ dvayaṃ cānyat sutrāṇye caṃ bhavaṃti ṣaṭ ||

caturdaśāramityevaṃ daśāraṃ ca prasiddhyati |
tatkoṭisaṃgataṃ paścāt sūtrayugmaṃ prasāritam ||

daśāramekaṃ kathitametadvaktra catuṣṭayam |
sūtrā dvitīyakāt ṣaṣṭādbrahmasūtrasya pārśvayoḥ ||

sūtradvayaṃ dvayaṃ devi madha ūrddhaṃ prasāritam |
sūtrātpaṃcamādūrvā dadyāt sūtradvayaṃ nayet ||

ūrddhaṃ cādhomukhamadhyastha aśamarmadvitīyā vadhi |
dadyāt sūtra tṛtīyaṃ ca tayo ranyonyasaṃgatam ||

p. 101)

evaṃ caturthamaṣṭāramekāraṃ vātrapaṃcamam |
iti saṃsidhyate cakra kāntiphānti kṛtaṃ same ||

tacchakti paṃcakaṃ sṛṣṭyānayaṃ nāmi catuṣṭaye |
paṃcaśaktiścaturvahnisaṃyogaccakrasaṃgamam ||

tatra ekāṣṭakaṃ madhye dviśāṃte ca caturdaśa |
caturdaśa daśadvaṃdvamaṣṭāraikaṃ maheśvari ||

sṛṣṭisaṃhārayoge ca śaktisthānānyanu kramāt |
vahnḥ padmadvayaṃ kuryādaṣṭaṣoḍaśa sacchadam ||

caturasraṃ tato datvā kuryād dvāracatuṣṭayam |
catuṣkoṇasamāyuktamevaṃ syānmaṃḍalottamam ||

raktena rajāsā sūryaṃścakraṃ(candra) śuklairdvāra caturthakam |
abhinnā parameśasya śaktirviśvasya saṃbhavā ||

navāmārūpasaṃpannā rūpakāyamayī śivā |
maṃḍanesāna saṃyuktaṃ śiva vyoma tathā ||

rūpadhāranirādhārā sarvadāmūrddhnivigrahā |
nābhibhūtātrajagati janmasthānorddha gāminī ||

jananī sarvabhūtānāma bhedasthitikāriṇi |
jagataḥ pralayo devi apraṇīnāṃ svarūpataḥ ||

sarveṣāṃ tu kṣayaṃ devi svāṃkenākṣaravigrahā |
vaddhā caturvidhāṃ yoni pūjāṃ kuryādanaṃtaram |

asyāmaṃtaratāvoṣṭau vaktrajaiḥ kavacaiḥ sudhīḥ |
jihvāgre vahi niṣkrāmedyāvadekaṃ guṇocchritā ||

cakrayonirayaṃ devi kavacaṃ śṛṇusāṃpratam |
kaniṣṭhānāmayormadhye vāmahastasya pārvati ||

praveśya dakṣiṇāḥ sarvā aṃguṣṭhājāmalau vṛtau |
tayormadhye tu vadhnīyādvāmahastakaniṣṭikam ||

mudreyaṃ hasti yoni sarvakāmaphalaparadā |
p. 101b) anyonyābhi mukhau pādau svastikāsanasaṃsthitau ||

tayormadhye bhagākārā cāsane naiva ciṃtayet |
eṣāpādāsane yoni jñātavyā sādhakottamaiḥ ||

evaṃ caturvidhā yoniṃ vadhvā dhyāyejjagatvikām |
trikoṇāṣṭakasaṃvikuṃ?śarīraṃ pariciṃtayet ||

ādhārabījasaṃviṣṭo dhyāyet tripurasuṃdarīṃ |
cakre ca tārite devi sādhakosyoddharetkaram ||

yāvan madhyagatā pūjāmaṇimuktāpravālakaiḥ |
abhāvakusumaiścāpi mūlamaṃtreṇa cārcayet ||

asūnyaṃ sarvadākuryāt kuryādvighnā bhyanekaśaḥ |
triaṃśasaśikhāmadhyelalāṭecakātnyaset ||

bhruvormadhye daśāraṃ ca kaṃṭhadeśe dvitīyakam |
caturdvāraṃ svāṃtasthaṃ nābhaucāṣṭadalaṃ nyaset ||

ādhāre ṣoḍaśadalaṃ pādayordvāracakrakam |
athātaḥ saṃpravakṣyāmi mahāsamayasaṃsthitām ||

mūlārūḍhasya yogena viṣedakulavigrahe |
pravidhvāsraṃkṣipatyaṃtaṃ kṛtvāmuṣkaraṃ * * * ||

natvaṃ tatvena saṃruddhaṃ jñātvā yogī vicakṣaṇaḥ |
karṇe karṇe gatāṃ devīṃ cakṣuścakṣurgatāṃ punaḥ ||

yatra yatra ca śrotaḥ * sādhanaṃ kurusarvadā |
sā tatra sneha māyāti jñānasūtraprabhāvataḥ ||

khecarībyo namastestu bhūcarībhyo namo namaḥ |
aṃtarīksa caribhyastu sarvayogi gaṇeṣu ca ||

jaṃbhemehitathā kṣe kṣīṃ strīṃ kṣe kṣaḥ phaṭ tathaiva ca |
svāhāṃtaḥ praṇavādyāya valimaṃtra udāhṛtaḥ ||

yāgādua yāgamadhye ca yāgāṃte digvaliṃ haret |
p. 102a) cakrasaṃketako māno mudrāsaṃketakastathā ||

trividhāstripurādevyāḥ saṃketaḥ parameśvari |
ekādaśādikaṃ devi karālī krodhasaṃyutā ||

sāsimadhyagataṃ taṃtu tadūrddhe āsanasthitam |
yānevarṇa caturddhe ca nakulīśūladaṃ yuk ||

pūtanā divi madhyasthā mṛtyantaṃ prāṇadūrddhagam |
vāmamadhyaṃ mahādevi raktasthaṃ narake sthitam ||

adhastasya pradātavyaṃ śūladaṃḍaṃ na saṃśayaḥ |
pūṣṭyāthabhairavaṃ devaṃ narake yannage sthitam ||

pūtanāṃ biṃbamadhye ca krodhadaṃḍāsana sthitām |
māyādevīṃ ca tasyārddhe mūrdhni akrūrabheditam ||

trailokyakṣobhane devi svayaṃ rājasuradrumaḥ |
viṃśa * * nāśane devī atyayaṃ * * yatnataḥ ||

raktabhairava ūrddhe ca bhairavaṃ raktasaṃyutam |
punastaṃ raktasadbhūtyā korddhavarṇaṃ ca spaṣṭe |

biṃdumālinī ūrddhe ca yotpattaṃ pūrvasenakam |
eṣā vidyā sureśāni trailokyotpattimātṛkā ||

asṛṣṭyā svamāyā māyāsvamādhavarṇavibhūṣitam |
ādibījaṃ ca śirasivā svattaṃ corddhabheditam ||

māyayā saṃsthiti madhyaṃ kāmacakrabhagāsmitāḥ |
kandarūpāṣṭakā devaiḥ siddhasaṃtānapūrvakam ||

nakṣatradevatā yuktaṃ nyāsapūjādi sādhane |
kṛttikākhye ca nakṣatre devatāgni rudīritā ||

prajāpatiśca rohiṇyāṃ somomṛgaśire smṛtaḥ |
ārdre rudraḥ samākhyātaḥ punarvasuścāditirbhavet ||

puṣpe tu guru devatyamāśleṣā sarva devatā |
maghā ca pitaro devi phalguṇī aryamā bhevet ||

phalguṇyāṃ ca bhagaḥ prokto hastena savitāmatā |
p. 102b) citrā indrā svātī yā viśākhendrāgnirīritā ||

anurādhe ca mitraṃ syāt jyeṣṭa iṃdra udāhṛtaḥ |
pitaro mūlanakṣatre āṣāḍhe asya devatā ||

viśve devāstu āṣāḍhe śravaṇe viṣṇudevatā |
dhaniṣṭhāścāthavasavāḥ śakraḥ śatabhiṣe viduḥ ||

proṣṭhapade mahādevi atraikapadasaṃjñitaḥ |
punaḥ proṣṭhapade cāpi ahirbudhnanudīritaḥ ||

pūṣā ca revatī jñeyā aśvinī aśvinīṃ viduḥ |
bharaṇyaṃ caivakī nāśaṃ devī devena varṇakān |

jayaṃti devyo harapāda paṃkajaṃ prasannavāmāmṛtamokṣadāyakam |
anaṃtasiddhāṃtagatiprabodhakaṃ namāmi cāṣṭāṣṭakarohiṇī kulaṃ ||

yoginī cakramadhyasthaṃ mātṛmaṃḍala veṣṭitam |
namāmi śirasānāthaṃ bhairavaṃ bhairavī priyam |

anādi ghorasaṃsāra vyādhidhvaṃsaika hetave |
namaḥ śrīnāthavaidyāya kulauṣadhavidhāyine ||

āpādān duritān rogān samayācāralaṃghanāt |
sarvāṇi me vyayohaṃtu divyacakrasya melanāt ||

saṃpūjakāanāṃ pratipādakānāṃ jiteṃdiryāṇāñca tapodhanānāṃ | 
deśasya rāṣṭrasya purasya rājñaḥ karoti śāṃtiṃ bhagavān kuleśaḥ ||

naṃdaṃ tu saḍvidha samastajvaladalanāyo hiṃsāsanākhye catuḥ 
sārṅgamahārṇavāye |
naṃdaṃ tu siddhakulakaulikatā parāyeppa?nye viśeṣapadabhedakaśāṃbhavāye 
||

naṃda tu siddhaguravastadanu kramaughā jyeṣṭhā yugāḥ samāyino vaṭukāḥ 
kumāryaḥ |
p. 103a) saṃyoginī pravaravīrakule prasūtānaṃdantu bhūmi patayo 
dvijasādhuvargāḥ ||

naṃdaṃ tu nītinipuṇā niravadyaniṣṭhānirmatsarānirūpamāni 
rūpadravāścānityaṃ nirañjana ratā niravoniraṃśāḥ śāntāśca śāṃtaso 
kṛtaśokaśaṃkyaḥ ||

naṃda?tu yoganiratā kulayoga yuktā mukhyāryameka sādhakaputrakāśca |
gāvo dvijā yuvatayo yatayaḥ kumāryodharmeranaṃtavighnā gurubhaktirastu ||

naṃdaṃ tu kulayogījā naṃdaṃ tu kulaputrakāḥ |
naṃdaṃ tu tejasā cāryā ye cānye kuladīkṣitāḥ ||

naṃdaṃ tu sādhaka kulā aṇimādi siddhisādhakāḥ |
pataṃ tu samaya dviṣadyoginīnā sāsmādbhavī  sphuṭanukāriṣamādhārasthā 
yasya guroścaraṇa paṃkaja evalabhyaḥ ||

yāścakrakramabhūmikā ca sataśo nāḍīṣu yāḥ saṃsthitā yāḥ 
kāyābhyaṃtararomakūpanilayā yā saṃsthitā dhātuṣu |
ucchrāsormimahattaraṃga śalile yā nītvāśayānāśca ye tā devyaḥ 
paripaṃthitakṣaṇaparāstū yāṃ nu kenārjitāḥ ||

yasyā ājñāprabhāvena svastitaṃ bhuvanatrayam |
namastābhyaḥ samastābhyo yoginīkṣo niraṃtaram ||

śrīmaccaṃdrapuraṃ vihārabhuvanaṃ tūrṇīkṛtā līlayā |
śiṣyaṃ sāpādalagaṇitaṃ nītapadaṃ kṣecaram ||

tanmadhye narakaṃ ca bhūmi patinā svājñāsamākarṣitā |
taṃ devaṃ bhavavoduḥkhadoṣaśamanaṃ śrī ādināthaṃ prabhuṃ ||

śrīmānmitrīśanāthaḥ kṛtayugacaritastretayā oḍhranāthaḥ |
p. 103) ṣaṣṭīśadvāpareśaḥ kaliyugacaritāściṃciṇī viranāthaḥ ||

sā devī mitradevī kulakamalamukhī siddhidā maṃgalā vā teṣāṃ 
pādābjayugmaṃ kṣititalacaraṇāmastakastāṃ namāmi ||

satata biṃduścakra bhāsāvitāniśramasamohinībhiḥ |
pralayaghananibhābhirbhāvitā bhūtabhāvā bhuvanabhuvitabhaṃgo bhairavo naḥ 
punātu ||

ṣeyaṃ bhujāsyānavasiddhirodhamadhānasaṃcāritadivyam |
nirastraśāstrārthavikalpajānā devā śmaśānekaravīrakākhye ||

jayatu paramatatvaḥ prātasoprādānasaṃkṣakṣaḥ paramapadavikāśellāsi 
tatvādhiruḍhāḥ |
sakalabhuvanalīlāmaṃgaloddāmadṛṣṭirvikasatubhuvilakṣmīḥ 
pīṭhajāmaṃgalaśrīḥ ||

śrīḥ pīṭhakailāsanivāsinībhyo divyāsuraprāvaraṇapriyebhyaḥ |
yogīṃdra cūḍāmaṇi cuṃvitābhyo namaḥ svasaṃvit kulayoginībhyaḥ ||

prabhūtabhūtehaniravedyanāmāmāsāgrā madotkaṭābhyaḥ |
digvārasaṃcāritacarvikābhyo vaktroktikaṃ prāśyasukhī bhaveyam ||

dūrasthāśca ye ye ca sāmayikā janāḥ |
naṃdaṃ tu sakalāstejo divya cakrasya melanāt ||

ye ye sāmayikā lokāḥ paralokamitogatāḥ |
teṣāṃ khecariṇāṃ pānamamṛtāṃ yo ya kalpatāṃ ||

kāmāḥ phalaṃ tu vīrāṇāṃ yoginīṃ yogasiddhaye |
rājāno dharmikāḥ saṃtu kāle varṣaṃ tu vāridāḥ ||

me divyakrama bhūsamayināṃ mānaṃ dakāmatsarā dveṣṭāraḥ kulayoginī 
janamanaḥ pāpaṃkuladveṣiṇaḥ |
p. 104a) vīradravyaviniṃdakākulavadhū vai hāsyakā pūjano hādhīna dhiyaḥ 
patatu ta ime ṣadyoginimaṃḍale ||

rakṣāṃ parva na hākinī tvarigatā kṛsthitā kākinīlākinyā miṣasaṃśritā 
hitatayā medasthitā kākinī ḍākinī |
pūrṇāsusthita kākinī parivṛtaṃ hākinyato majayā guhyaṃ parvatayākinā 
paridhṛtaṃ śrībhairavo jīvitam ||

divyādi guruparyantaṃ brahmādistha sukhaṃ matam |
kālāgnyādi śivāṃtaṃ ca jagatsvargeṇa tṛpyatu ||

nityame tatpaṭhetsūtraṃ śubhasaubhāgyabhugbhavet |
śāṃtiśca sarvapāpebhyaḥ sarvapāpanikṛntanam ||

vyādhayastasya naśyaṃti jīveṭ * śatasukhī |
iti devī samākhyātaṃ merumārgavinirgatam ||

iti kubjikāmate paṃcāśatitamaḥ paṭalaḥ || 0 ||





bhagavanpuṣpasaṃketaṃ saṃtānakramasaṃbhavam |

śrutaṃ ca kālikā proktaṃ tripuraśca sureśvara |
yā vidyā parameśāni devāanāmupakāriṇī ||

merumadhye kathaṃ devi vidyā mahiṣavardhinī |
vidyāharervidyā vidyācārya sureśvara ||

etat sarvaṃ gaṇādhyakṣa vadame vistaraṃ prabho |
yasyāḥ kaṭākṣamātreṇa daityadānavarākṣasāḥ ||

vidravaṃti mahādevi siṃhatastu yathāmṛgāḥ |
krameṇa śṛṇu deveśi vidyāmaṃtapadārṇavam ||

kailāsamaṃdire devi vāmāṃtaṃ sṛṣṭibhairavam |
madhyamapaṃcākṣvaṃ vilvamadhyāsane sthitam ||

khavarṇaṃ ca śirasinorvena sa vibhūṣitam |
ruvavarte dhruvāṃtasthaṃ punastau śūladaṃḍa yuk ||

p. 104b)

vajriṇī horddhamālikhya sargamadhyena nācchidam |
labddhaṃ dhruvādi saṃsthaṃ ca kevalaṃ vajriṇīṃ punaḥ ||

śūladaṃḍaṃ samālikhyamūrddhniśakti vibheditam |
biṃdumadhye * * * * putrānāṃtavibhūṣitāḥ ||

tadvatkālaṃ samālikhyavilyamadhyethamūrddhani |
meṣaṃ guṇontamādāya jñānaśaktyā ca bhūṣitam ||

yorddhadaṃḍāsanā dāṃtaṃ krūraṃ tasyā samālikhet |
akhilaṃ bhūsaṃbhāre kārike śaktimagrataḥ ||

vajriṇādaṃḍatejātavedaśe prāṇaśūla yuk |
sūkṣmātaṃ niṣadhaṃ krūraṃ jvaladvadrū prakīrtitam ||

udvaddharṣapunarvāpyasiṃhakrodhāgnikubjakam |
pathijāne mahādevi yoṣṭuṃ vākūramastakam ||

eṣā vidyā mahājātā mahiṣasyātigaṃdhinaḥ |
nāśāyabhagavatī devī vidyā mahādivyanivāsinī ||

hṛdayaṃ bhuvanārsmaiśca rudrārṇaiśca śiroruṃtam |
grahārṇaiśca śikhābhūtyā bhūtārṇaiḥ kavaca saṃsthitam ||

netrasārasārṇaiśca rastrārṇaikṣarastrakaṃ nyaset |
rakāśimuṣalaṃ śūlaṃ kapālaṃ ṭaṃkameva ca ||

kodaṇḍaṃ kheṭakāṃ cāpi vibhrāṇāṃ nīrada prabhāṃ |
nakhadaṃṣṭrāmudhābhīṣmāṃ pārśvasthāṃ paṃcānanāṃ ||

sumukhiṃ durmukhīṃ caiva nyānyā siṃhamukhīṃ tathā |
siṃhāsanasthitāṃ caitānihāsarvādito yajet ||

svabhūtibhiḥ samāyuktāṃ lokātribhirāvṛtām |
bhūtalakṣajapenāthalakṣaikaharaṇena ca ||

japatarpaṇapūjādyaiḥ siddhosaunātrasaṃśayaḥ |
athānyaṃ śṛṇu deveśi mamavākyaṃ prayatnataḥ ||

p. 105a)

paṃcāṃtake bhagontasthaṃ vāyavyaṃ tasya dāpayet |
asyāgnejāṃtamālikhya sṛṣṭivarṇaṃ svayaṃ likhet ||

svanantaṃ gaṃdhaśikhare tasya mūrdhni pradāpayet |
bhe ca madhye mahākālaṃ daṃḍādīni tadagrataḥ ||

pāvakīpāṃśubhābhinnā vāyavyāṃ ca niyojayet |
vāṃtamagre tadanyā ca mūrddhnimadhye gavākaram ||

nyase tu bhairavaṃ prāṇe puratastasya dīpanam |
umākāṃtasya agne ca krodhavarṇaṃ ca kārayet ||

kālikātorddhakaṃ ḍhīśaṃ kalārddhe daṇḍamālikhet |
bhairavaṃ dīpanaṃ yuktaṃ trimūrti viṣaye sthitam ||

lokādiśirasaṃbhinnaṃ devī bījamudāhṛtam |
lohitamūrddhajaṃ devi pānontaṃ daṃḍasaṃsthitam ||

kāṃtavartmaṃ kevalaṃ tu ḍevumadhyaṃ ca chāgalam |
yāṃtaṃ gāntaṃ mudaṃ devi gontaṃ sūkṣmavibhūṣitam ||

dehamadhye tadagre ca vāme madhyaṃ tu kevalam |
pānotmājusaṃstha ca kāntamekādasaṃsthitam ||

vāmādi śirasaṃbhinna dharo arddhaṃ ca pāṃsu yuk |
yā ca vajriṇī yuktaṃ punastaṃ sūkṣmabheditam ||

bhujaṃgabhairavaṃ devi narake śāṃti śakti yuk |

tādhanāṃtāṃdhasargaṃ ca vāmāstaṃ godhvasośritam |
kṣetamekādaśaṃ krūraṃ triṃśadvarṇaṃ sa paṃcakam ||

navārṇairvasubhiścaika digbhiśca grahasaṃkhyakaiḥ |
brahmādyāḥ saṃsthitā devi pade svasthānapaṃcake ||

nāgaiśvarya padaṃ devaṃ bhogamokṣakaraṃ yajet |
pustakaṃ cākṣamālāṃ ca jñānamudrā padaṃ punaḥ ||

śaṃkhādi nalinaṃ devi gadākauśeya vāsasaṃ |
p. 105b) jīmūtasaṃnibhaṃ cāthadhavalātmā maheśvaram ||

nivṛtyādyāḥ kalādikṣu vedānadikān yajet |
brahmāṇyādyāstagre ca iṃdrāgniḥ pitṛpatiḥ punaḥ ||

kṣapācaraḥ sa varuṇo vāyuśca dhanado haraḥ |
brahmānaṃtagame pūjyā svavāhanāḥ sa bhūṣaṇāḥ ||

ṭhakārapaṃcake devi kimidatvā prayatnataḥ |
kramisvena samāveśya tadvarṇaṃ cakramadhyagam ||

karṇikāyāṃ tu tanmadhyā devaṃ tadbāhyato nyaset |
vargāṣṭakaṃ surenātha mātṛkāṃ bāhyato nyaset ||

etat sarvaṃ maheśāni pravarpeṇa surāṃkitam |
purajñasya sahāyognistripādaḥ sa śirastathā ||

yatredaṃ tiṣṭhate devi likhitaṃ gehamadhyagam |
tasya sarvāṇi sidhyanti yāvaṃtīha mahītale ||

siddhayaḥ sarvadā devi yathoktaṃ parameṣṭhinā |
asya svarūpaṃ vakṣyāmi surāsuranamaskṛtam ||

anayā devi caṃdratvaṃ prāptavān saśatakratuḥ |
brahmatvaṃ ca tathā rudrak īśvaratvaṃ makheśvaraḥ ||

guhyādguhyatarāvidyā itaḥ pūrvaṃ sureśvari |
noktākutrāpi sā vidyā sāṃrataṃ śṛṇu cakramam ||

kṣorddhānnaukrūraśirasau mahārāvaṃ ca sūtikam |
āṣāḍhī yatra deveśi tasyādho varṇamālikhet ||

muratastasya deveśi yādhaṃ dhāṃtāṃtamūrddhagam |
vāmanāṃkastaśikhare yāmoṃtara valaṃ likhet ||

nārāyīkāṃta hastāyāmīśasyoparito nyaset |
vāguraṃ mūrddhi deveśi dhruvāṃtorddhaṃ samālikhet ||

paramadhyaṃ tadagre ca meṣaśūlārṇaṃkaiḥ punaḥ |
gatvānaṃtena saṃbhinno dharmarudraṃdagrataḥ ||

p. 106a) someśvarasthitaṃ kāṃtaṃ vāyusaṃbhavam |
saṃvarttako mahādevi tasyādhaḥ śavakaṃ sthitam ||

svastinastu vijānīyādraktaṃ tasyāsanaṃ dadet |
śaktivarṇaṃ śive nyasya śūnyanādasamanvitam ||

purobhāge nyaseddūtī vargāṣṭakasamanvitā |
vidyāparamakalyāṇi sarvāpadvinīvāriṇī ||

prabhāvamasyā deveśi vaktuṃ kalpaśatairapi |
na śakyate varārohe śrotuṃ vākena citphalam ||

vidyādau varṇayagaṇaṃ muhālakṣmīṃ ca vaiṣṇavam |
hṛdinyāsaṃ prakartavyaṃ śirodeśethavārakam ||

sadāśivenasaṃyuktamaṃśumānilapūrvakam |
devyārudreṇa vāgīśa śikhāmadhye ca vinyaset ||

tadagre vartmani īśvareṇa ca tāṃ punaḥ |
yugmantu brahmagāyatryā netrāṇāṃ kuruṇe sthitam ||

gaṇāṃ * mabhyasaṃ śāstraya padaṃ saṃvat |
pheṭkārī sarvato devi nyaset sarva * * * * * ||

ekaikaṃ ca tato gehe śirobhāge bhruvau punaḥ |
dūṣaṇāsāgaṃḍakaṃṭheṣu vāhu hṛnnābhiśūvake ||

kaghāṃjānu dvaye gulphe pādāgre ca pravinyaset |
vyāpakaṃ śiṣṭavarṇānāṃ navātmācādi saṃyutam ||

pūrvoktekāramadhye tu smarennārāyaṇe prati |
śayāna mahibhogeṣu kamalāliṃgitastanum ||

dugdhābdhi dvipanicaye ṛṣibrahmādi sevite |
nānādrumalatākīrṇe nānājanasamāvṛte ||

sthitaṃ vā devadeveśaṃ śaṃkhacakrakapālinam |
vidyāmakṣaguṇaṃkaṃvu gadāṃ pūrṇaghaṭā mṛtam ||

kirīṭahārakeyūra kaṭakādyairupasthitam |
svāṃkastha padmakarayā sarvalakṣaṇalakṣitam ||

p. 106b) daityāvalokanarataṃ devadevaṃ maheśvaram |
upacāraiḥ samārādhya gāyatrīṃ brahmaṇaḥ sutāṃ ||

sāvitrī viṣṇunā devi vāgīśaṃ vākyamāvṛtam |
dhanadaṃ ca śriyāyuktaṃ yonāvevaṃ prapūjayet ||

mātaroṣṭau dalāgreṣu vāsudevaṃ ca madhyataḥ |
saṃkarṣaṇaṃ dakṣabhāge pradyusnaṃ paścime yajet ||

uttare cāniruddhaṃ ca āgneyādi prapūjayet |
śāṃti śrīsarasvatyau ca tribhirbahu vibhūṣitāḥ ||

japtvālakṣaguṇaṃ devi kilviṣaiśca vimucyate |
rasalākṣā bhavedośo svayaṃ brahmasadāśivaḥ ||


iti śrīkubjikāmatenavakoṭisaṃhitāyāṃ caturaśītisāhasrepaṃcāśatitamaḥ 
paṭalaḥ || 0 ||





devyuvāca ||


tripurānāmavikhyātā raktāṃgaṃ nyāsasaṃyutā |
nityākāmadughānāmasaṃvarttā maṃḍalakramam ||

liṃgaṃ ca kīdṛśaṃ jñeyaṃ yathānyadapiśaṃkara |
kathayasva prasādena pūjāhomaṃ ca tarppaṇam ||


śrībhairava uvāca ||


udbhūtaṃ ca purādevi yanmayā tripurārṇave |
tadadyasaṃgraheṇātra susṛṣṭaṃ yādṛśaṃ bhavet ||

sarvāgamānāṃ sāraṃ hi udbhūtaṃ merusaṃgrahe |
tasmānmeruṃ padāpaśyet svahṛṣṭe nāṃtarātmanā ||

kasyagomayagaṃdhena kuṃkumāgurusaṃbhaveḥ |
liptvā bhūmau paṭevātha śrīdrumaṃ merumeva ca ||

nābhimadhyāsthitaṃ bījaṃ tasyāgreṣṭabhisaṃbhavam |
puratastasya dātavyaṃ rudrabījaṃ tṛtīyakam ||

caturānanamuddhṛtya turīyaṃ nābhimaṃdire |
raktaṃ rudrasthitaṃ kuryādvidyābījaṃ śive sthitam ||

śṛpūrvaṃ śirasākrāṃtaṃ stheṇo madhyaṃ tathāgrataḥ |
dakṣaśūlaṃ samuddhṛtya kevalaṃ vāyusaṃbhavam ||

p. 107a)

ubhāvetau punarlikhya karālībījamagrataḥ |
bhārabhūtisthitiṃ kuryātmajjārudrasamā likhet ||

deṇomadhyagataṃ kṛtvā vidyādhārekuṃṭhamagrataḥ |
niraṃtarapadaṃ bhūyaḥ śvetapūrvaṃ sa bhairavam ||

tasyādho raktarudraṃ tu maṃdarasyaṃ śivesthitam |
asyāgre dārukaṃ bījaṃ mūlaṃtaṃ tadanantaram ||

mālinī hṛdayaṃ devi pūrvoparisaṃsthitam |
tayormūrdhniśataṃ kuryānmālinī yogamuttamam ||

ṛṣi mālā tu muddhṛtya tadādiścaivamagrataḥ |
pūrvokta mānadaṃ devi tatpurastātvalohitam ||

śūladaṃḍāsanārūḍhaṃ navādimupavinyaset |
maṃdarasthaṃ vijānīyādrudraṃdhātriśamagrataḥ ||

meṣorddhe kevalaṃ bījaṃ vāmādi śirasā nyaset |
kūrmarudrasya puratastat pūrvaṃ kevalaṃ lekhet ||

jñānaśakti samopetaṃ śukraruhe maheśvari |
lakṣmībījaṃ samuddhṛtya nityaṃ bhīmāsamagrataḥ ||

urumadhyasthitaṃ kuryādbhairavaṃ krodhasaṃbhavaṃ |
lokāṃtena priyā pātaiḥ paṃcamaṃ praṇavaṃ bhavet ||

mālinī parathamaṃ bījamasyāgre kālamālikhet |
lokāṃtaṃ śirasākrāṃtaṃ kāraṇāni punarbhavet ||

eṣā vidyā purājānā mūlāṃḍakṣobhakārikā |
svargā pavargaphaladā sadyaḥ pratyayakārikā ||

iyaṃ sā bhairavī nityā siddhosau makaradhvajaḥ |
kaṃdarpamaṃdire bhīmā kathādīna kramānnyaset ||

kaṃdarpyādyaṃtapuragamaṃte saṃvarttakaṃ nyaset |
viṣaṃ sa visadvitveti nivartakramarodhitā ||

eṣa saṃvarttanāmānaṃ maṃḍalaṃ surasuṃdari |
p. 107b) saṃvarttānte smṛtaṃ taddhi saṃvarttakṣayakārakam ||

saṃvartte nisphale bhūte viṃdatyātmaniyatsukhaṃ |
nirvikalpaṃ ca saṃciṃtya hetu dṛṣṭāaṃta varjitam ||

tatsukhaṃ paramaṃ devi yatredaṃ sa carācaram |
utpatti vyāpakaṃ tasmāt tattadviśvaṃ pratiṣṭhitam ||

kriyāyāḥ śaktayasteṣu triṣusthāneṣu bhairavāḥ |
vāmajyeṣṭhe ca raudrī ca madhye vā nabhasaṃbhavāḥ ||

śaktirviśvasya jananī mūlādbhidyamātṛkā |
vamanī yathācāreṇa kalpārka bhayaggaṃ viduḥ ||

saṃvarttakramayogoyaṃ devi gopyaṃ prakāśitam |
ānaṃdaṃ vimalaṃ jyoti śaktirātmaphalapradam ||

sarvātmapadayogaṃ ca itaratvāntu dikpunaḥ |
tadvadrūpa ṛṣiprokto tathā devī prayatnataḥ ||

mātṛkāvarṇayogena gurupiṃḍasya madhyagam |
bhairavī piṃḍamadhyasthā duḥkharāśi vimocinī ||

dvātriṃśallakṣaṇopetāṃ bhairavīṃ viṣṇunā saha |
gāyatrīṃ vā tathālakṣmīṃ sarvalokaikamātaram ||

yā lakṣmībhairavī devī rūpaṃ ca rasasaṃnibhā |
paṃca dīrghe caturhrasvaṃ paṃca sūkṣmaṃ ṣaḍbhutam ||

saptaraktaṃ trigaṃbhīraṃ dvivistīrṇaṃ yathocyate |
bāhunetradvayaṃ kukṣi jānunī castanāṃtaram ||

paṃcadīrghaṃ samākhyātamathaśrīvāca jaṃghikā |
pṛṣṭaṃ gehaṃ ca catvāri hrasvānyuktāni ceśvari ||

sūkṣmāṇyaṃguli parvāṇi keśāstvak daśanānakhāḥ |
kakṣaṃ kukṣiśca vakṣaśca ghrāṇaskaṃdhau lalāṭikā ||

ṣaḍaivamullataṃ jñeyamata ūrddhaṃ ca yatpaśuḥ |
pāṇipādatalerakte netrau pāṇitalāni ca ||

p. 108a) tālujihvādharauṣṭā ca saptaraktāḥ prakīrtitāḥ |
svaraḥ satvaṃ ca nābhiśca trigaṃbhīraṃ prakīrtitam ||

urolalāṭikā caiva dvivistārṇaṃ maheśvari |
dvātriṃśallakṣaṇo petairdevadevaṃ calāpati ||

vidyāmakṣaguṇaṃ dvābhyāmitarābhyāṃ cakrakaṃbukam |
anyonyā liṃganakarau * * * * śivāṃkagāṃ ||

mukhesaṃyojyaveṇu ca gāyaṃtaṃ kṛṣṇamacyutam |
āsaṃsaṃprekṣavāsyena tārahāravibhūṣitau ||

rathāṃgaśaṃkhavidyā ca akṣadāmāhiśūnyapi |
bhūtapuṃdrukayuktākhyāmabhayaṃ vibhratī girām ||

bhairavāsanamāruḍhāṃ muṇḍasragbindu locanā |
paramānaṃdasaṃdohasaṃpūritavilocanām ||

guṇaṃsargamitiproktaṃ sūkṣmaviṃnāṃ mayaṃ bhavet |
saṃvarttakanirālaṃbe tatkaṭisamanvite ||

mahāpadmaratnaraśmidhavalāṃga suśītale |
tatrāveśmanijaṃ bhāvaṃ devaṃtaṃ lakṣaṇānvitam ||

śivāṃśivāsanārūḍhāṃ baṃdhūkakusumāruṇam |
paritaḥ śaktayaścāṣṭau dikṣuśaktyāgrimūrtayaḥ ||

lakṣmī kamalamuccārya vasinīmaṃkuśaṃ punaḥ |
raktabhairavamārūḍhāṃ pūtanāpragnahānvitāṃ ||

paṃcātmikaṃ ca deveśiṃ varaṇāmagrataḥ kuru |
dhare dharaṇi śabdaṃ ca bhāvayedagni diggatam ||

śukrabījaṃ samuddhṛtya vargamadhye ca nākulaṃ |
ekaṃ daśādi purato navake ca tṛtīyakam ||

tadā dikṣusamākrāṃtaṃ bījaṃ lokādi mastakam |
anaṃtādi krameṇaivaṃ varṇānāṃ purato likhet ||

na niśca bhāgavatyaṃte svaṣṭhamnā dhyānasāmyataḥ |
p. 108b) kāliṃdī satyabhāmā bhūyo mitraviṃdāsu naṃdinī ||

lakṣaṇānagnajityākhyā mūrtayaḥ kramato bhavan |
aparā devi kurvīta tripurasya yathā parā ||

asya jāpenahomenalakṣamānenayadbhavet |
mṛtapāṣāṇena dāruṇya dṛṣṭyā taccetanaṃ bhavet ||

divimartyajanecātha pātāle pannagālaye |
svacchaṃdacarito vīro yathāsau devakī sutaḥ ||

tatonyāṃsaṃpravakṣyāmi vidyāṃ trailokya mohinīṃ |
trailokya mohane taṃtre purāte prakaṭī kṛtā ||

dhruvaṃ nābhigataṃ bhūyo mārāṃkaṃ krūramastakam |
modakaṃśukramastaṃ ca mādanaṃ śukramūrddhani ||

adhastātkārayecchaktiṃ guṇoṃtaṃ śirasi sthitam |
paṃcasamopetaṃ garthānaṃ tena nādinī ||

dīpanī ca tathā vidyā māyā madhyaṃ tadagrataḥ |
māninīṃ vāmanetre tu ramyaṃ rudraṃ samāvanam ||

viṣṇuṃ vāmastane nyasya pūrvavat parito likhet |
punaḥ samuddharedanyaṃ devi gopyaṃ prakāśitam ||

eṣā viṣṇusamudbhūtā vidyāpaṃcākṣarī śubhā |
evaṃ vā bhairavīṃ devīṃ kaṃṭhakaṃ ca navākṣarām ||

pūrvokte caṃḍikā madhyehaṃ vāsaṃvādhanāṃ kuru |
viṣṇave bhagavan bhūyastathā śaṃbhuśca pārvati ||

dakṣiṇāmūrtiyogena saiva vidyā maheśvari |
māyā vāgīśvarī saisāhaṃ sa vāgīśvarīṃ śṛṇu ||

haṃsasya purato devī samāṃtaṃ haṃsa śaktikam |
bhūyaḥ sarasvatīmagre nati svāhā vaṣaṭ api ||

vāguraṃ viṣṇu devīṃ ca vāyurudraṃ samagrataḥ |
kāmaṃ sohaṃ samuddhṛtya adya syāhā samanvitam ||

p. 109a)

prapaṃcamuṃḍamālāyāṃ gaditaṃ tripurārṇave |
eṣa viṣṇuḥ śivaḥ prokto brahmāvāk taraṇi bhairavī ||

punaste dhvaṃ?bikā devī mojamadhye vyavasthitam |
maṃdārāvasthitaṃ kuryān mālinyā urusaṃbhavam ||

kāṃtamekādaśe śvetamadhastadvilvasaṃbhavam |
śrīkaraṃ ca samākhyātaṃ darpitā pāvakasya ca ||

dugdhābdhimadhyasaṃbhūtaṃ nalinottaratārkṣagam |
aṣṭabāhuṃ samāsīnaṃ bhairavādhiṣṭhito'cyutaḥ ||

taptakāṃcanasaṃkāśaṃ baṃdhūkāruṇavacchirām |
nalināsigadācakraṃ kaṃbupāśāṃkuśaṃ punaḥ ||

vidyākapālamaniśaṃ manasaṃsthāmanolayam |
aṃgāni vinyaset paṃca bhībhīsatrāsamadā ||

pṛthuṃsavakṣabījādyaiḥ sarvajñādi bhiranvitaiḥ |
padairaṣṭā yudhairvījairdaśasthāneṣu taddaśa ||

brāhmaṇosya mukhamāsīdvāhūrājanyameva ca |
urūtadasya yadvaiśyaḥ padbhyāṃ śūdra iti kramāt ||

āpādasvatalaṃ devi pādorddhaṃ vitalaṃ punaḥ |
jaṃghāpādatalaṃ bhūyo nitalaṃ jānasarvagam ||

talātalañca urubhyāṃ śubhasthāne rasātalam |
pātālaṃ kaṭisaṃdhisthaṃ tadvaddūrddhaviciṃtayet ||

bhūrllokaṃ nābhimadhye ca bhūvarlokaṃ hṛdisaṃsthitam |
svarllokaṃ hyurasijñeyaṃ maharllokaṃ tu kaṃṭhakam ||

janalokaṃ tālumadhye tapolokaṃ lalāṭake |
satyalokaṃ brahmarandhre bhuvanāni caturdaśaḥ ||

pūrvokta vidhimānena pūjādravyāṇi sādhayet |
p. 109b) mṛtībījatrayaṃ bhūyo māninīṃ śirasaṃbhavam ||

kūrmarudrasya puratonyadvarṇaṃ ṛṣimaṃdire |
agnyādi bījamuddhṛtya garthānaṃtaṃ śive nyaset ||

vasanānaṃtapurato vecu madhye samālikhet |
saṃvartako mahādevi mahākātyāsanasthitaḥ ||

asya dvārapradeśe ca narake dīpanīṃ nyaset |
arghāśamāsanārūḍhā vāyave cāsane nyaset ||

kṣepaṃcamakṣaraṃ devi nāmādi mastakeṃgajaḥ |
varṇāni purato bhūyastrī bījaṃ punare vahi ||

yathā vidyeyamākhyātā vidyāpaṃcākṣarī matā |
bījāni tāni coddhṛtyarudrāṇāṃ ṛṣimaṃdire ||

kālonāmamahārudro vāmāntena vibhūṣitaḥ |
mādanaṃ bhūtavarṇāni guṇavarṇāni pūrvavat ||

evametā mahāvidyā tadvadbhūtākṣarī matā |
sarvajāpeṣu saṃkhyātamaṃtrārthaguṇarūpataḥ ||

vidheyaṃ sarvayatnena lakṣatrayamathā vidheḥ |
bhrūṇahā śudhyate devi naraḥ kālenapīḍitaḥ ||

lakṣadvādaśakairvipro rājñaḥ ṣoḍaśasaṃkhyakaiḥ |
vaiśyastu viṃśatiḥ prokto yathāstrī sudrajātibhiḥ ||

pūrvavanmaṃḍale vatsamaniṣicyasahasradhā |
vimuktaḥ sarvapāpebhyo svehayā ca rate mahī ||

jyeṣṭhaṃ kaniṣṭhamanyeṣāṃ sarveṣāṃ saṃyatātmanām |
tapa evagarīyo bhūtsvabhāvaścātrakāraṇam ||

brahmakṣatraviṣastrīṣu dīkṣā saṃskārabhedataḥ |
jāyate śuddhabhāvastu na dharmaṃ liṃgasādhanam ||

dvijatvaṃ na bhavetvājñā yataḥ parāśaro muniḥ |
p. 110a) gardabhe gardabho bhūto vyāsaḥ kaivartakī sutaḥ ||

maḍavyomāṃḍakī garbho ṛṣiśṛṃgo mṛgodbhavaḥ |
vasiṣṭho gaṇikājjāto mātaṃgopi mahāmuniḥ ||

hastinyāmavalojātaḥ kauśikyāṃ keśavo balaḥ |
kuśāpaśca murodbhūtaḥ kauśikaśca mahāmuniḥ ||

taitrīsutastu taitrīyastathāgasyo ghaṭodbhavaḥ |
viśvāmitrastu cāṃḍālyāṃ vālmīko mlecchasaṃbhavaḥ ||

anyepi ṛṣayaḥ sarve dvijātī bhedagotriṇaḥ |
teṣāṃ yāvat kulaṃ nāsti dvijānāṃ ca kulaṃ gataḥ ||

jātihīnāpite sarve tapasā dvijatāṃ gatāḥ |
tasmājjāti kulenaiva namanye dvijatāṃ gatāḥ ||

na vipro vipravāryena śūdrī garbhoyadāna hi |
prātaḥ saṃdhyādibhiścāpi mātātadbrāhmaṇī katham ||

śūdropi śuddhatāṃ yāti vipraścāṃḍāalatāṃ vrajet |
saṃskārabhāvitā sarve tapasādhautakalmaṣāḥ ||

tasmāddīkṣādi saṃskāraiḥ kuryyāccamathā vidhi |
vidhihīnaṃ na kartavyaṃ vidhinā pūrvacoditam ||

dīkṣitasyāpi doṣeṇa sāpitā vidhinā bhavet |
bhaktiḥ śāṃbhavabhedena caturddhā paripaṭhyate ||

nivṛtyādyanyabījābhyāṃ pādādi sthāna paṃcake |
brahmāṇḍavyāpakā rudrā saṃvarttake sthitāḥ ||

tatvabodha * * * * dhā bhinne matapārvati |
kriyāmayī ca pūrvoktya kuṃḍamaṃḍalaputrikāḥ ||

caitre duḥkhāya dīkṣāsyādvaiśākhe sarvasiddhidā |
jyeṣṭhe mṛtyu pradāsāsyādāṣāṭevaṃ dhunāśinī ||

p. 110b)

śrāvaṇe vṛddhidā nṝṇāṃ nabhasye duḥkhadā matā |
āśvine sarvasiddhyaisyā kārtikeṃ jñānavṛddhidā ||

śubhakṛnmārgaśīrṣe yā pauṣe jñānavināśinī |
māghe medhā vivṛddhā syāt phālguṇe sarvavaśya kṛt ||

haimarājatatāmrādīn kalaśātravaśobhanān |
tadbahiḥ ṣoḍaśādvāpi dvātriṃśadviguṇaṃ punaḥ ||

svastikaṃ sarvatobhadrañcakrābjaṃ prāksamīritam |
dūkūla veṣṭitān sarvān taṃtunā triguṇātmanā ||

haimaratnādi gaṃdhāḍyaiḥ paṃcāmṛtapavitritān |
cakramabhyarcya vidhivadaṣṭa ṣoḍaśasaṃkhyakaiḥ ||

dvātriṃśaccatuḥ ṣaṣṭibhairavyo bhairavāgamāt |
bhakṣabhojyāni sarvāṇi pradadyāttarpaṇāni ca ||

yathopacāravibhavaiḥ saṃpūjyānāmapūrvakam |
vaikuṃṭhaṃ paraśaṃbhuṃ ca parāparamatheśvarīṃ ||

divyādivyakramaṃ jñātvā gurutaḥ saṃkrama kramāt |
kramasiddheḥ paraṃjyotirnityāne sānnidhyatāṃ bhavet ||

nirvāṇapadasiddhiḥ syād devairapi sudurlabhā ||


iti śrīkubjikāmate ekapaṃcāśatitamaḥ paṭalaḥ || 0 ||




śrībhairava uvāca ||


śṛṇu devi paraṃguhyaṃ vidyāśaṃketasaṃjñitam |
brahmāṇḍe kṣobhakā rudrāstathā viṣṇu samudbhavāḥ ||

mālinī tadgrahagraṃthi catuḥṣaṣṭi krameṇa hi |
mātṛkā sarvavidyānāmuddhṛtānasurāsurān ||

parāparādi kramataḥ sāpinānā vidhā bhavet |
devānāṃ purato devī navātmānaṃ navātmikām ||

śrīkaṃṭhaṃ vinyasen mūrddhni saṃvartātmānameva hi |
p. 111a) amṛtākarṣiṇīndrāṇī aiśānīṃ caṃdramā punaḥ ||

ūrddhakeśī ṛdddhidārārūṣāṣṭā nyaset punaḥ |
ekāyāmathaiśvaryamokārīmauṣadhātmikām ||

aṃbikāmakṣayāṃ jñānaṃ dhātrīścātīmeva hi |
gāyatrīṃ ghaṃṭadhātrīṃ ca jīrṇātmā na vinyaset tanau ||

caṃḍācchāyā jayābhūmau kaṃkārī jñānarūpayā |
ṭaṃkarasrāthaṭaṃkārī ḍāmarī ṭaṃkāriṇī punaḥ ||

śākinī tāmasī sthāsye dākṣāyaṇyatha rātrikā |
nādā ca pārvatī caiva phaṭkārī vaṃghanīti ca ||

bhadrakālī mahāmāyā yaśasvinyathavā mayā |
laṃbauṣṭhi varadāśrī ca ghaṃṭā caiva sarasvatī ||

tākinī dasavatyā ca kṣayābhūyo imāḥ kalāḥ |
ante ca namasā devi devī vāsā ca caṃḍikā ||

tatvato bhairavaṃ devaṃ sarvatraivaṃ pravinyaset |
dhavalāmṛtasaṃkāśā nyastavyāḥ piḍitaḥ ||

mātrādaṃḍadharādevyo vidyākṣasrakkapālakāḥ |
kapardakalitāmaulau valeṃdukṛtaśekharāḥ ||

athavā devi kurvīta tattannāma purassaram |
tataḥ krameṇavarṇāni bhautakāni viniṣkramāt ||

punareva maheśāni jñātvā vidyākṣa sūtrakām |
kailāsamaṃdire devi ādivarṇaṃ samālikhet ||

krūravarṇaṃ śive nyastaṃ vāmāṃtaṃ bhūtisaṃbhavam |
lokādimastakāśrāṃtaṃ śivenyastaṃ prakīrtitam ||

anaṃtaṃgaṃdhaśikhare śvetapūrvaṃ samuddharet |
madhyāsanāsīnaṃ niṣadhe vadasaṃbhavam ||

eṣā śirasi vinyasyet pūrvalokasaṃbhavam |
p. 111b) mukhavṛttaṃ vijānīyāt maṃdare sūkṣmamagrataḥ ||

ekādaśe turīyāṃtu tadaṃte tu grahaṃ nyaset |
lokāṃtaṃ śirasikrāṃtaṃ vinyased dakṣa cakṣusī ||

punaḥstrimūrtiṃ bhautīśaṃ vāmanetre pravinyaset |
dakṣiṇaśravaṇe devi amarīśaṃ samanmathaṃ ||

bhrūvānte kṣayaṃ cānyaṃ śravaṇe vāmake viduḥ |
sthānāni kramayogena mātṛkāsurasuṃdarīm ||

punaranyaṃ pravakṣyāmi bhautikaṃ cādisaṃbhavam |
makaradhvajasya purato'naṃtāgarthasya maṃdire ||

anyasya purato bhūyaḥ sūkṣmāgraṃ biṃdu bhairavam |
evamabhyasyataḥ puṃsaḥ ahanyahani bhaktitaḥ ||

aghaughaṃ kṣīyate varṣāt tejasābhāskaro bhavet |
śītale caṃdra tulyāsau brahmaviṣṇuśivātmakaḥ ||

evamanyaṃ sureśāni śṛṇuguhyaṃ sanātanam |
surāṇāmaṃtare devi trāṇaṃ jīvaṃ sa bhautikam ||

akrūraṃ mūrddhni vinyastaṃ surasthāneṣu vinyaset |
tadvadacchevakaravat saṃdhyāneṣu tathā punaḥ ||

vedavarṇāḥ śiro devi tvattvanālaṃ kṛtaṃ bhavet |
pāṃcanāmavalāṃtā nāmaṃ te haṃsaṃ prakīrtitam ||

kṣetramanugrahaṃ mūrddhni biṃdunālaṃ kṛtaṃ kuru |
viṃśatsahasrabhedena vidyaiṣā parikīrtitā ||

iṃdragopanibhāṃ bālāṃ bālendukṛtaśekharām |
kuṃkumāruṇamuṃḍasra ṅamaṃḍitāmuttatastanīm ||

śvetapadmāsanāsīnāmaṣṭaukāmasaroruhe |
athavā bhautikīṃ devīṃ saṃvarttāntaṃ vinyaset ||

dvitīyantatvadeveha tṛtīyamapi pārvati |
etatkāmakalārūpamakṣaraṃ śirasi sthitam ||

p. 112a)

māyā bījaṃ maheśāni asyāgre rudrasaṃbhavam |
ādivarṇāntabhautīśaṃ rudraṃ caivavarātmakam ||

mālinī vaktravarṇasya sūkṣmarudraṃ samūrddhani |
mālinyo devamuddhṛtya umākāntaṃ tadagrataḥ ||

vaśamadhyagataṃ devi narake ṛṣimaṃdire |
meṣo nāma mahārudro rudrāṇāmabdhi śaṃkare ||

kālaṃ teṣvaṃtamālikhya lokāṃtaṃ cāntadeśataḥ |
evaṃ brahmā ca rudraśca durgāvāganya devatā ||

tattadbījayutāṃgāni phaṭkārāṃ paṃcamīṃ nyaset |
astrāntanevamaṃtrāṇi nyastavyā vaiṣṇaveṣu ca ||

mātṛ brahmādi lokāṃtaṃ pūjanīyāḥ pṛthak kramāt |
devī gopyaṃ pravakṣyāmi yanmayā gopitaṃ purā ||

bījānītamasoddhṛtya haṃsāptau sohameva ca |
ādi varṇasya śirasi lokādi mastake nyaset ||

dviraṃḍabhairavī devīṃ bhīṣaṇīṃ ṛṣimaṃdire |
dhrūvāṃtamāsanaṃ dadyāt antaṃ vānta deśataḥ ||

guṇoṃtamakṣaraṃ devi gṛhāṇa ṛṣimaṃdire |
arddhamātrā samāyuktaṃ devī bījaṃ tu kevalam ||

vārentaṃ vajriṇī devī sūkṣmarudravibhūṣitā |
tārākṣagrahamālāyāṃ mojamadhyāsanaprabhām ||

mālinī cakravarṇasya vārāhī raktamūrddhani |
punarasyā videśe tu māyādi mastakaṃ kuru ||

kāṃtarudrasya purato nyastavyā vāyusaṃbhavam |
amarīśāṃsanāruḍhā mahākālīnu pūrvikā ||

bhuvobhago bhavedbhūyo devasya purato dhanam |
grahaśaktistu deveśi nakulīśāsanānvitā ||

ṛṣimadhyapurato cāmuṃḍā purataḥ sthitā |
aṃtare biṃdurudraṃ hi siddhijonastadagrataḥ ||

pracodayāditikṣepā vyavasthitā * * * * |
eṣā vidyā purājātā brahma * * * * * * ||

p. 112b)

kiṃ cāśāgre vidhātavyā sārātsārabhavaṃ punaḥ |
dhruvānte kālamālikhya asyāgre gaṃdhasaṃbhavam ||

pāvanī pātu saṃbhinnā tejavarṇo raso mṛtam |
brahmabhūtyā tṛtīyā nāmate vai nāmto bhavat ||

brahmakṣetraviśānāṃ hi ādyā brahmapradarśinī |
brahmavipre muditānāṃ devānāmapi durlabhā ||

brahmādyāśāṃtyatītā devatāvaktrapaṃcake |
te sarvātmane saktyaṃ taṃ sa vaktrādyaṃgaṣaṭsu ca ||

pādādi piṃḍamadhyepi vinyaset tatva ciṃtakaiḥ |
ādi varṇādi pītāṃtaṃ padbhyāṃ loke pitāmaham ||

ājānu guhyaparyantaṃ viṣṇusvaiṃ kramasaṃbhavam |
hṛdayādbrahmaparyantaṃ rudraṃ kṛṣṇa prabhaṃ yajet ||

īśaṃ viṣṇuprabhaṃ dhyāyedākacāddhṛdayāvadhi |
dhavalā mṛtasaṃkāśaṃ tadūrddheśaṃ sadāśivaṃ ||

te tatraiva samārādhya phalānīha prapedire |
bījāni madhyakoṇe ca bhānuṃ biṃdulake nyaset ||

pādāni tatvasaṃkhyāni niṃbecānugatena ca |
kuṃjaraṃ haṃsavarṇena udbhavenaiva viṣṇunā ||

rudreṇaveṣṭitaṃ sarvaṃ viṣṇunālaṃ kṛtaṃ kuru |
tanmadhye bhāvayeddevīṃ tantu rūpāṃ trayī mayīṃ ||

tripadāṃ tryakṣaraṃ brahmaviṣṇurudratriśaktikam |
mukhavāharupādeṣu brahmakṣatraviśetarāḥ ||

abhayaṣṭāṃ kuśāpāśa kapāla triśikhā kulāṃ |
śaṃsvāraṃ nalinī bhojamaṃḍanāne kamaṃḍitām ||

kuṃkumāruṇamuṃḍasraṅmaṃḍitāṃ padmagāṃ śivām |
sūryakoṭisamaprakhyā caṃdrakoṭi samaprabhām ||

p. 113a)

kapardakalitāṃ maulau aticaṃḍavisarjitām |
sarvajñakratumayā sūtraguṇarekhā manoharām ||

mekhalā guṇasaṃlagnāṃ kāṃcīratnanitaṃbinīm |
tarkavedāgamāścāpi raṇanmaṃjīrahaṃsinīṃ ||

romarājī latāvidyudvalayāṃgadakuṃḍalāṃ |
devyāsuramanuṣyāṇāṃ ṛṣiṇāṃ ca mahaujasāṃ ||

vānetaratkuśākīrṇaṃ maulimālābhirakṣitām |
bhāsvarāṃ priyagāṃ devīṃ vartulāṃ yugamaṃtharām ||

jitakaṃdarpalāvaṇyavaṃdhurāmṛtavarṣiṇīm |
kuṭṭime haimaratnāghe latākalpamahīruhe ||

āsīnāṃ śaṃbhaveśānīmarcayedīkṣito niśam |
śaktiṃ vasane tyādi bhagavatī pāduke punaḥ ||

ṛgyajuḥ sāmarūpāyai tatsthā bharaṇamuktaye |
jñānaśakti namastubhyaṃ varāśirame śive ||

ādityaṃ bhāskaraṃ bhānuṃ deviṃ prāgādi dikṣu ca |
umācchāyā prabhā nityā pramātṛ vargadevatāḥ ||

tāmasī ca sarasvatyā varadā bhāvakā hvayā |
vareṇyā vā mayā bhūyo śākinī bhadrakālikāṃ ||

gāyatrī devakīrāmā śākinī rātrikāṃ punaḥ |
mahākālī hiraṇyā ca śrīmahī ca yaśasvinī ||

yogātmikā tanusthā ca varṇādyā rudrapūrvikāḥ |
nārāyaṇyā prabhāvadyā caṃḍādākṣāyaṇī yamāḥ ||

mātṛkā varṇabījādyā brahmātāśca sureśvarāḥ |
kaṣāphalādinīgmādyaiḥ pūrvokta kramataḥ kramāt ||

palāśāragvadho śīrṣapuṣpaṃ kāśmanameva ca |
varṇapūtajapo pāthatallakṣātsarvamāpnuyāt ||

viśvāmitraḥ purādevī asyā vidyā prabhāvataḥ |
p. 113b) brahmatvattapasājagmuḥ anye cāpi tapo dhanāḥ ||

janābhā seyaṃ mayā tasya sarvapāpanikṛntanī |
atha guhyaṃ pravakṣyāmi śṛṇu devi prayatnataḥ ||

ādi vidyā vibhedena vibhajettatvasaṃkhyayā |
dvitīyāṃ te tṛtīyādau vinyasedaṣṭamākṣarī ||

viṣṇuṃ samuddhareṃ ṅentaṃ padeṣu tripadāni ca |
dvitīyaṃ viṣṇurudrādyau ādaurcānaṃtameva ca ||

avādevi prakurvīta srajaṃ viṣṇuharaṃ triṣu |
ślokāni tāni vinyasya paṃcasthāneṣu vā punaḥ ||

evameva tathātāṃ hi labhyaṃte guruto'niśaṃ |
vāmāntaṃ bhautikaṃ krūramāhnikaṃ daśaśaktikam ||

kāmājñānaṃ ca tasyāgre paṃcāṃgaṃ biṃdumastakam |
mahīne ca punaṃ te ca māninī vāmanetraja ||

yānoṃtamuddhṛtya madhyasthaṃ samādiṃ mastake nyaset |
dīpanīṃ mūrddhi dātavyā prāṇajīvasi bhautikam ||

lokāṃttaṃ śirasākrātaṃ cchagalaṃḍaṃ sa dīpanīṃ |
punaretau sa vājīśaṃ mādanaṃ sarvalokataḥ ||

kalasā mastake devi vāmādi mūrddhni vinyaset |
khaḍgīśaṃ navake devi cāreṃtaṃ śavakaṃ bhavet ||

mānavādya vadhūrvarge caṃḍikāṃ mānavākṣarām |
ādau cāṃte sa devibhyāmaṃte vai pārthivaṃ nyaset ||

padāni padavinyasya mātṛkā vargasaṃkhyayā |
aṃgāni daśaṣaṭkaṃ vaikaṃ bāhumadhya pādayoḥ ||

bhūtavarṇaṃ samuccārya hṛdayethagaṇādhipam |
gaṇeśaṃ kutuvarṇena śive nyāsaṃsadā * * ||

taperastu tathādevi gaṇanāthaṃ prakīrtitam |
tadvakta cayamityuktamekadaṃtāya vai punaḥ ||

paṃcākṣaraṃ laṃbakarṇaṃ netreṣu purato nyaset |
p. 114a) raktatuṃḍāya bījāni hūṃ phaṭ svāhāstra diṅmatam ||

nyastvā dehasya sannāhaṃ mūrtiśaktyādi śaktikīm |
paṃcāśanrāgacakraṃ ca bhūtavarṇasamaprabham ||

nāgayajñopavītaṃ ca puṣpāsaṃpuṣṭavigraham |
niviṣṭapuṣkaraṃ tatra puṣpāmadanamaṃdire ||

udakaṃ viṣṇurāsīnāṃ śirasya śarkarāṃbujām |
śaṃkhacakraśaraṃśūlaṃ bījapūraṃ viṣāṇakam ||

gadābjamutpalāśaṃ ca ta yatkrūrapaṃkajam |
śaktyādi vamamūddhṛtya śaktyantaṃ gaṇapatiṃ nyaset ||

mātṛkokta vidhānena gaṇeśān śaktikā nyaset |
samyagabhyarcya vidyānāṃ prāpnuyādakṣayaṃ phalam ||

nābhimadhyesya purato devī bīijaṃ tadagrataḥ |
ākāśamaṃdirādhasthaṃ pūjanāsanamālikhet ||

saṃvartānte sthitaṃ bījamakrūraṃ śirasi nyaset |
paṃcāṃtako mahārudrorthimumadhye vyavasthitaḥ ||

mahattatva * * devi gaṇapatisamanvitam |
kṣetramadhye maheśāni māninyāmāhaśaṃkaram ||

maṃdarasthaṃ vijānīyāllohitaṃ raktamūrddhagam |
punastaṃ bījamādāya śuklaṃ satabhūṣitam ||

dhātrīśaṃ purato devi māninyā śivasaṃbhavam |
tasyāgre gaṃdhaśikhare anaṃtaṃ mūrdhnivinyaset ||

tristhāne svādibījaṃ hi nyastvādhasthaṃ vadhūṃ puraḥ |
hūṃ phaṭ namaśca vauṣaḍva trividhaṃ nyasta vartulaḥ ||

caṃḍikārā ca mā bījaṃ sthāne sthāne niyojayet |
athānyaṃ saṃpravakṣyāmi śṛṇu devi yathākramam ||

viṣṇurudraśca purato svarghāṃḍaṃ viṣṇusaṃbhavam |
p. 114b) dhārāṃte nistare devi ṅovu madhye varānane ||

apanīvarakedaṃtamaṃbikāṃ pūrvavat punaḥ |
rudrāntaṃ sadyamālikhya dharmarudrasya agrataḥ ||

bhāṣaṇī rukṣaṣi gehe vajramadhyaṃ tu bhūṣitā |
punarevamaheśani dīpanīmevasaṃbhavaṃ ||

yāte urumadhyastha ra ūrddhe yonimālikhet |
abhūpatitvaṃ ca * * mahākālasya mūrddhani ||

kāmādi vinyaset tadvad dhātrīśaṃ prāṇasaṃbhavam |
guhyaśaktisamopetaṃ dadāpaya sa caṃḍikāṃ ||

sūtibījadvayaṃ bhūyo vyatpayenāpi coddharet |
varmarudrasya purato lohitaṃ gaṃdhamādane ||

rutāṃtamagnataḥ kṛtvā dvisthāne dhātrisaṃbhavam |
sūkṣmo nāmamahārudro aṃbikāsanasaṃsthitaḥ ||

dahanībījamuddhṛtya rāgādiṃ mastake nyaset |
tadvaddevīṃ mahākālīmagnataḥ krūrasaṃbhavam ||

vāaruṇerddhe tu tārākṣīṃ punastāṃ sūkṣmabheditam |
rudrāṇīṃ navake bhūyoru antaṃ gaṃdhasaṃbhavam ||

lohitāstaruhārṇaṃ ca varāyate samālikhet |
pānomedhyagataṃ devi vyākṛtaṃtvavilomataḥ ||

dharmāgreṇa tamālikhya caṇo madhyaṃ tu kevalam |
punarasyādi bījaṃ hi anaṃtaṃ raktasaṃbhavam ||

āmoḍīkṣīramadhye ca meṣāṃte cāṃtacarma ca |
raktarudrāsane devi anantaṃ vinyaset punaḥ ||

mūladaṃḍāsanārūḍhaṃ śuklaprāṇaṃ samuddharet |
śubhavarṇaṃ mahākālaṃ rudrāṇāṃ ṛṣimaṃdire ||

vārāhaṃ pārthivaṃ rudraṃ viṣṇaṃ brāhmaṇameva ca |
evameva kramaṃ devi tathānyadapi pūrvavat ||

prathamaṃ hṛdayaṃ devi viṣṇave hṛdayaṃ nyaset |
p. 115a) dvādaśārddhaṃ śivājñeyā bhūyo dharmastathā bhavet ||

astraṃ harati sarvatra daśacaikaṃ prakalpayet |
brahmādyāśāśvatīnāntā bhūtārṇāḥ pūrvasaṃbhavāḥ ||

pādāni daśabhūtāni tattatsthāne srutāni ca |
paṃcabhūtasamaprakhyamayutārkaprabhāmiva ||

āyudhāni vicitrāṇi vicitrā bharaṇāni ca |
sarvakratumayaṃ saumyamaṣṭamesa caturdale ||

nibaṃddhe ca trikoṇe ca siṃhādimaṃtraṃ vinyaset |
ṣoḍaśe mātṛkvāvarṇa yogovaidarbha eva ca ||

varṇāntatragatorddhaṃ vai pārthivāntaṃ sureśvari |
kolavaktraṃ viśālākṣaṃ martyalokā vadhisthitam ||

tasyārddhaṃ vaktradeśe tu padbhyāṃ loke pitāmaham |
yogīśvarīṃ cakukāre śvāse prāṇaṃ samarcayet ||

kakuṭasya pathe devi saṃpūjyauditi cāśvinau |
hṛdaye ca mahāviṣṇuṃ raviśaśi dakṣavāmake ||

bhujekukṣau ca vaśakhau daṃṣṭrāgre ca vasudharā |
sarvadevamayaṃ devaṃ sākṣādbrahmasvarūpiṇam |

śaṃkhāni kheṭakhaḍgaṃ ca pāśāṃkuśagadādhanuḥ |
athavā nīlajīmūtaprabhārkā yutabhāskaram ||

dhavalāmṛtaśobhāḍhyamānaṃdabharitaṃ harim |
asya savyāpasavye tu ahaṃ brahmāgrato viṣaṃ ||

anantaṃ paścime yasmān mūrtādyā aṃgapūrvikāḥ |
prāguktavidhinā cātha varjayitvā vasuṃdharām ||

padmāpadmakarāmaṃkaviṣṭaraṃ pītabhāsvaram |
tattanmaṃtreṇa saṃpūjya nārasiṃhaṃ mahābalam ||

jitaṣaḍvargaśuddhātmā svadharmanirato janaḥ |
ṛjumadhyagataṃ devi nabhomadhyagatāṃ śivām ||

p. 115b)

raktabhairavamārūḍhāṃ tasyāgre vedabhairavam |
śubhaśakti samopetaṃ bhujaṃgakrūramagrataḥ ||

mahāviṣṇuṃ tamuccārya jvalantaṃ śaktimagrataḥ |
medaṃraktasamārūḍhaṃ tārākṣī pātu bheditā ||

āsyate saṃpadaṃ bhūyo sahasrārajñamastakam |
nārāyaṇaṃ caturthaṃ ca tasyāgre yādṛśaṃ śṛṇu ||

nārasiṃhaṃ punardevi bhīṣaṇaṃ bhīṣaṇaṃ punaḥ |
kātvamadhyaṃ tu dhātrīśaṃ raktarudraṃ sa biṃdukam ||

mṛtyuṃ ca purato devi guṇoṃtaṃ meṣasaṃbhavam |
bhe ca madhye samuddhṛtya gaṃdhānaṃtaṃ śive nyaset ||

mahākālaṃ samuddhṛtya ve vu madhyasane sthitam |
asyāgre aṃbikābījaṃ vāmāṃtaṃ ca tribījakam ||

evamanyāni bījāni bāhyā yena yathā bhavet |
dvātriṃśati prabhedena asyā devyā yathā kramāt ||

jñātavyaṃ purato devi rahasyante vadāmyaham |
na ca bhāgavibhāgaṃ ca devānāmapi durllabham ||

ādi vidyā vibhedena evaṃ devi navānavī |
dvātriṃśadakṣarī vidyā ādi prastāvasaṃyutā || 


iti śrīkubjikāmatetripaṃcāśatitamaḥ paṭalaḥ || 0 ||




śrīdevyuvāca ||


vaṃdyāsmi devadevasya saphalaṃ jīvitaṃ mama |
pūrvoktā yā mahādevī sarvapāśanikṛntani ||

na tadasyā kramedeva yathāvedmi punaḥ punaḥ |
sarvāgameṣu taṃtreṣu gopitā sarvakāmadā ||

mārtaṇḍabhairave deva tathā ca tripurārṇave |
sarvasaṃmohane taṃtre rurubhede ca yatnataḥ ||

idānīṃ śrotumicchāmi sā vidyā saṃpradāyataḥ |
āpurapatnīsāmāyā dṛṣṭādṛṣṭaphalapradā ||

p. 116a)

vaktumarhasi deveśa yadi tuṣṭosi me prabho |
aghavāmakrameṇaiva gehamadhyesu vistaram ||


śrībhairava uvāca ||


sādhupṛṣṭaṃ mahādevi purā vai śravaṇena tu |
brahmaṇā vāsave nātha viṣṇunā ca mahātmanā ||

vāgīśena tu śukreṇa samyagārādhitā satī |
sā vidyā paramā devi viṣṇave proktavānaḍaṃ ||

sāṃprataṃ kathayāmīyaṃ śṛṇuṣvāyatalocane |
pūrvoktakramato vidvān sa pūjyaśrīkramaṃ guruḥ ||

gorocanādibhirdravyairlikhitvā śuddhabhūtale |
meruṃ tatra mahādevī mātṛkā saṃyutaṃ tathā ||

paṃcapraṇavayogena tadgraheṇa ca mātṛkām |
vinysya vidhivadbhūya uddharenmaṃtramuttamam ||

paṃcapraṇavasaṃyuktaṃ taduddhāraṃ puroktavat |
navātmāntaritaṃ kṛtvā japakāle japennaraḥ ||

praṇamāmi mahādevīṃ sarvavidyātmarūpiṇīm |
vyomagāṃ gajamadhyasthāṃ yonyākārāṃ navāṃvikām ||

māṃkurve? tu yoginyo bhuvasthānma?ghaṣiṣṭhitāḥ |
uddhariṣyāmi tāṃ vidyāṃ śṛṇu tatra prayatnataḥ ||

kailāsamaṃdire devi śrīkaṃṭho nāmanāmataḥ |
ādivarṇaṃ vijānīyāt traipure bhavatvidaṃ ||

krūravarṇaṃ śivedattaṃ maṃdare tu tataḥ param |
bhūtamadhye caturthaṃ tu śaṃkhinī yatra saṃsthitā ||

tamādhāya ca medhāvīrupūrveṇa samanvitam |
dvitīyaṃ tu varārohe tṛtīyaṃ tu tataḥ param ||

maṃdare ṛṣimālāyāṃ te pūrvaṃ kevalaṃ bhavet |
tatormadhyagataṃ devī paṃcātaṃ yatra bhairavam ||

caturthaṃ kevalaṃ vidyāccaturthaṃ vāruṇaṃ bhavet |
bhagamadhyagataṃ guhyaṃ maṃdare sūkṣmasaṃyutam ||

p. 116b)

ṣaṣṭhaṃ taṃ tadvijānīyāt saptamaṃ cāṣṭamaṃ śṛṇu |
tristhāne haṃsavarṇau tukāṃbhavāntāgni biṃdumat ||

prathame varṇasāṃkalpe jñānaśakti vibhūṣitam |
dvitīye varṇasakale śavāruḍhaṃ sureśvari ||

karālīvarṇamuddhṛtya pūrvaṃ yaṃtrasaṃsthitam |
dhruvādivarṇasaṃyuktaṃ navamaṃdaśamaṃ tataḥ ||

maṃdare bhūtamālāyāṃ cchāgalaṃ śikhivāhinīṃ |
biṃdumadhyāsanārūḍhaṃ sūkṣmarudreṇasaṃyutam ||

krodhavarṇaṃ samuddhṛtya nūpureṇasamanvitam |
ekādaśaṃ mahādevī haṃsavarṇau punaḥ punaḥ ||

tristhāneṣu samuddhṛtya krūravarṇavibhūṣitam |
prathamenatamaṃyuktaṃ dvitīye mūrtisaṃyutam ||

tṛtīye tu dhruvāṃtaṃ ca tristhāne dīpanī yutam |
punaḥ śrīkaṃṭhanāmānaṃ devī paṃcadaśākṣarī ||

viśvamadhye śivasthāne ru pūrvaṃ gaṃdhamādane |
rudrave ca rudramālāyāṃ gāyatryā ca samanvitam ||

bhujaṃgabhairavī devī dīpanī yatra saṃsthitā |
kailāse vāmamadhye tu ādivarṇenasaṃyutam ||

punareva maheśānī ghoreti padasaṃbhavet |
punaḥ śrīkaṃṭhamuddhṛtya ghorāśabdaṃ na saṃśayaḥ ||

kālo nāma mahārudro bhe ca madhye vyavasthitaḥ |
dhruvādivarṇayogena trayoviṃśaṃ na saṃśayaḥ ||

ṭhaṭhi madhyagataṃ devi caturviṃśākṣaro bhavet |
ekādaśe ṛtusthāne cchāgalī yatra saṃsthitā ||

bhujaṃgabhairavārūḍhā dīpanī yatra saṃsthitā |
p. 117a) anaṃtā krūrasaṃyuktaṃ tattvasaṃkhyā na saṃśayaḥ ||

śūlaśca cchāgalī devī dīpanyā sūkṣma biṃdukā |
ekādaśādi varṇaṃ ca māyādevī yutaṃ kuru ||

nārāyaṇī martyadevī sūkṣmarudreṇa maṃḍitā |
aṣṭāviṃśati varṇoyaṃ krodhasūkṣmavibhūṣitam ||

punarnārāyaṇī devī sūkṣmarudravibhūṣitā |
vāmamadhyagataṃ devī vajriṇī yatrasaṃsthitā ||

sūkṣmarudreṇa saṃyuktārāyuktā ca dvidhā kuru |
kailāse ṛtupūrveṇa yuktaṃ rā * sureśvarī ||

dvātriṃśadakṣarī devī varṇarūpā vyavasthitā |
evaṃ devi mayākhyātaṃ āmnāye paścime tare ||

karāṃganyāsasaṃnaddhaṃ vaktranyāsamataḥ param |
varṇanyāsaṃ tato dehe mātṛkā śabdavat ||

trividyādyoryāṣṭakaṃ tu ṣoḍhānysāsaṃ tu deśikaḥ |
yathā devī purāproktā vistareṇa śuvidyayā ||

pīṭhanyāsaṃ tato devī saptaviṃśatisaṃkhyayā |
saṃpūjyaśrīkramānnāthān pādukāpaṅkti saṃyutān ||

yathopacārasaṃyuktapuṣpagaṃdhākṣatādibhiḥ |
pīṭhāṣṭakaṃ tato devī bāhyeṣṭakaṃ kramāt ||

samayācārayogena datvā karṇasamuttamam |
mudrāṃ badhānirālaṃbāṃ kṣamasveti visarjayet ||

sthitvā tadagrataḥ samyak sahasraṃ cāṣṭakaṃ śatam |
evaṃ jñātvā varārohe dhyānagamyāmatīṃdriyām ||

dhyātvātāmiṃdusaṃpūrṇa vadanāṃ bhojamaṃḍalām |
paṃcavaktrāṃ daśabhujāṃ pinonnatapayodharām ||

rahasyānāṃ rahasyaṃ tu gopyānāṃ gopyamuttamam |
pāvanaḥ pāvanīnāṃ ca maṃgalānāṃ ca maṃgalam ||

p. 117b)

ityuktaṃ kramayogena kathitaṃ ca sureśvari |
athānyaṃ saṃpravakṣyāmi svagopyaṃ devi durlabham ||

vidyādyabhedabhinnastu saṃkṣepāt kathayāmite |
pūrvaṃ devāsure devi madhyamāne mahārṇave ||

asurairnirjitā devā raviśakra purogamāḥ |
tasmādāgatyasarve te yatrāhaṃ sthitavān priye ||

praṇamyakāyavāgabhistu yathā vṛttaṃ nyavedayat |
iṃdrastu guruṇāsārddhaṃ vepamānaḥ suduḥkhitaḥ ||

tataḥ prasannacittohaṃ vīraśrīvijayapradam |
bhogadaṃ mokṣadaṃ caiva vākpradaṃ śivadaṃ śivam ||

jñānadaṃ rājyadaṃ devi khaḍgapātālasiddhidam |
aṣṭaiśvaryakhecaratvaṃ svargakāmaphalapradam ||

bahunoktena kiṃ devi sarvakāmaphalapradam |
idaṃ viciṃtya bahuśaḥ sarvadevamayātmakam ||

manumaṃtraṃ dvātriṃśadarṇaghaṃ kaule paścima dikyate |
athavidyāmayākhyātā kṛttikākulanāyikā ||

parāparavibhāgena vidyātrailokya mohinī |
proktā sā tu maheṃdrāya gurave deśikottamaḥ ||

pāraṃparyakramāyātā triprabhedā maheśvarī |
paryaḥ śakti samuddiṣṭā pāraṃ śivamihocyate ||

paraughaṃ prathamaṃ devi divyaughaṃ tadanaṃtaram |
divyādi tṛtīyaṃ ca guruḥ sarvatramāgatām ||

brahmāṇaṃ kāyaśuddhyarthaṃ vākśuddhyarthaṃ janārdanam |
manaḥ śuddhyarthakaṃ rudraṃ triśuddhiṃ sarvadā yajet ||

ulakramaṃ tu vijñeyaṃ madhyajyeṣṭhaṃ śekharam |
bhaktānāmeva rakṣārthaṃ yathāvatkathayāmite ||

api sarvāgamajñasya pūrvācāra parasya ca |
p. 118a) olitvā na vihīnasya praṇāmaṃ naivakārayet ||

praṇamāmi mahādeviṃ brahmāviṣṇīśa sevitāṃ |
siddhacāraṇasaṃghaiśca pannagaiḥ sa surāsuraiḥ ||

sṛṣṭisthiti layaṃ asyāṃ vaṃdetāṃ kulakubjikām |
evaṃ bhūtāṃ tu tāṃ vidyāmuddharenmerumadhyataḥ ||

pūrvavatsamayaṃ devi saṃpūjya ca kulakramam |
maṃdare ṛṣimālāyāṃ yatra saṃsthitām ||

prathamaṃ devi vijñeyaṃ tanormadhyaṃ dvitīyakam |
vāmamadhyaṃ tṛtīyaṃ tu bhāgamadhyagataṃ punaḥ ||

māyā devi samāyuktaṃ medare ca caturthakam |
vāgīśvarī valokāṃte paṃcapaṃca na saṃśayaḥ ||

cchāgalīṃ bhūtamadhye tu śikhinīṃ karṇamadhyataḥ |
kailāsaṃ maṃdare devi sapūrveṇa samanvitam ||

saṣṭhaṃ taṃ ca vijānīyāt saptamaṃ cāṣṭamaṃ śṛṇu |
navātmānaṃ tatoddhṛtya bhūyastvaṃ parameśvari ||

sāsimadyagataṃ bījamaṃvikāsthānagaṃ kuru |
trimūrtibhairavaṃ devi pūrvaniṣadhālaye ||

śivapradeśasaṃbhinnaṃ kubjiketi sureśvari |
haṃsavarṇaṃ tridhā kṛtvā sṛṣṭisthitilaya kramāt ||

trividhopi samāyukta kāṃtadhārṇāgni biṃdukam |
anaṃtena śivobhinnaṃ sṛṣṭibījaṃ tu madhyame ||

pūrvaṃ niṣadārṇenabhinnaṃ saṃhārakaṃ punaḥ |
dhruvāṃtaṃ devi saṃyuktaṃ daśagaukaṃ na saṃśayaḥ ||

karmarudraṃ tato devi cāmuṃḍā ṛṣimaṃdire |
kevalaṃtaṃ samādāya vilamadhya gataṃ priye ||

mahākālaṃ kevalaṃ tu bhe ca madhye vyavasthitam |
ekādaśe krodhavarṇaṃ kalālī tatra saṃsthitam ||

p. 118b)

dhruvādi varṇasaṃyuktaṃ bilvamadhyagataṃ punaḥ |
jñānaśaktiyutaṃ devi kailāse ratnamaṃdire ||

kūrmarudraṃ samārabhya kulapaṃcākṣarī yutam |
punareva maheśāni ādi uddhārayogataḥ ||

ekonaviṃśadyarṇoyaṃ śrīkaṃṭhastvatraviṃśatiḥ |
ekādaśe tu ghorākhyā sa pūrvaṃ gaṃdhamādane ||

sadyojātenasaṃyuktā gāyatrī yatra pārvati |
narake śūladaṃḍaṃ ca bhujaṃgo nāma bhairavaḥ ||

anaṃtena śivobhinnaṃ dvātriṃśadgaṃdhamādane |
mahākālaṃ punardevi dhruvādyāsanasaṃsthitam ||

ṭhvimadhyagataṃ gṛhya gāyatrī saṃkhyakokṣaraṃ |
haṃsavarṇaṃ dvidhādeva sṛṣṭisthiti vibhedataḥ ||

dhruvādyavarṇaṃ deveśi cchāgalīnāmanāmataḥ |
śūladaṃḍāsanārūḍhaṃ anaṃtaṃgaṃdhamādane ||

akrūreṇa śirobhinnaṃ ādyahaṃse sureśvari |
dvitīye cchāgalī devī śūlaḍaṃḍaṃ trimūrtinā ||

varṇāvetau sureśāni trailokya vaśamānayat |
karāli varṇamuddhṛtya dhruvādīti padaṃ bhavet ||

varṇāvetau punardīptā tāramadhyaṃ tu sūkṣmakam |
maṃdare ṛṣimadhyau ca cāmuṃḍā kūrmabhairavaḥ ||

dvidhā ca proccareddevi kailāse vāmamaṃdire |
jñānaśaktisamopetaṃ ru pūrvaṃ taṃ na saṃśayaḥ ||

sārātsārataraṃ caiva guhyādguhyaṃ mayoditam |
pūtapravaṇakaṃ cāthakramotkramagataṃ bhavet ||

prāgbhavaṃ haṃsavarṇaṃ ca śaktibīje tathā bhavet |
mā bījeva tathā devi haṃsādhaḥ kramayogataḥ ||

maṃḍiśo bhairavastatra kāṃtapātāgni biṃdu yuk |
punaścānyātha deveśi vāruṇaṃ viniyojayet ||

evaṃ bhedaiḥ kramairvāpi mātṛkāmaṃtrasaṃpuṭaiḥ |
p. 119a) nyasvādau tadgṛhinyāsādūrddhaprāgdakṣapaścimam ||

uttaraṃ vaktrakaṃ cātha arddhavarṇaṃ tato nyaset |
pūjā dhyānaṃ ca saṃdiṣṭaṃ yathāpūrvaṃ na saṃśayaḥ ||

ṣaṇmāsābhyāsa yogena sādhakomatsamo bhavet |
idramagniṃ yamaṃ rakṣaṃ varuṇaṃ viśvagaṃ śaśim ||

īśānaṃ pūjayen madhye devī saptāṣṭakaṃ tataḥ |
dharmādi preta paryantaṃ paṃcapaṃceti bhedataḥ ||

punaḥ ṣoḍhāprabhinnena aṣṭāviṃśakrameṇa tu |
yonimadhyagataṃ liṃgaṃ yonyo deva puṭī kṛtam ||

tanmadhye vāmano rūpaṃ lakṣayecca punaḥ punaḥ |
madhye maṃthānayogena kālāgni jvalite kila ||

madhanācchivaśaktistu tadānaṃdaḥ prajāyate |
niścalatvaṃ bhaved devi śivaśaktyo bhavedataḥ ||

tenāmṛtena cātmānaṃ plāvyamānaṃ viciṃtayet |
etat tu paramaṃ divyaṃ paramānaṃdalakṣaṇam ||

etat te māṇipūrasya rahasyaṃ prakaṭīṃ kṛtam |
gopitaṃ taṃ ca maṃtreṣu lakṣmī tulyaṃ prakāśitam ||

durlabhaṃ siddhamārgasya kiṃpunastvitareṣu ca |
uktakālena siddhyante avaśyaṃ nādṛtaṃ vacaḥ ||

kiṃtu jyotirlatākāraṃ cakramārgasya madhyagam |
yadvā mahātradevānāṃ vaṃdetāṃ śāṃbharī majām ||

dadhrāpremāṇatāyābhiḥ prayakṣaphaladaṃ yataḥ |
sarvāgama virodhena na nityaṃ yoginī maham ||

pravṛtte bhairavī taṃtre sarve varṇā dvijātayaḥ |
niṃvṛtte bhairavī taṃtre sarve varṇāḥ pṛthak pṛthak ||

ityevameva deveśi rahasyaṃ prakaṭī kṛtam |
bhaktiyuktā yadātavyaṃ tvayā sarvatra naṃdini ||

p. 119b)

lakṣaṃ yato japastena tadarddhastu hatakriyā |
manaso vīśvarorddhesi vasudeva * * * * ||

vidyeyaṃ parameśāni sarvakāmadudyā bhave |
saptakoṭīścarī devī trilakṣāt siddhibhāgbhavet ||

svāhā śīrṣake sañjite ṅe vāhasaṃkṣreṃ hrīṃ hrāṃ juhātejasaṃkarṣaṇī 
nāmā vidyā sagha phalapradā ||

hāsvāmanāṃ kraṭokṣu vopracohaṃkaṃ hrīṃ oṃ aiṁ juḥ saḥ havīśvanayetva 
manākaśīrṣakaṃ svaḥ yanohaṃsaṃ hrīṃ ruḥ svaṃ u || 0 ||

iti śrīkubjikāmatetripaṃcāśatitamaḥ paṭalaḥ || 0 ||




śrīdevyuvāca ||


bhagavatsarvadharmajña viśvakāraṇakāraṇa |
navakoṭipramāṇena yaduktaṃ kubjikā mate ||

utpannaṃ mama vijñānaṃ nirvāṇa padasiddhidaṃ |
aneko vāsanākhyehaṃ bhavatāmedya vistarāt ||

punaḥ pṛcchāmi tajjñānaṃ vrajenmuktiṃ ca śāsvanīm |
śaive ca nākule paṃcakalpavedādi nirgatam ||

paṃcarātrādirāddhāṃtaṃ jñānaṃ nirvāṇadaṃ param |
brahmatvaṃ tapasājagmurye naite ca tapodhanāḥ ||

atra te darśayiṣyāmi tebhya uddhṛtyasaṃvanāt |
devā api na jānaṃti kiṃ punastvitare janāḥ ||

sadāśivāntā varṇādyāstanu sthāneṣu vinyaset |
śivaṃ hakāramityuktamauṃkāraḥ śaktirucyate ||

biṃdunādātmakautadvadukāraṃ biṃdumastakam |
śrīkaṃṭhaṃ purato nyasya asyādiścāṃta devatāḥ ||

vāmāṃtaṃ vinyasenmūrddhni mālinī śirasaṃbhavān |
ādadhettaṃ madhyamaṃ ca māninī prathamaṃ punaḥ ||

dīptāya purato devi śrīkaṃṭhasyāṃta deśataḥ |
merurudrasya agre tu rutāṃtaṃ gaṃdhasaṃbhavam ||

khiṃnamadhyaṃ samuddhṛtya eṣa tasyā sanaṃ bhavet |
p. 120a) devavarṇaṃ śivedattaṃ cihnamadhyaṃ samedakam ||

gaṃdhānaṃtenasaṃbhinnaṃ māṃsaṃ tasyā tato dadet |
rūpiṇe ca punardevi ata ūrddhaṃ ca yacchṛṇu ||

ānaṃdā hyapadaṃ bhūyaḥ prāṇarudraṃ tu kevalaṃ |
medonāmamahārudraḥ śubhe bhaktyāsamanvitaḥ ||

rudrāṇāṃ narake raktaṃ raktarudrāsanasthitam |
tayormūrddhi pradātavyaṃ maṃdare sūkṣmasaṃbhavam ||

bhāgamadhyagataṃ devi tārākṣīṃ kevalaṃ viduḥ |
sāsimadhyagataṃ śukla kālaṃ vāpaṃ ca maṃ punaḥ ||

jñānaṃ ca purato devi raktasyopari lohitam |
sūkṣmarudrasthitaṃ kuryyācchakrasyopari pārvati ||

dhātrīśaṃ kāṃtamadhye tu kālaṃ vāmānu mūrddhagam |
brahmacārī jitakrodhī vratacaryā samanvitaḥ ||

svacchaḥ svacchāṃvaraḥ sragvī nanktāśī sudṛḍhavrataḥ |
dhyānaṃ kuryāt triṣusthāne dhyānaḥ śuklavistare ||

iduṃ gokṣārakuṃdābhaṃ devadevaṃ jagadgurum |
anantena samavāvaddha kakṣaikavicaraṃ śivam ||

ahaṃ vaddhajaṭāmaulī śaraccaṃdravirājitaḥ |
anaṃtā veṣṭitaṃ vāme na kuṭaṃ sumanoharam ||

nānāmālāgaṇaiḥ kḷptāṃ dakṣiṇe varṇamālikām |
paṃkasūtramanaṃtaṃ hi puṃḍarīkājināṃ caram ||

padmāśanaṃ śivaṃ śāṃtaṃ svacchaṃdagatirīśvaram |
svacoktāni ṣaḍaṃgāni ṣaṭpadānīhatantayā ||

dvātriṃśadakṣarāṃ devi piḍamadhye ca vinyaset |
umādurgāprabhāsatyā cāmuṃḍāhaṃsyāpi pāsinī ||

vijayā virajā viśvā jayā ca sumukheśvarī |
vāmājyeṣṭhā ca raudrī ca aṃbikecchā bhayapradā ||

p. 120b)

dākṣāyaṇī surānaṃdā bhadrakālī kulāṃganā |
kulaśābhā mahādevī hṛllekhā gaganāsatī ||

raktākarālikābhūyo mahāsūkṣmā sarasvatī |
saṃhāravo nāma mahākālāsanasthitaḥ ||

pāvakenārddhasaṃyukto vāyuvegāsusaṃyutaḥ |
anugrahaśivepetaḥ svayaṃ pūrvāpare tu gam ||

tattatsthāneṣu takṣetra vidyābījāni saṃsthitaḥ |
pūrvapaṃcārṇavatkuryāṃ śeṣamanye sureśvari ||

dūtīśasyāgrato devi cātmani samālikhet |
ākāśamaṃdire ghore grahāṇāmuttamaṃ śive ||

mūrddhnibiṃduputaṃ kuryān māninyā urusaṃbhavam |
śuklaṃ hṛdayamadhyasthaṃ madhyabhāgaṃ tadagrataḥ ||

vāgīśamāsanaṃ dṛdyādakrūraṃ śirasi nyaset |
paraṃbrahmapadaṃ devi puruṣaṃ kṛṣṇameva ca ||

pāmo madhyagataṃ bhūyaḥ śuṣmaṃ krūrasya mūrdhagam |
śivāṃ ca vilamadhyānta bhairavaṃ biṃdumagrataḥ ||

dhruvāṃtaṃ kātvamadhyasya dhenumadhyāsanaṃ bhavet |
khaṃḍapāvakamāruḍhaṃ vetaṃsanakare punaḥ ||

sūkṣmarudrā sthitaṃ kuryān māninyā cakramuttamam |
sa pūrvaṃ navake pūrvaṃ dhruvāntāsanasaṃsthitam ||

rudrāṇāṃ maṃdire sadyaḥ pūrvaṃ cāsyāgrato likhet |
nābhivarṇaṃ samuddhṛtya ha madhye sa biṃdukam ||

kaṭakaṃ viśvarūpāya vajriṇī bhautikena ca |
praṇāmaṃ cādyabījaṃ hi praṇukta kramatoddharet ||

aghoravasunarddevi pūjāhomaṃ ca tarpaṇam |
bījāni bhūtavarṇāni caturyuktaṃ sureśvari ||

yajāmahe tu devīṃ tu mṛtyormukṣīyavarddhanam |
bījaṃsantaṃ saṃbhūyaścānaṃtaṃciṃtameva ca ||

sarvaṃ varddhaye saṃte punarevasamuddharet |
p. 121a) amarīśādimaṃ bījaṃ sthāṇu rudrādi saṃyutam ||

agre tasyāta uddhṛtya tasyāṃ taṃ purataḥ sthitam |
tristhānāttausamuddhṛtya sthāne svaṃgāna ṣaṭpadaiḥ ||

rakṣohano valahana sumukhaḥ kālarūpakaḥ |
vijayo jayaṃtaḥ prajayo manonmanāya balāya ca ||

bhūtaśaktyākṣarairmūrttiṃ padaiḥ saṃkraṃdanādibhiḥ |
arcayitvā punastasya rudralakṣaṃ yatavrataḥ ||

mṛtyurvilāsakairdevi maṃtro'bhīṣṭaphalapradaḥ |
viṣṇubījaṃ samuddhṛtya ru aṃtaṃ hasya agrataḥ ||

lokopi śirasākrāṃtaṃ ekarudraṃ tṛtīyakam |
ākāśamaṃdarāṃtasthamātmādī biṃdusannibham ||

sthāpitavyaṃ prayatnena śivaṃ tasyārddhasaṃbhavam |
māninyāsakravarṇasya śiverudraṃ tṛtīyakam ||

dhātrīśasyāsanaṃ dayāddṛṣīṇāmaṃtare sthitam |
dīpanī ta?gra deśe ca sādhane taṃ samālikhet ||

nāsikāgre punardevi asthirudrāsanaṃ bhavet |
sahasrāṃśe sphuratyaṃte dadhamadhyaṃ dhruvāṃtakam ||

kākānāmaṃ mahārudrā dīpanī sūkṣmamūrddhagāḥ |
ekādaśādi purato kāṃtaṃ gāṃtaṃ pravinyaset ||

turīyaṃ bhūtamadhye tu kāṃtaṃ vairojamadhyataḥ |
mahākālyāsanatvena āliṃgaṃ gaṃdhamādane ||

śākamanye śarīrāya praṇāmāstu paraṃ padam |
ātmānaṃ yute devi vijānīhi puroktavat ||

aprakṛtyātmakaṃ nāma saṃbhūtaṃ paramārthataḥ |
bījānīmāni bhūyaśca śaṃkaraṃ lokaśaṃkaram ||

nārāyaṇaṃ caurthyantaṃ navātmānaṃ purasthitaṃ |
catvāri devavasūrṇaistathā vahniḥ svabhūtakaiḥ ||

p. 121b)

nyastavyā vidhiyogena kāyamadhye sureśvari |
arddhanārīśvaraṃ devaṃ na sarvatrāvataṃsakam ||

somārkamaścitrai śvanānākalpamanoharam |
dvātriṃśallakṣaṇairvādyaṃ devadevamumāpatim ||

pāśāṃkuśaṃ vāmadakṣe akṣasrak nṛkapālakam |
taptahāṭakasaṃkāśaṃ somaṃsiṃdūrasaṃpratam ||

siṃhāstu viṣṭyave devi pūrvavat paritoṣayet |
aṃbujaṃ paścime pūjyaṃ bāṇaṃ dhanadadiyagataṃ ||

rudramākhaṃḍaleyaṣṭā īśvaraṃ svarasannibham |
ananādi catuṣkeṣu hṛllekhāṃ ca gadāṃ punaḥ ||

raktāṃ karālikāṃ devaṃ mahatsūkṣmaṃ maheśvaram |
vaṃdasūtpaladhāriṇyo veṇībaddhāsvalaṃkṛtāḥ ||

ṣaṣṭamedaṣṭapūrvādirutaṃ vasūṃ caraṃ navam |
rutejāmagni digbhāve gojaleśo vare yajet ||

avajāmarcayedvājau atrijāmaiśakoṇake |
brāhmaṇyādi catuḥṣaṣṭi vidhānena prayatnataḥ ||

homaṃdakṣiṇakuṃḍe tu lājātilasataṃdulaiḥ |
svayaṃbhūkusumaiścāpi nṛpa bilvamahīruhaiḥ ||

caṃdrakastūrikonmiśra guḍakṣaudraghṛtaplutam |
arddhalakṣaṃ japet pūrvaṃ tathāhomaṃ sa tarpaṇam ||

tarpaṇāni punardadyānnavaṃ svāghaṃ manoharam |
tribhirvarghairyatāhāro vratacaryāsamanvitaḥ ||

palamathā śvābhistṛptau paramamīśvarau |
pradadyādīpsitaṃ sarvaṃ khecaratvā pavargakam ||

bhūpradeśaṃ samaṃ kṛtvā caṃdanāguru carcitaṃ |
kṛtvāmaṃḍalakaṃ tatra catuṣpatraṃ triyonikam ||

tataḥ krameṇavarṇāni vinyase tisrabāhyataḥ |
p. 122a) ādīkṣāṃta akārotau vinyasejjagadīśvarīṃ ||

tanmadhye pūrvavadrūpaṃ saṃvicintya hṛdaṃbuje |
manasāvartate yogī yāvadbrahmā sa jīvati ||

haraṃhariṃ dakṣavāme vā dakṣe haraṃharim |
triśūlaṃkaṃbukaṃ devi devaṃ cakraṃ śivaṃ hariḥ ||

tāraṃ sa biṃdukaṃ devī hṛdolkaṃ prathamaṃ hṛdi |
sarvāntapadabhūyo meṣaṃ tadvacchirasi nyaset ||


maholka nityatṛptāṃte vīraṃ vīrolkameva ca |
śikhāyāmanādi bodhaṃ tṛtīyaṃ parameśvarīm ||

ādi varṇatṛtīyānte hyuktaṃ svātaṃ ameva ca |
madogaṃdhena sahasā sarvaṃ sa paṃcamaṃ punaḥ ||

nidyaganteśca praśaktī netrāṇāṃ rakṣaṇe nyaset |
vāgīśaṃ ca sahasrolka anaṃtaśaktistu kaṃṭakam ||

pūjayet pūrvavadbhaktyā bhedābhedaṃ na kārayet |
saṃśayātmā praṇaśyati na sukhaṃ na parāṃgatim ||

viśvaset paramaṃ dharme adharme naiva viśvaset |
dharmeṇa kratuviśvāpi yathā bhojyaṃti devatāḥ ||

tasmānmaṃtramayaṃ lokaṃ dharmaṃ maṃtramayaṃ viduḥ |
dharmeṇa pṛthivīṃ jitvā divyayānairmanoharaiḥ ||

krīḍantedyāpi dṛśyante devāścāpi tapodhanāḥ |
tenadharmātmake maṃtre yeṣāṃ prajñā na vartate |

teṣāṃ svargaṃ ca mokṣaṃ ca ihamūtra na hi kvacit |
uttaraṃ dakṣiṇāṃ caiva dvāvetau sarvadehinām ||

paṃthānau tau paribhraṣṭe adharmaṃ varddhate niśam |
darddharīdaṃdaśūkādi yonistarjayate khalu ||

teṣu duḥkhānyanekāni bhujyaṃte duḥkhasaṃbhavam |
na tatra sukhale śostihākaṣṭaṃ parameśvari ||

garbhavāseti duḥkhārto vālye ajñānamohitaḥ |
p. 122b) yauvane viṣayāpanno vārddhake maraṇonmukhaḥ ||

mṛtasya janmasyā devaṃ ghaṭiyaṃtra bhramo yathā |
tasmāccastvajanoloke śrutivākyairniyamyate ||

apadādi doṣeṇa madhyamo vinivinyaset |
kaṣāya carmavanmadohaṭhenāyātimārdavam ||

tasmādurgamaye yaṃtre maṃtravitpaṃḍito janaḥ |
saṃdhāyavidhinā devi vidhānena puroktavat |

khecarī ādi siddhināmadhipo jāyate naraḥ |
athānyaṃ saṃpravakṣyāmi sugopyaṃ bhūvidurlabham ||

yenavijānamātreṇa kṣubhyate bhuvanatrayam |
devī bījadvayaṃ devī aṃte eti dvayaṃ punaḥ ||

yadvārṇagrahamālāyāmāṣāṭībhuviṃsaṃsthitam |
tṛtīyaṃ tadvijānīyād yavadhye * caturthakam ||

yasya varṇasya biṃdurddhekhikārasya adhaḥ sthitam |
mālinī vaktravarṇaṃ ca navake padmavasthitam ||

tārākṣīgrahamālāyāṃ vamoṃtaṃ mastakaṃ nyaset |
asyāṃte gaṃdhaśikhare anaṃtaṃ krūrasaṃyutam ||

nārāyaṇaṃ saneyuktaṃ kāmaṃ raktāsanaṃ bhavet |
rudrāṃtaṃ krūraśikharaṃ pinākī tasya ūrddhataḥ |

ekādaśe ṛṣisthāne arghīśo yasya ūrddhataḥ |
punarasyādi raktasthaṃ gaṃdhāṃkurasamanvitam ||

nanādi varṇamuddhṛtya mālinī dakṣamūlakam |
daṃḍavyamāsanaṃ tasya mālinī vaktrasaṃbhavam ||

ru aṃtaṃ purato nyasya viṭemadhyaṃ tathā punaḥ |
medonāma mahārudrośo urddhe kevalaṃ viduḥ ||

yadvarṇameṣa ūrddhe ca mālinyā taruyukpunaḥ |
raktarudrāsanatvena sūkṣmarudraṃ śive nyaset ||

p. 123a) mālinī prathamaṃ bījaṃ jhaṃṭīśenasamanvitam |
saṃvartakaṃ mahārudramastirudrosthitam bhavam |

raktaṃrudantamakrūraṃ prāṇaṃ meṣaṃ punarbhavet |
ekādaśe ca svasthāne yadvarṇaṃ biṃdumastakam ||

asyāgre ca sa phetkārī khaṃḍasomeśvarānvitam |
itīdaṃ paramaṃ guhyaṃ pavitraṃ sarvakāmadam ||

tasya saṃsmaraṇāddevi daityadānavarākṣasāḥ |
vidravaṃti mahāvīryāḥ siṃhatrastāmṛgā iva ||

asya jāpena homena bhuktimukti karesthita |
strīṣu vidyāsupaṭhati svasādhya kṛte hariḥ ||

teṣu vairaṃ na kartavyamaparādhaśatairapi |
vīthīdvārasamāyuktaṃ caturasraṃ samālikhet ||

graṇa?ḍapuṣkaraṃ kṣentamaṃkuśaṃ sarvato mukham |
varṇānu kramayogena ṣoḍasāraṃ kalānvitam ||

vinyased varṇasonmadhye madhye dvātriṃśadakṣaram |
vyaṃjanāti caturbāhye padmamaṣṭadalaṃ punaḥ ||

tadvadvarṇāni vinyasya vargiṇāha ṣaḍakṣaram |
bhūtacchadeṣu sarvatra brahmā anaṃta devatāḥ ||

punaragni sthitaṃ kuryyānnārasiṃhaṃ śivānvitam |
tatrasaṃlikhyakāryāṇi sādhayedvaravarṇini ||

saudarśanaṃ mahāsiṃhaṃ tatra vinyasya pārvati |
piṃgākṣaṃ daṃṣṭriṇaṃ bhīmaṃ bhadrakāyaṃ sudurjayam ||

tatra śaṃkhāsikheṭaṃ ca tomaraṃ mudgaraṃ punaḥ |
pāśāṃkuśau yadāpadma śarāsaśarameva ca ||

kranudaṃḍāgni varadaṃ devadevaṃ jagadgurum |
kauśeyavāsasaṃ raktaṃ raktābharaṇamaṃḍitam ||

vīkṣaṇaṃ caika vaktraṃ ca bhāskarā yuta suprabham |
tasyāhaṃ vaktraśe * ca brahmapādadvaye sthitaḥ |

p. 123b) siṃhikāmaṃ kuśaṃ devīṃ kukṣau sa ca smṛtaḥ |
netreṣvagni dusturāśca śakresaivāhamaṃḍale ||

sarvajñādi padādyante prathamaṃ saptavarṇakam |
dvitīyaṃ ṛtuvarṇena dvitīyaṃ sāṃkuśaṃ bhavet ||

devī bījaṃ parenyasya navabhiśca tanucchadam |
guṇavadakṣarairdevi netramaṃtra pade punaḥ ||

viṣṇave īśvarāyamodāya gaganāya vasavetapase anaṃtāya nārāyaṇāya 
rāmāya yamāya yajñapuruṣāya sarvakriyāya harāya sarvakāṃtāparati priyā 
yajanāya lakṣmīśvarāya varuṇāya nṛsiṃhāya skaṃdāya halinenaṃdāya 
hayagrīvāya naṃdakāya oṃkārāya phaṭkārāya svāhā pataye hariharāya 

śaṃkhacakrā bhayābhīṣṭa pāśāṃkuśaśarādhanuḥ |
pradhānamūrti pratimāḥ piṃḍamadhye vyavasthitāḥ ||

svarānukramayogena vījānāmuttare vibhuḥ |
śaktayonyaṃ jināstāstu mūrtayaḥ prathitā imāḥ ||

ramārākā prabhā satyā citrakṣomne hiraṇyagā |
dhāmājyeṣṭā surāraudrī umāsaṃdhyā vā niśā ||

śivā vijayā sarvadā nityā sarvāṇī sarvamaṃgalā |
lakṣmī śrīratiḥ kāṃtirmedhā prajñāsarasvatī ||

āhlādinī bhagānaṃdā vijayā sarvamaṃgalā |
citranyastā vidhivadyaṃtra madhye purorcayet ||

keśavādyā svarasthāne śaktimūrteṣṭa patrake |
ṣaḍaṃgān vinyaset koṇe patre viṣṭyādivevatāḥ ||

madhye trikoṇakoṇeṣu ahaṃbrahmā svayaṃ hariḥ |
viṣṭarenaṃtabhogākhye āsane yogaviṣṭare ||

p. 124a)

paścimerka yatābhāti phaṇebhiśyāna yatrayat |
ṛṣibhiḥ siddhasaṃghaiśca sevyamānaṃ mudānvitam ||

śādvalābhiḥ sureśāśca veṇī baddhāḥ svabhūṣaṇāḥ |
ekeṇaśliṣṭaramaṇāḥ pāśāṃkuśakapālakāḥ ||

bhūprade'seṣu vijane lakṣadvaṃdvadṛḍhavratāḥ |
tadarddhahomayo devi kiṃsuka prasavena ca ||

padmakṣīramatholājā tilataṃḍulavilvajaiḥ |
evaṃ kṛte bhavedīśaḥ svayaṃ brahmāhariḥ svayam ||

itovakraṃ na śaknomi kimanyaiḥ kṣudrasiddhibhiḥ |
yadi meruścalosthānāt sthānāccalati bhāskaraḥ ||

mahīmadhyervayatānaitanmithyā na bhaveddhruvam |
dhruvaṃ viṣṇuṃ ca rudraṃ ca manmathaṃ deva varṇa yuk ||

śivādi bījamārabhya ṣoḍaśāni samālikhet |
śūdrībījeramābījamathavāmanmathadvaye ||

tristhāne vā gataṃ maṃtraṃ ramābīje sa sūni yuk |
ṣoḍaśāṃte vilomena bhūtavarṇāḥ karesthitāḥ ||

keśajāladharaḥ saumyaḥ śukraśaṃkhakhalamadvayam |
otaptahemaśaṃkāśaṃ lakṣmīśaṃśāsanasthitam ||

nistāruṇājñā hastāntāṃ kuṃḍalī bhūtapannage |
* * * tāta patraiva āsīnaṃ sumanoharam ||

kanyā hi vaddhavilasaddhaimadvīpe payodadhau |
surapādatalākīrṇe vasucchada sa karṇike ||

dhyātvā tu taṃ puroktena dharmeṇā suprasiddhyati |
śṛṇudevi paraṃ cānyadviṣṇuṃrudrapitāmaham ||

asādhanaṃ savarṇaṃ tu śvetapūrvaṃ tu kārayet |
rūtāṃtādi sthitaṃ kuryāllokādiṃ mastake nyaset ||

kāṃtaṃ raktāsanārūḍhaṃ u oṃmadhyāṃtabhedinau |
p. 124b) ṅovumadhyorddhakairdevi bhāṣaṇīṃ ṛṣimaṃdire ||

khi ūrddhacakriṇī bījamante āṣāḍhīsaṃsavam |
mūrddhni tasya pradātavyaṃ rutāṃtaṃ vāmamaṃdire ||

vidyā tritayamuddhṛtya bhautīśaṃ lopyamūrddhani |
vedavarṇasya śirasibiṃduṃ dhātrīśameva ca |

kumaṃtamakṣaraṃ devi nāsikāgrāsanasthitam |
minarudrorddhagaṃkāntaṃ gaṃdhānaṃ tena saṃyutam ||

tṛtīyaṃ ṛṣigehe ca ekarudrādi bhūṣitam |
navādi dīpanī devī āṣāḍhīśāsanasthitā ||

vāgīśo bhairavī nāmavāmāntasyāśane sthitā |
asyāṃte narakaṃ tatvansahāśīnaṃ tu kevalam ||

asyāgre aṃbikābījaṃ yāmo madhyāsanasthitam |
medhā punaḥ prayatnaśca dūtībījadvayaṃ punaḥ ||

lokāṃtaṃ mastakā krāntaṃ dvitīyārtha hānvitām |
sūtībījadvayaṃ bhūyo bhautīśaṃ tejamadhyataḥ ||

varṇāni purato devīmantaḥ saṃkhyāvyavasthitāḥ |
mādanaṃ puratobhūyaḥ śukraṃlokāṃtasaṃyutam ||

asyāntaṃ ruvarṇāni anyaṃ prāṇaṃ sa biṃdukam |
tasyānte abddhivarṇāni śuklatatvaṃ niyojayet ||

bhūtātitāṃni coddhṛtya ṛtuvarṇāntamūrddhani |
nāmādi mastako'dhastādvāyavye viniyojayet ||

haṃsākṣarasya purato tadbījaṃ vācasaṃyutam |
pūrvavadṛtusaṃkhyāni navātmānte ca meva hi ||

meṣarudrādito devīṃ navātmīmaṃtare nyaset |
brahmaṇāvāsaveneyaṃ sādhitā ca prayatnataḥ ||

savyādi paṃcavaktreṣu hayabījādi bījakaiḥ |
nyastavyā vidhiyogena aṃśāyaṃ tu padaiḥ kramāt ||

hṛdayaṃ bhūtavarṇena śivopi syāt ṣaḍakṣaram |
p. 125a) bījadvayena ca śikhā tadvadeva tanucchadam ||

grahārṇaiḥ purato devi netrāṇāṃ rakṣaṇe punaḥ |
dikrutāni samākhyātā bhūtavarṇā yathākramāt ||

vāgīśvarī mahāmāyā śivadūtī śivāṃganā |
saivārādhyaśivānaṃdā śivājñā śivarūpiṇī ||

gāyatrīṃ caiva sāvitrīmarcayecca sarasvatīṃ |
vairiṃcādhokṣajorudro vahnerviśva dvaye punaḥ ||

vāmāṃ jyeṣṭhāṃ ca raudrīṃ ca madhyakoṇe sarasvatī |
vācayantīṃ śivāmagre paśumedasamaprabhām ||

aruṇāṃ maṃgalāṃ tanvīṃ smeravaktrāṃ sutaprabhām |
govī?vadhavalaprakhyāṃ padākuṃkumasaṃnibhām ||

vedāmṛtakuṃbhe dve mudrāmakṣaguṇaṃ kare |
anekā hi samāvaddha bhujaṃgamauli virājitam ||

caṃdrakhaṃḍadharaṃ devaṃ nāgayajño pavītinam |
rurucarmāṃvaraṃ deva padmaviṣṭare kalpapādake ||

pannagāsanamārabddha kakṣaika vicaraṃ śubham |
dhyātvāsaṃpūjayeddevaṃ lakṣamekaṃ yatavrataḥ ||

homayedvijapuṣpāṇi lakṣamekaṃ tilaiḥ śubhaiḥ |
abdatrayeṇa deveśi vāgīśo nāparo bhuvi ||


iti śrīkubjikāmatenavakoṭisaṃhitāyāṃ catuḥpaṃcāśatitamaḥ paṭalaḥ || 0 ||





śrībhairava uvāca ||


guhyaguhyaṃ sureśāni vistareṇa vadāmyaham |
nyastā dehasya sannāhaṃ grāme vāmantanepi vā ||

bhūtasaṃghe mahādāve'saṃghe pretabūmiṣu |
yakṣarākṣasaduṣṭoghaustṛṇe iva ca dṛśyate ||

āpo śrīkaṃṭhapurato rudrāntabiṃdu bhairavam |
p. 125b) ojamadhyesya śirasi japanti krūrasaṃyutā ||

yameśamaṃdirā bījā vā likhet pūrvameva hi |
bhūtavarṇāni śirasi vinyase devameva hi ||

dvitīyaṃ ca dvitīyena ṣaḍarddhena ṣaḍarddhakam |
caturthaṃ ca caturthena daśārddhena daśārddhakam ||

dvādaśārddhaṃ tathādevi saptame saptamaṃ viduḥ |
vidyāṣṭamaṣṭamaṃ yojya aśatārddhaṃ pravinyaset ||

śabdarāśi ṣaḍaṃgeṣu nyastavyaṃ suprayatnataḥ |
yathāha agninā vidyā vidyā paṃcākṣarī tathā ||

ṣṛṇu devi vidhānaṃ hi yatsurairapi durllabham |
yenānuṣṭhitamātreṇa pāśaṃ vicchidyatekhilam ||

sarvaśaṃkaradoṣaśca grāmyomūrkhaḥ śaṭhopi vā |
sopi śaktāyate sadyaḥ suredraṃ ivabhūtale ||

māninīdakṣajaṃghasthaṃ rudrāṃtaṃ biṃdubhūṣitam |
purataḥ keśavī devī māninī śivasaṃbhave ||

yavamadhyaṃ samuddhṛtya nāvarṇaṃ carmabhairavam |
mīnarudrorddhagaṃ yasya māninīkṣīrasaṃbhavā ||

vecumadhyaṃ punardevi prāṇarudraṃ śivānvitam |
tatpūrvatastṛtīyaṃ hi lokāntaṃ śiraśi sthitam ||

ru ādivarṇayogena tadvījaṃ purato nyaset |
merubhairava ūrddhe tu lokādiṃ śirasi nyaset ||

evameṣā mahāvidyā asyāgre bhakṣiṇīṃ punaḥ |
bījāni tāni coddhṛtya nārāyaṇa manaṃ tataḥ ||

natipūrvakadeveyaṃ vidyāsarvārthasādhikī |
rasabūtārṇamadhyasthaṃ māninīśirasaṃbhavam ||

yavamadhyagataṃtaṃtu nārāyaṇamanaṃtayuk |
vāgīśasyāgratohaṃsaṃ vailomenasamuddharet ||

nābhimadhyaṃ samuddhṛtya vyāhṛtināmanaṃtakaḥ |
p. 126a) prāṇarudrasya purataḥ śulvarephasamanvitam ||

ru aṃtaṃ navake daṇḍaṃ haṃpharṇānāsamanvitam |
varṇa(rma)ṇāpanatiścāṃte hṛdyā vāmānāmuddharet ||

vyaṃjanādi krameṇātha madhyagāni suraiḥsaha |
kruddhādi padayuktaiśca tāraśaktyarṇameva ca ||

guruvakropadeśena sarvājñādi padaiḥ kramāt |
varṇaḥ kalādvijātīnāṃ pūrvavatsurasuṃdari ||

ālikhet prathamaṃ cakraṃ vāgbhavaṃ vāgbhavena tu |
tanmadhye śaktigaṃ devī vasuvarṇaṃ nṛpānvitam ||

haṃsaśaktisamāveṣṭyavarṇān tāragatān punaḥ |
śaktyaṣṭakaṃ likhenemi vedāṃtasaṃjitam ||

ṣoḍaśaḥ ṣoḍaśāni syuryadānyaikaikacāṣṭake |
haristatkoṇamadhye ca dhruvaṃ uttarasaṃbhavam ||

tanmadhye ciṃtayentīla jīmūtaraṇi prabham |
prāguktalakṣaṇairyuktamaṣṭakāṃḍantanardinam ||

śaṃkhacakragadāpadmapāśāṃkuśasamanvitam |
dhavalāmṛtasaṃkāśamaruṇobjasamaprabham ||

vidyāmakṣaguṇaṃ kṛtvā rathāṃgaṃ ca caturbhujam |
śarekṣupuṃḍrakaṃ devaṃ pāśāṃkuśasamanvitam ||

tadahaṃviṣṭarāsīnāṃ pūrṇendu sadṛśānanām |
dakṣiṇā śliṣṭaramaṇāṃ padmamanyena susthitām ||

taptakāṃcanasaṃkāśāṃ sarvāmaṃḍanamaḍitām |
kaṃṭakaṃ vāmamāyāṃ ca manmathaṃ parameśvarīṃ ||

tadbījaṃ maṃtradebhūpa vetātaṃraktabhairavam |
tayormūrddhni sthitaṃ kuryāt kāmamadhyāṃtamakṣaram ||

mālinīśuklavarṇasya śive vāmādi bheditam |
śuklaṃ raktaṃ samuddhṛtya mālinyā hṛdi kevalam ||

mālinī śiva ūrddhe ca kūrmādyāsanasaṃsthitam |
p. 126b) devādimastakedevi khaṃḍāsāṭhī tu agrataḥ ||

ṛṣimālādi bījaṃ hi svastikātrādi saṃyutam |
vijayeti mahādevi prasīdapadameva ca ||

bījāni tāni coddhṛtya ṛṣimadhyādi kevalam |
anaṃtamāsanaṃ dadyādekarudrāṃtadeśataḥ ||

te ūrddhaṃ narake māṃsaṃ kevalaṃ tu tadagrataḥ |
ekādaśādi vījasya dātavyaṃ purataḥ sthitam ||

karālīmagrato nyasya vāgīśaṃ bhautikena tu |
guṇoṃtaṃ kevalaṃ bījaṃ kālīśenāsanasthitā ||

lakṣmīvidyā sureśāni bahubhogakarī smṛtā |
āyuḥ kāṃtiryaśaḥ kīrtirllakṣmī saubhāgyameva ca ||

asya jāpena homena labhate śāśvataṃ padam |
arghaṃ sa hetukaṃ vahni oṃ namomāṃtu maṃḍalam ||

saṃpūjya sakalī kṛtya bhāvayecca sudhāpalam |
mūlavidyāmanusmṛtya śuklaṃśuklāmṛtasravam ||

tenātmānaṃ yāgavastūni prokṣayetsusamāhitaḥ |
vidyayā pūjayennityamuktamārgeṇa suṃdari ||

jayīdhīro devatā hṛdayaṃ gamaḥ |

ityācāraparaḥ śāntaḥ satyavādī dṛḍhavrataḥ |
bhikṣāśī cāthanaktāśī payaḥ śaktu phalāni ca ||

āsanaṃ ca śucau deśe cailakṛṣṇāṃ varānvite |
upaviśyanijaṃpiṃḍaṃ vasulakṣaṃ yathāvidhi ||

nyagrodhapippalaplakṣanuduṃvara tilāni ca |
brīhibhiḥ kamalairatnairhomayettarkasaṃkhyayā ||

homayedbrahmavṛkṣeṇa saṃsārabhayabhīruṇā |
anulomavilomastu karmāṇi vividhāni ca ||

japahomārccanābhistu siddhināmadhipo bhavet |
mūrtiśaktitrayaṃ devi aṃgapūjā ca nāmataḥ ||

prāgdale vā cyutaṃ vahnai kāminī bhānu yāmyake |
p. 127a) niśācare manojñaṃ ca jaleviśvatanurbhavet ||

vimalāṃ cāpau samāropya hariṃcottarake dale |
aiśebhadrāmalaṃgāni gatamaṃ vādipūrvavat ||

yaṃ yaṃ kāmayate nityaṃ svarge martye ca pannage |
sopi tallabhate samyak satyaṃ satyaṃ na cānyathā ||

tatonyaṃ saṃpravakṣyāmi durllabhaṃ śaṃbhumuttamam |
yenasaṃsmaraṇāddevi bhavadhvāntaṃ vināśayet ||

dhruvasūtīṃ samuddhṛtya kṣe aṃtaṃ aṃbikāṃ śivam |
mālinī prathamaṃ bījaṃ tṛtīyaṃ ṛṣimaṃdire ||

navakeśavakaṃ devi dvidhāsūkṣmeśamūrddhniśam |
mālinīraktabījasya puratonaṃtamālikhet ||

dharmarudrasya puratohaṃsaṃ tatpratilomataḥ |
vahnidūtīsamāyuktaṃ tribījaṃ navake hariḥ ||

śivāyameṣarudrāṃte nṛdvarṇaṃ te ca madhyagam |
haṃsānte tadvadeveha uddharedaparaṃ śivam ||

nābhimadhyaṃ samuddhṛtya vālinī śivamagrataḥ |
ṛṣimadhyādi śavakaṃ kṣeyasyorddhe ca sūkṣmajam ||

khaḍgīśa bhairavasyāṃte rutāṃtaṃ gaṃdhamādane |
vāmabāhustha śikhare haṃsaṃ vai lomato bhavet ||

arghīśamaṃdarārddhe ca kālalokāṃtabhūṣitaḥ |
bījāni tāni purato dhruvāṃte cāthavā bhavet ||

aṃbikāṃte anaṃtaṃ ca kṣenaṃtaṃ puratastathā |
syātāmādī pate devī navātmānau dhruvāṃ tataḥ ||

caṃḍikābhyāṃ samāpoknaṃ punaranyāni tacchaṇu |
prāsādasārabhūtaṃ tu aṣṭadhā śṛṇusuṃdari ||

tāvadāmiṣṭade kṛtvā raktasyo parimārutam |
lokādi mastakākrāṃtaṃ kṣayaṃtaṃ śuklabhairavam ||

dhātrīśaṃ gaṃdhasaṃyuktaṃ śivaṃ jñeyaṃ tameva ca |
p. 127b) labhyate hi prathamaṃ yāsi madhyaṃ ca mārutam ||

anugrahaḥ samāyuktaṃ biṃdunoparibhūṣitam |
dvitīyamuddhare devaṃ śivāyenatipaścimam ||

tṛtīyaṃ viṣṇubījāṃte maheśvarāyeti tatpatha |
caturthaṃ cāsti vā krūraṃ svāntaḥ svagaḥ śivānpatha ||

caramenatiruddiṣṭaṃ maṃtraṃ gaurīśivātmakam |
śivamartyaṃ punardevi viddhisarvaṃ purovṛtam ||

kailāsamaṃdire devi śrīkaṃṭhaṃ prathamaṃ viduḥ |
viṣṇuṃrudraṃdhruvaṃ haṃsaṃ dhruvāya tu vijomataḥ ||

purāvṛttani maṃtrāṇi na kulādi vibhedataḥ |
śuddhasphaṭikasaṃkāśamakṣaṃdaśabhujaṃ śivam ||

paṃca vakradharaṃ devī paṃcamūrttisamanvitam |
padmāsanopasaṃviṣṭaṃ sphuraṃtaṃ dīptatejasam ||

jaṭāmukuṭadharaṃśāṃtaṃ sarvābharaṇabhūṣitam |
akṣayaṃ ca daśopetaṃ mālayā ca pariskṛtam ||

divyāṃvaradharaṃ devaṃ vidyāvāhanamathojvalam |
paraśuvraja * * * abhayaṃ ca * kṣetrikam ||

ghaṃṭāṃ nāgaṃ ca vahniṃ ca vāme pāśāṃkuśaṃ tathā |
samajaṃghorucaraṇau pramadājitakūrmakau ||

dhyātvaivaṃ pūjayennityaṃ devaṃ caivārddhanāriśam |
ādhāramāsanaṃ proktamāpaśca paramaḥ śivaḥ ||

lāṃgaliṃkamiti proktaṃ biṃduṃpīṭhamiti smṛtaṃ |
na śivenavināśaktirna śaktirahitaḥ śivaḥ ||

puṣpeṣugaṃdhavajñeyastileṣu tailavattathā |
dehe ca prāṇavacchaktistasmācchaktiṃ prapūjayet ||

śaktirevāsanaṃ proktaṃ paṃcānaṃtādayaḥ kramāt |
pārthivāṃḍamavaskaṃdha nālaṃ vaddhyaṃtameva ca ||

tanonatyasanaṃ pūrvaṃ siṃhāsana dvitīyakam |
p. 128a) yogāsanaṃ tṛtīyaṃ tu caturthaṃ padmāsanaṃ bhavet ||

paṃcamaṃ vimalākhyaṃ ca paṃcabhūtātmkaṃ ca te |
vṛttākāramanaṃ tastu sūryakoṭi prabhānvitam ||

pādāścatuṣka koṇeṣu siṃharūpeṇa kalpayet |
daṃṣṭākarālavadanāstrinetrābhīmarūpiṇaḥ ||

koṭikoṭibhiḥ saṃghaiśca pratyekaṃ paripuritam |
bhūtarūpeṇa koṇeṣu pādākṛtayugādayaḥ ||

śvetaṃ raktaṃ pītakṛṣṇaṃ siṃharūpeṇa bhāvayet | 
avyakto niyatiḥ phalaḥ kālaśceta catuṣṭayam ||

avyaktasphaṭhikā bhāso niyatiḥ kālāṃjanadyutiḥ |
phaleḍāḍimamuṣpābhāḥ kālābhinnāṃ janaprabhāḥ ||

* * * * vibhyaḥ proktomavyayādenmanonmanī |
evaṃ yogāsanaṃ proktaṃ padmāsanamataḥ param ||

māyātatvaṃ vṛttaṃ graṃthiṃ sūtravidyānnaśobhitam |
vidyeśvaradalacchatraṃ śakti keśarasaṃyutam ||

vidyātatva samaṃtatvaṃ kāṃtakāyāṃ prakalpate |
bījaṃ pradhānavarṇābha maṃkuraṃ rajataprabham ||

tasya nālaṃ tu vai rūpyaṃ kaṃṭakaṃ raktapiṃgalam |
śaṃkhavarṇadalāgrasya mūlā vā padalā smṛtāḥ ||

kiṃjalke nīlapītāni madhyesyuḥ keśarāṇi tu |
navabījāni tanmadhye hemābhānibiṃduḥ kramāt ||

vāmādi śaktayo devi keśareṣu prakalpayet |
iṃdrādīśānaparyantaṃ nyasenmadhye manonmanīm ||

ātmatatyaṃ nyasedagre vidyātadvarṇasaumyake |
dakṣiṇe śivatatvaṃ ca madhye sarvātmatatvakam ||

evaṃ padmāsanaṃ proktaṃ tasyorddhe vimalāsanam |
maṃḍalatrayamatraiva kalpaivaṃ vimalāsanam ||

ātmānaṃ caturobhyarcya śeṣamanyaṃ puroktavat |
p. 128b) liṃgamaṃḍalamadhye vā kārṇakodararaṃjike ||

somasūryāgni viṃbānāṃ kālānāṃ tatra saṃjapet |
aṣṭavargakalābhistu maṃtrāṣṭaka dalāṣṭakam ||

bhūtayaṃtrasya madhye ca satyādi caturo nyaset |
brahmāviṣṇuśca rudraśca īśvarohādidīrgha yuk ||

pūrvokte nakṣaṇopetamupacāraiḥ prapūjayet |
ciṃtayed devamīsānaṃ śāśvataṃ śivamavyayam ||

jaṭāmukuṭasaṃkāśaṃ cadrārddhakṛtaśekharam |
vṛṣadhvaja samāruḍho bhavānī sahito vibhuḥ ||

vāmādi śaktipūjaśca āsīnaḥ padmaviṣṭare |
sa īśvara iti proktaḥ sarvakāraṇakāraṇaḥ ||

īśvarāddakṣiṇāṃ śetu brahmāviṣṇaṃ tamudbhavaḥ |
īśvarādvāmapārśve tu viṣṇucchighaṃtamudbhavaḥ ||

ādityo dakṣiṇe vakṣau caṃdro vai vāmalocane |
jātavedālalāṭākṣe āsye vedāḥ samudbhavāḥ ||

ghrāṇovāyuḥ samutpannaḥ śrotrekhila diśodbhavaḥ |
gaṇeśogaṇasamudbhūto hṛdaye saṇmukhodbhavaḥ ||

nābhau vai dvādaśā dityāḥ sarvalokaśca kukṣijaḥ |
sarve caikādaśā rudrā adhareṣu samudbhavāḥ ||

kaṭideśeṣu vaśavo meḍhre caiva prajāpatiḥ |
urubhyāmaśvinau jātau pādābhyāṃ gaṃdhagastathā ||

devayakṣamaṃdarasiddhavidyā dharādayaḥ |
gaṃdharvopsarasaḥ sarve pādamūle samudbhavāḥ ||

aṣṭāviṃśaddeṃvakoṭyaḥ sarveromeṣu codbhavāḥ |
rūpasya ca tathākoṭyo kotyo romāśca saṃbhavāḥ ||

īdṛgbhūto mahādeva īśvarastrīdaśeśvaraḥ |
bhavānī sāmavarṇābhāṃ dvinetrāṃ dvibhujānvitāṃ ||

dakṣikukṣikarāṃ devīṃ prasāryaika karānvitāṃ |
p. 129a) saukṣmyamūlevalāṃ devi kiṃcitprahasitānanāṃ ||

sarvābharaṇasaṃpūrṇāṃ mukuṭādyupaśobhitāṃ |
dukūlavasanāṃ devīṃ pīnorujavanastanīṃ ||

caṃdramaṃḍala cakrābhāmādiśaktimalaṃ parām |
sarvākāreṣu vāmāṃśe gaurīmevaṃ viciṃtayet ||

puliṃgaṃ sarvamīśānaṃ strīliṃgaṃ bhagavallabhaḥ |
umārudrātmajāḥ sarve prajāḥ sthāvarajaṃgamāḥ ||

lakṣmīdhātrī mahādhātrī sāvitrī ca sarasvatī |
gāyatrī rasakoṇeṣu devī bījādi bīja yuk ||

durgādevī mahādevī yoginī ḍākinī punaḥ |
jyeṣṭhāyāstu parāśaktiḥ puṇyasauryāḥ samudbhavāḥ ||

guhyaṃ cādhuta aṃteṣu nirṇītaṃ hi mayāpurā |
sārabhūtaṃ paraṃ guhyaṃ śṛṇu devi vadāmite ||

piṃḍasthaṃ piṃḍamadhyasthaṃ piṃḍena prakaṭī kṛtam |
piṃḍena piṃḍitaṃ piṇḍaṃ piṃḍeṣu ca vinikṣipet ||

piṃḍena piṃḍitaṃ kṛtvā piṃḍarūpamudāhṛtam |
etacchiva mayaṃ piṃḍaṃ sarvatatva? mayaṃ viduḥ ||

dalāṣṭakeṣu vitteśāntadagreṣu umādikān | 
pūjayitvā vidhānena maṃtrairetaiḥ sadāśivam ||

oṃ hāṃ śivāsanāya namaḥ ||

oṃ mahā?śivamūrttaye namaḥ ||

oṃ hauṃ vidyā dehāya namaḥ ||

oṃ hāṃ hauṃ śivāya namaḥ ||

ṛtvagairbhūtamaṃtrārṇairbhūtamaṃtrākṣaraṃ pṛthak |
athavāṣṭākṣarairdevi aṣṭāṃśāni prakalpayet ||

pratyekamakṣarākṣaiśca tathāṣṭākṣarānvitaiḥ |
viyadvāyuragnijalalakṣāmaye śaravyamṛtamukhyātmane caṃḍeśadurgāśunī 
digghrāṇapayonasainyo vṛṣabhaḥ krameṇa ||

pūjayedbhuktamārgeṇa lokeśakuliśādibhiḥ |
p. 129b) vajrikāyaṃ tataḥ kuryāt kuṃḍeṣu daleṣu kramāt ||

hastamātraṃ khanedbhūmau vistāraṃ tāvadeva tu |
mānāṃgulena kartavya malyaṃnaivanu kārayet ||

ekāṃgulapramāṇena parito viṣṭaraṃ nyaset |
tadvāhye mekhalākāyāṃ vistāreṇanavāṃgulā ||

adhastātmekhalākāyā vistāreṇa daśāṃgulā |
utsedhastatsamojñeyaḥ aṃguṣṭaṃ madhyamekhalā ||

triraṃgulasamutsedha vistāre tāvadeva hi |
caturaṃgulaṃ tadūrddhastu vistārotsedhatatsamaḥ ||

yoniḥ paṃcāṃgulā jñeyāmānamaṣṭāṃgulaṃ bhavet |
gomayenopa limpeta prokṣayedarghavāriṇā ||

rekhāṣaṭkāntaṣaṭkoṇaṃ paṃgavaṃ yonimālikhet |
vahnimathanasaṃbhūtaṃ sarvātharvaṇisaṃbhavam ||

kuṃḍamadhye tu saṃskuryādīkṣaṇaprokṣāṇādibhiḥ |
garbhādhānādikāvahneraghoreṇa pṛthag bhavet ||

dhyātvaivamagniṃ vidhivadakṣamānena cātmanā |
ādau paścimataḥ sthānamūrddhataḥ śiva ucyate ||

dakṣiṇottarabāhubhyāṃ pārśve cakṣuśca karṇayoḥ |
mukhamadhyagataṃ yastu jihvāgre ca prakīrtitam ||

yatrakāṣṭhāstataḥ śrotrāstatra bhūmena nāyikā |
kāṣṭhādagni jvalā netrā mūrddhisthānena saṃśayaḥ ||

mūrddhnisthāne kṛtaṃ kaścidrajorājyaṃ vinaśyati |
prajvalagniṃ tathā vahniṃ varddhamānamācaret ||

śāṃḍilyagotrasya jamadagni ṛṣirjītavedāgnirdevatā triṣṭuvrachaṃdaḥ |
kālīkarālīlo dvīpītīśvetī dhrūmra piṃgasapta jihvā aruṇamātā varuṇapitā 
pāvakanāmāgramāvāhayet ||

ekahṛdayaṃ dvivackraṃ dvināsikarma trimekhalaṃ tripādaṃ ca 
saptajihvābhirācitam |
p. 130a) meṣārūḍhaṃ catuḥśṛgaṃ jaṭāmukuṭamaṃḍitam ||

daksiṇe ca caturhaste vāmabhāge trihastakam |
śaktyaṣṭakaṃ tathā caiva śrukśruvaṃ dakṣiṇe kare ||

tomaraṃ vyajanaṃ vāme ghṛtapātraṃ viśeṣataḥ |
sudīptaṃ svarṇa vapuṣaṃ jayapiṃgalalocanam ||

evaṃ rūpamimaṃ dhyātvā sarvakāmārthasādhanam |
saṃpūjyavidhivadvahniṃ vahnimaṃtreṇa homayet ||

vyāhṛtīnāmaṃtrabhūyasvāhā vai saṃjñapūrvakam |
jalenajapahomena vahniḥ prīṇāti nityaśaḥ ||

aṣṭottarasahasraṃ vā tadarddhaṃ cāṣṭaśataṃ tu vā |
nyagrodhoduṃbaraśamatilarājīhaviryavaiḥ ||

yadyatprārthayate maṃtrī tattallabhyeta vai cirāt |
maṃtrānāṃ sādhane devi tridho pāyāḥ prakīrtitāḥ ||

japataḥ phalapūjā ca vaikalpaṃ dhyānato bhavet |
budhaprasādanatve ca prasādaḥ sarvadehiṇāṃm ||

sarvadājapate jīvo yathāgaurī tathā śṛṇu |
haṃsasya puratastā vadguṇānimati paścimam ||

brahmahā śuddhyate nityaṃ japahomārcanādibhiḥ |
natasya duṣkṛtaṃ kiṃcidgrahaṇadevamucyate ||

dhruvādi ṛtu varṇāni sarvajñāni padāni ca |
sthāneṣu ṣaṭsu vinyasya purokta dhyāna pūrvakam ||

tetrāṃtaṃ ca śivaṃ jñānaṃ śeṣonte kālabhairavam |
dhrūvādi paramaṃ maṃtraṃ dhruvaṃ prānaṃ ru aṃtagam ||

biṃdubhairavasaṃbhinnaṃ śivāyāṃta dhruvāṃtakam |
māninī śivavarṇaṃ ca ṛṣimadhyādi bijakam ||

tatonyaṃ saṃpravakṣyāmi viśvamadhyasya agrataḥ |
devī bījaṃ samuddhṛtya nāvāṃte dharmabhairavam ||

p. 130b)

tṛtīyaṃ grahamālāyāṃ anaṃtaṃgaṃdhamādane |
yāmomadhyaṃ kevalaṃ tu viṣṇoranati paścimam ||

pūrvavansādhyataṃ maṃtraṃ biṃdusarvārthasiddhidam |
rasabhūtāṃ tu madhyasthaṃ mīnarudrārthabiṃdukam ||

nārāyaṇaṃ caturthyantaṃ devī bījaṃ tadagrataḥ |
lokāṃtaṃ śirasākrāntaṃ guṇoṃtaṃ kālasenayuk ||

evametatparaṃ viddhi netrāṃte evameva hi |
tadvadīśaṃ tathānyā hi satyaṃ lokādisaṃbhavam ||

jñānaṃ raktāsanaṃ kṣentamastakaṃ biṃdubhairavam |
asyāgre pūtavarṇāni yadvāsaptākṣarī bhavet ||

narāṇāṃ nayanaṃ tasmānte na nārāyaṇaḥ smṛtaḥ |
natiśca tasmai devīya dhruvaṃḥ sohaṃsa ||

paṃcabhūtamayaṃ viśvaṃ śarīraṃ paṃcabhautikam |
tanmayaṃ ca śivaḥ proktaḥ śivohamiti bhāvayet ||

tenanirvāṇameveti satyaṃ satyaṃ varānane |
dhruvaṃ viṣṇuṃ samuddhṛtya vahniṃ caivasamādanam ||

ekanetrasya purato yadvarṇaṃ kṣetrabiṃdukam |
asyāgre tu śivaṃ jetumāṣāṭhīsāṃmagrataḥ ||

ṛṣyādi ṛṣigehe calukādi mastakaṃ kuru |
aparaṃ manmathārddhe ca vintāratnaṃ pravinyaset ||

bījānitāni purataḥ saṃkarāyanamoritam |
aṣṭavarṇastathādevi dvādaśāṃtamataḥ śṛṇu ||

bījāni bhagavadbhūyo tādarthenāpi saṃyutam |
yasyorddhe prāṇavarṇaṃ ca ṛṣyaṃtaṃ gaṃdhasaṃbhavam ||

pūtanā yasya ūrddhe ca dhruvādi āsanaṃ kuru |
kāttvamadhyasya śirasi vāmādiṃ mastake nyaset ||

vāme madhyasya purato māninīstanamālikhet |
vāgīśabhairavasyāgre rudrāṇāṃ ṛṣisaṃbhavam ||

p. 131a) kūrmādivarṇayogena bījānāṃ ca vilomataḥ |
keśavādi vibhedena mūrttināṃ dvādaśa kramat ||

ādityaravibhedena gāyatrī mūrtānāṃ dvādaśa kramāt |
ādityaravibhedena gāyatrī saṃyutena ca ||

nyāsāṃgamūrtimaṃtrāśca japapūjādi tarpaṇaiḥ |
pūrvoktena vidhānena ātmayogapurassaram ||

liptvā bhūmausamaṃ kalpya tatvedharmanihetarau |
mātṛkāntaṃ sa śaktiñca tatradhyāyeta acyutam ||

kaumodakīṃ rathāṃgaṃ ca śaṃkhaṃpadmapariskṛtam |
kuṃḍale vanamālāṃ ca sarvakalpavimaṃḍitam ||

lavaṃgena samāveśya viṣṭvādiṣṭādi bodhitam |
keśavādyā dalāgreṣu vāyu devādi vāsavān ||

kabaṃdhaniyame sūkṣme śūnye biṃdu vivarjite |
nirālaṃbe ca sā veśyamūlā devaṃ viciṃtayet ||
