tenamuktipadaṃ gacchennapunargabhasaṃbhavam || 


iti śrīkubjikāmate paṃcāśatitamaḥ paṭalaḥ || 0 ||




śrībhairava uvāca ||


athātyaṃtarahasyaṃ tu vakṣyāmyatrasudurllabham |
devā api na jānaṃti kiṃ punarmānuṣādayaḥ ||

sādhitavya prayatnena japapūjāadi tarpaṇaiḥ |
nābhimadhyasthitasyāgre devī bījaṃ sa kheṭakam ||

śukraṃ raktāsanārūḍhaṃ asyāgre gajabiṃdukam |
karālī bījamuddhṛtya svayaṃ raktaṃ sa biṃdukam ||

kṣīravarṇasya śirasikekādiṃ mastake nyaset |
asyāgre mādanaṃ bījaṃ vāmāṃtaṃ krūramastakam ||

māninīkrūravarṇasya puratoghātamālikhet |
adhastātkārayedaiṃdralokādiṃ śirasi nyaset ||

nityakālīṃ ca dhātrīśaṃ punastaṃ raktamāsanam |
śaramadhyaṃ tadaṃte ca dharmaṃ vai vastukaṃṭhakam ||

p. 131b)

viṣṇubījaṃ samuddhṛtya apitaddayitāṃ punaḥ |
vidyānīnayatākeyaṃ sarvasatvaraśaṃkarī ||

sarvasaṃpatpradākīrti rathasvargā pa vargadā |
eṣāvidyā mahādevi brahmaviṣṇīśabhedataḥ ||

sādhitā śaktibījaṃ ca sarvajñādi padānvinā |
madadraveti nāmāgre yojayet parameśvarī ||

iṃdragopanibhāṃ devī gokṣīrasadṛśopamām |
pāśāṃkuśavarābhītiṃ padmagāṃ śaśiśekharām ||

oṃkārī hrīṃ ca phetkārī sarvānaṃdā ca aṃkuśā |
pāpāśā hrīṃkārarupā ca aiṃkārī vajrarūpiṇī ||

nidrācakrā bhayābhītimadadākṣāyaṇī harā |
vedāvahu kṛtī caiva ṣoḍaśādyāḥ samarcayet ||

madhya ṣaṭkeṭakārādyā ādibrahmāṣṭake * * |
japtavye dveṣṭapurataḥ lakṣamekaṃ palā * taḥ ||

tarpaṇāni nivedyāha homayed biṃdusaṃbhavaṃ |
kusumaiśca brahmajānīnāmoṃkārādiṣu homayet ||

tribhirvarṣairjitātmānaḥ siddhānāmadhipo bhavet |
devī bījasya purato dhruveṃduścaturānanaḥ ||

punastaṃ bījamuddhṛtya prāpavarṇaṃ sapūrvakam |
asyāgre viṣṇurudraṃ ca grasaccaṃdrahasaṃ tathā ||

heṣayadvitayaṃ dharma trailokya padameva ca |
nāśinī siṃharūpeṇa nimekaṃ ca tridhā kuru |

aṃte prāgva hirvarṇaṃ ca tridhādharmāgni ca dvayam |
eṣā sā siṃhikānāmaṃ siṃhāṃte ca daśāṃsataḥ ||

japyeyamathavā siṃhamanaṃtaṃ devi pūrvakam |
kṛtvānyāsaṃ ca pūrvoktaṃ saṃdehādi yathā kramāt ||

asya jāpena homena pūrvokta siddhibhāgbhavet |
p. 132a) sāvitrīṃ viṣṇuṃ rudraṃ cānupūrvaṃ purato nyaset ||

adhastātkārayedraktādraktintadbiṃdu bhairavam |
ḷpūrvaṃ devavarṇaṃ ca surenaṃtaṃsrabiṃdukam ||

likhato devavarṇasya ḷpūrvaṃ naṃtimagrataḥ |
bhagavatī raktagaṃdhena prāṇāsthaścaturānanaḥ ||

dahanī corddhasaṃyukta rājavelaistanāṃtara |
mālinyā nābhivarṇasya mūrddhni pūrvakānagam ||

prāṇo bīja sa viṣṇuścamābījaṃ satyabiṃdukam |
lakṣmīśarītivikhyātā bahubhogaharāvasā ||

kāmadevaḥ svayaṃ devi cintayansomabhāskaraṃ |
mānuṣī rūpamāśritya yathāsau carate mahīm ||

tenāgni ṛtu varṇāni guṇādviguṇameva ca |
tripurāvatsamārādhya nyāsayogasamanvitam ||

vārripūrṇaṃ ghaṭaṃ cakre saṃpūjyacakramāditaḥ |
tarpayeduktamārgeṇa māsamekaṃ yatavrataḥ ||

ahorātraṃ japedvidyāṃ homayet padmavillakaiḥ |
madhūkṣitaṃ tilaṃvātha sarvathālabhate vasu ||

khikārāvasthitaṃ bījaṃ pūtanāsanasaṃsthitam |
navādimūladaṃḍastu ru aṃtaṃ maḷpūrvakam ||

eṣaviṣṇuḥ śivobrahmā gaṇeśovāksarasvatī |
athavā devi kurvīta vedavarṇaṃ śive punaḥ ||

māpūrvāmṛtadūtī ca bhūtavarṇāvyavasthitāḥ |
dhruvaṃ vittaṃ samuddhṛtyapiṃḍaṃ ca nāti paścimam ||

aparaṃ bhūtavarṇaṃ ca śaktibījaṃ samādanam |
mābījāṃtyakṣaraṃ devi aparaṃ akṣaraṃ śṛṇu ||

dhṛtibījasya purataḥ karālī tatra saṃsthitā |
lokāntaṃ śirasā krāntaṃ rudrabījaṃ ca śakti yuk ||

lakṣmī bījasya puraviṣṇu piṃḍadvayaṃ likhet |
athabījaṃ maheśāni piṃḍavedavibhūṣitam ||

yadvā sidhāntasaṃbhinna siddhāṃte bhūmimālikhet |
p. 132b) dūtībījasya purato lokaṃ keśaviṇopitam ||

bījānī māni deveśi ṛtuvarṇamudāhṛtam |
mādanaṃ śaktipurato mābījaṃ bhūtisaṃyutam ||

netramadhyasthitaṃ piṃḍaṃ dhruvāṃte śaktimālikhet |
anaṃtaṃ prathamaṃ devīṃ sudarśanasurānvitaḥ ||

śivānvitaṃ śiraso bhūyo vicakraṃ ṅeyutaṃ nyaset |
sucakraṃ svasādevi trilokyaṃ marmaṇi sthitam ||

astreśivānvitaṃ bhūyaścakraṃ śaivāsurāṃtakam |
aṃgāni paṃcavinyasya varṇāni pṛthagevavā ||

viraṇatkiṃkiṇījālanavajaṃ ghātamaṃḍitam |
nīlasarojanilayaṃ aruṇābja vilocanam ||

navanītaṃ pāyasaṃ ca dorbhyāṃ gopavadhū kṛtam |
pūjayeduktamārgeṇa lakṣahomaṃ sa tarpaṇam ||

iṃdratulyasamolakṣaṃ tatsamaśca parākrame |
punaranyaṃ pravakṣyāmi tāraśaktiśca mādanam ||

māninī prathamaṃ devi * * * prathamo bhavet |
viṣṇurmāṃ bhagavan ṅentaṃ raktarudraṃ dhruvādigam ||

sūkṣmaṃ majjādi vā diṣṭaṃ deśo madhyaṃ samālikhet |
cakraṃ māsayugaṃ bhāstrapiṃḍaṃdūtīmanaṃtaram ||

bījāni hṛdayaṃ devī ṛtuvarṇaṃ śivānvitam |
eṣu sakhā vivarṇāni varṇayugmāṃtarakṣaṇe ||

rukmiṇīñca dalāgreṣu vāsudevādi vāsavāḥ |
paṃkajāruṇamadhyasthaṃ vipakṣaskaṃdāsane sthitam ||

puṣṭaraṃjanasaṃcāraṃ gadīhaimādi saṃnibham |
aṃteniviṣṭaṃ kāṃtāyāgātraśaktamadānvitaṃ ||

homaṃ daśāyutaṃ lakṣaṃ daśavidyāpārijaiḥ |
dūtīrudraṃ samuddhṛtya ṛṣimālāṃtadeśataḥ ||

tadādigaṃdhasaṃbhinnaṃ māsacakraṃ guṇānvitam |
p. 133a) ajaṃvaktrasthitaṃ sūkṣmaṃ tanotaṃ jaṃghabheditam ||

aṃgulīdakṣamālinyā navādi āsanaṃ kuru |
vāgīsaṃ vāmakarṇena anaṃtaṃ krūramastakam ||

pitāmahasya madhye tu vasurudraṃ samālikhet |
dhātrīśaṃ bidugaṃdhenamedaṃsūkṣmāsane nyaset ||

rudrāṃtamāsanaṃ devi śivāmagre pradāpayet |
piṃḍaṃ ca varmarudraṃ tu kāṃtasthaṃ gaṃdhasaṃbhavam ||

ekādaśādimuddhṛtya ākāśārddhena bheditam |
mādanaṃ dāntacakrasthaṃ raktaṃ devaṃ sa bindukam ||

devībījaṃ dhruvaṃ devi uddhṛtavyaṃ prayatnataḥ |
lokānāṃ sthiti kartā ca kṛṣṇosau daityamardanaḥ ||

yadāhānirbhave loke dharmasya parameśvarī |
māyājālaṃ ca kurute rūpāṇi tu vahūnyapi ||

svayaṃ svacchaṃdacaritaḥ ajaḥ satyaḥ sanātanaḥ |
catvāri vedavarṇāni agrādvisthāna paṃcake ||

aṃgāni paṃcaṣaḍcakra vinyaset paṃcapaṃcakam |
pūrvoktāṣṭāṣṭakaṃ bhūyorukmiṇyādi tathā vasūn ||

keśavādyādinījanpāśaktiḥ kṣeṃśumeva ca |
śikhipicchasamāvaddha nīlanīlālakuṃtalam ||

īsavasmitamukhaṃ rakta caṃdrarekhā vibhūṣitam |
dvātriṃśallakṣaṇairyuktaṃ sarvagaṃ sarvagaṃ prabham ||

nānābharaṇasaṃdīptaṃ cakraśaṃkhasuveṇukam |
nitambabiṃbavilasat pītāṃvaradharaṃ hariṃ ||

bhrājamānaṃ śriyābhūmyā śrīvatsādyupaśobhitam |
ketucakrāṃbujaḥ śaṃkhaḥ svastikāṃkita padadvayam ||

gopīgopaśarāṇāṃ hi vṛṃdavṛṃdārakāvṛtam |
vasiṣṭanāradādyaiśca vṛttaṃ devāṃ ganājanaiḥ ||

p. 133b)

iti saṃcintyamanasā yajediṣṭvā svaśaktibhiḥ |
vāsudevādi nādyantaṃ mūrtibhiḥ keśavādikaiḥ ||

dadhihaiyaṃgavīnāśaiḥ kṛśarāntaṃ nivedayet |
nūpopavārasaṃyuktaṃ yathā prāgīritakramai ||

brahmāviṣṇuśca rudraśca gāyatrīṃ tvagnikoṇake |
tadbījapūrvakaṃ devi sāvitrīṃ ca sarasvatīm ||

evaṃ lakṣacatuṣkaṃ hi juhuyācca daśāṃśataḥ |
sādhukṣitaṃ paṃkajaṃ paṃkajavillamathavātilapāyasam ||

varṣaistribhiḥ sureśāni japatarpaṇahomataḥ |
etaistribhirupāyaistu ājñayāsau prasīdati ||

punarāvṛttihīnaṃ ca vakṣyehaṃ śṛṇusuṃdari |
devībījaṃ maheśāni biṃdumadhyasthasūkṣmagaḥ ||

sa evagaṃdhasaṃyuktaṃ kātvammadhyaṃ sa daṃḍavat |
grahamadhyasthitaṃ kuryādyantipānoṃtasūkṣmagam ||

māninīdaṃḍamūlaṃ ca prathamaṃ tattagrataḥ |
śukla ādisaṃbhinnaḥ punastaṃ kevalaṃ bhavet ||

ekādaśāṃtaṃ purato urumadhyāṃtakārayet |
dordaṃḍamaṃ caiviṃdūrddhegaṃdhānaṃ tenasaṃyutaṃ ||

māṃsarūpapadaṃ devi ghanaśyāmaṃ ca dāsakam |
bhagavanviṣṇu ṅejante calāṃgalī cāruśāsanaḥ ||

uttaraktau sthitau bhūyo vedavarṇe śive nyaset |
lokādiśirasākrāṃta māyāṃtaṃdayitāgrataḥ ||

aṃgānimanubhūtārṇaiḥ punastribhistribhiḥ kramāt |
raṃdhreṣu prativarṇāni daśasthāneṣu ṣaḍdaśa ||

paṃśca ślokasamāyuktaṃ prāṇemānaṃ niyojayet |
tāpikaṃ bhāskaraṃ devaṃ nepadāmadibhūṣitam ||

jālālakavarāsnigdha śikhipicchāvataṃsakam |
p. 134a) pītakauṣeya vasanaṃ maṃdasmeropaśobhitam ||

pādukeratna khacite padbhyāṃ vānyasta śakṛtaiḥ |
sthitaṃ niktapriyāsakta vāmapādastanādadhaḥ ||

anenapaśuyaṣṭiṃ ca lokānāṃ rakṣaṇāni ca |
pūrvavatpūjitaṃ juṣṭaṃ hutaṃ maṃtraṃ svasiddhidaṃ ||

sitamālyānulepaśca sitaṃ vastravibhūṣitaḥ |
sitapadmasthito maunī dūrayuktamano bhuvaḥ ||

sitadravyāṇi saṃpādyaviṣṭare mṛgacarmaṇi |
sthitvānu pratyahaṃ devaṃ dhanadaṃ vā vicintayet ||

kaivalyapadamāpnoti satyamevaṃ varānane |
sarvamaṃtra prasiddhāṃtaṃ mātṛkāmastrayogataḥ ||

maṃtreṣu japyamāneṣu yatramānopataddhataḥ |
vratāni vividhānyevamācaredbhakti tatparaḥ ||

bhaktihīne nare devi bhasmanī bahutaṃ haviḥ |
athānyaṃ saṃpravakṣyāmi tavasnehena suṃdari ||

krūrāstu mānavāḥ sarve mohayaṃti parasparam |
na prakāśyamiyaṃ vidyā devānāmapi durlabhā ||

amartyādānavāyuktā manuṣyāśca tapodhanāḥ |
somavidyā prabhāvena svecchayā carate mahīṃ ||

asyaprabhāvamatulaṃ kaṃpate sa carācaram |
tarpayitvā kramaṃsarvaṃ sahasraṃ pratyahaṃ japet ||

dīkṣayitvā śiśuṃ śuddhaṃ vidyā tasmaidadekaḥ * |
uddhāraṃ tatra kurvīta atramerau suśobhane ||

kailāsamaṃdare devi vāmāṃtaṃ krūramastakam |
asyasaṃgraṃthitaṃ rudraṃ mānavatvaṃ samālikhet ||

āsanaṃ cakracorddhaṃ ca dīpanī krūramālikhet |
niṣadhe vedavarṇe tu śivobhinnaṃ tu kārayet ||

śivaśaktiṃ sasaṃyogya japaṃtī kṣajjamadhyagaḥ |
bhairave ajaradrasya a * satvena dīpanī ||

p. 134b)

yāmomadhyaṃ tu vāgiśaṃ dhruvāṃtaṃ āmanaṃ dahet |
ekādaśāṃtasaṃbhinnaṃ lokādi mastake nyaset ||

etadvīravarābhadreṃ sṛṣṭiṃ saṃhārakārikā |
dakṣāṃgulisamūha * makṣaraṃ bhairavānane ||

tasyāthadeśasaṃbhinnaṃ devī bījaṃ tu kārayet |
punarevamaheśāni rudraṃ viṣṇuṃ pitāmahaṃ ||

somavidyāṃ samādāya sadyaḥ saṃstutpadāyikam |
mahādāridryadaurbhāgyaniśanādi vimokṣaṇī ||

cakramabhyarcyavidhivadajarāmaratāṃ labhet |
vasuvṛttaṃ likhenmadhye tvaṣṭa patraṃ śaroruham ||

kālāraṃkarṇikāyāṃ ca piṃḍarūpaṃ tu vinyaset |
kalāstu vinyasedādau ṣoḍaśāre sa biṃdukam ||

dvitīyaṃ hṛdayasthaṃ ca tṛtīyaṃ ca dalaṃ prati |
caturthaṃ tṛtīye tu caturthadalapaṃktike ||

lāṃgalī paṃcame vṛtte medarudraṃ sa biṃdukam |
ṣaṣṭhe tu śuklaṃ vinyasya saptame aṃbikā bhavet ||

kāmakrūraśirasthā tu tadvahiḥ sākṣarāṇi tu |
ṣoḍasānyatha vṛttāni biṃduyuktāni vinyaset ||

taddale vasuvṛttasya nṛdhādi patiṃ likhet |
chadāgreṣu śaśijvālaṃ sudhābījānvitaṃ likhet ||

likhitvaivaṃ mahācakraṃ arcayet somamīśvarīm |
susame bhūmibhāge tu nimnontata vivarjite ||

gomayā lepite śuddhe darpaṇodarasannibhe |
vitāne śubhraśobhāḍhye cāmarādisuśobhite ||

sugaṃdhi caṃdanā lipte mālatī kusumānvite |
p. 135a) puṣpamāse subhelagne paurṇamāsyāṃ tu sādhakaḥ ||

gurordikṣāṃ ca saṃpādya maṃtrī saṃyatamānasaḥ |
apāṃvāmaṃḍalaṃ kṛtvā tanmadhye sthaṃḍilaṃ likhet ||

akṣakaiḥ sa catuṣkoṇairmaḍalaṃ pūrvavat punaḥ |
cakramasminsamālikhya madhye kuṃḍaṃ suśobhanam ||

arkalakṣaṃ dṛḍhaṃ śuddhaṃ taṃ tu triguṇaveṣṭitam |
navaratnāni niḥkṣipya droṇatoyaprapūritam ||

hayādvīni cāṃgānānakaśānāḍhakodakam |
aṣṭau bāhyasya vṛttasthāṃ niṣkordvādaśakaṃ mitam ||

tadarddhārddhasaṃkhyābhistu ṣoḍaśābāhyamaṃḍale |
raupyeṇanirmitaṃ rūpaṃ madhyakuṃbhe vinikṣipet ||

kalaśeṣu nyeṣu cākṣipyaratnānāṃ navakaṃ kramāt |
dukūlayugmaiḥ saṃveṣṭya tatruddhautana vai kramāt ||

kṛtvākṣatā sugaṃdhādyaiḥ puṣpāṇi sakalānyapi |
madhyakuṃbhe samābāhya somaṃsaṃpūjyasādaram ||

smṛtvā kuṃbhaṃ japed vidyāṃ sahasraṃ cāṣṭaśataṃ tu vā |
maṃtrāgniṃ ca samādhāya somakuṃḍe caturṣvapi ||

catvāro ṛtvijastatra puṇyāhaṃ vācayet tathā |
samāpyaśikhinaḥ pūjāṃ juhūyānmadhurokṣitaiḥ ||

sarpiṣāpāyase nāpi śālitaṃḍulakaistilaiḥ |
tāvadbhistarpaṇaṃ devi janaiḥ śuddhaiśca saṃyutaḥ ||

nyāsatrayaṃ purākṛtvā ātmānaṃ mantravigraham |
khapaṃkaje piṃḍamāpūritaṃ ra tathā vai śiṣyavigraham ||

saṃhṛtyotpādyaniribhiḥ śuddhāyai maṃtraṃ samādiśet |
kṛtvābhiṣekaṃ vidhivajjapenmaṃtramathā yutam ||

aṣṭottarasahasraṃ vā kṛtvā vāgavaśājjapam |
mālatī jāti punnāgaṃ rājāpadmaṃ madhukṣitaiḥ ||

p. 136a)

evaṃ kṛte mahāvidyā siddhausau vatsarāṃtare |
māsatrayavidhānena sarvasiddhimavāpnuyāt ||

guruvargo hi labhyaṃ hi devatā maṃtrasaṃhitāḥ |
tenadattenaguravaḥ pūjanīyāḥ prayatnataḥ || 0 ||


iti śrīkubjikāmateṣaṭpaṃcāśatitamaḥ paṭalaḥ samāptaḥ || 0 ||
